### [1] [Cross-Benchmark Generalization in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00181)
*Sushant Mehta,Logan Ritchie,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Two-stage SFT→RL post-training on diverse long-horizon multi-tool tasks yields transferable behavioral improvements in a large MoE model, evidenced by gains on multiple external benchmarks and recurring cross-domain working-pattern changes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agents trained in self-contained RL environments often exploit environment-specific regularities (tool schemas, grader parsing, task templates) to get reward rather than learning transferable skill. The authors argue the core question should be behavioral — how the agent acts — and that cross-benchmark transfer is the appropriate evaluation to detect genuine changes in working style.

Method: Selected an open-weight MoE base (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) informed by Toolathlon for base-family and SFT-teacher choices, then applied supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning on 363 MCP tasks spanning 27 categories. No external-benchmark tasks or graders influenced training rewards, hyperparameters, checkpoint selection, or stopping. Evaluation used greedy pass@1 on multiple external benchmarks; an exploratory paired-trajectory qualitative analysis identified recurring behavioral differences.

Result: Post-training an open-weight MoE model (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) on 363 long-horizon MCP tasks across 27 categories with a two-stage SFT-then-RL pipeline produced consistent improvements at greedy pass@1 on five external benchmarks: Toolathlon (+9.6 pp), τ^2-Bench (+5.3 pp), BFCL-V4 (+3.5 pp), SWE-Bench Pro (+5.8 pp), and Terminal-Bench 2 (+2.8 pp). Notably, software-engineering benchmarks improved despite no software-engineering tasks in the training set. Paired-trajectory analysis found four recurring behavioral shifts (careful local-goal formation, constructing goal-relevant working state, stabilizing parent goals during local repairs, and verifying completion) that generalize across domains.

Conclusion: Long-horizon multi-tool post-training can change an agent’s working patterns in ways that transfer beyond the training domain; the evidence is descriptive (behavioral patterns and cross-benchmark score gains) rather than a causal mechanistic account, and further controlled analyses are needed.

Abstract: For reinforcement learning (RL) in self-contained environments, a policy can get rewards by exploiting environment-specific regularities (tool schemas, grader parsing, task templates) rather than by acquiring transferable skill, and an in-distribution holdout shares those regularities. We argue that the discriminating question is behavioral, namely how a trained agent acts, and that cross-benchmark transfer is the right place to look for it. We post-train an open-weight mixture-of-experts model (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) on 363 long-horizon Model Context Protocol (MCP) tasks across 27 categories, using a two-stage SFT-then-RL pipeline. Toolathlon performance informed the initial base-family and SFT-teacher choices, but no external-benchmark task or grader entered training and no external score informed the reward, training hyperparameters, trained-checkpoint selection, or stopping. At greedy pass@1, the trained model improves over the base on five reported external evaluations: Toolathlon (+9.6 pp), $τ^2$-Bench (+5.3 pp), BFCL-V4 (+3.5 pp), SWE-Bench Pro (+5.8 pp), and Terminal-Bench 2 (+2.8 pp). Both software-engineering benchmarks improve despite the training collection containing no software-engineering tasks. An exploratory paired-trajectory analysis identifies four recurring behavioral differences (more careful local-goal formation, building goal-relevant working state, keeping parent goals stable through local repairs, and verifying completion) that appear in analogous forms across office workflows and code. These results provide descriptive evidence that long-horizon multi-tool post-training can change ways of working that transfer beyond its training domain.

</details>


### [2] [Source Code Authorship Attribution Does Not Generalize from Competitions to Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00227)
*Serhii Yemets,Marek Horváth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在竞赛数据上效果好、在课程作业上几乎无效——说明竞赛基准不能外推到教育场景，需要在目标域上做验证与适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验现有基于竞赛数据的源码作者归属研究在实际教学环境（防抄袭、作业鉴定）中的适用性与鲁棒性，评估基准数据的外推性。

Method: 将预训练模型CodeBERT在三类数据上微调：公开的Kaggle GCJ提交、经整理的GCJ归档，以及某理工大学采集的课程作业数据；在多轮GCJ与课程作业上评估Top-k准确率，并用一个多模型基准在额外配置上复现试验以检验普适性。

Result: 对比实验证明：在编程竞赛数据（GCJ）上微调的CodeBERT能达到很高的作者识别率（例如10作者Top-1=92.6%，1000作者Top-1=70.7%），但在真实教学作业数据上表现接近或低于随机基线（如690作者Top-1=0.2%，812作者Top-1=0.06%）。多模型基准测试显示该差距并非CodeBERT特有，在其它模型家族上也存在。作者分析了数据集与任务属性（如题目统一性、代码规模、样式可识别性等）并得出结论：以GCJ为基础的基准会高估在教育场景中源码作者归属的实用性，必须在目标课程上下文中验证。

Conclusion: GCJ类数据集提供的高性能并不能代表在课堂作业中的可用性；数据分布与任务设置差异是主要原因，研究者应避免盲目用竞赛数据评估教学场景的源码作者归属方法。

Abstract: Source code authorship attribution aims to identify the author of a program fragment from its writing style. We fine-tune the pre-trained transformer CodeBERT on three sources of data: publicly available Google Code Jam (GCJ) submissions from an open Kaggle repository, a curated GCJ archive, and institutional coursework datasets collected at a technical university. On multi-round GCJ data, CodeBERT reaches 92.6% Top-1 accuracy for 10 authors and retains 70.7% Top-1 (88.2% Top-10) for 1000 authors. On the examined coursework datasets, the same pipeline performs at or below the corresponding chance baselines: 0.2% Top-1 on a closed-assignment dataset of 690 authors and 0.06% Top-1 on open-ended assignments evaluated over 812 authors. A companion multi-model benchmark provides consistent cross-model evidence across additional dataset configurations, indicating that the observed performance gap is not specific to CodeBERT and persists across the evaluated model families. We analyze dataset and task properties that plausibly explain this gap and argue that GCJ-based benchmarks overestimate the practical applicability of authorship attribution in educational settings unless they are validated on the target coursework context.

</details>


### [3] [Modelling Android applications through static analysis and systematic exploratory testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00228)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过将传统静态分析与高效的系统化探索性测试结合，DroidGraph 构建更全面的 Android 控制流模型，从而显著超越随机探索在交互覆盖与代码级模型丰富性上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用快速发布要求自动化测试。纯静态分析受 Android 事件驱动与多样实现机制限制；纯随机动态测试低效且知识贫乏。为此需一种混合方法生成更全面的应用模型以改进自动测试生成。

Method: 先用传统静态分析构建应用的初始模型（从方法语句到 UI 结构），再运行高效的系统化探索性测试以收集运行时事件与回调信息，最后将动态观测与静态结果整合，输出包含更多组件与回调链接的控制流模型。

Result: DroidGraph 用静态分析结合系统化探索性动态测试生成 Android 应用的详细控制流模型。在 19 个应用上的评估显示：平均比常用的随机探索（random exploration）多交互 18% 的界面元素且用更少的交互（平均少 345 次）；结合动态分析后，平均额外发现 51 个组件和 49% 更多的界面回调关联。

Conclusion: 将动态探索结果补充到静态分析中可以显著提高对 Android 应用界面和回调关系的识别，进而支持更高效的模型驱动自动测试生成。

Abstract: Mobile application development is a fast paced industry with frequent releases. While the development pace increases, so too does the need for automated test generation. Model-based test generation is one of the most common and successful approaches to support this need. Understanding and modelling the application under test is integral to producing comprehensive, dependable and effective tests. Unfortunately, mobile platforms such as Android, introduce a host of difficulties. Static analysis struggles with Android's event-based nature and the growing variety of mechanisms available for developers to implement different features. Additionally, dynamic analysis, implemented by popular random test generators, is slow, inefficient, and limited by a lack of application knowledge.
  This paper introduces DroidGraph, a framework to generate a comprehensive control flow model of Android applications using traditional static analysis and efficient systematic exploratory tests. DroidGraph provides a detailed model of an Android application, from low level method statements to high level user interface structures. This model can be used to support automated test generation. We apply DroidGraph to 19 diverse apps and show that our efficient exploratory tests, on average, interact with 18% more of the app than commonly used random exploration in 345 less interactions. Integrating the dynamic analysis results provided by these tests complements our static analysis, and uncovers on average 51 more components and 49% more interface callback links in the application code.

</details>


### [4] [PADRAIG: Precise Android Automated Input Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00230)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者提出了基于模型的Android自动输入生成框架PADRAIG，利用详细的控制流模型生成测试，号称在19个F‑Droid应用上比3个现有工具平均多覆盖16%代码行并将生成运行时降低84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法权衡：随机法易维护但效率低，系统化/搜索法覆盖好但生成耗时大，模型法建模有额外开销但有潜力实现更快的测试生成。

Method: 构建被测应用的详细控制流模型（model-based），基于该模型进行测试输入生成；与三种代表性工具（分别代表随机、系统化/搜索型等方法）在19个开源Android应用上比较线覆盖率与生成运行时。

Result: 在19个F‑Droid应用上，PADRAIG平均提升线覆盖16%，并将测试生成平均运行时降低84%。

Conclusion: PADRAIG通过构建详细控制流模型，能在更短生成时间内获得更高线覆盖率，相比随机、系统化和搜索型工具具有显著优势。

Abstract: Android automated test input generation has been a highly researched topic for over a decade and has shown promising results with a variety of approaches. Random input generation is commonly used and the easiest to maintain, but ultimately inefficient. Systematic and search-based approaches produce effective tests but require a disproportionally large generation runtime. Model-based approaches have the additional overhead of modelling the application under test (AUT) but they result in a faster test generation.
  In this paper we present Precise AnDRoid Automated Input Generation (PADRAIG), a model-based test input generation framework that uses a detailed control flow model of the AUT to generate tests that can achieve higher line coverage, with a lower test generation runtime than the state of the art. We compare the line coverage achieved, and the generation runtime of PADRAIG against 3 state of the art tools, each of which uses a different test input generation technique. Our results, using 19 randomly selected Android apps from the F-Droid application store, show that PADRAIG achieves, on average, 16% more coverage of the AUT than the state of the art and it can generate tests with, on average, 84% less runtime.

</details>


### [5] [Improving Mobile User Interface Testing with Model Driven Monkey Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00236)
*Jordan Doyle,Takfarinas Saber,Paolo Arcaini,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Build a control-flow model of Android UI elements and use DFS-guided exploration to replace random events; delivers much faster, higher-coverage testing (Monkey++ vs. random Monkey).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Random input generators like Exerciser Monkey lack application structure awareness, leading to slow, low-coverage tests; a model capturing interactive elements can guide testing more effectively.

Method: Control-flow modeling + DFS-guided testing

Result: A control-flow structure for Android apps that encodes interactive elements; integrating it into Exerciser Monkey (Monkey++) and using DFS yields test suites that are ~10x faster and achieve full interface coverage while removing redundant tests.

Conclusion: Structure-aware, DFS-driven testing significantly improves efficiency and coverage over random event generators, and enables more targeted automated testing techniques.

Abstract: Testing mobile applications often relies on tools, such as Exerciser Monkey for Android systems, that simulate user input. Exerciser Monkey, for example, generates random events (e.g., touches, gestures, navigational keys) that give developers a sense of what their application will do when deployed on real mobile phones with real users interacting with it. These tools, however, have no knowledge of the underlying applications' structures and only interact with them randomly or in a predefined manner (e.g., if developers designed scenarios, a labour-intensive task) -- making them slow and poor at finding bugs.
  In this paper, we propose a novel control flow structure able to represent the code of Android applications, including all the interactive elements. We show that our structure can increase the effectiveness (higher coverage) and efficiency (removing duplicate/redundant tests) of the Exerciser Monkey by giving it knowledge of the test environment. We compare the interface coverage achieved by the Exerciser Monkey with our new Monkey++ using a depth first search of our control flow structure and show that while the random nature of Exerciser Monkey creates slow test suites of poor coverage, the test suite created by a depth first search is one order of magnitude faster and achieves full coverage of the user interaction elements. We believe this research will lead to a more effective and efficient Exerciser Monkey, as well as better targeted search based techniques for automated Android testing.

</details>


### [6] [SMS Opt-In/Opt-Out Consent Record Architecture in Enterprise CRM Systems: Compliance Patterns for Multi-Tenant Managed Packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00248)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一种适用于CRM原生多租户托管包的同意记录架构，包含数据模型、基于关键字的接收同意捕获、哈希唯一性去重、发送时抑制与多租户约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级短信系统受法律（如TCPA）约束，需要可证明的显式opt-in/opt-out记录，而常见CRM并未将同意作为第一类实体建模，多租户托管包需提供通用解决方案。

Method: 在CRM托管包内部定义同意记录对象与字段，使用关键字解析来捕获来自短信的同/退订意图；对标识符采用哈希去重策略；在发送管道上实施实时抑制检查；并遵循多租户安装包不能修改宿主组织架构的约束。

Result: 在生产环境跨医疗、金融与销售等行业部署并运行，满足合规与实用性需求，但需进一步完善哈希隐私、审计链与高吞吐抑制策略。

Conclusion: 该架构在受限的多租户环境中可行，满足TCPA类法规对显式记录与即时撤销的要求，但在哈希策略、跨租户去重与审计不可变性等方面需谨慎设计以兼顾隐私、性能与可证明合规性。

Abstract: Regulatory frameworks such as the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA) impose strict consent requirements on enterprise messaging systems: organizations must obtain and record explicit opt-in consent before sending SMS communications, and must immediately honor opt-out requests. While CRM platforms provide rich contact and lead data models, they do not natively model consent state as a first-class record type. This gap becomes architecturally significant in multi-tenant managed packages distributed via enterprise application marketplaces, where the package cannot assume or modify the installing organization's schema. This paper presents a production consent record architecture, generalized from patterns implemented within a CRM-native messaging managed package serving independent enterprise organizations spanning healthcare, financial services, and sales operations. We describe the data model design, keyword-based consent capture, a hash-based uniqueness strategy for deduplication, suppression enforcement at message send time, and the multi-tenant constraints that shaped these design decisions.

</details>


### [7] [LoopsBench: From Harness Engineering to Loop Engineering in Benchmarking Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00267)
*Han Li,Zhemin Fang,Rili Feng,Yingqi Zhao,Jiaheng Liu,Pengfei Gao,He Ye,Dayi Lin,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LOOPSBENCH 是一个针对“循环工程”（loop engineering）的长远基准，任务由具有源证据的依赖有向无环图构成，运行时沿“就绪前沿”释放测试并保留已完成节点作为回归义务。数据集包含112个真实任务、8种语言和9个领域。评测显示最强配置解决25%的任务，计划仅恢复部分先决DAG，且仍存在回归事件。代码与数据开源于 microsoft/Loopsbench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编码代理从一次性“驾驭工程”转向持续、多循环的“循环工程”，现有基准过于关注局部任务或终态结果，无法反映长时程执行中的依赖传播、回归与持续验证负担，故需新基准评估代理在长期开发中的表现。

Method: 构建基于源证据的依赖DAG，将开发单元拆分为可单独测试的节点；设计流感知运行时，按就绪前沿逐步释放测试并把通过节点作为回归义务保留；收集112个真实任务并在多语言、多领域上评估多种前沿代理与循环策略。

Result: LOOPSBENCH 包含 >5,300 个开发单元和可执行测试，评测表明最强配置（Opus-4.7 + Claude Code + outer continuation）解决率为25%，记录的计划无法完全重建源恢复的先决DAG，且不同循环剖面均观测到回归事件；全部数据与代码开源。

Conclusion: LOOPSBENCH 提供了更贴近长期软件开发场景的基准——强调依赖流和回归义务，但现有前沿代理与循环实现尚无法稳健解决大多数任务，暴露出 DAG 恢复不完全和回归管理的缺陷。

Abstract: Coding agent infrastructure is shifting from harness engineering toward loop engineering as coding agents are deployed for sustained long-horizon software development. Existing benchmarks often center on localized tasks or end-state outcomes, offering limited insight into sustained execution. We introduce LOOPSBENCH, a long-horizon benchmark for loop engineering in coding agent evaluation. Each task is a dependency DAG over separately testable development units with source-evidenced prerequisite edges. LOOPSBENCH comprises 112 tasks from authentic sources spanning 8 programming languages and 9 domains. Its flow-aware runtime releases tests along the ready frontier and retains completed nodes as regression obligations. We evaluate frontier coding agents paired with widely used loop implementations. The strongest configuration, Opus-4.7 with Claude Code and outer continuation, resolves 25.00% of tasks. Recorded plans recover only part of the source-recovered prerequisite DAG, and regression events remain visible across the evaluated loop profiles. We open source the benchmark data and code, including all tasks, more than 5,300 development units, and executable tests, at microsoft/Loopsbench.

</details>


### [8] [OrEdge: Efficient Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection in Distributed Software Systems via Orthogonal-Domain Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00309)
*Amr M. Zaki,Farhoud Jafari Kaleibar,Honggeun Ji,Komal Sarda,Marin Litoiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OrEdge 用正交域时间建模替代注意力/图结构，联合处理日志/指标/链路追踪，实现极小参数量（≤9.6K）和在 Raspberry Pi 上的亚秒实时检测，同时保持竞争性的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态异常检测方法多依赖注意力机制或图/训练成本高且参数量大，难以在边缘设备上实时运行。需要一个在保证检测性能的同时显著减小参数量和推理延迟、适配 Raspberry Pi 等资源受限平台的轻量化替代方案。

Method: 提出轻量级框架 OrEdge，以正交域时间表示（orthogonal-domain temporal representations）为核心，联合分析日志、指标和链路追踪等多模态观测数据；核心模块为 OrEdgeCore，一种轻量的正交域重构模块，用于捕捉周期性/重复的时序模式并抑制瞬态噪声，从而实现异常检测。设计目标是通过正交基变换减少信号冗余与模型复杂度，避开计算代价高昂的注意力和图神经网络结构，以便在资源受限的边缘设备上实时部署。

Result: 在三个真实微服务数据集（MSDS、SN、TT）上达到有竞争力的检测效果，同时将重构模块参数量缩减至至多 9.6K（对比现有方法的 20K–143K）；在 Raspberry Pi 平台实现亚秒级推理，推理延迟比现有方法低一个数量级以上。并通过消融、灵敏度分析、正交基评估与定性案例研究验证各组件有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 正交域时序建模为多模态微服务异常检测提供了一个有效且高效的替代路径，在检测准确性与计算效率之间实现良好权衡，适合边缘环境下的实时部署。

Abstract: We introduce Orthogonal-Edge (OrEdge), a lightweight framework for real-time anomaly detection in multi-modal distributed software systems. Unlike existing approaches that rely on computationally expensive attention- and graph-based architectures, OrEdge leverages orthogonal-domain temporal representations to achieve accurate anomaly detection with substantially lower computational complexity and model size. It jointly analyzes heterogeneous monitoring data, including logs, metrics, and traces, to identify abnormal software behavior, capture temporal dependencies, and reduce redundancy across observability signals. At its core, OrEdge incorporates OrEdgeCore, a lightweight orthogonal-domain reconstruction module that captures recurring temporal patterns while suppressing transient variations. Evaluated on three real-world microservice datasets (MSDS, SN, and TT), OrEdge achieves competitive detection performance while reducing the reconstruction model size to at most 9.6K parameters, compared with 20K--143K parameters in existing methods. This compact design enables efficient deployment on resource-constrained edge devices: on Raspberry Pi platforms, OrEdge achieves sub-second inference and reduces inference latency by over an order of magnitude compared with existing approaches. Extensive ablation studies, sensitivity analyses, orthogonal basis evaluations, and qualitative case studies further validate the effectiveness of each design component. Overall, OrEdge demonstrates that orthogonal-domain temporal modeling provides an effective alternative to computationally intensive attention- and graph-based architectures, achieving a favorable balance between detection accuracy and computational efficiency for real-time multi-modal anomaly detection in edge environments. The code is available at https://github.com/theamrzaki/MicroService_Twin_Original.

</details>


### [9] [Artificial Intelligence and Modeling & Simulation: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00366)
*Niclas Feldkamp,Philippe J. Giabbanelli,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该报告系统性梳理了人工智能（AI）与建模与仿真（M&S）之间的双向关系，指出AI可增强或替代仿真环节，而仿真可作为AI的数据源与测试床，并提出按M&S生命周期分层的分析与路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动因包括：两学科研究需求趋同、生成式AI等技术快速发展、以及数据与计算资源显著增加，促使探索AI如何改变仿真实践和仿真如何服务AI研究。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法：沿M&S的生命周期（模型规格、输入建模、执行、试验设计、V&V、输出分析）逐阶段筛选并举例说明AI技术的应用，结合若干代表性研究并提出概念性路线图以帮助读者导航生态。

Result: 核心发现包括：1) AI能支持仿真各环节（自动建模、数据驱动参数化、代理模型、智能实验设计、后处理分析等）；2) 仿真可作为安全可控的AI训练与评估环境；3) 生成式模型与LLM在仿真文档生成、需求解析与场景构造上已展现价值；4) 若干重要问题未解（V&V、可解释性、分布外泛化、计算成本与伦理/安全问题），并提出了一个面向研究者与工程师的概念性路线图。

Conclusion: AI与M&S正深度融合：一方面AI技术（尤其生成式模型、LLM等）能在建模、参数估计、执行与结果分析等环节提供显著提升；另一方面仿真为AI提供可靠的数据生成、训练与评估环境。融合虽有潜力，但存在可解释性、验证/验证（V&V）、数据偏差与计算成本等关键挑战。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and Modeling & Simulation (M&S) are increasingly intertwined, reflecting converging research needs across both communities, rapid technological advances such as the rise of generative AI, and the growing availability of data and computational resources. This report provides a structured overview of the intersections of AI and M&S. The relationship goes both ways: AI can support, augment, or even replace components of simulation studies, while simulations can serve as data generators, training environments, and evaluation platforms for AI. We organize this landscape along the stages of M&S from model specification and input modeling to execution, experimentation, verification and validation, and output analysis. Selected studies at each stage illustrates how techniques such as Large Language Models have reshaped simulation practices, while highlighting limitations and open challenges. This report also provides a conceptual roadmap that helps readers navigate a rapidly changing ecosystem.

</details>


### [10] [CASPER-Change-Aware Slice Prioritization for Efficient Regression Testing of LLM-based systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00378)
*Biruk Asmare Muse,Lionel Briand,Yiwei Lu,Keheliya Gallaba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过进化式切片识别与基于日志的变更感知优先级排序，CASPER能更高效地发现并优先检测LLM系统在prompt或模型变更后受影响的行为切片，实验表明其在一致性和回归发现效率上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统的回归测试两端都存在难题：单个测试用例的失败信息太少，难以指示系统级回归或定位受影响行为；仅依赖整体性能变化又过于粗糙，无法指出哪些行为退化。因而需要一个中间粒度（test-suite slices）来更好地识别和优先检测回归。

Method: 提出了CASPER，一种change-aware的切片优先级框架：首先用进化切片识别算法（evolutionary slice identification）在测试集上找出语义上相关且性能特征一致的回归切片；针对给定的prompt或模型变更，基于执行日志中提取的行为信息，对切片按回归可能性进行优先级排序。论文在软件问题定位/解决领域实现并评估该框架，比较对象包括基于聚类的切片识别基线和随机排序基线。

Result: CASPER在实验中比聚类基线生成了更一致的切片，同时在语义一致性上保持相当或更好的表现；在回归切片优先级排序上，相较于随机基线在不同类型的LLM变更和不同测试预算下都有改进。

Conclusion: 将测试用例按语义/性能一致的切片组织并基于变更与执行行为进行优先级排序，是应对LLM回归测试信息稀薄与粗粒度指标不足问题的有效策略；CASPER证明了切片化+变更感知优先级在实际领域（软件问题解决）中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Regression testing for LLM-based systems poses unique challenges because individual regression instances provide limited information about system-level regressions. A failure in a single instance does not necessarily indicate a meaningful regression or provide sufficient information to diagnose affected behaviors. Conversely, detecting regressions based only on overall system performance changes is too coarse-grained, as it does not identify which behaviors are affected. This motivates analyzing regression instances at an intermediate level through test suite slices.
  To address this challenge, we propose CASPER, a change-aware slice prioritization framework for efficient regression testing of prompt-level and model-level changes in LLM-based systems. CASPER first identifies regression slices containing semantically related instances with consistent performance characteristics using an evolutionary slice identification approach. Given a change to an LLM-based application, CASPER prioritizes slices according to their likelihood of regression using behavioral information extracted from execution logs. We instantiate CASPER in the software issue resolution domain and evaluate slice identification against clustering-based baselines and regressed slice prioritization against a random ranking baseline. Results show that CASPER generates more consistent slices while maintaining comparable or better semantic coherence and improves regressed slice prioritization across different LLM-based system changes and testing budgets.

</details>


### [11] [Distilling Reasoning Traces into Advisory Prompts for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00437)
*Faizan Faisal,Prem Devanbu,Toufique Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过从少量启用“思考”的成功示例中抽取并由大模型提炼成简短建议提示，可在不持续启用昂贵思考模式的情况下，显著减少中小型语言模型的代码错误，且提示在一定程度上可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成与处理的语言模型会出错，且训练数据包含大量人类编写（可能有缺陷）的代码。推理/思考模式能减少错误但资源消耗高。目标是找到在不额外训练且不总是启用昂贵“思考”模式下，降低模型出错率的推理时方法。

Method: 步骤包括：1）在目标小/中等模型上运行两种模式（普通与思考/chain-of-thought），识别思考模式能纠正的错误案例；2）将这些对比案例交给更大模型，让其生成关于为何思考能避免错误的说明；3）用大模型将说明汇总并压缩为简短的、可直接作为提示的建议语句（advisory prompts）；4）把这些提示回用到原模型或其他模型的输入端，评测错误率下降与迁移效果；5）对模型出错类型做分类、并分析何类错误最能从该流程中受益。

Result: 提出一种流程：在小/中等模型中识别“思考模式”能避免的错误案例；由更大模型对这些案例生成理由解释，再将解释浓缩为简短的“建议型提示”（advisory prompts）；将提示用于推理时替代持续启用思考模式。实验显示该方法能在许多中小型模型上提升性能，某些提示可迁移到其他模型。并给出模型常见编码错误的分析和该方法何时有效的表征。

Conclusion: 该方法为一种资源高效的替代方案：通过把“思考中发现的纠错理由”内化为简短可复用的提示，能够在许多情况下复现思考带来的收益。但效果依赖模型规模、错误类型与提示质量，对需要深度探索或复杂搜索的问题帮助有限。建议把该方法与选择性触发思考模式结合使用，并注意构建多样且有代表性的示例集以提高迁移性。

Abstract: Language models are widely used for generating and otherwise processing code (e.g., identifying code hallucinations, possible inputs, or predicting outputs); however, LLMs can make mistakes, which can be serious. One key issue is that models are trained on (still) largely human-written, and thus imperfect, code; it's not easy to find sufficiently large code corpora that are entirely free of bugs. Thus, other inference-time ways of reducing LLM errors, without additional training, are desirable. "Reasoning" or "thinking" modes, exposed as a togglable feature by hybrid reasoning models, do reduce errors; however, reasoning consumes additional resources. This paper asks if better performance can be achieved without always incurring the cost of reasoning. Human students of programming learn to avoid mistakes by (a) identifying them, (b) reflecting upon the cognitive lapses that led to them (essentially, "thinking through" the errors), (c) inferring general rules or lessons from these reflections, and (d) internalizing these lessons into rules. In tutorial sessions with an instructor, this is a common Socratic interaction. Examples of such internalizable rules might include the nugget "Before coding, restate the requirements to clarify them." Inspired by this process, this paper describes an approach where we first identify examples in which "thinking mode" in a (low-resource) LLM avoids errors. These errors, and their avoidance via "thinking" in the same LLM, are then examined by a bigger LLM to generate summary explanations; these are then summarized by a large LLM into brief advisory prompts. This approach works on many modest-sized models; in some cases, the "advisory prompts" thus learned can also be gainfully transferred to other models. We also present investigations into the nature of coding errors that language models make, and a characterization of when this approach can be helpful.

</details>


### [12] [AiFlow: Token-Native Reactive Orchestration with Bounded Backpressure for Streaming LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00558)
*Qunhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly operate as streaming workflows combining retrieval, tool calls, safety filters, and multi-agent coordination. Although contemporary frameworks expose provider deltas, workflow nodes often treat generation as coarse request-response steps, leaving queue management, worker allocation, ordering, and backpressure to ad hoc callback code. This paper presents AiFlow, a token-native reactive orchestration model that normalizes provider deltas into typed Context<T> events propagated through a directed streaming graph. Each node is managed by a Node Guardian that declares and enforces local queue bounds, worker concurrency, ordering, overflow policy, cancellation propagation, and retry discipline. We formalize the bounded-memory property, present the compilation from a compact DSL and JSON graph form, and provide static validation for type safety, state concurrency, and injection compatibility. Controlled microbenchmarks, captured DeepSeek trace replay (30 runs), descriptive online runs, LangGraph baselines, a streaming RAG workload, and an Ollama local-backend check show that AiFlow does not alter provider-side Model TTFT but reduces Application TTFPT by 70.9-94.7\% versus aggregation and keeps runtime-owned queue depth within declared bounds (93.7-96.5\% MaxQ reduction versus unbounded policies). The supplementary artifact contains scripts, raw traces, machine-readable tables, checksums, and an API-free smoke test; the public implementation is available through the FIT Framework repository.

</details>


### [13] [Can Perplexity Serve as a Cognitive Signal for Code Understandability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00624)
*Xiaokai Rong,Mohammadali Sefidi Esfahani,Aashish Yadavally,Rigby Peter,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 困惑度对局部代码混淆是有用的认知信号，但不能直接通过简单聚合推广为可靠的片段级可理解性指标，需采用面向代码的聚合策略、考虑人类一致性并进行模型敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明token级困惑度可以与代码理解过程中的局部人类困惑对齐，提出问题：这种signal能否扩展为对整个代码片段的可理解性评价？

Method: 实证研究：在多个人类基准数据集（方法级可理解性判断、输出预测任务、被接受的可理解性改进补丁）上计算并比较基于大语言模型的token级困惑度的整段聚合指标（均值、中位数、峰值等）；并对token困惑度分布的偏态与重尾、困惑度来源（标识符、字面量、类型、分隔符、分词伪影）、人类标签一致性以及模型/分词器差异进行深入分析。

Result: 简单的片段级聚合（均值、中位数、峰值）与人类可理解性标签之间没有可靠的相关性。原因包括token困惑度分布高度偏态且由多种非语义因素驱动、可理解性标签缺乏共识、以及不同模型和分词器之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 困惑度是有潜力的工具，但在支持可重复且可靠的代码可理解性测量前，需要开发代码感知的聚合方法、基于共识的评价框架并针对模型/分词器差异做系统化分析。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that token-level perplexity from large language models can align with localized human confusion during code comprehension. This raises a natural question: can perplexity also serve as a snippet-level signal for code understandability? We conduct an empirical study of this question across multiple human-grounded datasets, including method-level understandability judgments, output-prediction tasks, and accepted understandability-improvement patches. Despite prior token-level evidence, we find that simple snippet-level aggregations of token perplexity, such as average, median, or peak perplexity, do not reliably correlate with human understandability. We then investigate why this happens. First, token perplexity is highly skewed and heavy-tailed across code structures; extreme spikes arise not only from semantically meaningful constructs, but also from identifiers, literals, types, separators, and tokenization artifacts. Second, human understandability labels often lack consensus, making whole-snippet difficulty a noisy target. Third, perplexity distributions and their alignment with human difficulty vary substantially across models and tokenizers. These findings explain why prior token-level perplexity--confusion alignment does not directly transfer to snippet-level understandability. Overall, our study positions perplexity as a promising but delicate cognitive signal: useful for localized code confusion, but requiring code-aware aggregation, consensus-aware evaluation, and model-sensitivity analysis before it can support reliable code-understandability measurement.

</details>


### [14] [Understanding Online Failure Prediction in Linux Through Complementary Multi-View Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00651)
*Diogo Dória,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出并评估了一个可解释的 Linux 在线故障预测流水线：检测对跨工作负载泛化良好（91–94%，FPR<1%），但故障诊断对工作负载漂移敏感，未见模式在 LOMO 测试下无法诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Operators need more than accurate failure prediction: they require diagnostic insight and trustworthy, generalizable alerts that do not exploit workload-specific noise. The paper aims to build and explain an OFP pipeline for Linux that supports interpretation and evaluates how detection and diagnosis generalize under workload shift.

Method: Pipeline：使用共识式特征选择提高检测稳健性，时间起始分析（temporal onset analysis）量化提前预警窗口，子系统级因果分析用于定位和解释故障，并辅以若干互补诊断机制。评估在严格的跨工作负载设置下执行，训练产物冻结，包含 Leave-One-Mode-Out（LOMO）测试以检验对未见故障模式的诊断泛化。

Result: An explainable OFP pipeline combining consensus-based feature selection, temporal onset analysis, subsystem-level causal analysis, and complementary diagnostics. Under frozen training artifacts and strict cross-workload testing it achieved 91–94% detection on unseen workloads with <1% false alarms. Diagnosis was much less robust to workload shift; early-warning lead times varied 38–215 seconds by failure mode; Leave-One-Mode-Out evaluation yielded 0% diagnosability for unseen modes with related training modes.

Conclusion: 检测能力比诊断能力在工作负载变化下更稳健；预警提前量高度依赖故障类型；训练集中没有出现的故障模式不能仅依赖相关模式进行可靠诊断。必须把可解释性机制与检测结合，以判断预测信号是否反映可迁移的故障结构并识别诊断泛化的断层。

Abstract: Accurate Online Failure Prediction (OFP) has been shown to be feasible in Operating Systems (OSs) settings, but prediction alone is not sufficient for practical adoption. Without diagnostic insight, operators have limited basis to trust alerts or decide how to respond. Moreover, even when predictive accuracy is high, it is often unclear whether models are capturing meaningful failure processes or merely exploiting workload-specific noise and incidental correlations in telemetry. This paper reports a practical experience building and evaluating an explainable OFP pipeline for Linux OSs. We combine consensus-based feature selection for detection with temporal onset analysis, subsystemlevel causal analysis, and complementary diagnostic mechanisms to support failure interpretation. Evaluated under strict crossworkload conditions with frozen training artifacts, it achieved 91-94% detection on unseen workloads without retraining, while maintaining false alarm rates below 1%. However, failure mode diagnosis proved substantially more sensitive to workload shift, and several diagnostics mechanisms showed limited effectiveness for specific failure types. Our experience highlights three main lessons: i) detection generalizes more robustly than diagnosis across workload changes; ii) early-warning capability depends strongly on the failure mode, ranging from 38 to 215 seconds in our study; and iii) unseen failure modes are not reliably diagnosable from related training modes alone, providing 0% accuracy under Leave-One-Mode-Out (LOMO) evaluation. Taken together, these results show the value of complementary explainability mechanisms for interpreting accurate failure predictions, revealing when predictive signals reflect transferable failure structure and when diagnostic generalization breaks down under workload variation.

</details>


### [15] [Unreliable in Practice? A Comprehensive Study of Errors in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00661)
*Rodrigo Pato Nogueira,Marco Vieira,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统化分析了86,726个含错误的LLM生成代码样本（7个模型、4种编译语言），通过自动+人工标注揭示了常见错误模式：语言/模型间差异大但简单错误普遍，常见缺陷包括缺少输入校验与内存安全检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs are increasingly used to generate production code, but they still fail on complex tasks. A comprehensive, cross-model and cross-language understanding of error types is missing; identifying common and model-/language-specific errors is necessary to set expectations and design mitigations.

Method: 收集多模型多语言生成代码，执行以收集编译或运行时失败样本；用LLM对错误根因进行自动分类，再人工抽样验证与纠正；基于标注数据统计错误在模型、语言与题目难度上的分布并提取模式。

Result: Collected 86,726 code samples with compilation/runtime errors from seven LLMs across four compiled languages; used an LLM to label error causes and manually validated labels. Found that error distributions strongly depend on language and model, large models still make simple mistakes frequently, and generated code often lacks input validation and memory-safety checks leading to overflows, resource exhaustion, and reliability/security issues.

Conclusion: 误差类型受语言和模型影响显著，但普遍存在低级错误与安全/鲁棒性欠缺。需要针对性缓解措施（语言/模型感知的提示、静态分析、自动测试、运行时检查），并将错误分布作为评估指标纳入模型和工具链设计。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being widely used for coding, with reports indicating that AI now generates an increasing share of production code. Studies show that LLMs can significantly improve developer productivity, yet they still struggle with more complex coding tasks. Just as understanding error modes in human-written code has been central to improving software quality, identifying and characterizing the errors in LLM-generated code is critical for setting realistic expectations and designing mitigation strategies. Prior research has been limited in scope, often focusing on a single language, a small number of problems, or a limited selection of models. As a result, there is still no comprehensive understanding of which errors are common and which are specific to certain models or languages. To address these gaps and develop a deeper understanding of the quality of LLM-generated code, we analyzed a corpus of 86,726 code samples that contained compilation or runtime errors. These samples were generated by seven LLMs across four compiled languages. We classified errors by their underlying causes using an LLM, manually validated these classifications, and performed a comparative analysis. This labeled data is then used to measure error prevalence by model, language, and problem difficulty, to identify common error patterns. Results show that, although error types vary strongly across languages and models, even the largest models frequently make simple mistakes. We also observe that generated code often omits basic input validation or memory-safety checks, which can lead to overflows, resource exhaustion, or other reliability/security issues.

</details>


### [16] [Vul4Py: Benchmarking Automated Vulnerability Repair in Python with Paired Exploit and Functional Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00692)
*Tan Bui,Ting Zhang,Ferdian Thung,Yunpeng Xiong,Penghao Jiang,Xin Zhou,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Vul4Py 提出“exploit+pytest”配对判定器并构建 100 条可复现的 Python 漏洞基准；在此基准下软件工程 agent 明显优于直接提示的 LLM 与专门工具，配对判定器能显著提高判定可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Python AVR 基准依赖 exploit 或上游测试之一，易漏判功能回归；需要一个既能验证漏洞被去除又能保证功能不回归的可复现基准来进行可信比较。

Method: 收集 100 个真实漏洞（60 个项目、60 个 CWE、2017–2025 年）；为每个漏洞打包可复现环境并构建两个 oracle：1) exploit oracle（需在漏洞版失败，在修复版成功），2) pytest 功能性 oracle（需对两版均通过）。用这一基准比较 6 种方法并评估修复成功、误判率和语义等价性（人工确认）。

Result: 构建了 Vul4Py —— 一个面向 Python 的 AVR 基准：100 个真实漏洞，配对判定器（exploit oracle + pytest 功能性 oracle），并在每个条目中提供可复现的环境与时间跨度（2017–2025）。用该基准比较了 6 种方法（专门修复工具、直接提示的 LLM、软件工程 agent），发现工程 agent（OpenHands）修复成功 41/100，而最强的直接提示 LLM 为 4/100，专门工具为 2/100。配对判定器能剔除 exploit-only 会误判的补丁（剔除 15/119），并且对 104 个接受的补丁中有 98 个经人工确认与开发者补丁语义等价。

Conclusion: 配对判定器与可复现环境是可信 AVR 评估的关键；在相同模型骨干下，工程 agent 的策略与流程设计能显著提升修复成功率，说明系统工程与提示/搜索/回归测试的结合比单纯模型生成更有效。

Abstract: Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR) has advanced rapidly across program analysis, machine learning, and Large Language Models (LLMs), but a verifiable, head-to-head comparison of AVR approaches on Python is still missing. Python underpins critical web, data, and machine-learning infrastructure, yet existing Python benchmarks accept a patch on the strength of a proof-of-concept exploit alone, or apply a functional test only on the subset of entries whose upstream project happens to ship one. Both therefore miss functional regressions, in which a patch defeats the exploit but breaks unrelated behavior. We present Vul4Py, a Python AVR benchmark in which every entry carries a paired oracle: an exploit oracle that must fail on the vulnerable revision and pass on the fixed one, together with a project-native pytest functional oracle that must pass on both. Vul4Py comprises 100 real vulnerabilities from 60 open-source projects, spanning 60 distinct CWEs and the years 2017 to 2025, each packaged with a pinned, reproducible per-instance environment. Using Vul4Py, we compare six approaches in three categories: a specialized vulnerability repair tool, directly prompted LLMs, and software engineering agents. The agents dominate: OpenHands repairs 41 of 100 vulnerabilities, against 4 for the strongest directly prompted LLM and 2 for the specialized tool, despite all three sharing the same backbone model. The paired oracle is what makes these counts trustworthy: it rejects 15 of the 119 patches that an exploit-only oracle would accept, and 98 of the 104 patches it admits are manually confirmed to be semantically equivalent to the developer's patches

</details>


### [17] [EduPluginBench: Executable Assurance for AI-Generated Educational Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00739)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出EduPluginBench并证明其在检测治理类违规插件上有效，但当前代码生成模型在这些治理约束上的迁移能力接近于零，且事后修复可能掩盖真实合规状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代码生成模型能生成可执行组件，但编译与功能测试无法保证合规性（最小权限、遥测同意、可追溯来源、特权写入、生命周期约束、受限失败等），需要可执行的基准与准入流程来量化与阻止不合规插件进入治理软件生态。

Method: 构建EduPluginBench：包含规范化测试、激活检查的第一阶变异体（total 1,440）和分阶段P0–P4准入策略；进行冷冻迁移研究（600次生成）、独立标注的Moodle漏洞/修复对比（16对）以及早期540次诊断生成的后验修复实验；公开原始生成、行级决策和审计数据以保证可复现。

Result: 在1,440个变异体上，P0–P4相较P0–P2将发布阻断缺陷召回提高74.7个百分点（规范簇95% CI 73.4–75.8），在120个干净参考中未观测到拒绝（Wilson 95%上界3.1%）；600次冷冻生成中300个能被解析但无一通过P0或P0–P4（95%上界0.64%）；Moodle研究中通用PHP检测器未识别任何易受攻击的修订；早期540次诊断的后验有界修复生成112个P0通过，但全部不合规，召回从13.4%提升到100%；工件包含完整可重复性材料。

Conclusion: EduPluginBench提供了一个可执行基准和分阶段准入方法，能显著提高对发布阻断类缺陷的召回，同时在干净参考样本上保持低误拒率；然而对现有通用代码生成模型的迁移评估显示它们在满足治理约束方面几乎完全失败，且事后修复可能导致假合格或不可解释的保证估计。

Abstract: Code-generation models can produce executable components, but compilation and functional tests do not establish compliance with least privilege, telemetry consent, provenance, privileged-write authority, lifecycle constraints, or bounded failure. We introduce EduPluginBench, an executable benchmark and staged admission method for generated plugins in governed software ecosystems. Across 1,440 activation-checked first-order mutants from 30 specifications, P0-P4 increased release-blocking-defect recall by 74.7 percentage points (specification-clustered 95% CI 73.4-75.8) over P0-P2, with no observed rejection among 120 clean references (95% Wilson upper bound 3.1%). A frozen transfer study of 600 unmodified generations from two current coding models found that 300/600 parsed, but none passed P0 or achieved P0-P4 conformance (95% upper bound 0.64%); downstream assurance estimands were undefined. An independently labelled Moodle study retained 16 vulnerable/fixed pairs; the frozen generic PHP detector found no vulnerable revisions. These negative transfer results prevent controlled contract consistency from being read as independent real-defect effectiveness. An earlier 540-generation diagnostic found that post-hoc bounded repair yielded 112 P0 passes, all nonconforming, with recall increasing from 13.4% to 100%. The artifact retains protocols, public-source provenance, raw generations, row-level decisions, audits, analysis code, and reproduction instructions.

</details>


### [18] [Less Is More: Tuning Configurable Systems with Imperfect Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00759)
*Yulong Ye,Miqing Li,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出MFTune，通过在成本更低但不完全一致的“非完美保真度”环境中部分调优来产生高质量的种子，从而在完美保真度下更高效地完成系统配置搜索，实现少量测量即可带来更好性能（“less can be more”）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统配置空间极其庞大且测量代价高，直接在目标环境中穷尽或昂贵优化不可行；发现低成本、相似环境（非完美保真度）可以用来提升预算利用效率和搜索效果。

Method: 构建保真度概念框架，定义并搜索>10^4的非完美保真度设置以找到兼顾“宽度”的有用场景，基于这些场景生成高质量种子，然后在目标（完美）保真度上做深度调优以确保“深度”。

Result: 对多个真实系统进行长时间（19个月24×7）运行评测，相比10个SOTA方法，在83.33%的案例中表现更好，最高提升19.34%，并实现整体工时/预算节省。

Conclusion: 在多数实验场景中，MFTune优于10个最先进调参器，能在83.33%的案例中带来显著提升（最多19.34%）并节省大量测量预算，表明利用非完美保真度进行先行探索是一条有效策略。

Abstract: Configuration tuning is essential for optimizing the performance of highly configurable systems, e.g., throughput or runtime, under a given environment. Yet, this is a challenging process as there can be many options to tune, and configuration measurement is often highly expensive. In this paper, we demonstrate the phenomenon of ``less can be more'': system configuration tuning can be greatly improved with much superior budget utilization by partially tuning under the imperfect-fidelity---an environment that is similar, but cheaper to measure, compared with the concerned perfect-fidelity of environment under which the system should be tuned. We codify a conceptual framework of fidelity for configurable systems, drawing on which allows us to propose MFTune, a tuner that proactively explores in the space of $>10^4$ possible imperfect-fidelity settings to approximate a useful one, which strikes for the wideness of tuning. This creates high-quality seeds for the perfect-fidelity, which in turn ensures the tuning depth. Experiment results against $10$ state-of-the-art tuners, obtained from running diverse real-world systems for $19$ months $24 \times 7$, show that MFTune performs considerably better on $83.33$\% cases with up to $19.34\%$ improvement while achieving hours of budget saving in general.

</details>


### [19] [Turning Interaction History into Execution State: A Runtime Layer for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00808)
*Zehao Wang,Yisen Xu,Chenglin Li,Chao Peng,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Ledger通过维护显式、紧凑的执行账本并在决策与执行边界使用inform与govern路径，显著提高长期编码代理的正确率并降低成本，而无需额外的语言模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期任务中代理积累大量动作与观测，但历史记录并未显式标注哪些观测仍然反映当前仓库状态，导致代理基于过期信息做决策或重复执行已完成工作。需要显式的执行状态以避免过期/重复行为。

Method: 在模型决策前后插入确定性运行时层Ledger，维护一个在线执行账本（execution ledger）。在每步前通过inform路径把精简的运行状态视图附加到提示（prompt），在拟执行命令前通过govern路径对命令与账本进行校验：若发现先前有效结果则返回以替代重执行，并标记可能冗余的重复操作。该层不进行额外的语言模型调用，可包装未修改的代理。

Result: 在500个SWE-bench Verified实例上，将Ledger附加到GPT-5 mini使Pass@1从56.2%提升到64.2%，附加到MiniMax M2.5使Pass@1从75.8%提升到81.0%；同时分别降低总成本28.9%和31.8%。附加到OpenAI Codex增加3.4个百分比的Pass@1并降低24.4%的成本。消融研究表明：govern主要带来正确率提升，inform主要带来效率提升，两者组合效果最佳。

Conclusion: 长期编码代理缺乏的不是更短的历史视窗，而是一个明确的、自身执行状态的账本；提供这样的显式状态可减少过期或重复的操作，提升性能与效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding agents accumulate hundreds of actions and observations in their trajectories, yet nothing in this record indicates which observations still describe the repository as it currently stands. Before every decision, the model must implicitly infer the execution status from raw history, and when this inference falls short, the agent acts on outdated file contents or re-executes work whose results are still valid. We propose Ledger, a deterministic runtime layer that distills an agent's completed interactions into an explicit execution state: what has been observed, what has been modified, and what has been attempted. Ledger keeps this state in an online execution ledger and applies it at two boundaries of every step. Before the model acts, an inform path appends a compact runtime state view to the prompt; before a proposed command runs, a govern path checks it against the ledger, returning still-valid earlier results in place of re-execution and flagging likely-redundant repetition. The layer adds no language-model calls and wraps an otherwise unmodified agent. Across all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, Ledger raises Pass@1 from 56.2% to 64.2% with GPT-5 mini and from 75.8% to 81.0% with MiniMax M2.5, while cutting total cost by 28.9% and 31.8%. Attached to OpenAI Codex, it adds 3.4 percentage points of Pass@1 at 24.4% lower cost. Ablations attribute most of the resolution gain to govern and most of the efficiency gain to inform, with their combination performing best. What long-horizon agents lack, we conclude, is not a shorter view of their history but an explicit account of their own execution state.

</details>


### [20] [Structure-Aware Semantic Chunking with Title-Chain Prefixes: A 1600-Query Evaluation and the Measurement Trap in Text-Transform Ablations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00824)
*Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 使用文档标题链作为候选前缀的三阶段语义分块，零额外LLM调用即可显著提升RAG的检索质量；但要注意：常见的同池前缀开/关评测会破坏注释一致性，应改为检索时按候选前缀评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RAG中分块是下游检索/生成的第一步，分块边界决定可检索单位并严重影响检索质量。目标是设计一个不增加额外LLM调用的语义分块方案以提升检索效果并保持可部署性。

Method: 三阶段流程：1) header-split：按文档标题/层级初步分块；2) semantic merge：用语义相似性合并过短或碎片化的段落以避免信息丢失；3) title-chain prefixing：为每个chunk附加其文档的标题链作为前缀上下文以提供消歧而不借助额外LLM生成摘要。评估采用1600条分层查询、双注释人工判定及MRR@5，另包含若干消融与失败尝试记录。

Result: 提出三阶段仅对chunk侧操作的语义分块流水线：header-split、semantic merge 和 title-chain prefixing（复用文档自身标题层级作为前缀），无需额外LLM调用。在1600条分层查询的生产Markdown知识库评估中，MRR@5从0.374提升到0.463（+23.8%）；在可回答子集（n=563）从0.828提升到0.925（+11.7%）。双注释者Cohen's kappa=0.45（16,000对评分）。报告了若干失败尝试，并发现一个关键评估陷阱：同池前缀开/关消融导致kappa从0.45崩塌到0.04，说明去掉title-chain会剥夺注释者判定相关性的消歧信号。基于此，作者建议在检索时对每个候选项进行前缀评估。

Conclusion: 方法在生产级Markdown KB上效果显著且工程开销低；论文的核心贡献不仅是分块方法本身，还有对评估协议陷阱的系统揭示——保证候选的上下文一致性是可靠评测的先决条件，建议采用按候选前缀的检索时评估流程。

Abstract: Chunking is the first and most consequential step in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): every downstream retrieval decision inherits the chunk boundaries. We present a three-stage, chunk-side-only semantic chunking pipeline---header-split, semantic merge, and title-chain prefixing---that costs zero additional LLM calls: the title chain reuses the document's own header hierarchy instead of a generated summary. On a 1600-query stratified evaluation over a production Markdown knowledge base, the pipeline improves MRR@5 from 0.374 to 0.463 (+23.8%) on the full set and from 0.828 to 0.925 (+11.7%) on the answerable subset (n=563), with dual-annotator Cohen's kappa 0.45 (unweighted, 16,000 score pairs). We then report what we tried and what failed: three query-side or architecture-level follow-ups are design dead ends (unevaluated---no comparable run artifacts), one measured failure (prefix weight decay), and one protocol-level failure that is the paper's central methodological finding. In a same-pool prefix on/off ablation, dual-annotator agreement collapsed from kappa 0.45 to 0.04 under identical prompts---stripping the title-chain context strips the disambiguation signal annotators need to agree on relevance. This measurement trap invalidates a common evaluation practice in chunking research and motivates retrieval-time per-candidate prefix evaluation, the direction we recommend from all our evidence.

</details>


### [21] [Documentation vs. Code Patterns: What Drives LLM-Based Exception Oracle Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00884)
*Soneya Binta Hossain,Matthew B. Dwyer,Tasfia Tasnim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Despite high reported accuracy, TOG systems often rely on shortcut signals rather than documented exception semantics; evaluations must test grounding of predictions, not only correctness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Determine whether LLM-based TOG systems use explicit exception documentation (Javadoc @throws) or exploit recurring patterns/shortcuts in code, tests, and documentation when predicting exception oracles.

Method: Large-scale intervention-based study across three TOG systems (classifier-based and generative architectures, ~110M–7B parameters) evaluated on three benchmarks (two generated-test datasets and a new developer-written test benchmark). Interventions: remove Javadoc @throws clauses and run attribution-guided substitution ablations to identify tokens/signals models rely on.

Result: Removing @throws caused only marginal accuracy changes (largest drop <1 percentage point), indicating @throws is not central. Attribution-guided ablations revealed models either hinge on a few structural tokens or spread reliance across many lexical cues, confirming shortcut-driven predictions.

Conclusion: Structured exception documentation (Javadoc @throws) is not the primary driver of high exception-oracle accuracy; models often exploit shortcut signals (small sets of structural tokens or distributed lexical cues) instead of robust exception semantics. Evaluation should measure whether predictions are grounded in meaningful evidence, not just accuracy.

Abstract: LLM-based test oracle generation (TOG) methods report high accuracy on exception oracle generation, but it remains unclear what evidence drives these predictions. In particular, do models use explicit exceptional-behavior documentation such as Javadoc @throws clauses, or do they rely on recurring patterns in tests, code, and documentation?
  We investigate this question through a large-scale intervention-based study of three TOG systems spanning classifier-based and generative architectures and model sizes from roughly 110M to 7B parameters, evaluated on three real-world benchmarks comprising two generated-test datasets and a new benchmark of developer-written tests. We first remove Javadoc @throws clauses and find that accuracy changes only marginally, with the largest drop below one percentage point. This indicates that structured exception documentation is not the primary driver of exception-oracle prediction. We then apply attribution-guided substitution ablations to identify the signals that predictions depend on. The results show that high accuracy can be driven by shortcut signals: some models are highly sensitive to a small number of structural tokens, while others distribute reliance across many lexical cues. These findings challenge the assumption that strong exception-oracle accuracy reflects robust use of exception semantics. Future TOG systems should therefore be evaluated not only by whether they predict the correct oracle type, but also by whether their predictions are grounded in meaningful exception-triggering evidence.

</details>


### [22] [Joint Optimization of Human Headcount and Stochastic AI Resource Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00886)
*Marco Montes de Oca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在含偏度修正的CLT框架下，将随机预算机会约束与认知摩擦的生产函数耦合，得到可解析的人力与token联合最优配置，并揭示波动性、偏度与分散-均值关系对替代性和规模的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的token消费具有重尾波动性，标准确定性规划忽视了token使用的高波动和审计认知成本，导致财务风险与组织配置误判。作者旨在在受限预算下联合分配人力与token容量，纳入波动性与认知摩擦。

Method: 将认知摩擦调整的生产函数与含机率约束的随机预算前沿相交，假设每个工程师的token使用为i.i.d.、非负且右偏，仅用均值、方差和偏度，利用中心极限定理并用Cornish–Fisher偏度修正将机会约束化为确定型等价物，构建拉格朗日并求解得到闭式最优人员规模和超越方程式的单人token强度，同时用边界海森判断二阶条件。

Result: 得出闭式最优人员规模、一个关于人均token强度的超越方程，并证明在常见波动/人员比率下二阶条件成立。比较静态表明：若个体token波动上升，则最优人均token强度上升且人员规模收缩；偏度相当于纯粹的无谓税收，缩小可用预算但不改变边际替代关系。若使用的离散度随均值同比例放大，则上述替代关系符号反转。

Conclusion: 结论可为组织在有限预算下调配人力与生成式AI容量提供可检验的政策指引，但依赖i.i.d.和CLT近似、离散度行为等关键假设；实证区分“离散度不变”与“离散度随均值增长”两种制度性情形对结论至关重要。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are rapidly becoming standard for coding and other business operations. However, while these tools can drive productivity, their unpredictable, and often significant, costs are putting severe financial strain on organizations. The leadership of such organizations must split a fixed budget between human headcount and generative-AI token capacity, yet standard deterministic planning ignores both the heavy-tailed volatility of token consumption and the cognitive cost of auditing AI-generated output. We model the joint allocation by intersecting a cognitive-friction-adjusted production function with a chance-constrained stochastic budget frontier. Per-engineer token usage is treated as i.i.d., non-negative, and right-skewed; no parametric family is assumed, as only its mean, variance, and skewness enter the analysis. The chance constraint is reduced to a deterministic equivalent via the Central Limit Theorem with a Cornish--Fisher skewness correction. Solving the resulting Lagrangian yields a closed-form optimal headcount, a transcendental condition for optimal per-capita token intensity, and a bordered-Hessian second-order condition that holds for any reasonable volatility-to-headcount ratio. Comparative statics show that rising individual token volatility raises optimal per-capita token intensity while contracting headcount: although human labor and tokens are complements in production, the stochastic budget makes them substitutes at the margin. Individual usage skewness acts as a pure deadweight tax that shrinks the budget without altering the substitution dynamics. Both conclusions presuppose that usage dispersion is invariant to the planned mean allocation. In the case where dispersion instead scales proportionally with the mean, the substitution reverses, so which regime applies is a sharp, empirically testable question.

</details>


### [23] [RefactorAssist: Agentic Refinement for Reliable Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00924)
*Jonathan Cordeiro,Shayan Noei,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过故障归因与一个先静态修复再基于测试与上下文的代理式LLM修复流程（RefactorAssist），显著提升了LLM生成重构的单元测试通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能生成重构但经常引入微妙错误从而改变功能行为并导致单元测试失败，阻碍实际采纳；目标是提升LLM生成重构的功能正确性，使其可更可靠地集成到开发流程。

Method: 对10个开源Java项目及其原生测试套件，手工分类LLM生成重构失败原因；提出RefactorAssist代理流程：先做静态修复（缺失导入、不平衡括号、编译错误等），对剩余失败利用单元测试日志、错误解释、项目上下文检索和代码diff，迭代调用LLM进行测试驱动修复。

Result: 通过人工归因得到失败原因分布（主要包括上下文误解/幻觉24.3%、重命名错误15.3%、新增功能/变量13.7%等）；静态修复先行降低成本，对剩余失败在最佳配置下获得70.8%的修复率，累计最高通过率94.2%。

Conclusion: 静态检查结合基于测试的、上下文敏感的代理式修复能大幅提高LLM生成重构的可靠性，但需更多关于泛化性、成本、模型/数据依赖和人类参与度的评估以推动实际部署。

Abstract: Code refactoring aims to enhance the internal structure of source code without affecting its functional behavior. The recent advancements of Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential for automating software engineering tasks, such as code refactoring. However, the refactorings produced by LLMs often introduce subtle errors, leading to functional behavior changes and failed unit tests, which limit their practical adoption. To address the limitations of LLM-generated refactorings, we analyze the root causes of their failures and develop the RefactorAssist agent to improve the functional correctness of LLM-generated refactorings. To this end, we use 10 open-source Java projects with their native test suites and manually evaluate why LLM-generated refactorings fail unit tests. We then design an agentic approach that leverages unit-test logs, error explanations, project context retrieval, and code diffs to guide the iterative refactoring. Our findings show that the main reasons for failure are context misunderstanding/hallucination (24.3%), incorrect or inconsistent renaming (15.3%), adding new functionality or variables (13.7%), code incompleteness (11.3%), syntax and structural errors (9.7%), edge cases not handled (9%), improper type handling (8.7%), and variables outside scope (8%). To make our approach cost-effective, RefactorAssist first applies a static repair step for missing imports, unbalanced brackets, and compilation errors without LLMs. For remaining failures, RefactorAssist incorporates error logs and code diffs, achieving up to a 70.8% repair rate on the remaining failures and a 94.2% cumulative pass rate under the best-performing configuration. These results indicate that static checks and test-guided, context-aware agentic repair can increase the reliability of LLM-generated refactorings, bringing them closer to practical integration within developer workflows.

</details>


### [24] [Claim Plane: Reliability Gains and the Limits of Selective Concurrency for Parallel Coding Agents: A 30-Pair, Three-Seed Confirmatory Study of Deterministic Pre-Write Admission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00947)
*Maxim Nikolaev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 预写入准入（Claim Plane）能显著提升合并可靠性，但在本实验中几乎通过序列化实现，导致无法获得实际并行加速；动态模式更有希望但受范围覆盖与修订处理不足影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于并行编码代理往往各自产生在本地看似有效但合并时失败的改动；需要一种确定性的预写入准入机制（基于版本化更改意图）来阻止不可组合的改动进入代码库，从而提升集成可靠性。

Method: 确认性实验：使用30个冻结的CooperBench特性对（15冲突/15无冲突），三种编码器随机种子，四种协调策略（臆见为无约束并行、静态准入、动态准入等），总计360次执行。DeepSeek V4 Pro一次性生成并冻结60条功能级规划声明，DeepSeek V4 Flash执行编码。主要量化指标：pair pass、integration success、序列化率及失败原因归类；用成对任务簇差异与聚类自助法（bootstrap）做统计推断。

Result: Static Claim Plane materially improved correctness metrics but at the cost of near-complete serialization. Specifically, static admission raised pair pass from 23.3% to 50.0% (paired difference +26.7 percentage points, 95% bootstrap CI 9.6 to 60.0) and integration success from 65.6% to 96.7%. For conflict-labeled pairs, pair pass rose from 6.7% to 60.0%. However, static admission serialized 96.7% of executions (including 93.3% of clean cases). Dynamic admission was more selective in serialization (serialized 66.7% of conflict cases and 13.3% of clean cases) but suffered from many “failed closed on undeclared scope” blocks (46 of 90), lowering pair pass to 22.2%; 45 of those 46 targeted files already declared in frozen planner outputs, indicating region undercoverage and poor amendment handling rather than wholly unknown files. No useful wall-clock parallel speedup was observed because provider calls were physically sequential and the conservative policy largely serialized work. Study artifacts and analysis are publicly released.

Conclusion: 预写入准入是一种有效的可靠性机制：它能把局部有效却组合失败的并发改动转化为更高的集成成功率。但当前实现倾向于通过串行化恢复可靠性，从而牺牲并行性与实际速度。动态准入策略若改进范围检测与修订（amendment）处理，有潜力在保持可靠性的同时减少不必要的串行化。

Abstract: Parallel coding agents can produce locally valid changes that fail when combined. Claim Plane addresses this failure mode as deterministic pre-write admission over versioned change intents. This paper reports a confirmatory study on 30 frozen CooperBench feature pairs, balanced between 15 conflict and 15 clean labels, with three coder seeds, four coordination arms, and 360 completed executions. DeepSeek V4 Pro generated 60 feature-level planner declarations once; the declarations were frozen across all arms and coder seeds, while DeepSeek V4 Flash performed the coding work. Static Claim Plane raised pair pass from 23.3% under unconstrained parallel execution to 50.0%, a paired task-cluster difference of +26.7 percentage points (95% bootstrap CI 9.6 to 60.0), and raised integration success from 65.6% to 96.7%. On conflict-labeled pairs, pair pass rose from 6.7% to 60.0%. However, static admission serialized 96.7% of executions, including 93.3% of clean cases, and therefore recovered reliability largely by collapsing toward serial execution. Dynamic admission was more selective, serializing 66.7% of conflict cases and 13.3% of clean cases, but 46 of 90 executions failed closed on undeclared scope, reducing pair pass to 22.2%. Forty-five of those 46 blocks targeted files already present in the frozen declarations, indicating region undercoverage and insufficient amendment handling rather than wholly unknown files. The results support pre-write admission as a reliability mechanism, but they do not establish useful wall-clock parallel speedup: provider calls were physically sequential, and the conservative policy largely serialized the workload. The complete study artifacts, hashes, and clustered bootstrap analysis are publicly released for reproduction.

</details>


### [25] [AgenTag: Attribution of AI Coding Agents from Behavioral Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00966)
*Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgenTag是一个面向开放世界的多模态AI代码代理归因框架，在AIDev数据集（33,580个来自5种AI代理的PR与6,618个人类PR）上评估。它以文本（PR描述、commit消息）、行为与代码三种模态表示PR，并比较传统分类与监督式对比学习以实现开放集识别与少样本注册，能高效识别已知与未知代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理越来越多在开发者帐户下提交PR，真实作者被掩盖，准确归因对仓库治理、实证研究与AI影响评估变得必要。现有工作多聚焦封闭集合识别，缺乏开放世界情形的研究。

Method: 每个PR用文本、行为和代码三模态表示；在AIDev上比较传统分类器与监督对比学习（用于开放集识别与few-shot enrollment），评估已知代理识别、AI/人类判别与未知代理检测。

Result: 已知代理识别加权F1=0.96（macro F1=0.84）；区分AI与人类-balanced F1=0.89；检测未知代理AUC=0.84。进一步发现PR描述与commit消息几乎包含所有归因信号，代码diff贡献甚微；在移除显式自报标记后，风格信号仍可保留归因能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明：对AI代码代理进行可靠归因在实践中可行；归因主要依赖PR描述和commit消息的行为/风格信号，而非代码diff；风格指纹在删除显式标识后仍然存在。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly author pull requests (PRs), often under developers' own accounts, obscuring who actually produced a change. Reliable attribution is important for repository governance, empirical studies of AI-assisted software development, and measuring the impact of AI coding agents. Existing work focuses on closed-set identification of known agents, leaving the practical limits of open-world AI coding agent attribution largely unexplored. In this paper, we present AgenTag, a multimodal framework for open-world AI coding agent attribution, evaluated on AIDev, comprising 33,580 PRs from five AI coding agents and 6,618 human-authored PRs. We represent each PR using textual, behavioral, and code-based modalities, and compare conventional classification with supervised contrastive learning for open-set recognition and few-shot enrollment of previously unseen agents. AgenTag identifies authoring agents with a weighted F1 of 0.96 (macro F1 of 0.84), distinguishes AI- from human-authored PRs with a balanced F1 of 0.89, and detects previously unseen agents with an AUC of 0.84. We further show that PR descriptions and commit messages provide nearly all of the attribution signal, whereas code diffs contribute little across multiple representations, indicating that coding agents are distinguished primarily by how they communicate changes rather than by the code they generate. Moreover, these behavioral fingerprints persist after removing explicit self-disclosed markers, demonstrating that attribution relies largely on latent stylistic characteristics. These findings show that reliable attribution of AI coding agents is feasible and clarify the practical trade-offs between attribution accuracy and the information required to achieve it.

</details>


### [26] [Registry Descriptions Go Stale Unevenly: An 89-Day Measurement of Model Context Protocol Drift, and Why Drift-Ranked Re-Auditing Under-Covers It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00997)
*Gautam Bharti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究以120次观测（88.6天）重建官方MCP注册表的演化（3,510→18,966服务器，19,099个不同服务器），测量被审计文本的“保鲜期”。得出：描述变更稀疏、很多变更出现在新到达者，使得仅靠历史漂移排名的重审在覆盖上受限；推荐内容绑定+周期全扫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对MCP生态的安全研究都在单一时间点审计注册表，但没有报告审计时验明的描述在多长时间内仍然“当前”。作品旨在量化描述级证据的保鲜期，评估常见重审策略的实际覆盖与局限。

Method: 重建官方MCP注册表的面板：120次快照、持续88.6天，覆盖19,099个不同服务器（注册表从3,510增长到18,966）。统计描述与描述符（descriptor）上的变更频率、分布（重尾）、按历史漂移排名的重审覆盖效果，并对有≥10次观察的服务器子集做更细致分析。

Result: 核心结果是策略性：仅重审历史上“漂移最多”的服务器无法保持描述级（description-level）审计发现的时效性；更有效的控制是内容绑定（在描述哈希变化时即时重新验证）加以按大小定期的全目录扫描。

Conclusion: 重审策略若预算有限，不应仅依赖历史漂移排名；对于描述级审计，最佳实践是：对描述哈希的每次移动立即重新验证（content-binding），并按目录规模周期性做全量扫描。漂移历史排序顶多作为辅助、对新到达者盲区的部分控制。

Abstract: Security studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem share a design: each audits a registry at a single point in time. None reports how long the registry descriptions those audits judged stay current - a necessary condition for any description-level finding to still apply, though not a sufficient one: we measure the shelf-life of the audited text, not the validity of a security finding itself (Sec. 7.1). We reconstruct 120 observations of the official MCP registry over 88.6 days, covering 19,099 distinct servers as it grew from 3,510 to 18,966. Our central result is a policy one: you cannot keep description-level findings current by re-auditing the servers that drift most. At a top-5% re-audit budget, ranking by prior drift catches only ~20% of the previously-seen servers whose description changes in a held-out window - versus ~27% for descriptor drift overall - and only ~10% of all description changers. The limit is not unpredictability: the ranking still buys ~4x lift. It is that the description surface is sparse - 8.6% of servers ever rewrite one, against 24.8% for descriptors - and that roughly half of all description changes land on new arrivals a history ranking cannot reach, so the same lift buys far less coverage. The control that fits is content-binding - revalidate the moment a description's hash moves - plus a sized periodic full-catalog sweep; a drift-history ranking is at best a partial, blind-to-new-arrivals control. This is scanner hygiene for a description-level auditor, not a runtime trust signal. Of servers observed across at least ten intervals, three-quarters never change, the most active 5% generate 61% of all change events, and only 11.9% of a cohort's descriptors change within 30 days; naive compounding predicts 35.8% at 30 days (73% at 89), a heavy-tail overestimate we use only as a diagnostic. We release the panel, figure generator, and analysis code.

</details>


### [27] [What Could the Agent See at 19:05? Generating Temporal Enterprise Scenarios from Real Research and Replaying Them to Evaluate Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01042)
*Tezan Sahu,Himani Arora*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过模式推断+确定性/LLM混合重建方法，把企业数据重构为人格驱动、时间演进的世界，并把所有时刻的差异预缓存，允许对任意时刻的代理回答进行快速、可复现的离线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线评估仅使用单一静态快照（通常是终止时点），无法覆盖整个时间序列中不同瞬间的数据可见性和正确答案；重建每一时刻的完整租户快照代价高且易泄露未来信息，因此需要一种低成本、可复现且忠实的时序重现方法。

Method: 构建可回放的时间演进企业世界，并对代理进行离线评估

Result: 提出一个由模式推断驱动的时序描述，结合确定性逻辑与大模型完成记录的历史状态重建；由于可查询时刻有限，所有重建结果被预计算为紧凑的差异缓存，从而实现无需模型参与的快速、可复现的回放查询，并在早期实践中用于评估多应用、多角色企业代理。

Conclusion: 该系统同时解决了生成现实感时序企业场景与低成本回放评估两大问题，具备可扩展性和实际应用价值；需注意LLM依赖、模式推断错误、时刻集合选择及隐私/访问控制等限制，并可通过更多基线实验与公开化数据集推动评估可信度。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents act across many apps whose data changes continuously, so an answer is correct only relative to what data existed and who could see it at the moment it was asked. Offline evaluation today grades against a single static snapshot, effectively the end of the episode. So, it can only evaluate one situation, the final one, even though every earlier moment of the episode is a different situation that invites its own realistic questions with its own correct answers. Recreating each of those moments as a separate snapshot would mean re-provisioning a whole tenant per instant, which is prohibitively costly; and even a single snapshot leaks future state hidden inside records and cannot represent the multi-app, time-ordered way real work happens. Our system closes two gaps at once: it generates a realistic, persona-driven, temporally-evolving enterprise world from real research, and replays that world at any chosen moment to evaluate any pluggable agent. A schema-inferred temporal description drives a deterministic-plus-LLM rebuild of each record's past state; because the queryable moments are finite, all rebuilds are precomputed into a compact difference cache, making evaluation a fast, reproducible lookup with no model in the path. We describe the design, an architecture spanning both flows, and early experience evaluating enterprise agents.

</details>


### [28] [MADE: Belief-Driven Dual-Agent Coordination for Autonomous Model Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01189)
*Yicheng Liu,Bolin Zhang,Weiran Liu,Yakun Zhang,Yangqin Jiang,Zhiying Tu,Dianhui Chu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 MADE，一个将开源模型自动部署为 ready-to-call API 的双代理系统，并给出 M2ABench 基准；实验显示其在部署成功率上明显领先现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 在通用能力上强，但在领域专用任务受限；大量开源模型存在格式与接口差异，手动封装为 API 成本高昂，因此需要自动化模型部署工具以扩展工具生态。

Method: 采用双代理（生成/构建代理与验证/执行代理）协同工作：输入模型资源后迭代生成部署工件、执行测试并收集反馈，基于反馈修正上游工件直到服务化。

Result: 在新构建的基准 M2ABench（122 个真实模型、标准化测试用例）上，MADE 的部署成功率为 68.85%，优于 SWE-agent（低 13.93 百分点）与 OpenHands（低 44.26 百分点）；代码与数据集已开源。

Conclusion: MADE 提出了一种双代理协调系统，通过迭代构建、验证并根据执行反馈更新部署信念，最终把开源模型自动部署为可调用 API，显著提高部署成功率。

Abstract: LLM-based agents now have strong general capabilities. However, they still struggle with domain-specific tasks, motivating the integration of external tools to broaden their capabilities. The open-source community offers a vast array of AI models typically released as heterogeneous research artifacts, whereas transforming them into ready-to-call APIs is costly and labor-intensive. Automated model deployment is therefore essential for bridging the gap between model resources and tool usability, yet it remains a long-horizon, multi-stage task that has not been sufficiently explored. To tackle this challenge, we introduce Model Automated Deployment Engine (MADE), a dual-agent coordination system. Specifically, given a model resource, MADE iteratively constructs and validates the deployment artifacts, updates its deployment belief based on execution feedback, and revisits invalid upstream artifacts until the model is successfully served as a ready-to-call API that can then be used by other agents. We further introduce M2ABench, a benchmark for the task of transforming Models to ready-to-call APIs. M2ABench comprises 122 real-world models with standardized test cases for evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that MADE achieves a deployment success rate of 68.85%, outperforming SWE-agent and OpenHands by 13.93 and 44.26 percentage points, respectively. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/HITDiSC/MADE.

</details>


### [29] [Doc2CI: A Multi-Service Study of CI Configuration Generation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01451)
*Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于 DOC2CI（3,363 个描述→YAML 对），对 14 个开源大模型（7B–34B）及 GPT-4o/4.1 共计生成 >53k 份 CI 配置进行评估。结果显示参考重现率极低（≤3.1%），97% 输出可被解析为 YAML，但仅 71% 满足服务模式(schema)。引入无需训练的 schema 指导修复可将模式有效性提升到 94%，微调提高相似度却降低独立有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI 配置多为易出错的 YAML，手写代价高。尽管 LLM 在软件工程中被广泛采用，但其跨服务、跨模型族生成正确 CI 配置的能力尚不明确。

Method: 构建 DOC2CI 基准（3,363 对，来自 4 个 CI 服务官方文档）；在 14 个开源模型和 GPT-4o/4.1 上生成 >53k 配置；用参考重合度与服务 schema 验证衡量结果；人工分析 385 个失败样例形成故障分类；尝试无需训练的 schema 指导修复与微调实验。

Result: 关键数值：参考完全重现 ≤3.1%；YAML 可解析率 97%；满足服务 schema 的比例 71%；larger 模型在结构有效性上表现更好，但代码专用模型并不始终优于通用模型；模型差异主要源于输出完整性（片段 vs 完整 workflow）；无需训练的 schema 指导修复将 schema 有效性提高到 94%，而微调提升文档相似度但降低独立有效性。

Conclusion: 生成与文档相似性（reference alignment）与结构/模式有效性(schema validity) 是两个不同目标：提高一个并不必然提升另一个。推荐将 schema 感知评估与修复集成到 LLM 驱动的 CI 配置生成流水线中。

Abstract: Adopting Continuous Integration (CI) often requires writing YAML configurations that are error-prone and challenging to maintain. Despite increasing LLM use in software engineering, their ability to generate CI configurations from natural language across services and model families remains unclear. This paper presents a large empirical study on using LLMs to generate CI configurations. We introduce DOC2CI, a benchmark of 3,363 description-to-YAML pairs collected from the official documentation of four CI services, and evaluate 14 open-weight models from 7B-34B parameters together with GPT-4o and GPT-4.1, producing over 53,000 configurations. We assess both reference alignment and schema validity to determine whether the generated configurations are structurally valid. We further develop a failure taxonomy from a manual analysis of 385 configurations and examine why LLMs disagree. Across models and services, exact reference reproduction never exceeds 3.1%, and while 97% of outputs parse as YAML, only 71% satisfy service schemas. Larger models improve structural validity, but code specialization provides no consistent advantage over comparable general models. Model differences are driven largely by output completeness: for the same request, some models generate the expected fragment while others produce a full workflow. Finally, a training-free schema-guided repair method improves schema validity to 94%, while fine-tuning improves similarity to documentation but reduces standalone validity. This suggests that similarity and validity are distinct objectives for CI generation and motivate schema-aware evaluation and tooling for LLM-based configuration generation.

</details>


### [30] [An Empirical Investigation of Soft Skills and Corresponding Challenges in International Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01469)
*Sajid Ibrahim Hashmi,Jouni Markkula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过两次焦点小组探讨学生在课程小组作业中需掌握的软技能（如沟通、跨文化协作、冲突管理、时间管理与角色分配），并指出国际环境下的主要挑战，旨在为高校课堂小组设计提供实践性建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程职业高度依赖团队合作能力，但当前高等教育中对软技能的训练不足，尤其在国际化课堂中更具复杂性，故研究旨在识别必要软技能并为教学实践提供指导。

Method: 采用质性研究方法：对两组学生或师生混合的焦点小组讨论进行收集，基于讨论内容进行主题分析以归纳出关键软技能与挑战。

Result: 从两次关于小组作业的焦点小组讨论中，作者识别并描述了在国际软件工程教育中对团队合作至关重要的若干软技能及其挑战，并提出为高校教师与教学设计者规划学生小组工作提供指导的发现。

Conclusion: 应将软技能训练系统地嵌入软件工程课程，通过明确任务分工、评估机制、跨文化沟通训练与教师引导来提升学生团队合作能力。

Abstract: Joint software development work requires the application of technical skills and professional practices. Soft skills, such as teamwork, remain essential to succeeding in a professional software development environment. The phenomenon is reflected by group work in SE (software engineering) education, where students can learn and practice teamwork-related soft skills. Therefore, SE education requires students to practice those skills so that they can actively embark on their professional careers in the software industry. In this research study, empirical findings from two focus groups we conducted on group work are presented, with emphasis on the soft skills necessary for teamwork in international software engineering education, specifically student collaboration in higher education. There are several soft skills students should learn as they practice teamwork in their course-related assignments and exercises, as well as the challenges that come with it in international settings. The findings serve as a guideline to university teachers and pedagogy experts to plan student group work more effectively.

</details>


### [31] [Deep Agentic Search for Repository-Level Code Question Answering: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01507)
*Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei,Bora Ilci,Alperen Kayim,Mehmet Egemen Uzun,Berat Can,Kaan Emre Kara,Ozan Orhan,Mehmet S. Aktas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对于可索引的只读仓库问题，传统的语义检索在准确性和成本上胜出；深度代理式搜索虽可降低上下文污染，但在子代理交接处引入大量‘沉默’型错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体大量时间消耗在定位仓库中正确代码片段上。业界两种主流设计存在争议：语义检索效率与深度代理式搜索防止上下文污染的理论优点，缺乏系统性比较与失败模式分析。

Method: 实证比较：在 SWE-QA（仓库级代码问答基准）上，将两种检索/探索设计——预先向量索引的语义检索（Semantic Search）与由规划器委派子代理在独立上下文窗口内搜索的深度代理式搜索（Deep Agentic Search）——在相同任务上进行对照测试，统计正确率与成本，并对失败案例进行人工编码与分类。

Result: 语义检索在该基准上表现更好且更便宜：正确率65.2% vs 46.2%，且每个正确答案的成本不到深度代理式搜索的一半。对失败案例的分类显示，深度代理式搜索引入了新的失败类——在规划器与子代理交接处的失误占其失败的最大部分（41.8%），通常表现为流畅但错误的回答。

Conclusion: 深度代理式搜索并非万无一失：它解决的上下文污染问题并非免费的，针对可索引的只读查询，检索+检索增强生成（RAG）仍是更强且更经济的选择；未来改进应聚焦于规划器—子代理交接的可靠性与验证机制。

Abstract: Code agents spend much of their effort simply locating the right code inside a repository. Two approaches dominate current practice. In Semantic Search, the agent retrieves code blocks from a vector index built from the repository in advance. In Deep Agentic Search (also known as grep-search by subagent), a planning agent delegates the exploration to a separate subagent that works in an isolated context window and returns only a condensed result. The second design, which is considered good context engineering practice, exists to protect the main agent from context pollution (also known as context rot), the loss of accuracy that occurs as unrelated material accumulates in the context window. Recent code agents (such as Claude Code, Codex, Antigravity, etc) have adopted it quickly, but there is little evidence on whether it produces better answers. We compare the two approaches on SWE-QA, a benchmark for repository-level code question answering. Semantic search answered 65.2% of questions correctly against 46.2% for deep agentic search, and it produced each correct answer at less than half the cost. To explain the gap, we then coded every failed run into a taxonomy of failure modes. The taxonomy shows that deep agentic search did not remove failures but introduced a new class of them: the single largest share of its failures, 41.8%, occurred at the hand-off between the planner and its sub-agent, and these were usually silent, ending in a fluent and confident answer that was wrong. Deep agentic search addresses a real problem and is now the preferred design in many code agents.
