However, our results show that the protection it offers may not be free, and that for read-only questions over a repository that can be indexed, retrieval was the stronger and cheaper option.

</details>


### [32] [Algorithm-driven Development: A Proactive Approach to Improving Software Quality and Reducing Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01533)
*Philippe Jawish,Pierre Evrard,Alexandre Lemerle,Adrian Genin,Layal Dergham,Séverin Lanfranchi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Algorithm-Driven Development（ADD）：将需求转为算法流程图并自动派生验收测试，从而提高可测性、可维护性与可靠性；在达索系统四年工业案例中，ADD 实现了持续高于95%的覆盖率、低缺陷率和稳定交付节奏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业实践中面临在紧迫进度和不断演化的需求下仍保证软件质量与可测性的长期挑战，需要一种把需求表达、测试设计和实现紧密连接的方法。

Method: 把需求翻译成算法性流程图（既做规格又做实现蓝图），从流程图系统地生成验收测试以覆盖功能场景和边界情况；在达索系统两个团队的真实项目上进行为期四年的纵向评估，并与 TDD 和测试后置方法对比分析，采用生命周期管理和 CI/CD 指标衡量质量与交付。

Result: 在真实工业环境中，ADD 支持代码覆盖率持续>95%，QA 与发布后缺陷密度低，交付节奏稳定；与 TDD 和测试后置的比较显示对不同复杂度 API 的测试覆盖与缺陷率有积极影响。

Conclusion: ADD 能有效把需求建模、测试生成和实现联结起来，在工业项目中带来高覆盖、低缺陷密度和稳定交付，证明其在提高软件质量和可测试性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Ensuring software quality while meeting deadlines and adapting to evolving requirements is a persistent challenge in software engineering practice. This paper introduces Algorithm-Driven Development (ADD), a methodology developed from industrial practice to address recurring challenges in translating requirements into reliable, testable, and maintainable software behavior. ADD translates requirements into algorithmic flowcharts from which acceptance tests are systematically derived. These flowcharts serve both as specification artifacts and as technical blueprints, supporting shared understanding between stakeholders and developers. By linking requirement modeling with automated test generation, ADD provides systematic coverage of functional scenarios, including edge cases, from the outset of development. The approach was evaluated over a four-year period within an industrial project at Dassault Systèmes, involving two development teams, 22,444 lines of production code for Team 1, and 157 APIs analyzed for Team 2. The evaluation combined longitudinal quality and delivery indicators with a comparative analysis of ADD, TDD, and test-last development practices across API functions of different complexity levels. Empirical data collected from internal lifecycle management and CI/CD systems show that ADD supported sustained code coverage above 95%, low defect density in both QA and post-release phases, and a stable delivery cadence. These findings provide evidence of ADD's potential to strengthen the connection between requirements, testing, and implementation in industrial software development contexts.

</details>


### [33] [How Well Do LLMs Generate Taxonomies in the SE Domain? A Multi-perspective Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01592)
*Sota Nakashima,Yuta Ishimoto,Masanari Kondo,Tao Xiao,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个系统性实证评估显示：自动化分类法在SE中可行但存在质量-成本-复杂度的权衡，TnT-LLM与CLIMB各有取舍，建议先在子集上试验再决定是否替代人工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工构建软件工程（SE）领域的分类法耗时且需领域专家；尽管在其他领域出现了基于大型语言模型（LLMs）的自动化分类法生成方法，但其在技术性强、术语密集的SE文献与工件上的适用性尚不明确，因此需要系统性评估。

Method: 系统收集七篇含公开工件与人工构建分类法的SE论文；选取两种自动化生成算法（TnT-LLM 与 CLIMB）并采用五种最先进LLM作为基础；设计多维评估指标（分类质量、与人类分类对齐度、独立注解下的可靠性、效率/成本/运行时间）；对比输出分类法与人工分类法，统计成本/时间，并分析在需要超越表层相似性的技术推理场景下的表现差异。

Result: 通过对七篇含公开工件与人工分类法的SE论文进行实验，比较两类自动化方法（TnT-LLM 与 CLIMB）及五个最先进LLM，构建多视角评估框架（分类质量、与人工分类对齐度、独立注解下的可靠性与效率），发现：TnT-LLM 能生成接近人工质量的分类法但代价高、运行慢且倾向过于复杂；CLIMB 在速度和成本上显著优于TnT-LLM（15–40× 更快、8–49× 更便宜），但在需要深入技术推理时质量较低。提出在实际使用前先用子集评估复杂度与成本以决定是否采用自动化方法。

Conclusion: 自动化方法可在SE领域实际应用，但不应盲目替代人工：当目标数据涉及深层技术推理或对分类复杂性敏感时仍需人工；对于大规模或对成本/速度敏感的场景，CLIMB类方法是可行的折中方案。本文为后续改进自动化分类法在SE适用性与可靠性的研究奠定了基线。

Abstract: Taxonomies provide a shared conceptual framework for organizing heterogeneous observations in software engineering (SE) research. Manually constructing such taxonomies is labor-intensive and requires annotators with expertise in the SE domain. While advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have led to the emergence of automated taxonomy generation methods outside the SE domain, their applicability to technically complex SE artifacts remains unclear. In this experience paper, we present the first comprehensive empirical evaluation of how state-of-the-art automated methods perform on SE artifacts through a multi-perspective evaluation framework, including taxonomy quality, alignment with taxonomies defined by human experts, reliability under independent annotation, and efficiency. To support this evaluation, we systematically collect seven SE papers with publicly available artifacts and human-defined taxonomies, and conduct experiments using two automated methods (TnT-LLM and CLIMB) with five state-of-the-art LLMs. Our evaluation reveals a clear trade-off: TnT-LLM constructs high-quality taxonomies comparable to human-defined ones but incurs substantially higher cost and runtime and tends to generate overly complex taxonomies, whereas CLIMB is 15--40$\times$ faster and 8--49$\times$ cheaper but tends to score lower on quality when technical inference beyond surface-level similarity is required. These findings suggest that TnT-LLM and CLIMB can be used in practical situations in the SE domain, while researchers should first assess the complexity of the generated taxonomies and their cost using a subset of the target data to decide whether to use automated methods or human experts. Our work represents a first step toward a systematic understanding of automated taxonomy generation in SE, offering actionable insights for future research and practice.

</details>


### [34] [AI-assisted Script Management for Requirements Elicitation Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01640)
*Anmol Singhal,Paulo Carvalho,Travis Breaux*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文设计并验证了一套将业务目标驱动脚本生成与实时脚本管理结合的AI辅助需求访谈工作流；实证结果表明AI辅助改变了访谈轨迹与产出，提升脚本质量并作为有效的获取脚手架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求获取访谈中被访者互动与话题覆盖、主动倾听和自适应探究之间的平衡困难，且现有研究多聚焦孤立任务（如脚本或跟进问题生成），缺乏对集成化支持和实时脚本管理如何影响访谈过程与产物的理解。

Method: 提出并实现一种AI辅助的需求获取工作流：结合以业务目标为基础的理论指导脚本生成、实时主题覆盖追踪与按需后续问题生成；并通过一项组间准实验研究评估该工作流（无训练的AI辅助组 vs 有训练的无AI组），使用基于需求获取最佳实践的评分量表评估脚本质量和访谈产出。

Result: AI生成脚本质量显著高于仅培训生成脚本（92.8 vs. 74.8/100）；AI辅助组访谈覆盖的话题更少（9.6 vs. 14.5），但遵循脚本问题比例更高（86% vs. 69%）、每话题后续问题更多（3.43 vs. 1.15），并产生更精炼的目标模型（最低层级目标比例0.653 vs. 0.598）。参与者认为脚本管理有用，86%认同主题追踪是最有用的功能。

Conclusion: AI辅助工作流在提高脚本质量、促使访谈更依脚本推进并生成更精炼目标模型方面具有优势，但同时导致覆盖话题减少——表明AI支持会改变访谈策略与产出结构，脚本管理被访者高度认可，未来研究可将AI作为获取过程的支架并进一步探索覆盖与深度的权衡。

Abstract: Requirements elicitation interviews require interviewers to balance topic coverage, active listening, and adaptive probing while responding to stakeholders in real time. Although prior work has explored AI support for isolated interviewing tasks, such as script generation and follow-up question generation, little is known about how integrated support affects the interview and what requirements artifacts emerge. Furthermore, script management---which helps the interviewer track topic coverage in real time and decide when to probe further---remains underexplored. This paper presents an AI-assisted elicitation workflow that combines theory-guided script generation grounded in business goals with live support for topic coverage tracking and on-demand follow-up question generation. We evaluate the workflow in a between-subjects quasi-experimental study comparing a no-training, AI-assisted condition with a training, AI-unassisted condition. Based on a rubric derived from elicitation best practices, the AI-generated scripts score higher than training-only scripts (92.8 vs. 74.8 out of 100). AI-assisted interviews cover fewer topics (9.6 vs. 14.5), cover more scripted questions (86% vs. 69%), ask more follow-ups per topic (3.43 vs. 1.15), and produce more refined goal models (lowest-level goal fraction 0.653 vs. 0.598). Participants find script management useful, rating topic tracking as the most useful workflow feature (86% agreement). Collectively, these results show that the AI-assisted condition is associated with a different interview trajectory and different elicited requirements than a training-only condition, positioning AI-assisted workflows as elicitation scaffolds for future studies.

</details>


### [35] [Coding Agents as Test-Suite Auditors: Finding What Official Suites Miss While Approaching What They Catch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01715)
*Shuyang Xie,Shuxiao Xie,Feng Zhu,Yanli Ji,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出用现成编程代理自动生成对抗测试并配合证实链验证被接受的有缺陷提交，实验证明该方法能在真实评测站点（AtCoder/Codeforces）发现大量被官方套件遗漏的错误，同时维持接近官方套件的覆盖率和在无官方套件场景下优越的发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作指出官方题库接受存在缺陷的提交，但这些审计多停留在提示问题层面，缺乏可操作的修复或验证手段。本工作旨在提供可实践的自动化审计与证实流程，提高以在线判定为基准的评估与数据集质量。

Method: 使用现成的编程代理充当测试套件审计器：对每个题目生成对抗性输入以暴露官方套件遗漏；对代理标注为有缺陷的提交，采用一条证实链来判定其真实性——多个独立实现达成一致时确定期望输出，若有分歧用暴力穷举解法解决，并用每题的输入合法性验证器认证失败输入。

Result: 在对AtCoder 20,375个被审计的通过提交中，单一代理识别并证实了589个被官方接受但确有缺陷的提交；对五个代理采用相同证实流程的并集下限为906个。五个代理在官方套件能捕获的逻辑错误覆盖率上，各自与官方套件的差距不超过1.7个百分点。在无官方套件的Codeforces后切截止题目上，所生测试方法在给定输入预算下使所有五个基线复现成功；在有官方套件的题目上，代理用于审计套件充分性，在无官方套件时则能捕获更多的有缺陷提交。

Conclusion: 官方在线评测与基于其构建的数据集并不总是可靠的“金标准”；通过自动化的对抗测试与证实链可以发现并验证被错误接受的有缺陷提交，从而改进评估与训练基准的质量。

Abstract: Online-judge verdicts and the datasets and benchmarks built on them are treated as ground truth for evaluating and training large language models for code. Yet prior audits have sounded a warning: official suites accept buggy submissions. These audits, however, stop at the warning and offer no practical remedy. Our remedy has two parts: an off-the-shelf coding agent, serving as a test-suite auditor, both builds adversarial test suites to expose what official suites miss and supplies these suites where no official suite exists; a certification chain determines whether each agent-flagged submission is genuinely buggy without relying on the official judge: multiple independently written accepted solutions agree on the expected output for every test, brute-force solutions settle disagreements, and a per-problem validator certifies each failing input legal. One such agent identifies 589 verified accepted-but-buggy submissions among AtCoder's 20,375 audited accepted submissions; extending the same certification to all five agents yields a union floor of 906 such submissions. Five agents, scored separately, each stay within 1.7pp of official-suite coverage on logic bugs those suites catch. On post-cutoff Codeforces problems with no available official suites, the same test-building method leads all five reproduced baselines at every tested input budget. Where an official suite exists, the agent audits suite adequacy instead of assuming it; where none exists, agent suites catch the most buggy submissions among methods we reproduced and tested.

</details>


### [36] [Effective and Efficient Context Retrieval via Partial Dependency Graph for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01927)
*Zhongxin Liu,Zhonghao Jiang,Zhifan Ye,Haoye Wang,Jiakun Liu,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DyRetriever：基于LLM的入口点选择与多跳语义验证，按需构建并丢弃部分依赖图，与相似度检索结合的DyCoder在两个基准上显著提升代码生成正确率并大幅降低依赖图开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG依赖相似度检索，常常漏检目标函数所依赖的代码；图检索虽能建模依赖但多依赖人工规则与全局静态图，灵活性和维护成本差；人类开发者常通过构建部分依赖图并沿其迭代检查来收集上下文，启发了该工作。

Method: 使用LLM作为策略器：先由LLM选择一组“入口点”函数，然后沿代码依赖图进行多跳检索；在多跳过程中用LLM的语义判断来验证某个被检索的函数是否对目标函数生成有帮助，从而避免手工规则；按需构建部分依赖图（partial dependency graph），检索完成后丢弃，降低静态全局图的构建维护成本；将该动态检索器与基于相似度的代码检索器结合，形成DyCoder。

Result: 在CoderEval和DevEval上，DyCoder相较于现有RAG方法在Pass@1上分别取得了相对提升25.63%和59.73%；同时在效率上，比基于静态依赖图构建的基线快约7.4倍。

Conclusion: DyRetriever通过将LLM用于检索策略和语义判断，实现了更灵活且高效的仓库级代码上下文检索，兼顾性能与成本；但其效果依赖于LLM对语义依赖的判断能力，且在极大规模或高度动态的依赖关系中仍需进一步验证与优化。

Abstract: LLM-based repository-level code generation aims to generate code using the context available in a software repository, requiring LLMs to reason over complex code dependencies. Due to limited context windows and insufficient repository-specific understanding, LLMs typically rely on retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to incorporate relevant code. Early RAG approaches primarily employ similarity-based retrieval, which often fails to retrieve code snippets that the target function depends on. Recent work introduces graph-based retrieval to model such dependencies, but typically relies on manually designed rules and static global graphs, leading to limited flexibility and high construction and maintenance costs. In contrast, human developers collect helpful context by implicitly constructing a partial dependency graph and iteratively inspecting along it. Inspired by this behavior, we propose DyRetriever, an efficient context retrieval method via partial dependency graphs. DyRetriever uses an LLM to first select a set of entry-point functions and then perform multi-hop reasoning along the code dependency graph. During multi-hop reasoning, it uses the LLM's semantic understanding to validate whether a function can help generate the target function, eliminating manually designed rules and enabling flexibility across scenarios. Instead of statically constructing a global dependency graph, DyRetriever builds a partial graph on demand and discards it after use, reducing construction and maintenance costs. We integrate DyRetriever with a similarity-based code retriever to build DyCoder and evaluate it on CoderEval and DevEval. Experimental results show that DyCoder achieves relative Pass@1 improvements of 25.63% and 59.73% on CoderEval and DevEval, respectively, compared with existing RAG-based methods, while being 7.4x faster than baselines based on static dependency graph construction.

</details>


### [37] [TELLER: Non-intrusive Cross-Layer Root-Cause Analysis for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01975)
*Ruilin Xu,Junyi Li,Pengfei Chen,Zongxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TELLER通过无侵入地采集GPU追踪与日志、重构请求级调用树，并用依赖感知切片+TPE结构化token压缩表示，辅以多模态根因模型，实现对多节点LLM推理的细粒度根因定位与可解释诊断，展示了压缩-准确度的权衡与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对由离线运行变为持续在线服务的LLM推理，定位性能/错误根因困难：单个请求跨越推理引擎、Python/C++后端、主机CUDA API、GPU内核以及分布式通信，现有剖析器只给出原始时间线、基于日志的诊断又往往缺失跨层语义与请求级结构。作者希望在不修改模型二进制的前提下，把追踪与日志结合以实现精细的逐请求根因分析。

Method: (1) 非侵入式采集：使用NVTX/CUPTI抓取GPU/主机追踪，同时收集服务日志；(2) 重构调用链：把追踪事件聚合到按请求的调用链树并对齐日志行；(3) 依赖感知切片：定义保持父子关系、时间顺序和通信关系的因果上下文切片；(4) Trace Pair Encoding（TPE）：把切片编码为含父节点、深度、时长属性的结构化token序列以压缩长度；(5) 根因模型：结合数值候选定位与一个多模态模型，联合预测异常步骤、定位可疑算子并生成自然语言解释；(6) 实验评估：在多节点GPU推理负载上对压缩-准确度权衡、不同故障先验、基线强化、模态消融、解释质量与追踪开销进行评估。

Result: 提出TELLER：一个非侵入式的追踪与日志感知的LLM推理根因分析框架。它收集NVTX/CUPTI追踪与服务日志，重构每个请求的调用链树并对齐日志行，引入保留父子结构、时间顺序与通信关系的依赖感知因果上下文切片；设计Trace Pair Encoding（TPE）将切片压缩为带父节点、深度和时长属性的结构化token序列。基于此，TELLER结合数值候选定位与多模态根因模型，联合预测异常步骤、定位可疑算子并生成自然语言解释。实验表明：适度的TPE词表能将每步追踪长度压缩80%+，在横向（跨节点通信）和纵向（节点内执行栈）视角上取得最佳诊断表现；而过度压缩则显著降低质量。

Conclusion: TELLER为LLM推理的故障/性能排查提供了实用的分流与证据定位基底：在不修改模型二进制的前提下，跨层对齐追踪与日志并用结构化压缩表示，可以显著降低数据量同时保持高质量诊断；但表示压缩有上限，过度压缩会破坏诊断线索。框架在低故障先验、增强基线与开销评估下表现稳健，为生产环境中的快速RCA提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference has evolved from an offline workload into a continuously operated software service, yet root-cause analysis remains difficult because a single request spans the inference engine, Python/C++ backend, host CUDA APIs, GPU kernels, and distributed communication. Existing profilers expose raw timelines, while log-based diagnosis often misses cross-layer execution semantics and request-level structure. We present TELLER, a non-intrusive Trace- and Log-aware LLM inference Root-cause analysis framework. TELLER first collects NVTX/CUPTI traces and service logs without modifying model binaries, then reconstructs per-request call-chain trees and aligns log lines with the corresponding execution steps. We introduce a dependency-aware causal-context slice that preserves parent-child structure, temporal order, and communication relations, and a Trace Pair Encoding (TPE) tokenizer that compresses such slices into compact structural token sequences with parent, depth, and duration attributes. On top of these representations, TELLER combines numeric candidate localization with a multimodal root-cause model that jointly predicts abnormal steps, localizes suspicious operators, and generates natural-language explanations. Experiments on multi-node GPU inference workloads show a clear compression-accuracy trade-off: a moderate TPE vocabulary reduces per-step trace length by more than 80% while achieving the best overall performance on both horizontal (cross-node communication) and vertical (within-node execution stack) views, whereas more aggressive compression substantially degrades diagnosis quality. Further analyses under low-fault priors, strengthened baselines, modality ablations, explanation-quality checks, and tracing overhead show that TELLER provides a practical triage and evidence-localization substrate for LLM inference RCA.

</details>


### [38] [VulnGym: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Repository-Level Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02001)
*Kexing Ji,Jiachen Liu,Enze Hu,Cuiyun Gao,Keke Lian,Yongheng Liu,Lei Zhang,Tian Dong,Hao Chen,Wang Bin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VulnGym提供了带行级追踪注释的真实仓库级漏洞检测基准（184通告、408条目、23仓库），支持端到端与诊断子任务评估，揭示当前编程代理在定位与证据构建上的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的漏洞检测工作和基准大多基于预选的代码片段（函数级），而不是完整仓库。为了评估具备自动探索与定位能力的编程代理，需要一个仓库级、真实世界且带有细粒度漏洞追踪的基准数据集。

Method: 手工/半自动将被审查的GitHub安全通告与相应易受攻击版本的仓库对齐，逐条注释行级入口点、关键操作与漏洞传播路径；基于这些注释定义端到端任务和三个oracle子任务（用于局部评估定位与证据构建）；采用若干主流编程代理做基线评估，分析其在检测率、定位精度和证据质量上的表现。

Result: 提出VulnGym：一个真实世界的仓库级基准，将审查过的GitHub通告与相应的易受攻击版本仓库对齐。数据集包含184个通告、408条漏洞条目、23个仓库，每条条目都带有行级入口点、关键操作和漏洞追踪注释。基于这些注释，定义了一个端到端检测任务及三个基于oracle的子任务，用于联合评估漏洞检测并诊断代码定位与证据构建的局限性。评估表明：现有编程代理在端到端仓库级检测与生成准确支持性证据方面仍存在不足。

Conclusion: VulnGym填补了现有基准不能评估仓库级自动搜索与定位能力的空白；借助细粒度追踪注释，可以更精确地诊断代理在代码定位与证据生成上的能力缺陷，未来需在导航策略、上下文管理与证据合成上改进代理设计。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-based vulnerability detection have shown promising results, while coding agents further extend this capability from isolated code snippets to complete repositories. This shift requires agents to autonomously explore repositories and locate vulnerability-relevant code, instead of performing detection on preselected functions. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on vulnerability classification over preselected code snippets, limiting their ability to evaluate coding agents in repository-level vulnerability detection. Moreover, without fine-grained vulnerability trace annotations, the capability limitations underlying the detection process remain difficult to explore. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{VulnGym}, a real-world repository-level benchmark for evaluating vulnerability detection by coding agents. VulnGym aligns reviewed GitHub advisories with their corresponding vulnerable version repositories. It contains 184 advisories and 408 vulnerability entries across 23 repositories, with each entry annotated with line-level entry points, critical operations, and vulnerability traces. Using this fine-grained ground truth, VulnGym defines an end-to-end detection task and three oracle-based subtasks to jointly evaluate vulnerability detection and diagnose limitations in code localization and evidence construction. Our evaluation indicates that current coding agents remain limited in both end-to-end repository-level vulnerability detection and the construction of accurate supporting traces.

</details>


### [39] [Pretraining on Call Graphs: When Binary Analysis Tasks Profit From Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02084)
*Samuel Valenzuela,Johannes Kinder*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 把调用图作为上下文能提高相似性检测和鲁棒性，尤其对上下文依赖型函数有利，但提升并不总能泛化到语义或语法的下游任务，且语义优化可能损害语法相关性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估并剖析将调用图（inter-procedural context）信息融入二进制函数嵌入模型的影响：是否能提升二进制代码相似性检测（BCSD）以及这种改进是否能泛化到其他逆向工程下游任务（语义或语法相关）；探索语义优化与语法任务之间的权衡与鲁棒性变化。

Method: 取两种SOTA二进制函数嵌入器作为基础表征；构造并训练多种图神经网络/图增强策略，将调用图信息整合到函数嵌入中；在多种任务集（包括BCSD、语义与语法下游任务）上评估性能与鲁棒性；通过数据集分层（如命名空间相关 vs 逻辑集中函数）、错误分析与消融实验来解释观察到的行为。

Result: 基于两种最先进的二进制函数嵌入模型，采用多种图模型对嵌入进行增强并在多个任务上评估。发现：将调用图上下文引入能在BCSD上带来提升，但该提升并不必然泛化到其他下游任务；针对语义相似性的优化往往与语法任务性能呈负相关；调用图增强能显著提高嵌入的鲁棒性，尤其在原始模型表现弱的情形下；上下文信息对于命名空间/依赖上下文强的函数更有益，对独立逻辑型函数帮助有限。

Conclusion: 在二进制函数嵌入的工程实践中，应根据目标任务谨慎选择是否引入调用图上下文并设定合适的训练/评估指标；单纯追求BCSD性能可能导致在其他任务上的退步；调用图增强适合用于上下文依赖的分析场景或提升模型在弱模型区域的稳定性。

Abstract: Binary function embedding models are trained to encode the semantics of binary code in such a way that they can be generalized to a variety of reverse engineering tasks, such as binary code search, vulnerability detection, or malware classification. While many models only take the function in question as contextual input, there have been successful attempts to improve function embeddings by leveraging information from the call graph. In this study, we dissect the implications of these embedding refinements. We conduct experiments using a range of graph-based models on the embeddings generated by two state-of-the-art binary function embedding models. Integrating inter-procedural context, we show that improvements on binary code similarity detection (BCSD) will not necessarily generalize to downstream tasks, neither of semantic nor of syntactic nature. More generally, we find that optimizing for semantic similarity tasks correlates with worse performance on syntactic tasks. By conducting an explanatory analysis on the dataset, we find that the call graph-based enhancements significantly enhance the robustness of embeddings, particularly in scenarios where the initial models struggle. Furthermore, we observe that the added context is more beneficial for namespace-related functions than for those focused on individual logic, confirming that the call graph can be leveraged most effectively in context-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [Lossless Tensor Compression as Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02162)
*Jieke Shi,Junda He,Wenjia Jiang,Weifeng Sun,Shidong Pan,Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Peixin Zhang,Yifan Jia,Zhou Yang,Thong Hoang,Xiwei Xu,Zhenchang Xing,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Brevis compresses tensors losslessly by synthesizing reversible DSL programs per tensor using learned priors and A* search, yielding ~34% storage reduction and competitive speed, outperforming general-purpose and some tensor-specific compressors.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Checkpoint files for machine learning are growing large and numerous; general-purpose compressors ignore tensor semantics and tensor-specific compressors use fixed pipelines, so a more flexible, tensor-aware, lossless compression approach is needed.

Method: Design a typed domain-specific language with reversible operators modeling tensor structures; learn a checkpoint-specific production prior from sample tensors; use bounded A* search guided by the prior to synthesize compact DSL programs for each tensor; execute synthesized programs for bit-exact decompression.

Result: Compressed 2.13 TB of checkpoint data to 1.41 TB (33.93% reduction) across 10 public checkpoints; produced archives up to 30.87% smaller than zstd/gzip and smaller than ZipNN and DFloat11; achieved 3.60 GB/s compression and 6.61 GB/s decompression while preserving every source byte.

Conclusion: Brevis formulates lossless tensor compression as program synthesis, using a typed DSL and learned priors to synthesize compact, reversible programs that reconstruct tensors bit-exactly; it achieves significant storage reductions and competitive speed while outperforming general-purpose and some tensor-specific compressors on tested checkpoints.

Abstract: Model checkpoints are growing in both number and size, which makes archival, transfer, and deployment increasingly costly. General-purpose compressors can reduce storage requirements but ignore tensor structure, whereas existing tensor-specific compressors rely on fixed and format-specific pipelines. We present Brevis, which formulates lossless tensor compression as program synthesis. We design a typed domain-specific language (DSL) that captures recurring tensor structures, such as repeated regions and floating-point fields, through a set of reversible operators. Given a tensor, Brevis synthesizes a self-contained DSL program that reconstructs it bit-exactly. A checkpoint-specific production prior, learned from a small representative sample of tensors, guides a bounded A* search to synthesize compact programs, which can later be executed directly for bit-exact decompression. On 10 public checkpoints spanning language, audio, and image generation models, Brevis reduces 2.13 TB of checkpoint data to 1.41 TB, a 33.93% storage reduction. It produces archives up to 30.87% smaller than those of four general-purpose compressors, including zstd and gzip, and smaller archives than the tensor-specific compressors ZipNN and DFloat11. Under a practical concurrency configuration, Brevis achieves 3.60 GB/s compression and 6.61 GB/s decompression while preserving every source byte.

</details>


### [41] [ECLAIR: A Causally-Grounded AI Framework for Scientific Discovery in Empirical Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02323)
*Alejandro Velasco,Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,Dipin Khati,David N. Palacio,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ECLAIR：一个人机闭环、以因果推断为基础的LLM科研框架；案例研究显示某些提示设计对代码生成准确性有小幅负面因果效应，为SE中的AI辅助实证研究提供结构化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统的复杂性阻碍传统科学方法的系统化应用；需要一种将LLMs安全、可解释地嵌入经验SE研究的框架，以满足因果解释和可重复性的认识论要求。

Method: 基于因果推断原则设计研究流程：将LLMs作为能执行假设生成、实验设计、分析与解释的代理人在人类监督下工作；在案例研究中对两种LLMs进行提示变体（说明式、长few-shot、签名增强等）干预并测量代码生成准确性，以估计因果效应。

Result: 在所测两种LLMs上，说明式提示、较长的few-shot示例和函数签名增强均对代码生成准确性产生小的负向因果效应；展示因果思路可用于解释软件现象的发生机制，并提出ECLAIR作为可复现的AI辅助研究方法论。

Conclusion: ECLAIR是一种以因果推断为核心的AI框架，将大语言模型（LLMs）作为“科学代理人”嵌入科学研究流程，并通过人机闭环降低不可靠自动推理的风险；通过提示工程对代码生成准确性的案例研究，报告若干提示特性对准确性的弱负因果效应，提出在软件工程实证研究中系统化使用LLMs的首个因果化方法论。

Abstract: The scientific method has long guided empirical research in Software Engineering (SE), but the complexity of modern software systems often hinders its systematic application. This paper introduces _ECLAIR_, a causally grounded AI framework that integrates Large Language Models (_LLMs_) into every stage of the scientific process, from hypothesis generation to analysis and interpretation. _ECLAIR_ treats _LLMs_ as active **scientific agents** operating under the principles of causal inference, within a human-in-the-loop design that safeguards against the risks of unsound automated reasoning. We demonstrate the framework through a case study examining how prompt design influences code generation accuracy in two _LLMs_. Results show that, for both models, instruction-style, longer few-shot, and signature-augmented prompts yield small negative causal effects on accuracy, illustrating how causal reasoning provides a principled foundation for explaining _why_ software phenomena occur. This study presents the first causally grounded structured methodology for embedding _LLMs_ within the scientific method in SE, designed around the epistemological demands of empirical SE research, establishing a basis for rigorous AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [42] [On Automated and Explainable Provenance of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02329)
*Alejandro Velasco,Nathan Wintersgill,Trevor Stalnaker,Oscar Chaparro,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 为代码生成AI提出“可解释溯源”愿景，结合开发者与合规需求，定义四个追溯维度并给出以大规模实证、因果分析与可解释性技术为核心的研究路线图，强调现有工具缺乏必要的来源解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式代码来源不透明，给开发者、组织和合规工作带来风险；现有缓解措施是事后标注问题输出但不解释成因，因而需要自动、可解释的溯源以提升透明度并指导改进。

Method: 基于NSF资助的研究愿景，作者用对软件开发者、模型使用者和合规/法律专业人士的实证调查作为需求证据，提出结合大规模用户研究与事后因果与可解释性技术的研究计划。

Result: 提出以可解释溯源为基础的CodeGenAI研究愿景，主张建立自动的事后可追溯性，将生成代码与提示组件、训练数据实例、全局数据特征和内部模型组件关联起来，并以实证研究为基础概述问题维度与开放挑战。

Conclusion: 实现可解释溯源的CodeGenAI既是必要的也是具有挑战性的：需要跨学科方法、可扩展的追溯机制和隐私/法律对策，社区需解决因果归因、规模化与评估基准等关键问题。

Abstract: Generative AI for code generation has transformed software development, but it has also introduced a critical transparency problem: the origins of AI-generated code are opaque to the developers who use it, the organizations that deploy it, and the compliance professionals responsible for ensuring its legal and quality standards. Existing mitigations flag problematic outputs after the fact without explaining why a model produced them or how future generation could be improved. We present a research vision, grounded in a U.S. NSF-funded research grant, that argues that the next generation of CodeGenAI tools must be built on a foundation of explainable provenance: automated, post-hoc traceability that links generated code back to the prompt components, training data instances, global data features, and internal model components that caused its generation. We grounded this vision in empirical evidence from studies of software developers, model users, and compliance/legal professionals, which show that provenance information is a practical necessity that current tools do not provide. We characterize the problem across four traceability dimensions, outline a research program combining large-scale empirical studies with post-hoc causal and interpretability techniques, and identify the key open challenges that the community must address to realize this vision.

</details>


### [43] [Profitability of Open-Source Software Product Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02398)
*Shivendu P. Singh,Narayan Ramasubbu,Chris F. Kemerer,M. Zia Hydari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对近千家美高科技企业的面板分析显示：开源开发能提高毛利（+4–5%），源于外部贡献带来的知识整合与劳动生产率提升，但需要外部贡献达到较高占比且与持续内部研发配合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估开放源码（open-source）产品开发对高科技企业绩效的实际经济回报，即开放式开发是否能提高企业利润与劳动生产率，并探究作用机制与条件。

Method: 基于977家美企2001–2025年的面板数据，作者通过回归分析估计开源采用与毛利的关联，进行生产率分解以验证途径，使用阈值/非线性分析识别外部贡献占比的临界点，并用组合配置分析（多路径/配置方法）识别高盈利的资源-实践组合。

Result: 使用2001–2025年977家美国产业样本的实证分析，发现采用开源开发的企业平均毛利率提高约4–5%，主要通过提升劳动生产率实现；显性条件是外部志愿者贡献占比达到约35%才呈现收益，并且高盈利路径均以持续内部研发配套为核心。

Conclusion: 开源应被视为一种知识整合战略而非仅仅是廉价劳动力来源；企业应结合自身资源配置、保证持续内研以实现盈利化；政策层面应支持技术志愿者社区与公私开放源码基础设施。

Abstract: Many technology firms now build software products in the open, inviting outside developers to contribute alongside their employees on platforms like GitHub. Does this openness in product development pay off? Analyzing 977 U.S. high-tech firms from 2001 to 2025, this study finds that open-source adoption raised firms' gross margins by 4-5% on average. These gains flow largely through higher labor productivity, as firms integrate external contributors' diverse knowledge into internal workflows, broadening the organizational knowledge base without a commensurate rise in labor costs. However, the payoff emerges only when outside volunteers supply a meaningful share of the work (around 35% in this sample), and hinges on the firm's resource configuration. While there are multiple pathways to profitability, pairing open-source product development with sustained internal R&D is a core condition present in all high-profitability configurations. For managers, the lesson is to treat open source as a knowledge-integration strategy matched to the firm's resources, not merely a means of sourcing external effort at lower cost. For policymakers, the findings underscore the broad economic value of open-source ecosystems and the need for policies that foster skilled volunteer communities and public-private open-source infrastructure.

</details>


### [44] [SWE-Touch: Benchmarking Coding Agents When Users Touch the Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02499)
*Yuqiao Tan,Jinxiang Meng,Fangyu Lei,Minzheng Wang,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Touch通过注入合理但冲突的用户补丁，揭示了编码模型在共享工作区场景中对代码变更感知与适应能力的不足，指出未来需增强变更检测、冲突调和与针对性验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实软件开发中用户与编码代理在共享工作区并行工作，用户可能在任务进行时查看或修改代码；现有基于仓库的基准通常评估独立工作的代理或仅允许消息交互，无法衡量代理对并发代码变更的理解与响应能力。

Method: 提出SWE-Touch框架，通过构建并注入“Counter-Edits”（对任务相关代码的合理但与任务冲突的修改）来压力测试代理。方法包括：从多条修复轨迹挖掘任务关键区域、用独立的用户补丁生成器构造补丁、在代理到达相关代码时伴随上下文用户消息注入补丁；在SWE-bench Verified及更长时域的SWE-Bench Pro和DeepSWE上评估九个编码模型，并对轨迹进行失败分析。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上引入Counter-Edit使平均解决率下降7.7个百分点，这种性能下降在更长时域基准中也持续存在。轨迹分析表明失败主要源于代理对演化中工作区的有限感知：代理可能保留冲突代码或在未充分重新检查与验证的情况下替换代码。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：当前强大的自主编码代理在共享工作区场景中仍然缺乏足够的“状态感知”与“自适应”能力，无法可靠地探测并与用户注入的冲突性修改协调，需在检测工作区变化、调和冲突性修改并对受影响行为进行验证方面进行改进。

Abstract: Real-world software development requires coding agents to operate in shared workspaces where users may inspect and modify code during an ongoing task, yet existing repository-level benchmarks typically evaluate agents working alone or restrict user participation to messages. This leads us to ask: how do coding agents understand and respond to code changes in a shared workspace? We introduce SWE-Touch, a framework that stress-tests this setting through validated Counter-Edits: plausible edits to task-relevant code that conflict with task completion. SWE-Touch mines task-critical regions from multiple repair trajectories, uses a separate User Patch Generator to construct the edits, and injects them with contextual user messages when agents reach the relevant code. We evaluate nine coding models on SWE-bench Verified, with additional experiments on longer-horizon tasks from SWE-Bench Pro and DeepSWE. Counter-Edit lowers average resolve rate by 7.7 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified, with degradation also persisting on both longer-horizon benchmarks. Trajectory analysis links these failures to limited awareness of the evolving workspace: agents may retain conflicting code or replace it without sufficiently re-inspecting the repository and validating the revised code with targeted tests. These findings show that strong autonomous performance does not yet ensure the state awareness and adaptive behavior needed for shared-workspace collaboration, and point to detecting workspace changes, reconciling conflicting edits with the task, and verifying the affected behavior as key capabilities for future optimization.

</details>


### [45] [Decomposing the Doer Effect in Programming Practice: Code Writing Stands Out Among Active Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02541)
*Arun Balajiee Lekshmi Narayanan,Gillian Gold,Jordan Barria-Pineda,Quinn K Wolter,Peter Brusilovsky,Paulo Carvalho*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 日志数据分析表明在计算机科学教学中存在“做者效应”：主动练习比被动观看更有效（3.2倍），其中代码书写问题与学习成绩的正相关最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Examine whether the "doer effect" applies in computer science instruction and whether different forms of active practice differ in their association with learning outcomes.

Method: Observational log-data analysis of 334 students across 11 semesters using an interactive practice system with five content types (Code Writing, Code Tracing, Code Completion, Code Visualizations, Code Explanations); compared associations between activity types (active vs passive, and among active types) and posttest performance.

Result: Active practice activities were associated with 3.2× better learning outcomes than passive activities. Among active types, code writing showed the strongest association with improved posttest performance; other active types did not show comparable associations.

Conclusion: 支持将高挑战性且带反馈的练习（如代码书写）作为教学核心，但由于研究为观察性关联分析，需以随机对照试验及更细致的过程数据分析来验证因果性与机制。

Abstract: The "doer effect" suggests that actively doing practice activities is more strongly associated with learning outcomes than passively viewing content. In the doer effect literature, "doing" refers specifically to active practice. However, this categorization treats different forms of active practice as equivalent, leaving open whether some types of active practice are more effective than others. In this paper, we investigate whether the doer effect extends to computer science instruction and whether some forms of doing stand out compared to other forms. We analyze log data from 334 students across 11 semesters of introductory and intermediate Java who used an interactive practice system with five content types: Code Writing, Code Tracing, Code Completion, Code Visualizations, and Code Explanations. Consistent with prior doer effect work, we find that active practice activities were associated with 3.2 times better learning outcomes than passive activities. Interestingly, among the active practice, code writing was the most strongly associated with improved posttest performance, while no other activity type showed a comparable association. These results highlight the importance of challenging, feedback-supported practice activities, such as code writing problems.

</details>


### [46] [ACEM: A Cost Estimation Model for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02582)
*Mohammad El-Ramly*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可操作的早期代理化成本估算框架（ACEM），将成本分为 LLM、HITL、基础设施三类，通过 RF、CF、HIS 三个构件表达代理动力学，并提供将传统规模度量映射到 token 消耗的路径，供社区用真实项目数据标定与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成本估算模型以人工劳动（设计、编码、测试）为主导，但智能代理（agentic）软件工程将大量实现工作交给自治AI，产生新的、非确定性的成本维度（LLM token 消耗、HITL 监管、人形设施/编排基础设施），因此需要新的估算框架以衔接现有规模度量与这些成本结构。

Method: 模型方法论：构造分项成本表达式（cost_LLM, cost_HITL, cost_infra）；将规模度量映射到基准 token 估计；通过乘以 RF 与 CF 得到预期 token 消耗并转化为金钱；用 HIS 将监管强度映射为人工小时；提出标定流程（收集事务日志、token 计数、HITL 时间、运行时指标，使用回归/贝叶斯/混合效应模型估计常数并进行交叉验证）。

Result: 提出 ACEM（Agentic Cost Estimation Model），把代理化开发成本分解为 LLM 成本、HITL 成本和基础设施成本三部分；引入修正因子（Revision Factor, RF）、上下文因子（Context Factor, CF）和 HITL 强度评分（HIS）；并提供将 Use Case Points、Story Points、Function Points 映射为估计 token 消耗的机制。模型给出完整结构和标定方法，但将常数保留为符号，等待实证标定。

Conclusion: ACEM 是一个全规格的、可扩展的早期模型设计，旨在填补传统成本模型与 agentic 开发成本之间的空白，呼吁社区用项目数据对模型常数进行标定、测试与扩展。

Abstract: Traditional software cost estimation models, such as COCOMO II, Function Points, and Story Points, assume that development effort is primarily driven by human labor in design, coding, and testing. Agentic software engineering, where autonomous AI agents perform substantial implementation work and humans focus on planning, specification, and validation, challenges this assumption. New cost dimensions arise: large language model (LLM) token consumption across agent actions, Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) oversight effort, and infrastructure costs for agent orchestration and tooling. These costs are nondeterministic: identical tasks may consume different tokens, follow divergent reasoning paths, and require varying human correction, phenomena absent in traditional development. A new framework is needed to bridge standard sizing metrics with this cost structure.
  This paper proposes ACEM (Agentic Cost Estimation Model), which decomposes total agentic development cost into three additive dimensions: LLM, HITL, and infrastructure cost. ACEM introduces three constructs for agentic dynamics: the Revision Factor (RF), modeling token overhead from output rejection and retries; the Context Factor (CF), capturing rising token consumption as context accumulates; and the HITL Intensity Score (HIS), a four-level oversight classification scheme. It further maps Use Case Points, Story Points, and Function Points to estimated token consumption, enabling organizations to reuse existing project-scoping data for agentic cost forecasting.
  ACEM is presented as a fully specified model structure and calibration methodology, with constants left symbolic pending empirical grounding. As an early-stage proposal, it invites the research community to calibrate, test, and extend the model through real project data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [47] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 行为等价并不意味着组织等价；CCE通过κ_T度量将两者区分开来，对理解与AI安全分析具有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视莱布尼茨的‘磨坊机器’、图灵的模仿游戏与西尔的中文房间争论，通过更精确的编码/保存角度澄清何为‘理解’并为AI安全议题提供新的分析维度。

Method: 在一个玩具符号设定中引入形式化指标：用W_{causal,T}衡量任务表现，用κ_T衡量保留的内部结构如何高效支撑该表现；然后对比无压缩映射表（lookup/standing store）与紧凑生成系统（generative system）在两项指标上的表现差异。

Result: 构造性地展示了两类系统可以实现相似的W_{causal,T}但在κ_T上有显著差别：映射表需要不断膨胀的未复用映射来维持表现，而生成系统通过复用紧凑结构获得更高的κ_T，从而支持不同的关于内部组织的结论。

Conclusion: 该论文通过CCE框架将外在行为成功（W_{causal,T}）与内部结构支撑效率（κ_T）分离开来，指出理解/意识的争论应关注组织形态而不仅是输出行为。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [48] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B微调，结合7阶段的自我演化循环和三条防退化机制，实现从自然语言到可执行OpenFOAM仿真的自动化生成与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低使用OpenFOAM等开源CFD求解器的技术门槛，缩短配置时间，推动CFD工作流民主化并加速原型迭代。

Method: 在Qwen-coder 2.5-14B基础上进行微调，使用252条文本提示覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y+感知数值策略；核心为7阶段演化循环，并引入RAG增强重试上下文、词典级修补与提示多样化三条防退化流。

Result: AutoFOAM引入了一种自我演化的大型语言模型代理，用于生成并执行OpenFOAM模拟配置，目标是通过自然语言指令自动化CFD工作流程。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将生成式AI与严格流体模拟结合的可行性，能显著降低使用开源CFD求解器的门槛并加速原型开发，但需关注自训练退化的风险及验证模拟结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [49] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的方案：VectorRAG与GraphRAG，用于减少中小企业（SME）中大型语言模型（LLM）生成回答的幻觉与误导性信息。通过在LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen等模型上评估，报告RAG方法能提高响应有用性、上下文相关性并降低幻觉风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SME在采用LLM辅助问答与决策时，模型幻觉导致错误信息和信任下降；RAG被视为通过引入外部事实性知识减少幻觉的可行方案。

Method: 提出两种RAG架构：VectorRAG（基于向量检索的外部知识检索与融合）与GraphRAG（结合知识图谱或图结构检索以强化关系与上下文），并在多种开源与商用LLM上进行对比实验，衡量有用性、幻觉风险、上下文相关性与人工可解释性。

Result: 实验证明在所选LLM与任务上，采用RAG（尤其GraphRAG可能在关系推理上更优）能降低幻觉率、提升有用回答比例与上下文相关度，同时增强人类可解释性。

Conclusion: RAG-增强的LLM在SME场景中能显著降低幻觉与错误信息，提升回答质量与可解释性，从而支持更可靠的决策制定。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [50] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在RTX4060Ti上对9款1B–7B模型进行了GPU级能耗测量，结果表明架构与量化比参数规模更能解释能效差异，并强调测量分辨率、生成模式与量度定义对结论的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补现有评测过度关注精度而忽视能耗的缺口，为本地部署与隐私保护场景下的模型选择和量化策略提供可复现的硬件级能效数据。

Method: 在单块消费者GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上使用Ollama推理引擎，对九个开源LLM（1B–7B）进行硬件级能耗基准测试；通过nvidia-smi以2Hz采样GPU功耗，使用固定提示集并计算平均/峰值功耗、每提示总能量（J/prompt）、每输出token能量（J/token）和吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: 能效与参数量并非严格相关：模型架构与量化策略对能耗影响显著。gemma3:1b与llama3.2:1b表现最优（0.56 J/token、0.65 J/token，吞吐量>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral每token能耗最高，为最优模型的约4.4倍。qwen3.5:2b因内部延长推理过程导致每提示能耗异常升高，提示需区分不同生成模式。

Conclusion: 建议在后续基准中加入更高分辨率与系统级能耗测量、明确量化与解码配置、区分内部推理与token生成、公开完整配置与数据。为稳健比较，应控制功耗上限与热节流、执行多次重复试验、使用外部功率计并结合内核/硬件性能分析工具（如nvprof/Nsight）。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [51] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出训练-free的CoT-Core：基于零样本链式思维轨迹的例题聚类，用逻辑等价替代表面相似，减少评估样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核心集选择要么依赖大量历史交互（冷启动），要么基于词面相似导致错过逻辑等价题；为减少持续开发中对LLM评估的开销，需要一种轻量且推理感知的无训练方案。

Method: 对每道题用目标LLM生成zero-shot CoT输出（推理轨迹），将轨迹表征为向量（可用句嵌入或序列编码器），在该潜在空间中对题目进行聚类，按簇代表性选择核心题目用于评估；无需训练或大量历史日志。

Result: CoT-Core通过让LLM生成zero-shot CoT推理轨迹并把轨迹投影到潜在空间来聚类问题，从而按逻辑等价选择核心题集，在GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, GPQA上大幅降低评估成本同时保持高保真评分估计，效果受任务复杂度限制。

Conclusion: CoT-Core在多数据集上能显著加速LLM评估并保持评分精度，但其优势仅在具有明显推理步骤的中高复杂度任务中明显，简单或过复杂/噪声任务效果有限。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [52] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用语义验证的合成优化问题语料构建检索上下文，可以在无需微调的情况下显著提升LLM的优化建模能力，是可行的工程替代路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在生成优化/约束建模（特别是组合最优化）时常出现结构性不一致或不完整的公式，且微调成本高，探究是否通过语义验证的合成示例+检索即可显著提升建模质量。

Method: 构建一个基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流水线：用LLM生成职业人设和基于Text2Zinc种子描述合成500个优化问题（含JSON规范与对应经验证的Python求解脚本），将这些问题的向量化表示存入Chroma向量数据库；推理时检索语义相似示例并作为上下文供LangChain代理与Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型使用。

Result: 在Qwen 3 30B Instruct上，经检索增强后的性能有明显提升：NL4OPT准确率从40%提高到72%，MAMO Easy从40%到56%，MAMO Complex从32%到56%。合成语料的语义验证与检索上下文显著改善解的正确性与结构完整性。

Conclusion: 合成语料与检索增强的组合为部署LLM驱动的决策支持工具提供了实用路径，减少对昂贵微调的依赖，但仍需扩展数据量、验证泛化与公开复现性细节。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [53] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当记忆条目的“分数”来自模型自身评估时，会产生Echo Gap：高置信但错误的案例被反复强化。纠正该问题要求额外信号既要能追踪真值又要与原始评分误差解相关。基于此提出的LUCID在text-to-SQL任务上实现了稳健的无答案性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署时缺乏真实标签，系统只能用大模型自身的评分为记忆条目赋值；若自评分与原始偏差相关，会使错误条目获得高分并被优先检索，从而通过记忆累积放大错误，破坏自我改进效果。研究旨在揭示并修复这一失效模态。

Method: 理论分析+实证验证：将记忆化自我改进过程视作用分数作为代理奖励的非参数隐式策略，形式化引入“误差独立性假设（EIA）”，证明其为纠正分数膨胀的必要条件；指出自评替代真实标签会产生“回音裂缝”（Echo Gap），并提出无答案去膨胀算法LUCID；在BIRD text-to-SQL基准上进行对比实验（Memento式自评分、无记忆基线、相似性检索等）。

Result: 发现并定义了Echo Gap：错误条目被高估并重复使用；形式化了EIA并证明其为去膨胀所必需（可恢复收益是两个量的闭式函数：信号对真值的追踪度和与记忆偏差的误差去相关性）；证明无论按分数检索还是按相似性检索都存在膨胀问题；提出LUCID算法在无需真答案的情况下去膨胀，并在BIRD上将执行准确率从自评分Memento的54.0%提升到56.9%，优于无记忆基线52.4%。

Conclusion: 自我改进的记忆机制若依赖模型自评分，会系统性放大错误，必须保证用于去膨胀的信号满足误差独立性（EIA）。LUCID展示了可行的答案无关去膨胀方案，显著提升了BIRD基准的执行准确率，但仍建议在更多任务、模型和评分器多样性下验证并探索更广泛的去相关化手段（如外部验证器、模型集合或不确定性建模）。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [54] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Token按比例分配不能可靠代表批处理下的边际能耗；通过离线计算的Shapley真值校准轻量特征（JCalib）可在在线场景下实现更公平、低成本的请求级能耗归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理提高吞吐但使能耗核算复杂；GPU功耗只聚合上报，然而可持续性报告与计费需要请求级能耗。既有基准和近作未提供请求级测量真值，无法衡量实际规则与公平分配（Shapley）偏离程度。

Method: 构建两阶段归因框架：1) 离线基准（replay harness）在vLLM上回放请求子集，使用可复现协议与GPU功耗遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能耗；2) 轻量校准模型JCalib在在线服务时基于廉价请求特征预测Shapley份额。另扩展采样Shapley以覆盖更大组规模。

Result: 在16个模型/负载实验中，按token比例分配与精确Shapley的归一化L1误差平均为静态批处理0.440、连续批处理0.458；JCalib将误差降到静态0.116、连续0.177，优于不可在线使用的独立测量基线并保持批处理能效。跨三种数据中心GPU重复得到相似差距；采样Shapley用于更大组规模时差距仍存在，单次离线校准是最准确的可部署规则。

Conclusion: 测量得到的Shapley真值可用于训练低成本在线校准器，从而在保持批处理能效的同时显著改进请求级能耗归因。Token归因误差大，采样Shapley和离线校准提供可部署且更准确的替代方案，但需要更多硬件/场景验证和对开销的折衷评估。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [55] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba 在保持 Mamba 线性时间状态递归结构的同时，引入受三节点网络motif约束的低秩循环通路，通过将隐藏态投影到紧凑动力学子空间、施加motif引导的交互，再映射回原始状态空间，增强跨维通信，从而在长序列外推、语言建模和脑机接口解码任务上优于原始 Mamba。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba 的对角（或近对角）状态转移限制了显式的跨维交互，降低模型表达力。引入结构化的低秩交互（受生物/网络 motif 启发）可在不破坏线性效率的情况下增强不同状态维度间的信息流动。

Method: 在 Mamba 框架上新增一条低秩循环通路：1) 将原始隐藏态投影到紧凑的动力学子空间；2) 在该子空间施加基于三节点网络 motif 的交互约束（限定连接模式与权重结构）以产生低秩递归动力学；3) 将动力学输出映射回原始状态维度并与原始对角转移相结合，保持整体线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在长序列外推、若干语言建模基准以及脑—机接口解码任务上，Motif-Mamba 相较于 Mamba backbone 表现一致改善，说明 motif 引导的低秩动力学能作为有效的结构先验提高长程序列建模性能。

Conclusion: 通过在保持高效线性递归的前提下加入 motif 约束的低秩动力学路径，Motif-Mamba 提供了一种有效的结构先验，提升了长序列建模的表现与跨维交互能力。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [56] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova 通过全图融合、解析性调度与结构化内核合成，在保证数值精度的同时提升吞吐与内存效率，将 eager 框架的执行能力推向接近或超越专有库和编译器的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eager 执行的高层张量框架缺乏全图可见性与对硬件/内存的精细控制，导致无法在物理硬件上达到最佳性能和内存利用。

Method: 通过捕获 eager 执行并将前向与后向统一到单一值语义方言，实现整体图优化；使用解析性配置器（Analytic Configurator）基于算术强度确定确定性的最优执行调度，从而消除搜索时间；结合结构哈希运行时从计算结构综合细粒度内核。

Result: 在 RTX 3060 上，Nova 在大多数 TF32 矩阵乘法形状上与 cuBLAS 和 XLA 相当或稍优，保持严格的 <5e-4 相对误差；在模型级别对比，针对 4200 万参数模型比 PyTorch 吞吐量高最多 10.6%，比 XLA 高 4.4%；相较 PyTorch，内存占用最多降低 29%，使 12 GB GPU 可训练 1.44 亿参数模型（PyTorch OOM）以 17900 tokens/s 的速度训练。

Conclusion: Nova 是一个为张量框架的 eager 执行设计的端到端 JIT 编译器，目标是实现对硬件映射的绝对控制，通过整合前向/后向计算、跨操作融合、内存层次优化和寄存器级调优以获得极高的硬件利用率。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [57] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN 将多种幻觉检测方法、查询可答性判断和交互式可视化整合成一个可扩展的开源工具，用于判断 LLM 输出是否被上下文证据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在检索增强或长期记忆情境下会产生上下文幻觉：回答看起来合理但并未由提供的证据支持。社区需要一个统一、可交互的工具来检测、比较和评估多种检测方法并方便集成到实际系统中。

Method: 通过在一个接口、配置系统和评估流水线中整合三类检测范式——表示探测（representation probing）、不确定性估计（uncertainty estimation）和判官式验证（judge-style verification），并加入预生成查询可答性（pre-generation query answerability）任务，支持白盒与黑盒场景下的响应级和片段级检查；提供可实时分析的 Web UI（幻觉得分、不支持片段高亮、检测器并列比较）和轻量插件化检测器扩展机制。

Result: 实现并开源了 SIRIN（仓库链接），并展示其在幻觉检测、查询可答性判定及作为长期记忆系统内的可信性门控器等场景的应用；提供响应/片段级指标、白/黑盒支持和可扩展的检测器接口。

Conclusion: SIRIN 是一个统一的工具包和交互式 Web UI，用于检测检索增强、基于代理和基于记忆的 LLM 系统中的上下文幻觉（即流畅但不受提供证据支持的回答）。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [58] [Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00035)
*Brian Song,Michael A. Lepori,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Goal-directed visual processing is a hallmark of human visual intelligence, resulting in representations that support downstream tasks such as categorization or search. Though vision-language models (VLMs) are often faced with these same tasks, their ability to recode visual representations when presented with goal-directed language remains poorly characterized. Indeed, prior work largely treats visual representations in VLMs as static repositories of visual information that are manipulated by language representations. In the present work, we provide evidence for two concrete instances of language-induced recoding of visual representations. First, we identify an abstract reference representation that denotes which objects are goal-relevant under a natural language prompt. We extract contrastive steering vectors corresponding to this reference representation and demonstrate that they are causally implicated in model predictions. These reference representations are abstract in that they generalize to different objects, different task contexts, and even from synthetic to naturalistic images. Second, we demonstrate language-induced attribute modulation: later layers selectively amplify goal-relevant attributes in visual representations of objects. We demonstrate this phenomenon across a range of different prompts. Finally, we provide a causal intervention that demonstrates that attribute modulation mediates a VLM's response distribution. Together, our results support a more dynamic account of cross-modality processing in VLMs -- rather than vision tokens serving as static repositories of information, they are modulated to support queries articulated in language.

</details>


### [59] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RagTester是一个自动化端到端测试框架，用于检验RAG系统在检索、嵌入、生成和提示构造交互下的可靠性。它自动生成检索文档、测试输入和期望输出，用LLM作为评判器执行并评估测试，重点覆盖复杂段落、不可支持查询和文档覆盖率。作者在8个LLM与6个嵌入模型（共24种配置）上运行72,000次测试，发现21,633次失败，比基线多6.6%，在24种配置中优于基线的有20种。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统的可靠性不仅取决于单一模型，而是检索、嵌入、检索机制与生成提示之间的相互作用；缺乏系统化端到端测试会使部署前的风险难以发现，因此需要自动化测试方法来揭示交互导致的故障。

Method: RagTester自动生成检索文档、测试输入与期望输出；用设计的生成策略针对复杂段落、不可支持查询与文档覆盖率生成用例；在多种LLM与嵌入模型组合上执行测试并用一个LLM作为判决器进行答案评估；与一个基线测试输入生成器比较检测到的失败数和类型。

Result: 在72,000次测试中检测到21,633次失败（比基线多6.6%），在24个配置中有20个配置被RagTester优于基线；失败类别包括检索错误、答案不可支持、未完整使用检索上下文以及对复杂段落理解困难。

Conclusion: 面向覆盖的测试生成能有效揭露由检索与生成组件交互引起的问题，能在部署前评估RAG配置并发现诸如检索不准、给出不可支持答案、未充分利用检索上下文和难以理解复杂段落等失败模式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [60] [H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00065)
*Shusen Zhang,Junyi Hu,Ye Feng,Ziteng Wang,Zhaoyuan Pan,Guosheng Dong,Xiaojun Yuan,Jiangshou Hong,Xiangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: H+ Embedding predicts variable-length, context-dependent phrases as retrieval units to balance quality and cost—achieving substantial gains over global vectors and approaching token-level performance with fewer vectors.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To bridge the gap between single-vector retrievers and token-level late interaction by using context-dependent variable-length phrase partitions as retrieval units, preserving important multi-word and compositional concepts while reducing indexing and scoring costs.

Method: Predict variable-length phrase partitions, keep uncovered tokens as singletons, perform importance-guided unit selection, and use weighted MaxSim interaction for retrieval scoring.

Result: H+ Embedding, a multi-granularity retriever predicting phrase partitions, preserving singletons, and using importance-guided unit selection with weighted MaxSim; phrase branch outperforms global branch by 6.91 macro nDCG@10, nearly matches Token with 13.7% fewer vectors, and outperforms content-independent grouping under vector budgets.

Conclusion: Context-dependent phrase interaction offers an effective middle ground between global vector compression and token-level interaction, delivering better retrieval quality-cost trade-offs for terminology-intensive domains.

Abstract: Terminology-intensive retrieval, especially in medical settings, depends on preserving multi-word entities, abbreviations, numerical constraints, and compositional concepts. However, existing representations lie at two extremes: single-vector retrievers often over-compress local relevance signals, while token-level late interaction retains every tokenizer subword at substantial indexing, storage, and scoring cost. This mismatch raises a natural question: can context-dependent phrases provide a useful retrieval unit between global vectors and tokens? We introduce H+ Embedding, a unified multi-granularity retriever that predicts variable-length phrase partitions, preserves uncovered tokens as singletons, and applies importance-guided unit selection with weighted MaxSim interaction. Across 16 scientific, medical, and bilingual tasks, its phrase retrieval branch exceeds the global retrieval branch by 6.91 macro nDCG@10. It also nearly matches Token while using 13.7% fewer document vectors and outperforms content-independent grouping rules under moderate vector budgets. Context-dependent phrase interaction therefore provides an intermediate quality-cost point between global compression and token-level interaction for practical retrieval systems.

</details>


### [61] [Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00101)
*Banruo Liu,Haoran Qiu,Íñigo Goiri,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Large-scale Copilot trace analysis reveals that coding agents produce high intra-turn locality but low inter-turn cache reuse, long-tailed resource usage, and long idle gaps; a lightweight idle-time predictor can reclaim most idle time, implying agent-native serving optimizations are needed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To empirically characterize AI coding agent workloads at production scale and identify system-level properties and challenges (caching, latency, resource orchestration) that differ from chatbot-style workloads, informing design of agent-native serving infrastructure.

Method: Production-scale trace analysis: sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026 covering 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens. Statistical characterization of session structure, turn boundaries, KV cache hit rates, token/time distributions, tool execution coupling, and development of a lightweight idle-time predictor evaluated by captured idle-time fraction (86–90%).

Result: Key findings: agentic turns show high within-turn KV cache hit rates (~90%) but much lower across-turn (~55%); model switches and context compaction drastically invalidate caches; token usage, tool calls, and time spans are diverse and long-tailed; quick agentic turnaround vs long user idle gaps; an idle-time predictor recovers 86–90% of total idle time enabling proactive resource decisions. These results motivate per-turn caching, idle-aware scheduling, and tool-aware execution orchestration.

Conclusion: Agentic coding workloads are materially different from chatbot workloads: they consist of sparse user turns that expand into autonomous LLM+tool loops, producing distinct locality, caching, and idleness patterns that break common LLM-serving assumptions. The empirical evidence supports building agent-native infrastructure that treats intra-turn and inter-turn behavior differently and uses idle-time prediction for proactive orchestration.

Abstract: AI coding agents like GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, and Codex interleave multi-step LLM inference with tool execution, creating a workload different from chatbots. We present the first production-scale characterization of this workload using sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026, comprising 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens.
  Our analysis reveals distinctive workload properties with important systems implications. For example, agentic coding sessions consist of sparse user-initiated turns, each unfolding into an autonomous agent loop of LLM calls almost always coupled with tool execution. This structure yields KV cache hit rates averaging 90% within a turn, but falling to 55\% across turn boundaries and drastically invalidated after events like model switches or context compaction. Diverse workflows and user behaviors are observed with variable and long-tailed token consumption, time span, and tool calls. We highlight the difference between quick agentic turnaround times and the minutes-long user idle periods at turn boundaries, and design a lightweight idle-time predictor that captures 86-90\% of total idle time, enabling proactive decisions for efficient resource orchestration. These findings challenge assumptions underlying current LLM-serving systems and provide an empirical foundation for agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [62] [Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00102)
*Shimaa Ahmed,Yiwei Cai,Mohsen Minaei,Rahul Rachuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了可精确评估的动态定价基准（Bazaar）；不同LLM在‘获客’与‘盈利’上表现不同；需求冲击下学习/修正速度存在权衡；即便最强模型与事后最优相比仍有较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理化商业（agentic commerce）从概念走向部署，关键问题是驱动这些代理的LLM能否在真实市场条件下进行有效定价；需要一个可控且可精确评估的基准来衡量其能力和局限。

Method: 设计一个动态密封出价、多属性拍卖环境（Bazaar），用封闭形式的顾客效用函数产生数据并允许精确计算事后最优利润；在包含隐藏偏好、竞争实时适应与突发需求冲击的设置下，横向比较了11款LLM代理的获客率、利润、学习速度与冲击恢复能力。

Result: 作者构建了 Bazaar——一个动态密封出价、多属性拍卖基准，利用封闭形式的顾客效用进行精确评估，测试了11个最前沿LLM代理在真实市场特性（隐藏偏好、竞争适应、需求冲击）下的定价能力，发现赢家在用户获取与利润上并不一致、在需求冲击下排序会变化且最强代理也只达不到事后最优利润的三分之一。

Conclusion: 当前前沿LLM在代理式商业定价方面已有进展，但仍无法靠近后验最优利润，且表现依赖于评价指标（获客vs利润）和市场动态，表明需要更好的在线学习、置信校准与鲁棒性机制。

Abstract: Agentic commerce is moving from concept to deployed infrastructure: payment networks, retailers, and AI platforms are setting the stage for agents to transact on behalf of merchants and consumers. Yet whether the LLMs behind these agents can price competently in real markets, where customer preferences are hidden, competitors adapt in real time, and demand can shift without warning, has not been systematically tested. We introduce Bazaar, a dynamic sealed-bid benchmark for multi-attribute auction under these conditions. Despite its dynamics, the benchmark is grounded in closed-form customer utilities, enabling exact evaluation. Across 11 frontier LLMs from four providers, the leading agents on customer acquisition (e.g. Gemini 3.1 Pro) are often not the leading agents on profit (e.g. Opus 4.6). The ranking shifts again under demand shocks: agents that learned fastest pre-shock are typically the slowest to revise their beliefs afterwards, while Gemini 3.1 Pro recovers fastest despite not leading on profit. However, even the strongest agent captures less than a third of hindsight-optimal profit, suggesting current LLMs are progressing in agentic commerce but leave substantial headroom.

</details>


### [63] [Shared Organizational Memory for Enterprise Coding Agents: System Design and Deployment Snapshot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00122)
*Harsh Rao Dhanyamraju,Leonidas Raghav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 企业级共享记忆系统把经验捕获并内嵌到编码平台：在任务附近自动收集、经贡献者批准后整理为可重用的问答记忆，阻断明显的安全/隐私风险，并供后续编码代理检索。已在生产部署并给出生命周期与运行快照，效果仍在评估中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业知识大量存在于内部DSL、专有平台、局部约定、临时修复和隐性流程中，这些内容不在公开训练语料或正式文档里，导致智能代理/工程师频繁重新发现相同知识。目标是把经验捕获从事后记录解耦，变成编码工作的一部分，从而提高知识可复用性与开发效率。

Method: 平台级自动捕获“任务相邻”数据→贡献者审核批准→编辑/整理成问答记忆（含出处与元数据）→对明显的安全/隐私风险做门控（自动/人工）→将记忆索引供后续编码代理检索并使用。文中给出生命周期流程与生产快照。

Result: 已在生产环境部署并提供运行快照（但无完整量化结果）。检索和编码任务上的影响尚在评估中，论文目前以系统描述与运维数据为主。

Conclusion: 将捕获直接嵌入编码平台是解决企业“隐形知识”重发现问题的有希望做法，但当前工作缺少关键量化评估与实现细节（如隐私治理、检索精度、可扩展性、审计和贡献激励），需要补充实验设计与指标以判断实际对开发效率与质量的影响。

Abstract: Enterprise coding agents rely on tools and retrieval, yet enterprise knowledge often remains outside public training data and formal documentation: internal DSLs, proprietary platforms, local conventions, recent fixes, and tacit workflows. Existing knowledge interfaces expose stored resources but still depend on agents recognizing and explicitly recording lessons worth reusing, disconnecting capture from the coding workflow and leaving development experience repeatedly rediscovered. We report an ongoing production deployment of a shared organizational memory system that makes capture a platform-level part of coding work: it collects task-adjacent experience with contributor approval, curates it into reusable question-answer memories, gates obvious security and privacy risks, and retrieves memories for future agents. This short paper describes the deployed lifecycle and an operational snapshot. Effects on retrieval and coding tasks remain under evaluation.

</details>


### [64] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentStream，通过三类流场景规范化评估自我进化代理，实证发现方法效果受场景与模型能力共同制约，且不存在万能方法，建议在现实任务流下进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多以独立任务评估自我进化代理，缺乏在现实连续、多样化任务流情形下的行为理解，需要一个跨方法、跨模型、跨场景的统一评估框架。

Method: 提出AgentStream统一评估框架：将多种agent基准组织为可配置的任务流，并在测试时实例化三种流场景（Isolated、Sequential、Interleaved），对五种代表性自我进化方法和三种前沿基础模型进行组合评估，以解耦模型能力、方法架构与流场景对自我进化的影响。

Result: 实验表明：自我进化的可靠性随流场景而异；自我进化的收益受限于模型能力，且随模型强度呈非单调关系；没有一种方法在所有模型和场景下占优。基于这些发现，给出了针对不同模型与场景选择自我进化方法的具体建议。

Conclusion: 主张将自我进化代理的评估从孤立单任务转向现实任务流；并依据模型能力与流场景选择合适的方法，以提高自我进化的可靠性和实用性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream.
