Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [65] [TRACE-TS: Attribution-Grounded and Traceable Sensor-Language Reasoning for Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00200)
*Sparsh Rastogi,Tanmay Kumar,Baiyu Chen,Jatin Bedi,Zechen Li,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TRACE-TS：利用专家归因构建有证据来源的 DAG 推理轨迹，并训练带传感器记忆与门控交叉注意力的紧凑 LMs 在不依赖归因的情况下输出可追溯推理，显著提升可穿戴活动识别的准确性与解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将可穿戴传感器信号简化为活动标签，或者用大语言模型生成流畅但与信号不契合的解释，导致解释不可验证。需要一种将语言推理与具体信号证据绑定的方案，以提高可验证性与可信度。

Method: 先用一个专家分类器对传感器时间序列做归因以定位显著的时空传感器区域；基于这些显著区域构建带有显式证据来源的有向无环图（DAG）推理轨迹；训练一个紧凑的语言模型，通过对传感器记忆 token 的门控交叉注意力生成这些轨迹，并在推理时联合输出活动标签与轨迹。

Result: 在七个可穿戴基准上，TRACE-TS 达到平均最高的准确率与 F1（84.43%/81.24%），在 F1 上较最佳 LLM 基线提升 17.96%。同时提出了 SNM（Semantic Node Match）作为以 LLM 为裁判的度量，用以诊断推理在观察、推断与综合层面的保真性，能定位标准 NLG 指标难以发现的幻觉观察与证据链断裂问题。

Conclusion: TRACE-TS 提供了一种面向可穿戴时间序列的可追溯、有证据来源的结构化推理框架，能在推理时同时输出活动预测与对应的证据链（DAG），且无需在推理阶段依赖额外的归因计算或教师干预。

Abstract: Wearable sensors capture fine-grained motion patterns that support rich behavioral understanding, yet most existing methods reduce these signals to activity labels. Recent LM-based approaches generate natural-language explanations for sensor data, but their reasoning is weakly grounded in the underlying signal, leading to fluent yet unverifiable explanations. We introduce TRACE-TS (Traceable Reasoning with Attribution-Grounded Evidence), a framework for structured and signal-grounded reasoning over wearable time series. TRACE-TS uses attribution from an expert classifier to identify salient spatio-temporal sensor regions, uses them to construct DAG reasoning traces with explicit evidence provenance, and trains a compact language model to generate these traces through gated cross-attention over sensor memory tokens. At inference, the adapted model jointly outputs the activity prediction and its reasoning trace, without requiring attribution computation or teacher guidance. We introduce Semantic Node Match(SNM), an LLM-as-judge metric that diagnoses reasoning fidelity at the observation, inference, and synthesis levels, localizing hallucinated observations and broken evidence chains missed by standard NLG metrics. Across seven wearable benchmarks, TRACE-TS achieves the best average accuracy and F1 among all evaluated methods (84.43%/81.24%), and outperforms the best LLM-based baseline by 17.96% in F1. Our code is available at https://github.com/SparshRastogi/TRACE-TS.

</details>


### [66] [Personalizing Large Language Model Agents with Small Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00215)
*Dian Jin,Zhi Zhang,Huichao Li,Yihe Pan,Rundong Huang,Doudou Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FABLE is an external, factorized bandit layer that personalizes frozen LLM agents via Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling over residuals subject to feasible-action constraints, with regret guarantees and empirical gains on preference-sensitive tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fine-tuning LLMs per-user is costly or infeasible and prompting/memory mainly expose user data without altering agent execution style. Personalization must therefore be done on a frozen agent using online feedback that is scalar and only observed for executed actions, motivating a bandit formulation and an external, lightweight policy that can adapt execution decisions without changing the host model.

Method: Design a lightweight external policy layer that: (1) factorizes execution decisions into memory, information-acquisition, and response choices so updates target relevant components; (2) filters candidate actions through a user-provided feasible set before exploring; (3) models user-specific residual preferences as linear adjustments to a fixed default-and-cost score; (4) learns via Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling and a calibrated variant to inherit expected-regret bounds under the linear residual-reward model; and (5) provides mechanisms to characterize unidentifiable preferences and control false-promotions anytime. Evaluated on personalized reasoning, controlled-feedback, and tool-use tasks against rule-only baselines.

Result: Provides a lightweight external policy layer (FABLE) that personalizes a frozen LLM agent by online bandit-style learning of per-user execution policies from scalar feedback for executed actions. Factorizes decisions (memory, information acquisition, response style), constrains exploration via a user-specified feasible set, and learns user-specific residuals relative to default-and-cost scores using Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling. Under a linear residual-reward model, a calibrated variant attains an expected-regret bound against the best feasible action; also identifies which preferences are unidentifiable under persistent feasibility constraints and gives anytime-valid false-promotion control. Empirically, FABLE improves preference-sensitive behaviors across personalized-reasoning, controlled-feedback, and tool-use tasks while remaining competitive on end-to-end performance.

Conclusion: FABLE demonstrates a practical, theoretically-grounded way to personalize execution choices of black-box LLM agents from bandit feedback: it adapts memory, questioning, and response style with provable regret under linearity, handles feasibility constraints, and yields empirical improvements on preference-sensitive behaviors without modifying the host model.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can retrieve memory, call tools, ask clarifying questions, and vary response style, yet adapting these execution decisions to an individual user remains difficult. Fine-tuning a separate LLM is costly or impossible for proprietary systems, while prompts and memory primarily expose user information to the agent rather than adapt its execution decisions from feedback. We formulate personalization of a frozen agent as online learning of a per-user execution policy from scalar feedback observed only for the executed action. We propose FABLE (Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution), a lightweight policy layer outside a potentially black-box host agent. FABLE factorizes memory, information-acquisition, and response decisions so feedback updates related choices; filters actions through an externally specified feasible set before exploration; and learns user-specific residual preferences relative to a fixed default-and-cost score via Bayesian contextual Thompson sampling. Under a linear residual-reward model, a calibrated variant inherits an expected-regret bound against the best feasible action. We also characterize preferences unidentifiable under persistent feasibility constraints and provide anytime-valid false-promotion control. Across personalized-reasoning, controlled-feedback, and executable tool-use evaluations, FABLE improves several preference-sensitive behaviors relative to rule-only control while remaining competitive on end-to-end task performance.

</details>


### [67] [More Debate, Same Evidence: Structural Limits of Homogeneous Multi-Agent Groundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00243)
*Yuelyu Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将LLM组织成同质三人面板并非一致改进groundedness判断：效果随数据集而异，且当前比较无法隔离讨论机制的因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验一个常见假设：将大语言模型作为多智能体评审小组组织，通过相互交换批评能提高“有无证据支撑”（groundedness verification）判断质量。

Method: 在六个公开事实验证与幻觉检测基准上，比较同质三智能体（homogeneous three-agent）面板与固定单智能体参考系统的系统级准确率。通过让三名相同模型的代理交换批评/讨论来形成面板判断，计算面板与参考之间的准确率差异。注意两套系统使用不同模型变体，因此比较反映的是完整系统差异而非单一因果“讨论效应”。

Result: 面板相对于单智能体参考的系统级准确率差异在+8.5至-4.4个百分点之间：两个数据集显示可靠增益，一个显示可靠下降，三个统计上不确定。由于参考与面板使用不同模型变体，结果描述的是完整系统间的差异而非单纯讨论机制的因果效应。

Conclusion: 多智能体面板在某些基准上能带来实质改进，但并非普适。要证明讨论/交换批评的因果收益，需要更严格的对照实验（相同模型变体、消融试验、不同面板配置与统计功效分析），并开展关于沟通模式、角色分配与置信校准的细致误差分析。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) judges are increasingly organized as multi-agent panels under the assumption that exchanging critiques improves judgment quality. We test this assumption for \emph{groundedness verification}, where a judge must determine whether a claim is supported by the supplied evidence. We evaluate a homogeneous three-agent panel on six public fact-verification and hallucination-detection benchmarks. Relative to a fixed single-agent reference, the panel's system-level accuracy difference ranges from $+8.5$ to $-4.4$ percentage points: two datasets show reliable gains, one shows a reliable loss, and three are statistically inconclusive. Because the reference and panel use different model variants, these differences characterize the complete systems rather than isolate a causal debate effect.

</details>


### [68] [Geometric Self-Supervised Pre-training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00270)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过等距变换诱导的几何自监督预训练，模型学到有鲁棒性的空间表征，从而显著提升NCO在大规模TSP上的零样本泛化性能与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习驱动的NCO模型在尺度外（如从小规模训练到大规模推断）泛化性差；而视觉/语言领域通过自监督预训练显著改善泛化，但路由图仅含二维坐标、缺乏复杂拓扑特征，使得如何设计有效的自监督目标成为挑战。

Method: 提出了一种几何自监督预训练框架，通过对路由图施加等距变换（旋转、轴对称反射等）来学习空间不变性并建模全局相对距离分布，在策略优化（强化学习）前对编码器进行预训练，从而增强神经组合优化（NCO）模型的横向泛化能力。

Result: 在零样本外推到大规模TSP（TSP1,000）时，相较从零训练的基线方法，巡回路线长度平均降低约7.23%；在超大规模实例上，推理速度相比精确求解器Concorde最高快两个数量级。代码和预训练模型已公开。

Conclusion: 几何预训练是改善基于学习的组合优化在尺度外泛化的有效手段；后续可通过明确的自监督任务设计、消融实验及向更复杂问题（如带约束的VRP）推广来进一步验证和提升方法的普适性与效率。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) techniques have emerged as a highly efficient alternative to traditional exact algorithms for solving routing problems such as the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). However, the generalization capabilities of these Reinforcement Learning-based models are severely hindered when scaling to high-dimensional instances. This issue has been mitigated in other domains, like computer vision and natural language processing, by adopting a self-supervised pre-training strategy. Nevertheless, its application to routing graphs, which lack complex topological attributes beyond 2D spatial coordinates, remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose a geometric self-supervised pre-training framework specifically designed to capture spatial invariance and global relative distance distributions. By applying isometric transformations, such as rotations and axial reflections, the model learns robust structural representations prior to the policy optimization phase. Empirical results demonstrate that this strategy consistently outperforms models trained from scratch (baselines), achieving a 7.23\% improvement in tour length for massive zero-shot extrapolation scenarios (TSP1,000). Furthermore, the proposed model exhibits remarkable computational efficiency, delivering speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over the exact solver Concorde at massive scales.
  The source code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/davidaguadocosano/TSP-GeoPretrain.git.

</details>


### [69] [RF-HOI: Recognize Human-Object Interaction with Radio Frequency Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00289)
*Lihao Wang,Linlu Gao,Jiacan Yu,Yanyu Lin,Yifan Yin,Jianxin Wang,Tianmin Shu,Renjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次提出仅基于RF（mmWave+RFID）的HOI识别框架，用多模态融合+合成数据增强解决单模态信息不足与数据稀缺问题，效果接近视觉方法并优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉方法在隐私、弱光或被遮挡情况下受限；单一RF模态难以同时识别动作与目标；真实标注RF数据难以规模化，因此需要一个隐私友好、可扩展的RF-only HOI识别方案，并借助合成数据解决数据稀缺问题。

Method: 1) 提出RF-HOI框架，输入为mmWave雷达回波与RFID读取，设计融合模块同时完成动作分类与交互目标识别；2) 构建RF数据仿真器，合成多样化的多模态RF样本用于预训练/数据增强；3) 在少量真实数据上微调并与多种基线对比评估。

Result: RF-HOI提出了首个仅使用RF信号进行人-物交互识别的框架，通过融合mmWave雷达与RFID实现动作识别与目标识别的协同，并通过合成器生成大规模多模态RF训练数据以缓解真实数据稀缺问题。实验证明其优于基线方法，逼近视觉模型性能，且合成数据显著提升了在真实场景下的表现。

Conclusion: 多模态RF（mmWave+RFID）联合以及大规模合成RF数据是实现隐私友好且鲁棒的HOI识别的可行路径，能在弱光/隐私场景下接近视觉性能，但仍需评估泛化性、仿真与现实域差异和部署限制。

Abstract: Recognizing Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is essential for intelligent systems, underpinning applications in virtual and augmented reality, embodied AI, and assistive robotics. However, vision-based HOI methods face challenges in privacy concerns and poor light conditions. In this work, we introduce RF-HOI, the first framework that only uses radio frequency (RF) signals for HOI recognition. A key challenge of RF-HOI is that single-modality RF sensing is insufficient to recognize both actions and the objects being interacted with. RF-HOI addresses this through a novel modality fusion that combines mmWave radar and RFID, enabling simultaneous action recognition and target identification. Another challenge is limited training data across diverse setups, which impairs the generalizability of the recognition model. To overcome this, we develop a simulator that synthesizes multimodal RF data for diverse HOIs at scale, allowing us to fine-tune with only a small amount of real-world data. Experiment results show that RF-HOI outperforms all baselines, approaching vision model performance, and that our diverse synthetic training data can significantly boost our system's performance on real-world scenarios. These results highlight the potential of multimodal RF sensing for robust and privacy-preserving HOI recognition as well as the effectiveness of our RF data synthesis.

</details>


### [70] [WM-Cov: Test Adequacy for Interactive World-Model-Style Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00298)
*Jianxun Cui,Ping Wu,Stanisa Peric,Marko Milojkovic,Vladan Devedzic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WM-Cov，给出一套多指标的、提供者无关的世界模型式测试充分性评估，并通过多环境实验展示了原始生成故障与有效证据之间的差异，建议以有效证据收敛判断测试充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型把静态回放测试变成依赖被测规划器的交互式情形，导致需要明确定义何种“危险”轨迹算作有效证据以及何时可以停止测试。

Method: 提出交互式世界模型测试充分性定义，并实现一个提供者无关的评估层 WM-Cov，负责把原始提供者输出映射到请求/实现/有效证据三个层级并计算多项质量指标；在 TeraSim/SUMO 事件、混合轨迹池和 DriveArena TrafficManager–WorldDreamer 矩阵上做实证评估（比较两种规划器、两种预测视野、六种提示条件与 360 条ego-route请求）。

Result: 发现“看起来危险”的事件中包含真实 ADS 故障、重复、部分实现和伪影；DriveArena 矩阵中 360 次尝试得到 304 次完全实现证据、56 次部分实现，另一个 80 请求切片得到 74 完全实现和 6 部分实现；因此支持以有效交互证据收敛作为评估准则。

Conclusion: WM-Cov 将世界模型式模拟输出统一转换为“请求的、已实现的、有效的”证据并通过覆盖增长、有效故障发现、故障模式多样性、现实性、伪影抑制、重复计数和有效证据精确度等指标来报告测试充分性，主张以预算下有效交互证据收敛来评估，而非单纯计数原始生成故障或提示覆盖。

Abstract: World models and generative simulators are emerging as interactive testing infrastructure for autonomous driving because they can react to the ego planner and produce counterfactual, rare, and safety-critical rollouts. This changes a test scenario from a fixed replayed trajectory into an interactive scenario family whose realized evolution depends on the planner under test. The unresolved question is therefore not only whether dangerous rollouts can be generated, but what valid closed-loop evidence is enough to support a specified testing intent and stopping decision. This paper formulates interactive world-model-style testing adequacy and introduces WM-Cov, a provider-agnostic evaluation layer that converts raw provider outputs into requested, realized, and valid evidence. WM-Cov reports adequacy through coverage growth, valid-failure discovery, failure-mode diversity, realism, artifact suppression, duplicate accounting, and valid-evidence precision. Studies on executed TeraSim/SUMO events, WM-like mixed trace pools, and a real DriveArena TrafficManager--WorldDreamer matrix show that dangerous-looking events can include valid ADS failures, duplicates, partial realizations, and artifacts. The DriveArena matrix evaluates two planners, two horizons, six prompt conditions, and 360 ego-route requests; 304 attempts become fully realized evidence and 56 remain partial. A disjoint 80-request route-slice check yields 74 fully realized and 6 partial attempts. The results support evaluating world-model-style testing by convergence of valid interactive evidence under budget, rather than by raw generated failures or prompt coverage alone.

</details>


### [71] [CrystalMem: Elastic Memory for Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Knowledge Crystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00303)
*Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现了“记忆滞后”（memory hysteresis）：云配额波动会导致自演化LLM代理在被压缩/删除后无法完全恢复能力。作者证明仅保留或丢弃条目的策略存在残余缺陷下限。提出CrystalMem：一个弹性记忆sidecar，通过四个保真度状态和“晶化能量”调度，以优势加权影响并考虑依赖耦合来排序降级，并在受限的计算/字节预算下通过验证的再晶化恢复能力。实验证明在7个环境、17种方法、6个backbone和多租户部署下均优于基线；在50%字节预算下达到了与全配额最强基线相当的性能；等预算时平均领先+4.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云平台配额会随负载/成本波动收紧和恢复，但现有记忆管理通常只假定字节预算增大或单向压缩/删除，忽略配额回升后无法恢复的能力损失。需要一个在弹性配额下能有保障地保全与恢复代理能力的记忆系统。

Method: 构建弹性记忆sidecar（CrystalMem）：
- 将记忆条目划分为四个保真度状态（crystallized/high/medium/low等）并允许在状态间双向迁移；
- 使用‘晶化能量’调度来决定何时/如何降级条目；
- 通过优势加权影响（advantage-weighted influence）并结合依赖耦合（dependency coupling）对降级顺序排序，优先保留对后续能力影响最大的条目；
- 在恢复阶段实施受限的、经验证的再晶化（recrystallization），确保在给定计算和字节上限内最大化能力恢复；
- 在多租户与真实边缘-云部署下进行评估，并与若干基线及backbone比较。

Result: 在7个环境、17种方法、6个backbone和多租户边缘-云部署下，CrystalMem在所有设置中实现了最高的恢复能力。关键量化结果：从50%字节预算出发，CrystalMem在每个环境中匹配最强的全配额基线；在相同预算下平均领先基线+4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 记忆滞后是结构性问题：一旦使用删除或单向压缩，系统会留下不可恢复的能力缺口。CrystalMem通过分层保真、依赖感知的降级排序与可验证的再晶化，能在受限资源下显著恢复代理能力并消除或缩小滞后。

Abstract: Memory for self-evolving large language model (LLM) agents is often provisioned as if its byte budget only grows. Cloud platforms, however, adjust quotas with load and cost, and we show that capability does not follow the budget back up: after a squeeze-and-recover cycle, the agent settles below its pre-squeeze level, a gap we call memory hysteresis. The cause is structural. Deletion and one-way compression discard the material needed for later rebuilding, and we prove that any policy that only keeps or drops entries carries a residual-deficit floor. We propose CrystalMem (Crystallized Memory), an elastic memory sidecar that demotes entries across four fidelity states under a crystallization-energy schedule, orders demotions by advantage-weighted influence with dependency coupling, and recovers capability through verified recrystallization under explicit compute and byte caps. Across seven environments, seventeen methods, and six backbones, with multi-tenant serving and a physical edge-cloud deployment, CrystalMem achieves the highest restored capability in every setting and closes the loop left open by every baseline. From a 50% byte budget, CrystalMem matches the strongest budgeted baseline at full provision on every environment; at equal budgets, it leads by +4.6 pp on average.

</details>


### [72] [Trust and Its Betrayal under Three Representational Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00321)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文把信任建模为一种虚拟式（subjunctive）的认识态度：信任分为对能力的信任（若P为真，B会知道）和对品格的信任（若B知道P，会向A透露）。作者给出三种形式化表示——词典序假设（lexicographic assumption）、Lewis–Stalnaker 球面系统的序数接近（ordinal closeness）和条件概率系统中的强信念（strong belief）——并研究在将“理性”假设替换为“可信赖性”假设后，Brandenburger–Keisler 不可能性是否仍然成立。结论是：任意有限深度的共同信任可实现，但“完全的”共同信任固定点出现困难；三种表示在困难如何出现以及在遭遇背叛时的表现上有根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统博弈论与认识论多把信念与预测混为一谈，但信任在被辜负时（背叛）具有质的差别。要把这种差别形式化，需要一种能表达“在虚拟情形下对他人知识与披露行为的信念”的表述，并比较不同形式化框架下共同信任与不可能性命题的命运。

Method: 把信任写成虚拟条件信念：能力信任为“若P为真，B会知道”，品格信任为“若B知道P，B会向A披露”。对该状态分别构建三种形式化表述：1) 词典序的假设表示；2) 基于Lewis–Stalnaker 球面的序数接近表示；3) 条件概率系统中的强信念表示。对每种表示，检查Brandenburger–Keisler不可能性在用“可信赖性”代替“理性”时是否依然成立，并分析同一背叛情形下各模型的不同后果。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 任意有限深度的共同信任可以构造实现；2) 完成的共同信任固定点会遭遇与Brandenburger–Keisler风格类似的困难，但三种模型在困难的表现形式上不同；3) 对具体背叛的分析显示，词典序模型会使代理人的水平排序被直接推翻，序数接近模型使接近序随背叛者的蓄意性程度被“污染”，而强信念模型则通过改变操作性的条件化假设来吸收背叛，保留内部一致性。

Conclusion: 三种形式化模型都允许有限层次的共同信任，但在完成的共同信任固定点处出现困难；背叛会以不同方式破坏信任表征：词典序下破坏层次顺序，序数接近下按背叛者的蓄意程度污染接近次序，强信念模型下仅改变条件化假设而保持信念结构一致性。

Abstract: Trust is a propositional attitude of a distinctive kind: to trust is to rely on another under conditions where reliance could be disappointed, and the disappointment of trust---betrayal---differs qualitatively from the disappointment of a prediction. We treat trust as a \emph{subjunctive} epistemic state: $A$ trusts $B$'s competence when $A$ believes that \emph{were $P$ true, $B$ would know it}, and $B$'s integrity when $A$ believes that \emph{were $B$ to know $P$, he would disclose it to $A$}. We develop three representations of this state---as lexicographic \emph{assumption} as \emph{ordinal closeness} in a Lewis--Stalnaker sphere system , and as \emph{strong belief} in a conditional probability system and for each we ask whether the Brandenburger--Keisler impossibility on common belief survives when the assumption of rationality is replaced by an assumption of trustworthiness. The three representations agree that every \emph{finite} depth of common trust is realizable while the \emph{completed} common-trust fixed point is the locus of difficulty, but they differ sharply in \emph{how} the difficulty manifests, and---our organizing finding---in how each survives a concrete betrayal. W show that the same betrayal refutes an agent's \emph{level ordering} under the lexicographic representation, contaminates her \emph{closeness ordering} in proportion to the betrayer's deliberateness under the ordinal representation, and merely \emph{shifts her operative conditioning hypothesis} while leaving her belief structure coherent under the strong-belief representation.

</details>


### [73] [Learning to Coordinate Symbolic Tools: LLM Agents for Verified Sum-of-Squares Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00326)
*Bohan Chen,Shivam N. Patel,Richard Hoffmann,Sam Looi,Tony Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建并训练了一个结合监督微调、强化学习（GRPO）与可执行符号工具的代理，用于生成并验证加权和-平方（weighted SOS）分解，从而证明多项式非负性。在合成数据集上，SFT+GRPO+工具链在加权SOS任务上显著优于基线，取得约79%的经验证成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种可在问题求解过程中调用外部可执行符号计算工具的方案，以可靠生成可机器检验的证明（多项式非负性证明），并研究如何通过训练与验证反馈协同提升工具调用代理的表现。

Method: 构造1.35M合成样本覆盖八个辅助多项式任务与加权SOS分解；先对代数子任务与模拟符号轨迹进行监督微调（SFT），然后使用任务专属的符号奖励通过Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行强化微调。评估时允许模型以原生SymPy工具执行展开、合并、重排、因式分解等操作；最终结果通过精确展开与系数比较进行形式验算。

Result: 在同源合成测试集上，完整系统（SFT+GRPO+工具）在加权SOS任务上取得78.96%验证成功率；基础模型（相同工具）为44.73%；在九个多项式任务上的宏平均准确率为91.75%。

Conclusion: 结合领域特定的代数技能训练、可执行符号工具调用和以验证器反馈为目标的优化，可显著提升模型在可检验符号推理任务（如加权SOS分解）上的可靠性和成功率。

Abstract: Tool calling allows large language models (LLMs) to invoke external computation during problem solving, a useful capability in various fields including AI for mathematics. We study this setting through weighted sum-of-squares (SOS) decomposition, a machine-checkable route to proving polynomial nonnegativity and hence polynomial inequalities. A candidate decomposition can be checked exactly, but finding one requires choosing among non-unique regroupings and coordinating multiple symbolic transformations. We develop an agent that combines algebraic task training, symbolic tools, and verifier-grounded optimization for this task. Rather than training only on the composite SOS task, we construct 1.35 million synthetic examples covering eight supporting polynomial tasks together with weighted-SOS decomposition. We first apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to direct algebra problems and simulated symbolic traces, and then use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-specific symbolic rewards. The SFT corpus contains no native tool-calling messages; at evaluation, the agent uses native SymPy calls for expansion, collection, reordering, and factorization. Every final SOS answer is checked by exact expansion and coefficient comparison. On held-out, same-generator synthetic problems, the full SFT+GRPO+tools system is the strongest of four evaluated configurations, reaching 78.96% verified success on weighted SOS, compared with 44.73% for the base model with the same tools, and 91.75% macro accuracy across nine polynomial tasks. Within this controlled setting, our work provides a case study of combining domain-specific skill training, executable tools, and verifier feedback, and may inform the design of tool-calling agents in other domains with exactly checkable outputs.

</details>


### [74] [RMSWeb: Reflection, Failure-Mode Mining, and Salvage-DS for Web Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00335)
*Chengbo Liu,Lifang Zhou,Ruijie Yan,Pei Tan,Ao Sun,Haojun Huang,Guichun Hua,Sining Wei,Yining Chen,Yingying He,Yutao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RMSWeb，一套用于小型网页代理（Qwen3-VL-Instruct 8B/32B）的训练流程：反思驱动的重试提高成功轨迹收集效率，失败模式挖掘将离线RL聚焦于关键状态，Salvage-DS用带极性语义的动作奖励与有条件采样和动作锚定处理被拒绝组的学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练小型网页代理成本敏感，成功轨迹稀缺且多含冗余步骤；直接在动作级别施加群体相对RL易导致微弱或误导性的更新，且对不适合更新的群组没有后备信号，因此需要提高数据收集效率并为离线RL设计稳健的奖励与采样机制。

Method: 三部分方法：1) Reflection-conditioned retries：利用模型反思触发重试以提高成功轨迹比例并缩短轨迹长度；2) Failure-mode mining：离线收集和优先训练那些由SFT策略暴露出的关键失败状态以强化学习覆盖盲区；3) Salvage-DS：结合动作语义化的极化奖励（polarized reward）、‘contrast-and-competence’门控动态采样策略以及对被拒绝组使用动作-only锚定，保证相对RL更新在群组不适用时仍有退路学习信号。

Result: 在三项基准上，使用RMSWeb的数据与训练流程能显著提升最终任务成功率与样本效率：成功任务动作步数降低最多19.7%，在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web、WebTailBench上相对SFT提升2.4–7.0（8B）和1.2–7.7（32B）个百分点；8B模型在开放权重同级别比较中取得Online-Mind2Web最强结果，并在成本-准确率上有领先表现（受评测协议差异的 caveat）。

Conclusion: RMSWeb在多项网页代理基准（WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web、WebTailBench）上均优于仅SFT的基线，8B/32B模型分别提升2.4–7.0和1.2–7.7点，并在样本效率（成功任务的动作步数减少最高19.7%）和成本-准确率权衡上表现领先。

Abstract: Compact web agents can reduce deployment cost, but training them poses challenges in both data collection and post-SFT reinforcement learning (RL). Successful trajectories are expensive to collect and often contain inefficient detours. After supervised fine-tuning (SFT), full trajectory corpora are dominated by routine states; moreover, when group-relative RL is applied to web actions, inadequately designed action-level rewards can yield weak or misleading relative updates, while groups rejected as unsuitable for such updates receive no fallback learning signal. We present RMSWeb, a three-part recipe for Qwen3-VL-Instruct at 8B and 32B. Reflection-conditioned retries increase collection yield and shorten successful trajectories; failure-mode mining concentrates offline RL on critical states exposed by the SFT policy; and Salvage-DS combines an action-semantic polarized reward, contrast-and-competence-gated dynamic sampling, and an action-only anchor for rejected groups. Policies trained with reflection-collected data use up to 19.7% fewer action steps on solved tasks. On WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and WebTailBench, RMSWeb improves over SFT by 2.4-7.0 points at 8B and 1.2-7.7 points at 32B. Our 8B model also achieves the strongest reported Online-Mind2Web result among similarly sized open-weight models in our comparison and a leading reported accuracy-cost trade-off on WebVoyager and WebTailBench, with the caveat that external evaluation protocols differ.

</details>


### [75] [Bayesian and Motivated Reasoning in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00339)
*Eddie Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出AI代理在相同数值证据下会因情境表述不同而得出不同结论，原因在于代理的先验信念影响判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理承担更开放性、可影响重要决策的任务，理解它们如何受表述和先验影响对于可靠部署至关重要。

Method: 在医学、选举鉴证和地缘政治预测等高风险领域，保持证据不变但改变证据出现的情境描述，比较多种代理在12项域-代理对比中的结论与推理行为差异。

Result: 发现代理更倾向对与其先验一致的命题形成肯定结论；不同表述还改变搜索深度、分析规范选择和证据评估方式，表明决策记录可能缺失关键先验信息。

Conclusion: AI代理的结论高度受任务表述和隐含先验影响，存在把决策外包给AI时隐性偏见不可见且难以审计的风险。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform open-ended tasks in settings where their conclusions can guide consequential decisions. We provide evidence that AI agents draw different conclusions from identical numerical data when the substantive framing changes. We demonstrate this behavior in high-stakes domains in medicine, election forensics, and geopolitical forecasting by holding the evidence fixed while changing the scenario in which the evidence appears. Across twelve agent-domain comparisons, agents' conclusions are strongly influenced by their prior beliefs. They are more likely to reach an affirmative conclusion when it is framed around a proposition they already regard as likely, while the reverse holds when the framing conflicts with their prior. The framing also changes how some agents work: they search more extensively, choose different analytical specifications, and evaluate the same evidence differently. These results identify a particular risk of delegating decision-making to AI agents, as their decisions may depend on prior beliefs that are neither specified in the task nor visible in the decision record.

</details>


### [76] [Gene Ontology-Guided Hierarchical Spatial Gene Expression Prediction from Histopathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00405)
*Zhiwen Xu,Xiaoming Yan,Chengkun Wu,Juan Chen,Haoang Chi,Liyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将GO本体嵌入解码器并采用多尺度残差细化，可插拔替换现有解码器，从而稳定且可观地提高组织学图像到空间基因表达的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将目标基因解码为平铺无结构向量，忽略基因间通过通路与调控产生的依赖；在配对数据有限的情况下，缺少显式结构会限制模型学习这些生物依赖，影响预测质量。引入GO作为结构先验可显著缓解此问题。

Method: 提出MSGR；利用Gene Ontology（GO）四层树作为显式结构先验，设计GO引导的解码器，从粗粒度功能域逐步向细粒度单基因进行预测修正，采用残差校正和尺度加权监督。该解码器仅作用于基因端，可作为对现有架构的插拔式替换，无需修改图像侧模块。

Result: 在HEST-1k基准的九个数据集上广泛实验表明：带GO结构的解码器稳定优于平面解码，且超过一项先进的生成式基线；消融显示性能提升来自生物本体结构本身，而非仅层次分解，优于结构等价但随机构建的层次（优势为+0.027）。

Conclusion: 使用GO作为解码端的结构先验是一种有效改进策略，能在不改动图像编码器的前提下提升预测；但对本体质量、基因覆盖与组织/任务的泛化性仍需深入评估和消融分析。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from histopathology images enables large-scale transcriptomic profiling without the cost of direct measurement. Existing methods decode the target gene set as a flat, unstructured vector, ignoring the inter-gene dependencies arising from shared biological pathways and regulatory programs. Without explicit structural guidance, models must infer these dependencies entirely from limited paired data, constraining prediction quality. We propose MSGR (Multi-Scale Gene Refiner), which bridges this gap by incorporating the Gene Ontology (GO), a curated functional hierarchy of genes, as an explicit structural prior. MSGR organizes target genes into a four-level GO tree. Its GO-guided decoder then progressively refines predictions from coarse functional domains to fine individual genes via residual corrections under scale-weighted supervision. Operating solely on the gene side, the GO-guided decoder serves as a seamless plug-in replacement that consistently improves existing architectures without requiring any image-side modifications. Extensive experiments on nine datasets from the HEST-1k benchmark provide empirical evidence for two central claims: GO-structured decoding consistently outperforms flat decoding, even against a state-of-the-art generative baseline, and the gain is attributable to biological ontology structure rather than hierarchical decomposition per se, as confirmed by a +0.027 margin over a structurally equivalent random hierarchy.

</details>


### [77] [Where did the ambiguity go? Examining how multimodal models interpret polysemous words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00410)
*Jasin Cekinmez,Addison J. Wu,Raja Marjieh,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，基础模型存在跨模态的多义性表达差异：尽管模型“知道”多义性，但图像-生成相较文本生成更易坍缩到少数含义，且都不及人类的想象多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多义词的多重含义如何在不同模态的生成模型中显现，评估语言理解能否等价地转移到视觉生成，以及揭示基础模型在表达意义时的模态差异。

Method: 对17个文本到图像模型和15个文本生成模型，输入无上下文的多义词并多次采样生成输出；对输出进行词义归类（sense labeling）并计算归一化熵以量化多样性；与人类想象的词义分布进行比较；另外，询问模型列举各词义生成频率以对比“自我报告”与实际生成的差异。

Result: 发现图像生成模型在多义词表达上显著集中于更少的词义（归一化熵约0.10），文本生成模型中等多样性（约0.25），而人类想象的词义更为丰富（约0.47）。此外，模型对自身生成分布的预测通常比其实际输出更为多样。

Conclusion: 该论文发现大型模型在多模态生成上存在“多义落差”（multimodal gap）：给出无上下文的多义词时，文本生成模型产出的词义多样性明显高于图像生成模型，但两者都低于人类想象的多样性。尽管模型在自我报告时能预测更丰富的词义分布，它们在实际生成中未能体现这种多义性。

Abstract: Human language is highly polysemous. Many common words (e.g., 'bank' or 'palm') carry several distinct meanings that shape what humans communicate and imagine. Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to understand this multiplicity of meaning, but much less is known about how polysemy surfaces in other modalities such as images. We study this across 17 text-to-image and 15 text-generation models by giving each a polysemous word with no context to fix its meaning and measuring which senses are produced over many samples. We find a clear multimodal gap, where within every model family, generated images settle on far fewer senses than generated sentences (normalized entropy 0.10 vs. 0.25), and both are far less varied than what people imagine for the same words (normalized entropy 0.47). However, when we instead ask a model to list how often it would generate outputs corresponding to each possible meaning of a word, it predicts distributions that are more diverse than the actual space of outputs. These results reveal a multimodal gap in how foundation models express meaning, and how their understanding may not transfer faithfully nor equally across modalities.

</details>

*Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) can produce fluent spatial reasoning traces, their intermediate relations may fail to support the final conclusion, making token-level confidence insufficient for final-answer reliability estimation. Existing formal verifiers provide stronger semantic evidence, but their applicability is partial: a parsed claim need not yield a definite semantic verdict. To address this issue, we introduce SymboUQ, a symbolic uncertainty quantification framework that estimates final-answer reliability from reasoning traces by distinguishing symbolizability, whether a claim can be represented in the verifier's formal language, from semantic determinacy, whether its execution yields an entailed or contradicted verdict rather than an unknown or not-evaluable outcome. SymboUQ comprises (i) a Layout Auditor that executes ordered spatial claims and extracts feasibility, conflict, and repair evidence; (ii) a label-free Determinacy Profile that characterizes effective executable coverage; and (iii) a Determinacy-Aware Reliability Composer that integrates constraint-based, representation-based, and decoding-based scores according to verifier applicability. Extensive experiments on five spatial reasoning benchmarks with four frozen LLM backbones show that SymboUQ achieves approximately an 8% relative improvement in AUROC and a 7% relative reduction in class-balanced Brier loss over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [79] [Mask-Based Priors Are More Persistent than Query-Key Initializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00418)
*Mingze Ma,Hemanth Saratchandran,Cameron Gordon,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Use task-informed additive attention masks (learnable logit biases) to encode persistent inductive biases in Transformers, fixing extrapolation failures on Boolean tasks that QK-based initializations cannot overcome.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformers exhibit a default inductive bias that leads to systematically incorrect extrapolation on certain controlled Boolean tasks; existing structural initializations (via QK projections) are overwritten during training and do not change extrapolation. Need a method to inject persistent structural priors into attention to correct this failure mode.

Method: Introduce a learnable additive attention-logit bias (mask) initialized from task-level interaction structure; compare to existing QK-projection based structured initializations and vanilla Transformers across Boolean extrapolation, low-data arithmetic, and standard vision/language tasks; evaluate extrapolation behavior and training dynamics.

Result: Mask-based initialization preserves the injected structural prior during optimization, yielding near-perfect extrapolation on Boolean reasoning tasks and improved performance in low-data arithmetic, while remaining competitive on vision and language tasks. QK-based priors are vulnerable to being overwritten and thus ineffective for changing extrapolation rule.

Conclusion: Initializing additive attention masks with task-structure priors enables Transformers to maintain persistent, correct extrapolation on Boolean reasoning tasks where vanilla and QK-initialized models fail; mask-based priors also improve low-data arithmetic and remain competitive on vision/language benchmarks.

Abstract: Transformers do not merely lack data on some Boolean extrapolation tasks; they generalize in a systematically wrong way. Recent work on generalization on the unseen has shown that, despite fitting the observed domain, Transformers often extrapolate according to a simpler minimum-degree interpolator rather than the true target function. These Boolean tasks are not practical applications, but controlled stress tests for understanding Transformer inductive bias. We ask whether this failure mode can be corrected by injecting explicit structural priors into attention. Existing structured-initialization methods alter Transformer inductive bias indirectly, by choosing query and key projections whose similarity scores approximate a desired attention pattern. However, we find that when applied to Boolean extrapolation, these QK-based priors can be rapidly overwritten during training and fail to change the learned extrapolation rule. We propose a simpler alternative: initialize the additive attention mask directly. Unlike standard hard masks used for causality or locality attention, our mask is a finite, learnable attention-logit bias initialized from task-level interaction structure. This separates the structural prior from content-dependent attention scores, allowing it to persist throughout optimization. On Boolean reasoning tasks, mask-based initialization achieves near-perfect extrapolation where vanilla and QK-initialized Transformers remain trapped by the default inductive bias. The same mechanism also improves low-data arithmetic performance and remains competitive on vision and language benchmarks. These results show that attention masks can serve not only as architectural constraints, but as a simple substrate for encoding persistent inductive bias in Transformers.

</details>


### [80] [TrAC: Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency for Efficient Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00422)
*Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrAC通过在单条完成推理痕迹上进行条件重引出并结合生成期间的标记不确定性轨迹，实现更高效且更准确的回答正确性估计，能在保持低生成成本的同时改善置信判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法要么被动利用标记级置信信号，要么以高代价采样多条完整推理痕迹，要么仅探测前缀，但都未在已完成的推理痕迹上主动重新引出答案以衡量其对原答案的支持与一致性；解决这一空白有助于更可靠的弃答/人工审查和自适应计算分配。

Method: 提出Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency（TrAC）框架：主动分量Prefix-Conditioned Elicitation（PCE）即基于已完成的完整推理痕迹再条件化生成一个简短答案，度量与原答案的一致性及其逐标记概率支撑；被动分量Trace Uncertainty Profile（TUP）在不额外解码的情况下总结原始生成中每一标记的不确定性演化；最后用一个轻量级头将两者合成得到响应正确性分数。评估在五个数学推理基准和三个LLM家族上进行，并与8样本自一致性等方法比较。

Result: 在只使用一条完整推理痕迹加一个缓存的短答案探针时，TrAC比八样本自一致性在宏观AUROC上提高约1.8%、在AURC上降低约3.4%；当已有八条样本时，将样本共识与重引出结果结合可进一步提高宏观AUROC约4.3%并将AURC降低约8.3%，且无需额外完整轨迹生成。

Conclusion: TrAC通过在已完成的推理痕迹上进行条件重采样（PCE）并结合原始生成过程中的逐标记不确定性剖面（TUP），在有限生成成本下显著提升了对回答正确性的置信估计，从而在数学推理基准上优于仅靠多样本一致性的方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent reasoning traces that nevertheless lead to incorrect answers, making response-level uncertainty estimation important for abstention, human review, and adaptive compute allocation. Existing approaches generally fall into three categories: passive single-trace methods use token-level confidence signals, sampling-based methods compare multiple complete traces at higher generation cost, and active prefix-based methods probe partial traces to study answer stabilization or preference transitions. However, none actively re-elicits an answer from a completed reasoning trace to measure its consistency with and support for the original answer. To address this gap, we introduce Trace-Conditioned Answer Consistency (TrAC), a correctness-supervised uncertainty quantification framework that combines active and passive signals anchored to one completed reasoning trace. Its active component, Prefix-Conditioned Elicitation (PCE), re-elicits a short answer conditioned on the completed trace and represents both its consistency with the original answer and its token-level probabilistic support. Its passive component, Trace Uncertainty Profile (TUP), summarizes how token-level uncertainty evolves throughout the original generation without additional decoding. A lightweight head then integrates the two representations into a response-correctness score. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three LLM families, TrAC improves macro AUROC by 1.8% and reduces AURC by 3.4% relative to eight-sample self-consistency, while using one complete reasoning trace and a short cached answer probe. When eight samples are already available, augmenting sample consensus with re-elicitation further improves macro AUROC by 4.3% and reduces AURC by 8.3%, without additional full-trace generation.

</details>


### [81] [Diagnose Before You Compress: Prediction-Independent Bottleneck Witness Refinement for LLM Serving Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00423)
*Liming Liu,Chao Hu,Mingfei Lu,Cong Tan,Yiwei Ge,Chijin Zhou,Yongjun Xie,Runzhe Wang,Xiaohai Shi,Heyuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BPW 是一种为 LLM 服务生成诊断重放套件的三阶段方法：候选提名（response-blind + 闭源测量）、覆盖优先的超边序列构建、以及基于目标系统直接测量的瓶颈真相验证，以保证每个组件都保留可验证证据，从而以更小的工作集提升瓶颈诊断可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全量 trace 重放代价高，且传统保留代表性请求的方法可能错过稀有但关键的瓶颈证据；为保证诊断可靠性，需要以保存每个瓶颈组件证据为目标来构建紧凑重放套件，而不是仅靠总体代表性。

Method: 三阶段流程：1) Workload Candidate Nomination：用 response-blind 特征与闭源侧测量挑选可能揭露各类瓶颈的请求；2) Coverage-Priority Sequence Construction：把多组件候选组织为超边并基于覆盖优先策略选取序列以优先弱且未覆盖的维度；3) Bottleneck Truth Verification：仅依赖目标系统直接测量对候选进行无预测的标签化，确定满足“每个组件两个独立证据”要求的最短前缀。

Result: BPW 提出了一种三阶段质量约束框架，用于从海量 LLM 服务请求中构造紧凑且诊断可靠的重放套件。第一阶段使用 response-blind 的工作负载特征与闭源侧测量进行候选提名，以识别可能触发调度、预填充(prefill)、解码(decode)或 KV-cache 瓶颈的请求；第二阶段将多组件提案组织为可重用的超边并优先覆盖弱维度以构建序列；第三阶段通过对目标系统的直接测量进行瓶颈真相验证，确定满足“每个组件两个证据”要求的最短前缀。实验在 BurstGPT、ServeGen 和 Mooncake 上显示 BPW 在验证门（verified gate）条件下用更小的工作集达到更好诊断性能，相较 16 个策略在 Mean prefix Macro-F1 和 WBRC-AUC 上分别改善 2.3% 和 16.3%，并进行了分阶段与敏感性分析。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 方法具有明确的诊断导向新颖性并在多平台上展现性能提升，但论证中需补充关于验证成本、闭源信号可用性、对更多组件/场景的泛化性、以及统计显著性等细节，才能更有说服力。

Abstract: Production LLM serving generates millions of diverse requests, making full-trace replay across serving configurations increasingly expensive. Existing trace reduction methods mainly preserve workload distributions or representative requests, but bottleneck-revealing workloads may be rare and non-representative. Moreover, evidence for one component cannot compensate for missing evidence in another, while using predicted bottlenecks as target truth creates circular evaluation. These limitations make it necessary to preserve evidence for every bottleneck component rather than rely on workload representativeness alone. We propose Bottleneck-Preserving Witnessing (BPW), a quality-constrained framework for compact and diagnostically reliable LLM serving replay suites. BPW first performs Workload Candidate Nomination using response-blind workload features and closed source-side measurements. This stage identifies workloads that may expose scheduler, prefill, decode, or KV-cache bottlenecks. Coverage-Priority Sequence Construction then organizes multi-component proposals as reusable hyperedges and prioritizes weak and uncovered dimensions. Finally, Bottleneck Truth Verification derives prediction-independent labels solely from direct target-system measurements. The verified results determine the earliest prefix satisfying the direct two-witness requirement for every component. Experiments on BurstGPT, ServeGen, and Mooncake show that BPW reaches the verified gate with a compact workload set and outperforms 16 policies, achieving relative improvements of 2.3% and 16.3% in Mean prefix Macro-F1 and WBRC-AUC, respectively. Stage-resolved and sensitivity analyses confirm the distinct contributions and local stability of its three stages. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/llmllmllm/BPW

</details>


### [82] [Ekova: A Personality-Support Agent for Self-Discovery Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00478)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将“帮助用户更清晰认识自我”的人格支持（PS）作为情感支持（ES）的互补范式，构建了DSD数据集、OrthoTune训练框架与多角色系统DeepSupport，并以Ekova实现持久化跨会话人格支持，实验显示显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持侧重即时缓解情绪，作者主张补充一种关注认知清晰与自我言说的服务：人格支持，明确避开临床诊断与症状缓解目标。

Method: 三层设计：1）数据层——DSD长期交互收集的8,590样本，覆盖五种最小角色单元；2）模型层——DeepSupport，使用OrthoTune框架，包含风格专用adapter与风格一致性正则化；3）系统层——Ekova，将五种角色统一为一个持久化代理，加入跨会话记忆、适应性路由与用户自选人格。

Result: Proposes Personality Support (PS) paradigm; dataset DSD of 8,590 Chinese samples across five persona units (Coach, Warm, Tsukkomi, Real, Gonzo); DeepSupport multi-persona system trained with OrthoTune (style-specific adapters + style-consistency regularizer); Ekova unified agent with cross-session memory and persona selection; reports OrthoTune models outperform baselines by avg 16.3% across metrics; code released.

Conclusion: 论文提出的PS范式有明确定位、实现路线与初步证据，贡献包括公开数据集、专用训练方法与系统工程实现。需要补充数据采集/标注细节、评估协议、消融与伦理安全讨论以增强说服力与可复现性。

Abstract: Emotional Support (ES) systems have long optimized a single objective: alleviating the user's emotional distress in the moment. We argue that a complementary need, helping users see themselves more clearly, defines a distinct paradigm we call Personality Support (PS). PS is not counseling or clinical intervention: it targets cognitive clarity and self-articulation, not symptom relief or diagnosis. We instantiate this paradigm in three layers. First, we present DSD, a Chinese self-discovery PS Dataset of 8,590 samples collected through real longitudinal interaction across five minimal units, Coach, Warm, Tsukkomi, Real, and Gonzo. Second, we build DeepSupport, a multi-persona PS system trained with OrthoTune, a PS-tailored framework with style-specific adapters and a style-consistency regularizer. Third, we unify the five DeepSupport personas into Ekova, a persistent personality-support agent with a unified cross-session memory layer, supporting both adaptive routing and user-customized persona selection. Experiments show that OrthoTune-trained models outperform all baselines with an average relative gain of 16.3% across all metrics over the strongest prompt-based baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Yukyin/Ekova.

</details>


### [83] [F-WANDA: Fisher-Reweighted Post-Training Pruning for Sustainable Deployment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00481)
*Himanshu Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: F-WANDA：在一次额外的反向传播中收集经验费希尔，将保留预算按信号重分配，实现了在质量与剪枝成本上的显著改进，无需额外数据或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一次性后训练剪枝方法在质量与计算/能耗间存在取舍（WANDA牺牲质量，SPARSEGPT牺牲计算成本）。目标是找到既能保持或提升下游任务质量，又显著降低剪枝墙钟时间与能耗的方案。

Method: 对 WANDA 的简洁修改：在同一校准语料上额外进行一次反向传播以收集预激活的经验费希尔信息（不更新权重），然后按该费希尔信号将每行的保留（keep）预算在输出神经元间重新分配，仍为一次性（one-shot）后训练无结构剪枝。

Result: 在 LLAMA-2-7B、50% 无结构稀疏度下：WikiText-2 困惑度 6.85，流畅性与 WANDA 持平；5-shot MMLU 比 WANDA 提升 +1.6 个百分点，比 SPARSEGPT 提升 +1.1 个百分点；剪枝墙钟时间与能耗约为 SPARSEGPT 的三分之一。

Conclusion: F-WANDA 在无需额外校准数据与微调的前提下，通过将每行保留预算按预激活的经验费希尔信息重分配，实现了在质量与一次性后训练剪枝计算/能耗之间的更优折中，位于权衡前沿。

Abstract: One-shot post-training pruning is the most energy-frugal compression strategy for largelanguage models (LLMs), yet existing approaches trade either quality (WANDA) or compute cost (SPARSEGPT). We introduce F-WANDA, a drop-in modification of WANDA that reallocates the per-row keep budget across output neurons in proportion to the empirical Fisher information of the pre-activation. The Fisher signal is collected in a single additional backward pass over the same calibration corpus WANDA already uses; no weights are updated. On LLAMA-2-7B at 50 % unstructured sparsity, F-WANDA attains WikiText-2 perplexity of 6.85, matches WANDA fluency, and improves 5-shot MMLU by +1.6 pp over WANDA and +1.1 pp over SPARSEGPT, while incurring only one-third of SPARSEGPT pruning wall-clock and energy. The headline trade-off is achieved without extra calibration data or fine-tuning, placing F-WANDA on the Pareto frontier of quality versus pruning cost for sustainable LLM compression.

</details>


### [84] [The Bayesian Reflex: A Predictive Coding Engine for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00492)
*Sourabh Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“贝叶斯反射”（Bayesian reflex）框架，把预测编码以可扩展的算法形式实现，核心为层次生成模型的信念维护、通过预测误差最小化的序贯贝叶斯更新、以及基于不确定性的主动推断。结合椭球分解、递归高斯过程和导数感知贝叶斯优化等最新算法，实现在持续学习、感知与决策中的可扩展、数学上有保证的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测编码理论缺乏可扩展的工程实现，难以适配高维、连续在线学习与决策任务。作者旨在填补理论与可行算法之间的鸿沟，从而把生物启发的感知-行动机制移植到人工智能系统中。

Method: 三大支柱：1) 层次生成模型用于信念维护；2) 通过最小化预测误差实现序贯贝叶斯更新（在线推断）；3) 基于不确定性的主动推断驱动采集行动。关键算法配套为：椭球分解用于精确独立同分布采样，递归高斯过程支持深层次的层级贝叶斯推断，导数感知贝叶斯优化用于不确定性引导的决策/采样策略。

Result: 构建了数学上有据、模块化且可扩展的框架，展示了在多领域任务（示例包括气候模型评估与质数发现）中的适用性，表明该方法能同时处理持续学习、感知和基于不确定性的决策问题。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯反射将神经理论（预测编码/主动推断）与现代贝叶斯算法工程结合，提供一套可扩展、可证明的路径来实现类脑的连续学习与决策。所述算法模块使得该理论在工程任务（如气候模型评估、质数发现）中可操作并具说明性。

Abstract: Predictive coding offers a powerful theory of cortical computation, but corresponding scalable algorithmic implementations for artificial intelligence have remained elusive. This paper introduces the Bayesian reflex, a computational framework that directly instantiates predictive coding through three pillars: belief maintenance via hierarchical generative models, sequential Bayesian updating via prediction-error minimization, and uncertainty-driven action via active inference. We show that recent breakthroughs---ellipsoidal decomposition for exact $i.i.d.$ sampling, recursive Gaussian processes for deep hierarchical inference, and derivative-aware Bayesian optimization---provide the missing algorithmic ingredients. The resulting framework enables mathematically principled, scalable, and brain-inspired continual learning, perception, and decision-making. We illustrate its versatility through applications ranging from climate model evaluation to prime number discovery, offering a blueprint for truly adaptive artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [85] [TaPR: Test-Aware Policy Refinement for Feedback-Conditioned Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00494)
*Aofan Liu,Jingxiang Meng,Fangxin Liu,Yongbiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TaPR：把执行反馈解构为每轮稠密测试通过率奖励，提升多轮代码修复的序列性能（Pass@3 +2.44 pp），并引入轮次感知评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的强化学习范式多以单次最终结果为奖励，混淆了首发生成能力与基于反馈的迭代修复能力，丢弃了中间轮次的细粒度执行信号，难以衡量策略是否真正学会自我修复；因此需要将执行反馈解构为每轮稠密信号并采用轮次感知的评估协议以准确度量与优化多轮修复能力。

Method: 在多轮代码生成场景下，TaPR将执行结果转换为每轮的测试通过率（test-pass-ratio）作为即时奖励，采用一致的多轮交互协议来计算和回传该稠密奖励；与结果型（outcome-only）强化学习基线（如GRPO）对比，评估指标聚焦于合并三轮成功率（Pass@3）与子集（Hard, 7B/8B）表现，并在多模型、多题集上进行配对试验和步次轨迹分析。

Result: TaPR将执行反馈转换为稠密的“每轮测试通过率”奖励，并在一致的多轮交互协议下训练与评估策略；在219个LiveCodeBench题目和六个模型上，TaPR使三轮合并成功率（Pass@3）提升了2.44个百分点；在7B/8B高头号子集上提升3.31个百分点（从30.25%到33.56%），并在配对试验中出现42次改进与13次回退。与仅使用结果奖励的GRPO相比，TaPR提供了前十步均为非零的稠密反馈，能在给定预算内达到更高的Hard子集峰值；总体贡献是提出一个奖励分解框架和面向轮次的评估协议，把首发生成质量与多轮自我修复能力解耦。

Conclusion: TaPR通过稠密的每轮奖励与统一交互协议，有助于强化模型的多轮修复能力，从而在多个模型和数据切片上带来稳健但幅度适中的改进；论文主要贡献在方法学（奖励分解与评估协议），实验显示早期反馈密度和修复峰值受益，但仍需更广泛基线、显著性检验和消融来巩固结论。

Abstract: Multi-turn code agents rely on execution feedback to repair incorrect programs, yet standard reinforcement learning paradigms optimize and evaluate policy performance primarily using single-shot outcome rewards. This misalignment conflates initial code generation with feedback-driven refinement, discards granular execution signals across intermediate turns, and fails to evaluate whether the policy actually acquires self-repair capabilities. We propose Test-aware Policy Refinement (TaPR), a framework that transforms execution feedback into a dense per-turn test-pass-ratio reward under a consistent multi-turn interaction protocol. Across six models on 219 code-generation problems from LiveCodeBench, TaPR improves the pooled three-turn success rate (Pass@3) by 2.44 percentage points. In the predefined 7B/8B high-headroom slice, pooled accuracy increases from 30.25% to 33.56% (+3.31 pp), with 42 improvements and 13 regressions in paired trials. On a matched Qwen3-8B ablation, the dense reward supplies nonzero feedback in all of the first ten steps and reaches a higher Hard-subset peak than outcome-only GRPO within the tested budget, although GRPO nearly matches pooled Pass@3 by step 300. Our primary contribution is a reward-decomposition framework and a turn-aware evaluation protocol that decouple first-shot generation quality from multi-turn repair competence.

</details>


### [86] [BayesSeg: A Bayesian Optimization Framework for State Segmentation of Electricity Consumption Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00513)
*Zhenya Zhang,Wendi Zhu,Ping Wang,Hongmei Cheng,Shuguang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BayesSeg通过双稳态分段、事件F1+NMI复合评价与TPE贝叶斯优化，自动化并加速了NILM时序分段配置，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NILM中自适应分段对电器识别至关重要，但现有方法在参数经验化、边界敏感性和评价指标判别力方面存在不足，需一个可自动化配置且评价稳健的框架。

Method: 分段层：基于序列尾值与前序子序列均值的双稳态判据，结合顺序抽取与补集解析策略，实现无监督的稳态/过渡态精确分割；评价层：将分段结果映射为二值开关态序列，构造复合指标——事件级F1（基于容差匹配）与归一化互信息（NMI）的加权组合，兼顾事件精确性与全局结构一致性；优化层：以复合得分为目标，使用贝叶斯优化（TPE代理模型）高效全局搜索参数空间。

Result: 在SustDataED2数据集上，贝叶斯优化约100次目标评估即可达到与穷举网格搜索最优解0.35%以内的参数区域；框架取得加权复合得分0.7149、event_F1=0.9340，将优化延迟从约5300秒降至<1秒，速度提升>5700倍。

Conclusion: BayesSeg提出了一个统一的三层框架，自动完成时序分段、评价与参数优化，显著改善了NILM中分段配置的可用性与效率。

Abstract: In Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring (NILM), adaptive segmentation of electricity consumption time series is critical for appliance recognition. However, prevailing methods face challenges including heuristic parameter tuning, boundary sensitivity, and metric saturation. This paper proposes BayesSeg, a unified framework integrating time-series segmentation, multidimensional evaluation, and automatic parameter optimization. The segmentation layer employs a dual steady-state criterion based on the tail value and mean of preceding subsequences, combined with a sequential extraction and complement-set parsing strategy, to achieve precise unsupervised partitioning of steady-state and transition-state segments. The evaluation layer maps segmentation results to binary state sequences and formulates a composite metric integrating an event-level F1 score (event_F1) with Normalized Mutual Information (NMI). The event_F1 quantifies switching-event precision and recall via tolerance matching, while NMI captures global structural consistency, jointly overcoming the boundary sensitivity and limited discriminability of point-wise metrics. In the optimization layer, the composite score serves as the objective function for Bayesian optimization, which constructs a TPE surrogate model for efficient global parameter-space exploration. Experiments on the SustDataED2 dataset demonstrate that Bayesian optimization requires only ~100 objective evaluations to locate a parameter region within 0.35% deviation of the exhaustive grid-search optimum. The framework achieves a weighted composite score of 0.7149 and an event_F1 of 0.9340 while reducing optimization latency from ~5300 seconds to under 1 second, a speedup exceeding 5700x. BayesSeg automates segmentation configuration and provides a scalable, efficient solution for time-series analysis in NILM and related domains.

</details>


### [87] [CURE: Local Uncertainty Repair for Block-Parallel Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00531)
*Aofan Liu,Jingxiang Meng,Fangxin Liu,Yongbiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CURE：一种基于预算的动态修复树，用于在并行草稿生成中针对高不确定性令牌进行局部修复，从而提升验收长度和整体速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行草稿后端在长距离生成时准确率迅速下降，导致高拒绝率与较差的实际加速表现。作者观察到错误并非均匀分布，而集中在少数高不确定性令牌，因而通过局部修复这些“脆弱点”可显著降低后续轨迹崩溃与拒绝率，从而提高验收长度与总体吞吐。

Method: 在块并行草稿输出上，使用预测置信差（confidence margins）动态定位潜在错误令牌（不确定性焦点）；仅在这些脆弱结点上展开有界的修复路径形成“修复树”，在验证阶段采用修复重同步（repair resynchronization）机制将修复后的状态与草稿重新对齐；通过预算约束限制树的扩张以控制验证开销；将该修复模块作为可插拔组件与现有并行草稿后端结合。

Result: 在代码生成（HumanEval、MBPP、LiveCodeBench-lite）和数学推理（GSM8K）基准上，CURE比无修复并行基线将平均被接受长度提高4.2–7.5%，并实现2.66–3.49×的端到端加速（相对于仅目标解码）；论文还给出了草稿计算量与验证效率的权衡分析，以及作为即插即用修复模块的实现。

Conclusion: CURE能在不显著增加树验证开销的情况下，有针对性地修复草稿中导致下游轨迹崩溃的高不确定性令牌，从而将平均被接受长度提高4.2–7.5%，并将端到端速度提升至2.66–3.49×（相较于仅目标解码）。同时CURE可作为即插即用模块兼容标准并行草稿框架，并揭示了草稿计算与验证效率间的折中关系。

Abstract: Speculative decoding mitigates the latency of sequential generation in autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) by interleaving draft generation with target verification. However, existing parallel drafting backends often suffer from rapid accuracy degradation over long horizons, leading to high rejection rates during verification and suboptimal wall-clock speedups. We observe that drafting errors are not uniformly distributed but typically stem from localized high-uncertainty tokens that destabilize downstream generation trajectories. Motivated by this token error pattern, we propose CURE, a budget-aware dynamic repair tree designed to repair errors at uncertainty focal points without incurring prohibitive tree-verification overheads. Specifically, our method uses predictive confidence margins to dynamically locate candidate error tokens within a block-parallel draft, expands bounded repair paths only at these fragile nodes, and employs a novel repair resynchronization mechanism to realign draft states post-verification. Evaluations on code-generation benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, and LiveCodeBench-lite) and mathematical reasoning benchmark (GSM8K) demonstrate that CURE increases the average accepted length by 4.2-7.5% over parallel baselines without repair, translating to an end-to-end speedup of $2.66-3.49\times$ over target-only decoding. Furthermore, we provide a plug-and-play repair module compatible with standard parallel drafting frameworks. We also characterize the trade-off between draft compute and verification efficiency.

</details>


### [88] [Through the LENS: Local Geometric Decomposition of Vision-Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00561)
*Shalom Kachko,Raz Lapid,Margarita Vald,Almog Dubin,Moshe Sipper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LENS方法：用混合因子分析（MFA）把VLM激活分解为局部低秩的高斯邻域，从而发现跨模态融合的层次轨迹，并用插值干预验证这些邻域的可解释性与因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释方法多关注全局线性方向，可能漏掉全局高维但局部低维的表征结构。需要一种能捕捉局部流形/子空间并用于可解释性与因果干预的工具，以理解图像与文本如何在残差流中交互融合。

Method: LENS：在Transformer残差流上用Mixture of Factor Analyzers拟合局部低秩高斯组件，得到每个激活的分解与系数；自动化多模态标注管道为组件分配语义标签；通过沿组件质心插值激活进行因果干预并与差值均值（difference-in-means）和VL-SAE比较；在LLaVA-1.5-7B与Qwen3-VL-8B上评估，包括人类判定与检索任务。

Result: 发现两类典型融合轨迹（LLaVA：后层逐渐混合；Qwen3-VL：早期混合→部分再分离→接近输出重组）；MFA插值在多数条件下优于baseline，在一项LLaVA视觉到视觉任务上达VL-SAE的5.7倍；人类评估显示MFA引导与提示法竞争而强于其他干预基线；在最深层，MFA系数空间将Qwen3-VL的图像到渲染文本检索R@1从14.9%提升到48.6%；消融表明轨迹对组件数、本地秩与模态纯度阈值稳健。

Conclusion: 局部几何邻域是分析VLM跨模态表示的有用可解释与因果单位；不同模型展示出不同的融合时间线，且基于MFA的表示与插值干预在生成控制和检索任务上显著优于多种基线，并在多项消融中保持稳健。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) process image patches and text tokens in a shared residual stream, but the local geometry through which the two modalities interact remains poorly understood. Most interpretability methods identify global linear directions, which may miss representations that are globally high-dimensional but locally low-dimensional. We introduce LENS (Local Explanation of Neighborhood Subspaces), a method that decomposes VLM activations into local low-rank Gaussian neighborhoods using a Mixture of Factor Analyzers. Applied to LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B, LENS reveals distinct depth-dependent fusion trajectories consistent with each model's fusion mechanism: LLaVA progressively mixes modalities at later layers, whereas Qwen3-VL mixes them early, partially re-segregates them, and recombines them near the output. An automated multimodal labeling pipeline assigns concise semantic descriptions to these neighborhoods. Interpolating activations toward neighborhood centroids causally redirects generation within and across modalities and outperforms difference-in-means and VL-SAE in most evaluated conditions; in one LLaVA vision-to-vision setting, MFA achieves 5.7 times the VL-SAE score. Human evaluation finds MFA steering competitive with prompting and substantially stronger than the other intervention baselines. Finally, the MFA coefficient space improves Qwen3-VL image-to-rendered-text retrieval at the deepest evaluated layer from 14.9% to 48.6% R@1. Ablations show that the reported fusion trajectories are stable across component counts, local ranks, and modality-purity thresholds. These results support local geometric neighborhoods as useful interpretable and causal units for analyzing cross-modal representations in the evaluated VLMs.

</details>


### [89] [Why Does the Future Branch? Identifiable Closure Tests for Stochastic Physical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00591)
*Yibin Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClosurePairs is an interventional evaluation protocol that identifies whether forecast branching is due to state-aliasing or intrinsic process noise—something standard predictive scores cannot detect—and it substantially improves attribution accuracy in multiple benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forecast accuracy and calibration (e.g., NLL) cannot distinguish whether multimodal/uncertain predicted futures arise from aliased observations or true dynamical randomness; that distinction is decision-relevant for downstream control and interpretation.

Method: The paper first proves non-identifiability of attribution between observation-aliasing and intrinsic stochasticity from standard transition data. It introduces ClosurePairs: an evaluation protocol that interposes compatible microstate pairings with repeated exogenous disturbances, then uses a two-way variance decomposition (and an independent-repeat variant when disturbances can't be reused) to quantify aliasing, process noise, and their interaction. Experiments compare ClosurePairs to baselines (e.g., total-variance router, deep ensembles) across Gaussian, Langevin, and pixel-conditioned recurrent settings.

Result: On likelihood-equivalent Gaussian systems, paired supervision reduces alias-fraction error 15.96× at identical test NLL. Across 18 nonlinear Langevin conditions, attribution MAE drops from 0.372 to 0.051 and sensing regret from 0.0138 to 0.0003 without changing NLL. On a pixel-conditioned recurrent model, a frozen shared-state probe reduces alias-fraction MAE from 0.584 to 0.130 in-distribution and from 0.630 to 0.170 OOD over ten seeds. In a matched-total-variance REFINE/BRANCH test, ClosurePairs attains 99.99%±0.02% accuracy versus 66.48%±1.06% for a total-variance router and improves selected NLL from -2.087 to -2.717 over five seeds.

Conclusion: ClosurePairs resolves an unidentifiability in stochastic world models by interventionally separating state-aliasing from process noise; it provides a measurable decomposition that standard forecast scores cannot, and yields large empirical improvements in attribution and decision-relevant metrics without changing predictive log-likelihood.

Abstract: Stochastic world models are usually evaluated by the accuracy and calibration of their predicted futures. These criteria leave a decision-relevant ambiguity: the same conditional future distribution can arise because an observation aliases different physical states, or because the dynamics remain random after the declared full state is fixed. We prove that this attribution is not identifiable from ordinary transition data, even with an optimal probabilistic predictor. We introduce ClosurePairs, an interventional evaluation protocol that crosses compatible microstates with repeated exogenous disturbances. A two-way variance decomposition identifies state aliasing, process noise, and their nonlinear interaction; an independent-repeat variant applies when disturbances cannot be reused. On likelihood-equivalent Gaussian systems, paired supervision reduces alias-fraction error 15.96-fold at identical test NLL. Across 18 nonlinear Langevin conditions, it reduces attribution MAE from 0.372 to 0.051 and sensing regret from 0.0138 to 0.0003 without changing NLL. On a pixel-conditioned recurrent model, a frozen shared-state probe reduces alias-fraction MAE against a deep ensemble from 0.584 to 0.130 in distribution and from 0.630 to 0.170 out of distribution over ten seeds. Finally, in a matched-total-variance REFINE/BRANCH test, a total-variance router reaches 66.48 percent plus or minus 1.06 percent accuracy, whereas ClosurePairs reaches 99.99 percent plus or minus 0.02 percent and improves selected NLL from -2.087 to -2.717 over five seeds. ClosurePairs therefore measures why futures branch, information that proper forecast scores cannot identify.

</details>


### [90] [Escaping Confidence Trap: Evolutionary Decoding for Mathematical Reasoning in Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00605)
*Zhenhong Sun,Hanqing Zhao,Yatao Bian,Rongcheng Tu,Liuyue Xie,Xu Zhang,Jue Wang,Davide Modolo,Daoyi Dong,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: They identify a 'diffusion confidence trap' in diffusion LLM decoding where local token confidence diverges from global mathematical correctness; propose Evolutionary Decoding, a training-free test-time method combining step-wise selection and block-wise mutation to maintain useful numerical-symbolic signals and escape incorrect high-confidence loops, improving LLaDA 2.0 on math benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion LLMs show strong general performance but fail in preserving consistent numerical-symbolic reasoning during progressive unmasking, with local confidence becoming misaligned with global correctness; need a test-time, training-free method to stabilize decoding trajectories and find correct reasoning paths.

Method: Analyze LLaDA 2.0 decoding trajectories to identify failure modes; model diffusion decoding as an evolutionary search over candidate reasoning states; apply step-wise selection to keep promising partial states and suppress repetition; apply block-wise mutation to introduce structured alternative continuations; evaluate on multiple math reasoning benchmarks against confidence-based decoding.

Result: Empirical results indicate Evolutionary Decoding improves correctness of mathematical reasoning by stabilizing sampling and escaping high-confidence incorrect basins, leading to higher accuracy on multiple benchmarks compared to confidence-based decoding.

Conclusion: Evolutionary Decoding reduces both sampling-sensitive and sampling-consistent failures in diffusion LLMs, yielding more reliable mathematical reasoning without additional training, outperforming prior confidence-based decoding on evaluated benchmarks.

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs, offering efficient generation through block-wise progressive unmasking. However, their strong general-purpose performance does not necessarily translate into reliable mathematical reasoning, where correctness depends on preserving coherent numerical-symbolic reasoning trajectories. In this work, we analyze the decoding trajectories of LLaDA 2.0 and identify a recurring diffusion confidence trap: local token confidence can become misaligned with global reasoning correctness during progressive block decoding. Our analysis reveals two representative failure regimes: sampling-sensitive failures, where correct paths exist but are unstable, and sampling-consistent failures, where repeated sampling converges to repetitive high-confidence but incorrect continuations. Motivated by this observation, we propose Evolutionary Decoding, a training-free test-time scaling framework that views diffusion decoding as an evolutionary process over candidate reasoning states. The framework combines step-wise selection, which preserves useful numerical-symbolic signals and suppresses repetitive patterns, with block-wise mutation, which introduces structured alternatives to escape incorrect high-confidence basins. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that Evolutionary Decoding improves LLaDA 2.0 over confidence-based decoding, leading to more reliable mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [91] [Slides2MindMap: Reconstructing Cognitively Efficient Knowledge Hierarchies from Lecture Slides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00610)
*Yuzhi Wang,Rongjun Ye,Shengyuan Chen,Huachi Zhou,Jiaqi Bai,Chuang Zhou,Zhicong Hong,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Slides2MindMap任务与S2M-Bench基准，设计认知驱动评估并提出AutoMindMap框架，实验证实其在自动从幻灯片生成心智图方面的有效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讲义幻灯片内容零散且异构，自动生成心智图能帮助学习者高效整合碎片化知识，但该方向缺乏成体系的任务定义、数据集与评估框架，且需在全局一致性与局部信息保真间权衡。

Method: 提出一种agentic框架AutoMindMap，包含Skeleton Laying（全局骨架搭建）、Iterative Knowledge Integration（结合上下文的迭代知识整合与摘要）、Dual-Stage Refinement（局部-全局解耦的双阶段细化）；同时设计基于认知科学的评价体系和多源评价（人工标注比对、结构一致性分析、VLM评判）。

Result: 构建了包含12,774页幻灯片与24门课程的S2M-Bench并提供专家标注的心智图；实验证明AutoMindMap在S2M-Bench上优于基线方法、在不同模型与场景下表现稳健，体现了较强的教学应用潜力。

Conclusion: 本文提出了Slides2MindMap任务并构建了S2M-Bench基准，提出AutoMindMap框架，展示了其在重建认知高效知识层级上的优势，实验表明其在不同模型与情境下均优于基线并具有较好鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generating mind maps from lecture slides can help learners efficiently assimilate fragmented knowledge, promising substantial benefits for intelligent education. However, dedicated automatic generation and evaluation frameworks remain underexplored and challenging, requiring a global-local knowledge focus balance and handling large-scale, heterogeneous slides. We formulate the Slides2MindMap task, which aims to reconstruct cognitively efficient knowledge hierarchies from a course's slide deck collection. For systematic evaluation, we introduce S2M-Bench, a benchmark comprising 12,774 slide pages with expert-annotated mind maps spanning 24 university courses. S2M-Bench includes a cognitive-science-grounded evaluation framework that integrates ground-truth-based comparison, structure conformity analysis, and VLM-as-a-Judge. To address this task, we propose AutoMindMap, an agentic framework inspired by the Structure Building Framework. AutoMindMap comprises Skeleton Laying for global scaffold anchoring, Iterative Knowledge Integration augmented by context-aware summarization, and Dual-Stage Refinement with a local-global decoupling mechanism. The framework reconciles local knowledge faithfulness with global coherence, and adapts to slide-specific features. Experiments on S2M-Bench demonstrate that AutoMindMap outperforms baselines and achieves superior robustness across different models and scenarios, underscoring its pedagogical application value.

</details>


### [92] [DASH: Decoupled Adaptive Surrogate - Acquisition Harness for Automated Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00641)
*Changquan Zhao,Yuxiang Sun,Ruihao Zhu,Cheng Hua,Yulian He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DASH 提出一种“解耦的自适应代理-采集器框架”（Decoupled Adaptive Surrogate–Acquisition Harness），针对 AutoBO 中代理模型与采集函数角色不一致的问题：用预测可靠性/不确定性校准/排序一致性选择代理，用两阶段配额控制+候选短表再由 LLM 最终挑选采集函数，并通过知识引导的 warm start 与结构化记忆落地领域知识。在四个化学优化任务上显著优于最好的 AutoBO 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AutoBO 要么只适配代理或只适配采集器，造成瓶颈；要么用统一准则联合选择代理-采集对，忽视两者不同的角色与侧重点（代理侧重预测可靠性，采集侧重策略与任务上下文），因此需要解耦并引入能利用语义/上下文信息的决策机制。

Method: （1）代理选择：基于预测可靠性、置信度校准与排序一致性评估并选择代理；（2）采集控制：两阶段配额分配周期性调整采集函数的使用频次，构建 BO 候选短表；（3）LLM 决策：将候选短表交给 LLM 完成最终采集函数选择；（4）集成 harness：知识引导的 warm start 与结构化记忆来利用领域先验与历史反馈；（5）评估：四个化学优化任务上的比较实验、消融和泄露/记忆检查。

Result: 在四个化学任务上与最优 AutoBO 基线相比，轨迹级加速因子（Acceleration Factor）提升 12.51%，终点增强因子（Enhancement Factor）提升 5.00%；跨多种 LLM 骨干表现稳定，消融实验证明各组件互补，且未发现基准记忆或源码泄露导致的伪增益证据。

Conclusion: 通过解耦代理与采集器的选择标准、引入 LLM 做最终采集决策并结合知识化的初始化与记忆，DASH 在化学优化任务上实现了稳健且可解释的性能提升，且对不同 LLM 骨干保持稳健。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) relies on a surrogate model and an acquisition function, yet the most suitable choices vary across tasks and optimization stages. Automated Bayesian optimization (AutoBO) addresses this variability by adapting BO components online. However, existing AutoBO methods either adapt one component, leaving the other mismatched and creating a bottleneck, or jointly select surrogate--acquisition pairs under a shared criterion, overlooking their distinct roles: surrogate selection depends on predictive reliability, whereas acquisition adaptation should respond to campaign context.In this paper, we propose DASH, a Decoupled Adaptive Surrogate--Acquisition Harness for large-language- model (LLM)-enhanced AutoBO. DASH selects surrogates by predictive reliability, uncertainty calibration, and ranking consistency; its two-stage acquisition controller periodically reallocates quotas across acquisition functions, builds a BO shortlist accordingly, and delegates final selection to an LLM. DASH also incorporates an integrated harness, consisting of knowledge-guided warm start and structured memory, to ground optimization in domain knowledge and accumulated feedback. Across four chemical optimization tasks, DASH outperforms the best AutoBO baseline by 12.51% in trajectory-level Acceleration Factor and 5.00% in endpoint Enhancement Factor. Results remain strong across LLM backbones, and ablations verify the complementary contributions of all components. Full-table and behavioral contamination checks find no detectable evidence that direct benchmark memorization or source-cell leakage explains these gains.

</details>


### [93] [HetGPS: Scalable Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Physics-Anchored Adaptive Safety for EV Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00679)
*Xiangwei Wang,Nanduni Nimalsiri,Yu Xia,Peng Wang,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HetGPS：用可学习的图风险（决定干预强度）和物理投影（决定方向）组合，实现对百万级 EV 车队拓扑感知的实时安全干预，既显著降低电压越限，又保持任务成功率，并以固定参数规模横向扩展与零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网络耦合多智能体系统必须在保护公共安全约束（如电压上限）与尊重个体任务策略之间取得平衡。传统基于物理的投影可能过度干预，纯学习方法又难以保证物理可行性与可扩展性。因而提出一个混合机制：把‘何时以及干预多少’交给学习（以适配复杂布局与状态），把‘如何纠正’交给物理模型，从而兼顾安全、性能与可扩展性。

Method: 框架名为 HetGPS：1) 策略：参数共享的异构图 soft actor-critic（SAC）实现拓扑感知的协同决策，模型大小与车队规模独立；2) 风险模块：动作条件（action-conditioned）的图残差模型预测状态相关的干预权威（即干预幅度调度）；3) 物理投影：物理模型给出纠正方向（如投影到安全可行域），二者分离使得学习只负责何时与多大程度干预，而物理保证纠正方向的可行性；4) 联合部署：在 EV 充电任务上将过滤器与策略耦合，比较固定权威与自适应权威的性能，评估尺度扩展与迁移能力。

Result: HetGPS 在 EV 充电场景中将学习到的图风险用于调度干预强度（Adaptive Authority），同时用物理模型确定纠正方向，从而在不过度干预任务策略的前提下，显著降低配电网电压越限。实验显示：在五个网络（200–3,218 辆 EV，100 天评估）中，未过滤时母线-步电压越限为 3.93–7.74%，引入 Adaptive Authority 后降至 0.52–3.44%，出发成功率维持在 99.06–100%；与固定权威的物理投影相比，Adaptive Authority 在所有网络上提高了平均奖励，并在四个网络上降低了平均安全得分（更低更好）。模型规模为 383,702 个参数，与之匹配的集中式 SAC 在 3,218 辆 EV 情况下约大 170 倍。用 8 变压器系统训练的策略可零样本迁移到 16 与 32 变压器系统，达到 0.57–0.75% 的越限率且出发成功率至少 99.99%。这些结果表明：学习的图风险可在大规模时段分配干预权限，而馈线物理学为纠正动作提供锚定方向。

Conclusion: 将学习到的、状态与动作条件相关的图残差用于调度干预“权威度”，并用物理约束确定纠正方向，可以在不显著牺牲任务性能的前提下，大幅提升配电网多主体安全性；此外，参数共享的异构图策略结构实现了与车队规模无关的模型大小与很好的迁移能力。

Abstract: Safety interventions for large populations of network-coupled agents must protect shared constraints without unnecessarily overriding task-oriented policy decisions. We present HetGPS, a hybrid graph-control framework synergizing learned graph risk with physics-anchored correction by separating intervention magnitude from corrective direction. An action-conditioned graph residual model schedules state-dependent intervention authority, while a physics model determines its direction. For electric vehicle (EV) charging, we couple this filter with a parameter-shared heterogeneous graph soft actor-critic policy, enabling topology-aware coordination with a learned model size independent of fleet size. Across five nested distribution networks with 200--3,218 EVs and 100 evaluation days, Adaptive Authority reduces bus--step voltage violations from 3.93--7.74\% without filtering to 0.52--3.44\%, while maintaining 99.06--100\% departure success. Relative to the same physics-directed projection with fixed authority, it improves mean reward on all five networks and lowers the mean safety score on four. The deployed policy-and-risk model contains 383,702 learned parameters at every scale; at 3,218 EVs, a matched centralized SAC actor is about $170\times$ larger. A policy trained on the eight-transformer system transfers zero-shot to the 16- and 32-transformer systems, attaining 0.57--0.75\% violation rates and at least 99.99\% departure success. These results show that learned graph risk can allocate intervention authority at scale while feeder physics anchors corrective action.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-Dimensional Assessment for AI Cognition (MAAC): A Theoretical Framework for Process-Oriented Cognitive Evaluation of Text-Based AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00680)
*Abdalla Doleh,Ratna Babu Chinnam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个以认知科学为依据的九维评估框架（MAAC），用于从过程层面诊断文本型AI的“如何思考”，并通过多种理论分析验证其一致性和可测性，旨在补充现有结果导向基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对人工智能系统的评估主要基于结果导向的基准（准确性、鲁棒性、公平性等），但这些基准无法诊断产生性能的认知过程，不能回答系统如何推理、整合记忆、管理复杂性或防止幻觉等核心问题。本文旨在把评估重心从“产出是什么”转向“如何思考”，构建可操作的过程级认知评估框架。

Method: 基于认知科学经典理论（Marr的三层次假说、Baddeley的工作记忆模型、Sweller的认知负荷理论以及统一认知理论等），构建九个认知维度；随后通过五种理论性分析工具来检验框架的覆盖性、非冗余性、与现有评价的差距，以及提供示例性诊断并提出可被实证检验的维度间依赖性假设。

Result: 提出并定义了MAAC（Multi-Dimensional Assessment for AI Cognition）框架，包含九个基于认知科学的维度：认知负荷、工具执行、内容质量、记忆整合、复杂性处理、幻觉控制、知识迁移、处理效率和过程-结果一致性。通过五种理论分析（维度-理论映射、覆盖矩阵、相对于现有评估的差距分析、示例诊断、先验的相互依赖性预测）提供初步的连贯性与可检验性证据。

Conclusion: MAAC为文本型AI提供了一个理论上有据且可操作的多维过程级评估方案，能够补充传统结果基准，促使更深入的认知层面诊断。下一步需要将维度具体量化、设计任务与度量、进行实证验证并检验维度间预测关系。

Abstract: Evaluating artificial intelligence systems has historically relied on outcome-based benchmarks that measure task accuracy, robustness, or fairness. While indispensable, these benchmarks provide limited diagnostic insight into the underlying cognitive processes that generate performance-leaving critical questions unanswered about how AI systems reason, integrate memory, manage complexity, or avoid generating false information. This paper introduces the Multi-Dimensional Assessment for AI Cognition (MAAC), a theoretically grounded framework for shifting evaluation from what text-based AI systems produce to how they think. MAAC defines nine cognitively motivated dimensions: Cognitive Load, Tool Execution, Content Quality, Memory Integration, Complexity Handling, Hallucination Control, Knowledge Transfer, Processing Efficiency, and Process-Outcome Alignment. Each dimension is grounded in established cognitive science theory-drawing on Marr's tri-level hypothesis, Baddeley's working memory model, Sweller's cognitive load theory, and unified theories of cognition. Five theoretical analyses provide initial support for the framework's coherence and empirical testability: dimension-to-theory mapping; a coverage matrix assessing breadth and non-redundancy; a formal gap analysis relative to current evaluation practice; a worked diagnostic illustration; and a set of a priori interdependency predictions for future empirical testing. MAAC provides a theoretical and operational framework for principled process-level cognitive assessment of text-based AI systems, complementing existing outcome-based benchmarks with cognitively grounded, multi-dimensional evaluation.

</details>


### [95] [When Does LLM Orchestration Pay Off? A Controlled Evaluation of Accuracy, Cost, and Task Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00685)
*Nicolas Leins,Nico Pelleriti,Jana Gonnermann-Müller,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在统一优化预算下，LLM orchestration通常只带来有限且模型/基准依赖的准确率增益，但消耗显著更多tokens；因此应进行模型特定的准确率—成本评估，而非默认采用更多推理结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验额外推理时结构（orchestration）在受控优化条件下是否真正带来显著性能提升，量化其准确率增益与推理成本（tokens）之间的权衡，并探究效果是否随任务难度或基础模型而变化。
