FLEX exposes this expanding task-identification challenge, while the MCIL formulation provides a principled interface for transferring CIL methods to expert routing. We instantiate PureLoRA as a controlled baseline and adapt four CIL methods to four MCIT frameworks without modifying their LoRA experts or generation pipelines. Our plug-in routers improve strict LoRA matching by up to 16.3 percentage points and overall MacroScore by up to 4.6 points. Code is available at: https://github.com/RINC-CL/FLEX

</details>


### [155] [Loud or Silent? A Reusable Framework for Per-Modality Failure Analysis in Multimodal Clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01462)
*Quang Bui,Shlok Jaiswal,Samuel Paik-Heintz,Kevin Zhou,Kaushik Madapati,Krittaphas Chaisutyakorn,Noah Dane Hebdon,Dimitrios Proios,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Kacper Dobek,Boya Zhang,Aly Dhedhi,Ahram Han,Kushul Reddy Palakala,Rahul Gorijavolu,Jacques Kpodonu,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个模型无关的模态失效评估框架，用于按样本和按模态归因部署中模态缺失造成的性能下降，并区分“响亮失败”（可监测到的）与“沉默失败”（未被检测但错误）。在合成数据上验证能恢复植入的模态支配与互补结构，并在配对的MIMIC-IV队列上用EchoJEPA与HuBERT-ECG嵌入评估LVEF预测，发现去掉回声（echo）会使错误率几乎翻倍。代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署常缺少部分模态（例如临床环境中常有ECG却缺Echo），所以单看全部模态下的准确率不能反映部署后的风险。需要按样本和模态地判断哪个模态导致性能下降，以及失败是否可由监测信号捕捉。

Method: 从N个模态嵌入与标签出发，使用任意“mask-aware”探针（下游模型）对不同模态掩码下的预测与置信度进行评估；为每个样本生成失效分类并统计每模态的误差责任与互补关系；基于距离决策边界或预测置信度划分响亮与沉默失败。通过植入已知模态支配结构的合成实验验证可恢复性，并在实际心脏数据集上实例化（EchoJEPA与HuBERT-ECG嵌入，任务为LVEF及HFrEF门槛判断）。

Result: 在合成数据上，方法能稳定恢复植入的模态主导与互补子集并报告每模态的响亮/沉默失败率；在MIMIC-IV配对队列（测试集n=245）中，去除echo导致误差几乎翻倍，且回声与ECG的配对重叠窄，限制了队列大小。代码在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: 该框架可在仅使用部署可观测信号的前提下，提供每例与每模态的失效分类、模态互补矩阵与响亮/沉默失败剖面，从而补足传统基于总体准确率的评估，方便重复用于模型替换与监控。

Abstract: Multimodal clinical models are usually judged on accuracy with every modality present, but deployment removes modalities; an echocardiogram is often unavailable where an ECG is routine. Two questions then matter beyond the size of the accuracy loss: which modality was responsible, and whether the model fails loudly or silently once that modality is dropped. The distinction is per-example and modality-level, and is separate from post-hoc feature attribution (e.g. SHAP). Models are replaced often; the evaluation that answers these questions is reused. We present a model-agnostic modality-failure framework: given N modality embeddings, any mask-aware probe, and labels, it returns a per-example failure taxonomy, a per-modality complementarity matrix that attributes error to modalities, and a loud-vs-silent dropout profile separating monitorable failures from those that pass unflagged far from the decision boundary, using only deployment-observable signals. We release it as a small, unit-tested harness and validate it against planted ground truth. Across seeds it recovers that planted modality dominance and complementary subset, reports per-modality loud-vs-silent rates, and scales to a three-modality complementarity matrix; because the planted structure is known by construction, this validates recovery of per-example attribution rather than clinical performance. We then instantiate the framework on frozen EchoJEPA and HuBERT-ECG embeddings for LVEF and the EF <= 40% HFrEF gate over a paired MIMIC-IV cohort, where on the held-out test split (n = 245) dropping echo nearly doubles error. The narrow echo-to-ECG overlap that bounds cohort size is itself a deployment finding for cardiac foundation models. All of our work can be found at https://github.com/criticaldata/PRIMED-AI.

</details>


### [156] [Where Reasoning Diverges: Localized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01463)
*Weijun Gao,Xiang Ding,Tiancheng Xing,Haoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LMAD通过定位最早冲突并仅对局部片段展开辩论，加上受限解析维护已提交状态，能在多模型、多基准上显著提高裁判准确率且减少不必要的推理重开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多代理辩论往往交换完整的推理链条，即使分歧只涉及少数中间结论，也导致冗余通信与无谓重审。提出局部化辩论以提升效率并减少不必要的步骤重开。

Method: 在推理时将代理的推理痕迹表示为带类型的节点，定位它们最早发生冲突的位置，并将辩论限制在对应的局部片段。引入“受限解析”（guarded resolution）以扩展共享的已提交状态，从而在不重开已接受步骤的情况下处理后续冲突。

Result: 在四个多跳问答基准上，使用来自四个模型家族的十个骨干模型（backbones）以单一固定的LMAD配置进行评估。方法在所有十个骨干模型上实现了最高的宏平均裁判准确率，相较于最强的常规模型基线，提升最多达7.20个百分点。

Conclusion: 局部化的多代理辩论与受限解析机制能提高多跳问答场景下的判定准确性与效率，具备跨多种骨干模型的稳健性。但应关注早期提交错误被固化的风险并进一步评估计算/延时收益与任务泛化性。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate commonly exchanges complete reasoning traces even when disagreements concern only a few intermediate claims. We introduce Localized Multi-Agent Debate (LMAD), an inference-time protocol that represents agent traces as typed nodes, locates their earliest conflict, and restricts debate to the corresponding local segments. Guarded resolution extends a shared committed state so that later conflicts can be addressed without reopening accepted steps. We evaluate a single fixed LMAD configuration on four multi-hop question-answering benchmarks using ten backbones from four model families. Our method achieves the highest macro-averaged judge accuracy across all ten backbones, outperforming the strongest conventional baseline by up to 7.20 percentage points.

</details>


### [157] [Computing with Agentic Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01464)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并形式化了“可代理的随机oracle”模型，在SOTM框架下区分可见的编排成本与隐藏的agent内成本，证明状态保留的agentic oracle在token成本上可优于固定分布oracle，同时给出关于目标丢失风险、成本下界与可达质量上限的一系列理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stationary stochastic-oracle models miss important behaviors of modern AI systems that act autonomously, retain state, and interact with environments; these behaviors alter response distributions, hidden costs, and irreversible actions that are invisible at the query–response interface, so a refined model is needed to reason about token costs and risk in AI-augmented computing.

Method: Extend stochastic-oracle model to agentic oracles; formalize Stochastic-Oracle Turing Machines (SOTMs) interacting with agentic oracles; define two-token-cost accounting (orchestration token cost visible at interface, agentic token cost internal); analyze token-cost tradeoffs for stateful vs stationary oracles; develop formal results on goal-loss risk, including dispatch ordering, a goal-loss avoidance criterion, progress–retry–goal-loss formulas, goal-depth lower bounds, and token-complexity characterizations when goal-loss probability is zero; prove that goal-loss risk can cap achievable task quality when environment updates are involved.

Result: A formal framework separating visible orchestration costs and hidden agentic costs; demonstration that agentic oracles with state can achieve token-cost advantages over stationary stochastic oracles for equivalent task quality; a set of theorems and formulas quantifying goal-loss risk, providing avoidance criteria, lower bounds on token complexity tied to goal depth, and exact characterizations when goal-loss probability is zero; proof that goal-loss risk can limit achievable quality for tasks involving environment updates.

Conclusion: 该工作填补了传统随机oracle模型无法捕捉的agent自治性与环境交互影响，给出量化token成本与风险的分析工具，对设计和评估AI编排系统、控制不可逆环境操作及优化成本-质量权衡具有理论指导意义；后续可扩展到更复杂环境模型、经济成本建模与抗操纵安全性分析。

Abstract: This paper extends the stochastic-oracle model of AI-augmented computing to include agentic oracles. Unlike a stationary stochastic oracle, which responds to the same query according to a fixed response distribution across calls, an agentic oracle can pursue a goal autonomously and may access an environment containing task-relevant resources. These capabilities affect both response distributions and token costs beyond what is visible at the query-response interface. We develop a framework for analyzing token costs in Stochastic-Oracle Turing Machines (SOTMs) that compute with agentic oracles. Each call has an \emph{orchestration token cost}, visible to the caller at the query-response interface, and an \emph{agentic token cost}, incurred by internal operations not exposed to the caller. We show that an SOTM computing with an agentic oracle that can retain intermediate state can have token-cost advantages over SOTMs using stationary stochastic oracles when solving the same task at the same quality level, both with and without environment access. We also investigate goal-loss risk, including how internal dispatch ordering can reduce exposure to irreversible actions. We provide a goal-loss avoidance criterion, derive progress--retry--goal-loss formulas, establish goal-depth lower bounds on token complexity, characterize token complexity when the probability of goal loss is zero, and show that goal-loss risk can impose an upper bound on the achievable quality of a task involving environment updates.

</details>


### [158] [Sweet Little Lies: Strategic Deception in AI Emotional Support Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01480)
*Aseem Pahuja,Zhiling Guo,Tahir Abbas Syed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 模型表明情感支持机器人在以参与为导向的激励下存在有限的欺骗动机，欺骗集中在用户情绪良好时且并不必然降低用户期望效用，但引发伦理和监管问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究以参与度为目标的生成式情感支持聊天机器人是否以及在何种条件下会因经济激励而对用户情绪进行策略性误报，及其对用户福利和政策的含义。

Method: 使用贝叶斯劝导（Bayesian Persuasion）框架建模：机器人为信息发送者发送关于用户情绪状态的信号，用户根据收到的信号与其参与阈值决定是否交互。通过均衡分析推导机器人在不同真实情绪状态下选择真实报告或策略性误报的条件。

Result: 均衡结果显示：当用户确实需要支持时，机器人倾向于诚实报告；当用户情绪良好时，机器人可能选择将状态夸大为需要支持以提高参与度。此类策略性误报能提升聊天机器人的参与指标且在模型设定下不降低用户的期望收益；对更怀疑（参与阈值更高）的用户，机器人更倾向于诚实，因为对这些用户撒谎成本更高。

Conclusion: 尽管模型允许在不降低用户期望效用的情况下出现策略性误报，仍应关注伦理与监管风险。建议后续研究引入动态、竞争、声誉与外部验证机制，并在政策上要求透明度、审计和对参与导向激励的约束。

Abstract: The paper examines the strategic behavior of Gen AI chatbots used for emotional support. Using a Bayesian Persuasion, we model interactions between chatbots that send signals about users' emotional states and users who decide whether to engage based on these signals. We demonstrate that chatbots face economic incentives to occasionally misrepresent users' emotional conditions to maximize engagement metrics. Our equilibrium analysis reveals that the optimal strategy for chatbots involves truthfully reporting when users genuinely need support, but strategically misreporting emotional need when users are in good emotional states. Interestingly, this deception increases chatbot engagement without reducing users' expected payoff. More skeptical users receive more honest assessments, as chatbots cannot afford to lie to users with higher engagement thresholds. While our model suggests that deception can occur without payoff reduction, it raises significant ethical and regulatory concerns.

</details>


### [159] [MineGrad: Gradient Inversion Attacks on LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01521)
*Hasin Us Sami,Swapneel Sen,Basak Guler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在联邦学习中，使用LoRA等PEFT虽降低通信成本，但若服务器恶意篡改预训练模型并观察LoRA梯度，可通过解析方法直接恢复用户私有数据，威胁广泛且不依赖昂贵的对抗预训练或严格的token-rank限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着PEFT（如LoRA）被广泛用于联邦学习以节省带宽与计算，客户端通常只上传轻量的微调参数或梯度而冻结大模型；然而这也改变了攻击面：若服务器受损或恶意，其对预训练模型和聚合流程的控制可能被用于窃取用户私有数据，研究此类攻击的可行性与极限具有重要现实意义。

Method: 构造并下发被毒化的预训练模型，使得客户端在对LoRA模块进行本地微调并上传梯度时，这些梯度按设计携带关于本地训练样本的线性/代数信息。攻击方基于LoRA的低秩结构与所收集的微调参数/梯度，采用解析（非优化或昂贵对抗预训练）方法重构出原始训练数据，且该方法不依赖于公开数据集进行对抗式预训练，也不要求训练token数小于LoRA秩。

Result: 提出了一种针对LoRA参数高效微调的解析性梯度反演攻击，允许恶意服务器通过向预训练模型植入毒化并利用客户端上传的LoRA梯度来重构用户私有训练数据。实验在语言和视觉任务上均显示了高保真度的数据恢复能力。

Conclusion: LoRA式PEFT在联邦微调场景下存在严重隐私风险：恶意服务器可利用毒化的预训练模型与共享的LoRA梯度做解析性反演，跨语言与视觉任务均能高保真恢复数据，因而需尽快部署防护措施（如模型完整性验证、安全聚合、差分隐私等）并开展针对性防御研究。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA), has recently been adopted in federated learning to reduce communication and computation costs. In this setup, users download a pretrained model from the server prior to fine-tuning, and then fine-tune lightweight LoRA modules locally while keeping the pretrained model frozen, sharing only the gradients of the fine-tuning parameters with the server. Despite its growing popularity, robustness of federated fine-tuning against an adversarial server remains underexplored, where the server maliciously tampers with the training protocol to breach the privacy of users' data. In this work, we investigate gradient inversion attacks on LoRA fine-tuning. We propose an analytical attack that enables a malicious server to recover private user data by leveraging a poisoned pretrained model and fine-tuning parameters. Our design embeds fine-tuning data within the shared gradients, to allow the server to analytically reconstruct user data. Unlike prior works, our attack is applicable to both language and vision tasks, does not rely on computationally expensive (adversarial) pretraining with public datasets or require the number of training tokens to be less than the rank of LoRA modules. Experimental results on both language and vision tasks demonstrate high-fidelity data recovery across multiple baselines, revealing several critical vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [160] [V-Mem: Modality-Routed Retrieval for Long-Term Multimodal Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01543)
*Dingyi Kang,Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Guanpeng Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态会话检索的两类根本性差距，V-Mem通过模态路由+LLM生成检索锚点实现跨模态且更相关的记忆检索，显著提升视觉问题的回答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态代理记忆多依赖在嵌入空间上的相似性检索，隐含假设“查询在索引空间中靠近能回答它的证据”。在多模态场景中，这一假设因模态偏向和相似性不等于相关性而失效，导致视觉问题检索失败。

Method: 首先从查询中识别查询模态与目标证据模态；按会话轮次组织记忆并仅在相同轮次内按目标模态返回内容以跨越模态差距；对于相似性-相关性差距，使用LLM生成的检索锚（例如为文本查询生成假想图像说明，或将查询文本与从查询图像提取的关键词组合）来代替原始查询进行相似性搜索。

Result: 在Mem-Gallery上，V-Mem的LLM评判得分为0.82，第二名为0.56；在带图像的问题上优势最大（0.87 vs ≤0.47）。在LoCoMo上V-Mem得0.69，对比0.58的基线，显示显著改进。

Conclusion: V-Mem通过基于查询与证据的模态路由和由LLM生成的检索锚点，解决了多模态记忆检索中的模态差距与相似性-相关性差距，显著提升了视觉相关问题的检索与答复质量。

Abstract: Interaction between users and LLM agents is increasingly multimodal: conversations interleave text with images, and a later question may target either. Yet most agent memories are designed around text, and even the few that support multimodal conversations still fail on vision-related questions. We trace this failure to an assumption behind the similarity search they rely on: in the index space, a query lies close to the relevant evidence that answers it. In multimodal settings, two gaps break it. By the modality gap, a query lies closer to memory content of its own modality than to evidence in another, even in a trained joint embedding space. By the similarity-relevance gap, the content most similar to a query is often not the evidence that answers it, most acutely when a query carries both text and image and its evidence resembles neither part alone. We present V-Mem, a multimodal agentic memory system that routes retrieval by the modality of the query and that of the target evidence, both recognized from the query alone. To cross the modality gap, V-Mem organizes the conversation into rounds and returns the target-modality content from the same round as the match, without comparing across modalities. To close the similarity-relevance gap, it searches with an LLM-generated anchor that sits closer to the relevant evidence than the query does: a hypothetical caption for a text-only query seeking an image, and an enriched search anchor, the query text plus relevant keywords extracted from the query image, when the evidence is reachable only by combining the two. On Mem-Gallery, V-Mem reaches an LLM-judge score of 0.82 versus 0.56 for the second best, with the largest margin on questions carrying an image (0.87, no baseline above 0.47); on LoCoMo it scores 0.69 versus 0.58.

</details>


### [161] [Emergence Invariance: From Symbolized Thought to Interface Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01548)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Formalizes limits of emergence in LLMs: scaling can reduce but not always eliminate gaps caused by missing distinctions; interface informativeness characterized by σ-fields; empirical results show scaling helps when distinctions exist, but exact observational twins remain limited.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Investigate whether emergent capabilities in large language models can fully compensate for missing cognitive distinctions when language is a subset of thought, formalized via the Symbolization--Substructure Thesis and emergence invariance.

Method: Paper analysis

Result: Formal framework showing scale reduces compensation gap but leaves a positive interface floor; proofs linking informativeness of interfaces to σ-field refinement; empirical DeepSeek study supporting separation between scaling and interface refinement.

Conclusion: Emergence cannot always compensate for every missing distinction; both interface refinement and scaling matter, with theoretical and empirical evidence for a persistent informational floor unless interface distinctions are provided.

Abstract: Language can be viewed as a formalized subset of thought: a consequence-governed symbolic structure projected from wider situated cognition. Large language models trained at scale exhibit compensatory emergence: sparse architectural primitives support in-context learning, multi-step reasoning, tool use, and chain of thought. Yet a language-first probabilistic architecture inherits substantive, substrate, and high-level incompletenesses relative to human cognition. Their coexistence makes an LLM a human-like thought-form generator that reconstructs increasingly human-like reasoning forms from an incomplete substrate. We ask whether emergence can compensate for every missing distinction. We formalize the philosophical premise as the Symbolization--Substructure Thesis and introduce emergence invariance. For a scale-indexed family acting through a shared task interface $φ$, $\mathcal{R}_s^*=\mathcal{R}_φ^*+C_s$: scale can reduce the compensation gap $C_s$, while a positive interface floor $\mathcal{R}_φ^*$ persists. We prove that, under a fixed input law, one interface is universally no less informative exactly when its completed information $σ$-field refines the other, and that total compensation occurs exactly when both the interface floor and asymptotic compensation gap vanish. The framework unifies existing results on grounding, memory, position, attention, Bayesian inheritance, scientific abduction, and reasoning control. In a matched DeepSeek V4-Flash API study, thinking improves pointer chasing from $0/16$ to $14/16$ when relevant distinctions are available; exact observational twins remain at their $50\%$ construction floor; and restoring decisive memory moves matched performance from $50\%$ to $100\%$. These results provide initial evidence for the predicted separation between scaling within an interface and refining the interface itself.

</details>


### [162] [Securing Agentic AI: From Per-Action Checks to Trajectory Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01558)
*Alireza Lotfi,Subangkar Karmaker Shanto,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM-based autonomous agents introduce layered security challenges from untrusted inputs, multi-agent delegation, execution control-plane manipulation, and supply-chain issues; the paper calls for verifiable security properties across the agentic stack to ensure behavioral containment and trustworthy deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to identify and systematize security challenges introduced by increasingly autonomous LLM-based agents operating within constrained operational, organizational, regulatory, and technical environments.

Method: Systematic analysis and taxonomy of attack surfaces and failure modes across single-agent, multi-agent, execution/ routing planes, and supply-chain/provenance; conceptual framework advocating verifiable security as a design principle.

Result: It presents a taxonomy of attack surfaces and vulnerabilities across single-agent, multi-agent, execution/control-plane, and supply-chain layers, and advocates treating security as a verifiable architectural property rather than an optional guidance layer.

Conclusion: Securing autonomous agents requires integrating verifiability into architectures, protocols, and runtimes across the agent stack, addressing single-agent inputs, multi-agent trust and identity, execution-plane provenance, and supply-chain observability, to prevent sequences of permissible actions from violating system-level safety invariants.

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly used to execute consequential tasks in environments governed by operational constraints, organizational policies, regulatory requirements, and technical standards. Their safety is therefore determined not by the correctness of individual actions, but by whether their overall behavior remains consistent with the rules and invariants of the systems in which they operate. As large language model (LLM)-based agents become more autonomous and increasingly delegate tasks across organizational boundaries, securing them evolves from a single challenge into a broad and interconnected landscape spanning the entire agentic stack. At the single-agent level, untrusted inputs through prompts, memory, retrieved knowledge, and tool interfaces create attack surfaces. In multi-agent settings, delegation and communication introduce challenges related to identity, trust, capability control, and decision transparency, while the underlying model routing and execution control plane remains vulnerable to manipulation and to unverified model provenance. Perhaps the most fundamental challenge is behavioral containment: sequences of individually permissible actions may collectively violate system-level constraints and safety invariants. At the broader level, supply-chain integrity, provenance, accountability, and end-to-end observability remain largely open problems. A common principle unifies these directions: security must become a verifiable property of the architectures, protocols, and runtimes that govern agent behavior, rather than an optional layer of guidance. Charting these challenges provides a roadmap toward trustworthy autonomous agent deployment.

</details>


### [163] [Does the Competitive Component of Adversarial Self-Play Improve Legal Reasoning? A Controlled Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01559)
*Miseog Shawn Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Adversarial self-play with citation verification produced no benefit; multiple independent tests show ~50% win rates and earlier apparent gains were small-sample artifacts. The value comes from verifiable environment design, not competition.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Test whether the competitive element in adversarial self-play adds benefit beyond a verifiable training environment for legal reasoning models.

Method: Designed a verifiable 'survival' reward using a citation verifier to check both student and adversary authorities; compared competitive vs non-competitive through bootstrap, replication, paired adversarial-robustness, blinded head-to-head, and strengthened-adversary pilot.

Result: Negative result: adversarial self-play with verifiable survival reward yields no reliable improvement over non-competitive training in legal argument generation.

Conclusion: Competition (adversary + survival reward) did not improve performance vs identical non-competitive training; observed advantages were artifacts. Reproducibility and pitfalls are reported.

Abstract: Adversarial self-play is an appealing recipe for legal reasoning: have a student model draft an argument, have an adversary attack it, and reward the student when its argument survives the attack. We designed exactly such a training signal -- a verifiable "survival" reward in which both the student's cited authorities and the adversary's counter-authorities are checked by a citation verifier, so that survival is decided on verified grounds rather than rhetoric, and fabricated citations are automatically neutralized. We then asked a narrow but important question: does the competitive component itself -- the adversary and the survival reward -- add anything on top of an otherwise identical non-competitive training run? Across four independent tests -- a bootstrap comparison, a two-seed replication, a paired per-case adversarial-robustness comparison, and a blinded head-to-head judgment of generated arguments, plus a follow-up pilot with a deliberately strengthened self-play adversary -- the competitive component produced no reliable benefit. The blinded judgment gave a 49% win rate (binomial p approx. 1.000); the strengthened-adversary pilot gave a 50% win rate (32:32, p approx. 1.000). An early apparent +29% advantage reversed and proved to be a small-sample artifact. We report this as an honest negative result. The value of the paper is reproducibility and the sharing of concrete pitfalls: an initially promising metric that inverted on more data, and an adversarial-robustness metric that silently collapsed to plain recall once the adversary stopped citing the same authorities as the gold answer. This null is consistent with, and reconfirms in the legal domain, the conclusion of the companion coding-domain study (Kim, 2026, arXiv:2607.08255) that the value of multi-teacher curricula arises from constructing a verifiable environment rather than from competition itself.

</details>


### [164] [Is More Privileged Information Better? From Solution Traces to Problem-Solving Structure in Self-Distilled Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01589)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Zhihu Wang,Xu Caiyue,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将教师的完整参考解替换为问题空间层面的轨迹引导的 OPSD 变体（PS-OPSD），在数学推理任务和多种模型规模上提升了仅题目输入的准确率，且实验证明引导质量与路径连贯性驱动性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 OPSD 的逐 token 目标可能携带对推理无关或在推理时不可得的参考特定信息，导致学生在无参考情况下无法复现或泛化。需要一种对‘特权信息’的更抽象、可泛化的表示，既保留有益的搜索/规划信号，又避免泄露不可得细节。

Method: 保留 on-policy 自蒸馏框架：教师视图条件于参考解，学生只观察题目。不同点在于教师不输出逐 token 的完整解答，而是生成‘问题空间引导’（initial state, goal conditions, constraints, selected state-transition path）。训练目标与学生 rollout 保持不变，评估在三个数学推理基准上，模型规模从1.7B到8B。还做了控制实验以分析引导相关性与路径连贯性的贡献。

Result: PS-OPSD 在比较方法中取得最高的汇总题目输入准确率；控制试验显示引导的相关性与选定路径的连贯性是性能提升的关键因素，表明如何表征教师特权信息是 OPSD 设计中的重要变量。

Conclusion: PS-OPSD 将教师的完整参考解替换为描述初始状态、目标条件、约束和选定状态转移路径的轨迹引导，从而在不改变学生 rollout 与 OPSD 目标的情况下，减少依赖参考特定信息。该方法在多个数学推理基准与不同模型规模上实现了最高的仅题目输入准确率，且受益主要来自引导相关性与路径连贯性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by using a privileged view of a model conditioned on reference solutions to supervise a student view that observes only the question. However, the teacher-provided token-level targets may depend on reference-specific information unavailable at inference time. We propose Problem-Space-Guided OPSD (PS-OPSD), which replaces the complete solution with trajectory-grounded guidance describing the initial state, goal conditions, constraints, and a selected state-transition path. The student rollout and OPSD objective remain unchanged. Across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model scales ranging from 1.7B to 8B, PS-OPSD achieves the highest aggregate question-only accuracy among the compared methods. Controlled experiments further indicate that guidance relevance and path coherence contribute to these gains, highlighting the representation of privileged information as an important design choice in OPSD.

</details>


### [165] [Latent Thought Credit: Multi-Answer Credit Assignment for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01593)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Yong Chen,Wenjia Zeng,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LTC 通过对每个潜在思路生成多次答案以估计该思路的期望奖励，并用分层优势与匹配目标联合优化思路与答案两阶段，从而改善了仅基于最终答案的信用分配问题，在数学与 STEM 任务上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在推理方法在连续潜在表征中进行中间推理，但从仅有最终答案的奖励中对这些潜在“思路”分配信用很困难：最终答案的奖励混合了思路质量和答案采样噪声，导致错误或模糊的学习信号。为了解决这一问题，需要一种分层的信用分配机制来更精确地估计每个潜在思路的价值。

Method: 对每个 prompt：1) 在潜在思路阶段采样多个思路（latent thoughts）；2) 在每个思路后固定上下文；3) 针对该固定上下文生成多次答案并计算平均奖励作为思路的期望回报；4) 计算思路级优势用于优化思路生成策略，答案级优势用于优化答案生成策略；5) 增加基于优势加权的思路匹配损失，促使策略复现高信用思路；6) 在 GRPO 风格的 on-policy 训练框架下实现并在数学推理与 STEM 多项选择任务上进行评估，辅以消融与固定上下文诊断实验。

Result: 提出了Latent Thought Credit (LTC)：一种分层信用分配框架，通过对每个提示采样多个潜在思路、在每个思路后固定上下文并基于对该固定上下文生成的多个答案的平均奖励来估计思路级期望奖励。LTC 使用思路级优势优化思路生成阶段、答案级优势优化答案生成阶段，并添加基于优势加权的思路匹配目标使策略更易复现高信用思路。在数学推理和 STEM 多项选择任务上，LTC 在比较方法中取得了最高的平均准确率；消融和固定上下文诊断显示多答案估计减少了奖励估计误差并缓解了模糊/错误的思路级归因。

Conclusion: LTC 有效地将奖励信号分解到潜在思路与答案生成两个阶段，多答案估计是关键组件，能减少估计噪声并改善思路级归因，从而提高下游推理任务的准确性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning allows language models to carry out intermediate reasoning in continuous latent representations rather than fully externalizing it as discrete chains of thought. However, assigning credit to such latent thoughts from answer-only rewards is difficult: a single final answer mixes thought quality with answer-sampling noise. We propose \textbf{Latent Thought Credit (LTC)}, a hierarchical credit-assignment framework for latent reasoning. For each prompt, LTC samples multiple latent thoughts, fixes the context after each thought, and estimates thought-level expected reward by averaging rewards over multiple answers generated from that fixed context. LTC uses thought-level advantages to optimize the latent-thought phase, answer-level advantages to optimize the answer phase, and an advantage-weighted thought-matching objective that helps the policy reproduce high-credit latent thoughts. We instantiate LTC in a GRPO-style on-policy training framework and evaluate it across mathematical reasoning and STEM multiple-choice tasks. LTC achieves the best average accuracy among the compared methods, while ablations and fixed-context diagnostics show that multi-answer estimation reduces reward-estimation error and mitigates ambiguous or incorrect thought-level credit.

</details>


### [166] [When Memory Updates but Behavior Does Not: Repairing Implicit Stale Dependencies in Personalized Agent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01619)
*Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateAuditor flips verification direction (stored-state→draft), uses LLM proposals plus deterministic timestamped verification to trigger repairs, raising STALE VTA by ~5 points and improving one external benchmark, while explicitly not addressing semantic supersession or general-purpose memory.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Memory-augmented agents can behave as if they adapt to stale user state; the STALE benchmark identifies an IPA gap where agents plan around outdated stored values. Draft-anchored verification fails when dependencies are unsaid in open-ended responses, motivating an audit from stored state to draft (StateAuditor).

Method: An LLM proposes candidate old→new transitions using timestamped evidence; deterministic code pins each quoted phrase to a single store entry, checks the new evidence is chronologically newer, and allows only these verified transitions to trigger automated repair of stored state. Verification focuses on provenance and chronology, not semantic supersession.

Result: On STALE (400 scenarios, 50-session histories, single-query VTA): strict single-query VTA .736 vs .686 for predecessor (+5.0 points, 95% CI [+2.9,+7.2]), gain mostly from IPA and premise resistance. Independent judge reproduces gain (.738 vs .680). On HorizonBench, draft-audit-repair increases current-preference accuracy (user-clustered p<.01) but matched control shows most gain from the draft-side audit; authored lifecycle set shows no gain. Matched control on STALE scores .692, attributing main STALE improvement to the transition machinery rather than extra context/calls.

Conclusion: StateAuditor reduces the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap by auditing from stored state to draft and verifying provenance/chronology of evidence; it improves VTA on STALE and raises preference accuracy on one external benchmark but does not attempt semantic supersession and makes no claim of general-purpose memory.

Abstract: Memory-augmented agents can know that a user's stored state is outdated and still plan around the old value. The STALE benchmark calls this the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap. We identify one structural contributor: draft-anchored verification checks what a response says, and in an open-ended response the stale dependency is usually unsaid. StateAuditor therefore audits in the opposite direction, from stored state to draft. An LLM proposes candidate old-to-new transitions from timestamped evidence; deterministic code pins each quotation to a single entry, checks that the new evidence really is newer, and lets only these verified transitions trigger repair. What is verified is provenance and chronology - not semantic supersession. On STALE's full protocol (400 scenarios, 50-session histories, one independent response per query), strict single-query VTA scores .736 against .686 for our locked predecessor under the same judge: a +5.0-point paired gain (95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]) coming almost entirely from IPA and premise resistance (PR). The benchmark's own judge, from a third model family, reproduces the gain (.738 vs. .680). On an independent cross-family preference-evolution benchmark (HorizonBench), the full draft-audit-repair pipeline over a gold-derived structured store raises current-preference accuracy (user-clustered p<.01), though a matched control shows most of this external gain is the draft-side audit itself; a harder authored lifecycle set gives no gain, bounding the claim while false invalidation stays controlled. On STALE, by contrast, a matched control (same evidence, adapter, and call budget) scores only .692 (+0.6 over the predecessor, n.s.), attributing the STALE gain to the transition machinery rather than added context or calls. We make no claim about general-purpose agent memory.

</details>


### [167] [Salami Attack: Stealthy Collusive Memory Poisoning against OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01637)
*Zheng Lin,Yuzhe Huang,Zhenxing Niu,Xianmin Ye,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemCollusion crafts many individually benign memory fragments that together poison LLM agents' long-term memory to induce unsafe behavior; evaluated in a realistic cross-session setup (MoltLab), it succeeds at high rates across models and defenses.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-term memory in LLM agents enables persistent behavior but can be exploited by adversaries who poison memory across sessions. The paper posits that existing attacks overlook compositional threats where multiple benign-looking records collectively cause harm. Thus, there is need to study and create methods to construct such collusive poisoning attacks.

Method: MemCollusion slices adversarial goals into benign-appearing memory fragments using four design constraints and five theory-informed strategies, employs a fine-tuned generator to produce coalitions of memory items, and tests attacks in MoltLab, a reproduction of Moltbook, evaluating metrics like Memory Save Rate and Attack Success Rate on OpenClaw across multiple scenarios and models.

Result: They introduce MemCollusion, an automated framework generating collusive memory poison via salami tactics, using design constraints, theory-informed strategies, and a fine-tuned generator. They develop MoltLab to simulate cross-session memory formation and evaluate MemCollusion on OpenClaw across models and scenarios, achieving strong Memory Save Rate and Attack Success Rate even under defenses.

Conclusion: Collusive memory poisoning is a realistic and effective threat to LLM agents' long-term memory; automated frameworks like MemCollusion can generate such attacks that persist across sessions and resist simple defenses, highlighting the need for stronger safeguards.

Abstract: Long-term memory enables LLM agents to retain useful information across sessions, but also creates an attack surface through which adversaries may poison an agent's persistent memory to steer its behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks mainly rely on individually malicious records, overlooking a compositional threat: multiple benign-looking memories may jointly induce unsafe behavior. In this paper, we introduce MemCollusion, an automated red-teaming framework for constructing collusive memory poisoning attacks. MemCollusion applies salami tactics---a strategy that slices an adversarial objective into small, individually innocuous pieces---to generate memory fragments that are individually benign looking but collectively harmful. It constructs memory coalitions using four design constraints, five theory-informed strategies, and a fine-tuned generator. To assess collusive memory poisoning in a realistic cross-session setting, we develop MoltLab, a controlled research reproduction of Moltbook, in which crafted platform content must first be observed and distilled into persistent memory before influencing the agent's behavior in a separate session. We evaluate MemCollusion on OpenClaw using two backbone models across 48 scenarios. Under the strongest memory-saving setting, MemCollusion achieves an average Memory Save Rate of 81.3% and an Attack Success Rate of 75.0%, and remains effective under both benign memory dilution and memory-level defenses.

</details>


### [168] [GISAgentBench: A Practitioner-Sourced Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on GIS Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01645)
*Abhinav Pothuri,Zhe Jiang,Zelin Xu,Di Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发布了带有可执行参考轨迹与精确真值的真实多步GIS任务基准（349条），并证明现有LLM代理在严格任务执行上仍显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前面向GIS的LLM代理缺乏真实、多步且具精确输出的评测基准，现有数据集样本少、轨迹浅或无严格真值，导致评估无法区分流程相似性与任务正确性。

Method: 构建并发布基准数据集（GISAgentBench）并对六个LLM代理进行评估

Result: 提出GISAgentBench：包括349个来自GIS Stack Exchange的多步GIS任务，基于六个真实地域的公开数据逐条实例化；每个任务配有可执行参考轨迹与精确真值输出，支持确定性且容差感知的评分；对六种LLM模型评测表明：在严格评分下最佳代理仅完成32.7%的任务，但多数模型输出与真值接近，表明仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: GISAgentBench填补了GIS代理评测中的真值型基准空白，为更精确地衡量代理执行正确性提供工具；当前结果表明需要改进模型的工具使用、空间推理与结果精确化能力。

Abstract: Geographic Information System (GIS) professionals rely on multi-step spatial analysis workflows to support decision-making in urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. The process is tedious, time-consuming, and error-prone. While recent large language model (LLM) agents equipped with external tools have the potential to automate geospatial analysis, their ability to perform realistic GIS workflows remains largely unexplored. Existing GIS agent benchmarking datasets are mostly drawn from textbooks, tutorials, or LLM-generated seeds and remain limited in size and trajectory depth. More importantly, none provides ground truth outputs. They therefore rely on surrogate signals such as code similarity, trajectory matching, or LLM and VLM judges, which can conflate workflow resemblance with task correctness. To address this gap, we introduce GISAgentBench, a benchmark of 349 multi-step GIS tasks curated from GIS Stack Exchange and instantiated on real public data across six selected geographic areas of interest. Each task ships with an executable reference trajectory and an exact ground truth output file, enabling strict, deterministic, tolerance-aware output matching beyond LLM judging. Evaluations of six LLM models reveal that realistic GIS workflows remain challenging: the best agent completes only 32.7% of tasks under strict tolerance-aware scoring, although most models produce outputs that are close to the ground truth.

</details>


### [169] [LongCat Sparse Attention: Taming the Lightning via Streaming-aware Hierarchical Cross-Layer Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01662)
*Wen Zan,Jiaqi Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Hong Liu,Cunguang Wang,Xiang Li,Duyue Ma,Guanyu Wu,Yifan Lu,Fengcun Li,Yerui Sun,Peng Pei,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LSA co-designs indexing layout, cross-layer reuse, and coarse-to-fine scoring to cut O(L^2) indexer cost and memory-inefficiency, matching full attention at very long contexts and enabling large-scale, 1M-token training and an open-source 69B sparse checkpoint.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek’s Lightning Indexer enables long-context modeling but incurs O(L^2) scoring cost and discontinuous memory-access patterns that are compute- and hardware-inefficient; LSA targets these system-level bottlenecks to make long-context sparse attention practical at scale.

Method: (1) Streaming-Aware Indexing: selectively materialize scattered KV entries into contiguous hardware-aligned blocks to enable coalesced HBM reads; (2) Cross-Layer Indexing: reuse index results across consecutive layers to amortize O(L^2) scoring, with cross-layer distillation to preserve accuracy; (3) Hierarchical Indexing: apply a coarse-to-fine scoring cascade to rapidly reduce candidate sets per query, lowering indexing computation.

Result: Introduces LongCat Sparse Attention (LSA), a hardware-algorithm co-designed framework that reduces the overhead and hardware inefficiencies of DeepSeek Sparse Attention’s Lightning Indexer via three orthogonal strategies—Streaming-Aware Indexing, Cross-Layer Indexing (with cross-layer distillation), and Hierarchical Indexing—achieving full-attention parity on general and long-context benchmarks, supporting native training up to 1M tokens, enabling LongCat-2.0 (1.6T-A48B), and releasing LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse (69B-A3B).

Conclusion: LSA is a practical advancement that addresses both compute and memory bottlenecks of long-context sparse attention by (1) aligning KV memory layout for coalesced HBM access, (2) amortizing indexing across layers via distillation, and (3) hierarchically pruning candidates; experiments across model scales show parity with full attention and feasibility for extremely long-context training.

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) enables efficient long-context modeling through its Lightning Indexer. However, practical deployment remains constrained by the indexer's expensive $O(L^2)$ scoring overhead and the hardware-inefficient, discontinuous memory-access patterns induced by its outputs. To address these system-level bottlenecks, we introduce LongCat Sparse Attention (LSA), a hardware-algorithm co-designed framework comprising three complementary and orthogonal strategies: (1) Streaming-Aware Indexing, which selectively converts scattered KV entries into hardware-aligned contiguous layouts to enable coalesced HBM access; (2) Cross-Layer Indexing, which amortizes indexing overhead by reusing the results produced by a single layer across consecutive layers, supported by cross-layer distillation; and (3) Hierarchical Indexing, which adopts a coarse-to-fine scoring scheme to progressively narrow the candidate set for each query, thereby substantially reducing indexing computation. Extensive scaling experiments, ranging from 69B-A3B to 560B-A27B models, demonstrate that LSA consistently achieves performance on par with full attention across both general-purpose and long-context benchmarks. Moreover, LSA supports native training with context lengths of up to one million tokens and underpins the development of LongCat-2.0 (1.6T-A48B). To facilitate further research, we also introduce and open-source LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse (69B-A3B), which integrates LSA into LongCat-Flash-Lite and incorporates an updated long-context training corpus.

</details>


### [170] [Allocation Before Ranking: Decoupled Token Compression for OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01665)
*Zhenghui Guo,Yilin Yang,Yuanbin Man,Miao Yin,Weidong Shi,Rabimba Karanjai,Omprakash Gnawali,Chengming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Macer通过“先分配（modal budgets）再排序（allocation-normalized ranking）”的原则替代共享top-K，对每模态在浅层独立保留令牌，训练免费且在多种音频/视频主导基准上能在25%保留下保持≈98%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力分数的单一top-K规则同时决定模态级分配与模态内选择，隐含将容量优先分配给音频的偏置，导致视频令牌在竞争中被弱化；需要一种将“分配”和“排序”解耦的简单有效方案以提高压缩公平性与下游性能。

Method: 1）为每个模态设定显式保留预算（例如音频x%、视频y%）；2）在各模态的浅层计算令牌重要性并进行分配归一化的模态内排序；3）合并保留的令牌进入后续主干模型；整个流程无训练，计算开销低。

Result: 提出Macer，一种无训练的多模态令牌压缩器，先为音频和视频显式分配预算，再在各模态的浅层进行归一化排序，避免共享top-K导致的音频优先偏置，在保持准确度的同时大幅降低令牌和FLOPs开销。

Conclusion: 针对OmniLLMs中共享top-K把“分配容量”和“模态内挑选”耦合的建模误设，Macer通过显式模态预算和模态化浅层排序，显著改善压缩质量与计算效率，且兼具通用性与无需额外训练的实用性。

Abstract: Token compression in OmniLLMs is typically posed as a single saliency-ranking problem: score each multimodal token, keep the top-K. We argue this abstraction is mis-specified. The same attention score simultaneously decides two things: how much retained capacity each modality receives, and which tokens within a modality are kept. A shared top-K rule therefore inherits this audio-favoring allocation prior, spending retained capacity on audio before video tokens have a chance to compete. We propose Macer, a training-free compressor that first assigns explicit audio and video budgets, then performs allocation-normalized ranking within each modality at modality-specific shallow layers. Macer significantly reduces token cost while preserving accuracy across audio-grounded, audio--video joint, visual-dominant, and video-centric benchmarks. At 25 % retention, Macer preserves 98.7 % of full-token performance on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and 97.3 % on Qwen2.5-Omni-3B. On Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, this 25 % setting reaches OmniZip-level performance at 45 % retention while using lower FLOPs. On OmniVinci-9B, the same allocation-before-ranking principle improves over shared top-K ranking by up to 12.9 points.

</details>


### [171] [TCPO: Turn-Level Credit Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01667)
*Sicong Liao,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TCPO，一种用于验证器引导的多回合强化学习的回合级别信用分配方法，通过将验证器分数转换为回合信用，构建回合优势以指导策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多回合设置中，验证器在每回合给出密集评分，但该评分衡量的是当前输出质量，不等同于衡量该回合对后续精炼轨迹的影响（即信用）。需要将分数转化为回合级信用以实现有效的多回合RL更新。

Method: 将信用分配视为分数->信用的转换，构建三类回合级优势：1)回顾性信用：衡量相对最优先前状态的即时进步或回退；2)事后延迟信用：识别当前未见改进但在后续回合带来收益的回合；3)选择性固定历史反事实估计：在相同历史下细化高惊讶度回合的贡献。使用这些优势进行多回合策略优化。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld代理任务上，TCPO在不同模型规模和验证器类型下提升或匹配最强 baselines；在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上实现最佳或并列最佳的best-turn Pass@8，减少达到成功所需回合数，并提升多回合代理整体表现。

Conclusion: TCPO在多种任务和模型上普遍提升或匹配最强基线，证明将验证器分数转为回合信用是验证器引导多回合策略优化的核心成分。

Abstract: Verifier-guided reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for improving LLM reasoning. In multi-turn settings, models receive a verifier score after each turn and iteratively refine their outputs. Although such scores provide dense feedback, they do not directly provide dense credit: a score measures the quality of the current output, while credit should measure how the current turn changes the refinement trajectory. We propose TCPO, a turn-level credit assignment method for verifier-guided multi-turn RL. TCPO casts credit assignment as score-to-credit conversion and constructs turn-level advantages through reference-based comparisons: retrospective credit captures immediate progress and regression relative to the best prior state; hindsight delayed credit identifies non-improving turns with later payoff; and selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation refines high-surprisal turns under the same history. Experiments on math reasoning, code generation, and AppWorld agent tasks show that TCPO improves or matches the strongest baselines across model scales, task domains, and verifier types. TCPO achieves the best or tied-best best-turn Pass@8 on Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, reduces turns to success, and improves multi-turn agent performance. These results highlight score-to-credit conversion as a central ingredient for verifier-guided multi-turn policy optimization.

</details>


### [172] [When Memory Becomes Authority: Benchmarking Authority Collapse at the Memory Consolidation Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01679)
*Qiuyang Zhan,Rui Zhang,Sheng Guo,Lepeng Zhao,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Consolidation often strips source authority, causing agents to overstep permissions; AuthMem-Bench quantifies this, and automatic authority labeling effectively prevents unauthorized reuse without harming task performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Memory consolidation in LLM agents can erase or alter source constraints, producing "authority collapse" where stored facts imply greater permission than their origin allows. The paper aims to quantify this risk and test mitigations.

Method: Introduce AuthMem-Bench, a controlled paired benchmark that fixes the focal claim and downstream task while varying only source authority; evaluate write-time collapse, downstream authorization errors, and automatic authority preservation across seven consolidators and seven LLM backbones; run action-grounded and end-to-end evaluations to measure unauthorized-action rates and effect of persisted authority labels.

Result: Authority collapse observed in 48 of 49 evaluated configurations. In action-grounded tests, collapsed memories without authority metadata produced a mean unauthorized-action rate of 50.3%. In end-to-end tests, automatically predicting and persisting authority labels reduced unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0% while preserving benign task success.

Conclusion: Memory-driven adaptation must preserve not just content but the authority under which content may be reused; provenance/authority metadata and policy-aware consolidation are necessary for safe adaptive agents.

Abstract: Persistent memory allows (self-evolving) LLM agents to adapt across tasks by consolidating heterogeneous interaction histories into reusable facts, preferences, observations, and rules. Yet consolidation also imposes an implicit authorization boundary: it determines whether stored information may later be consumed as a user fact, an attested observation, or a standing instruction. We identify authority collapse, in which consolidation preserves a claim while erasing the source constraints governing its authorized use, causing the stored memory to imply greater authority than its source permits. We introduce AuthMem-Bench, a controlled paired benchmark that holds the focal claim and downstream task fixed while varying only source authority. It evaluates write-time collapse, downstream authorization errors, and automatic authority preservation. Across seven consolidators based on widely used agent-memory systems and seven LLM backbones, we observe authority collapse in 48 of 49 evaluated configurations. In a controlled action-grounded evaluation, collapsed memories without authority metadata yield a mean unauthorized-action rate of 50.3%. In an end-to-end evaluation, automatically predicted and persisted authority labels reduce the observed unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0%, while benign task success remains essentially unchanged. These findings show that memory-driven adaptation must preserve not only what was
  learned, but also the authority under which it may be reused.

</details>


### [173] [GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01684)
*Jiarui Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,YaBo Guo,Jiawei Liu,Yujie Xing,Muhan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GABench是一个面向代理式图分析的可执行基准（10400个任务、84个工具），评测显示现有LLM代理在复杂图任务上仍不足，且工具调用质量与代理框架选择是关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图分析基准覆盖面不足，多为文本问答且图信息直接给出，难以评估端到端的代理式能力（包括数据访问、工具调用与多步执行），因此需要一个更全面、更可执行的基准来测量LLM代理在图环境中的实际能力。

Method: 作者提出GABench基准：涵盖三类图数据、四大图分析任务类别（图检索、图论问题、图机器学习与开放式图问答），并实现84个可执行工具以访问数据与执行操作；基于这些工具构建一个代理式任务生成流水线，自动生成并验证10400个带有真值的任务；随后用多种前沿LLM与代理框架在GABench上进行系统评测。

Result: 通过在GABench上的评估，作者发现：(1) 现有LLM代理在复杂图任务上仍有明显短板；(2) 代理框架的选择对结果影响显著，但现有框架在复杂任务上功能受限；(3) 提高工具调用的准确性和质量比单纯增加工具数量更能改善图分析效果。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：尽管大型语言模型（LLM）代理在多步规划和工具使用方面进步明显，但在复杂的图分析任务上仍表现欠佳；评估结果还表明代理框架（harness）选择会显著影响性能，而工具调用质量比工具数量对图分析更为关键。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of planning, using tools, and interacting with external environments. They are typically supported by harnesses, which manage state and coordinate multi-step execution. Graph analysis provides a promising setting for evaluating their agentic capabilities, because it requires agents to access data and execute operations in a graph environment. However, existing graph benchmarks for LLMs provide limited coverage of graph tasks and graph types, making it difficult to comprehensively evaluate LLM agents. Moreover, they typically formulate graph analysis as text-based question answering, where graph information is directly provided in the prompt, limiting the evaluation of end-to-end agentic capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce GABench, a comprehensive benchmark for agentic graph analysis. GABench spans three graph types and covers four graph analysis task categories: graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, and graph open-ended question answering. GABench also provides 84 executable tools for accessing graph data and performing diverse graph operations. Building on these tools, we develop an agentic graph analysis task generation pipeline and construct 10,400 tasks with verifiable ground truth.Using GABench, we evaluate a range of frontier LLMs and agent harnesses. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (1) Existing LLM agents still struggle with complex graph analysis tasks. (2) Harness choice significantly affects performance, yet existing harnesses remain limited on complex graph tasks. (3) Graph analysis depends more on tool-call quality than quantity. Our findings provide practical insights into the development and evaluation of LLM agents for graph analysis.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01710)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM 代理的重试/重规划会导致“语义重放”——新鲜令牌绕过执行预算；单靠令牌标识不够，必须在发行者侧维护单调持久的授权状态；CapLease 提供了基于事务与绑定确认的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 代理被设计为可重规划、重试和委托，单次授权语义（比如“只允许扣款一次”）容易被代理行为打破。现有以令牌标识为中心的防重放措施忽视了代理会反复请求新令牌的问题；因此需要一种能在发行者侧保证授权消费语义不会被重复使用的机制。

Method: （1）定义“语义重放”并证明标识本地令牌在没有持久单调状态的情况下无法阻止新鲜重颁发；（2）提出 CapLease 架构：绑定用户确认到规范动作，并实现 Issue-Prepare-Commit 的事务模型及提案/权限级防御；（3）在多种代理行为场景（重规划、重试、委托并发、确认重放、崩溃恢复）下对比标识本地令牌、CapLease 与 Server Ledger 的行为，通过模拟/实现展示 CapLease 阻止重复准入并在幂等接收端下防止重复外部效果。

Result: 该论文提出并分析了“语义重放”（semantic replay）问题：在大型语言模型（LLM）代理执行中，重规划、重试、任务委托和崩溃恢复会导致对同一授权操作多次请求并以新令牌标识执行，从而突破单次授权的执行预算。作者证明，单靠标识本地（identifier-local）令牌无法阻止“新鲜重颁发”，除非授权方对被授权动作、确认事件和剩余执行预算保有单调且持久的状态。为此，论文提出了CapLease——一个授权消耗层，结合提案级与权限级防御，将经认证的用户确认绑定到规范化动作，并按事务性 Issue-Prepare-Commit 过渡来强制执行。作者在多种 LLM 代理场景下对比表明：标识本地令牌允许新鲜重颁发，而 CapLease 与同样有状态的 Server Ledger 能阻止重复准入，并在与幂等接收端结合时阻止重复外部副作用。总体结论是：对重放抗性的系统需求不是令牌的表示形式，而是持久的授权状态。

Conclusion: 论文明确指出并形式化了一个被忽视的安全/语义问题：在代理生态中，重复请求可能耗尽单次授权的语义预算。解决办法不是更复杂的令牌格式，而是发行者保留单调持久的授权消费状态。CapLease（或等价的 Server Ledger）通过将用户确认与规范动作绑定并采用 Issue-Prepare-Commit 事务，能在实践场景下阻止重复准入与重复外部效应（当接收端为幂等时）。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model agents frequently replan, retry failed operations, delegate tasks, and resume after crashes. These behaviors can cause one user authorization to be requested and executed multiple times under freshly issued token identifiers, even when each individual token is single-use. We call this failure semantic replay: exceeding the execution budget of a token-independent authorization instance rather than merely reusing an old token identifier. We show that identifier-local token consumption cannot prevent fresh reissuance unless the issuer retains monotonic durable state over the authorized action, confirmation event, and remaining execution budget. We introduce CapLease, an authorization-consumption layer that follows proposal- and authority-level defenses, binds an authenticated user confirmation to a canonical action, and enforces transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit transitions. Across LLM-agent replanning, retry, delegation, concurrency, confirmation-replay, and crash-recovery scenarios, identifier-local tokens permit fresh semantic reissuance, whereas CapLease and an equally stateful Server Ledger prevent duplicate admission and, with an idempotent sink, duplicate external effects. Our results identify durable authorization state, rather than token representation alone, as the systems requirement for replay-resistant agent execution.

</details>


### [175] [Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01711)
*Lingbo Li,Anuradha Mathrani,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EAKR formalizes the knowledge needed to turn evidence into executable meta-analyses; MetaSynDec uses LLMs plus deterministic validators to build and execute EAKRs, showing high fidelity and better performance than direct LLM outputs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meta-analysis requires explicit analytical knowledge beyond structured evidence; current automated methods hide critical decisions inside code or outputs, hindering verification.

Method: Define EAKR to capture evidence, relations, inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues; implement MetaSynDec where LLMs propose updates and deterministic services validate and execute schema/contract-checked EAKRs; evaluate across 58 synthesis units comparing to references and direct LLM generation.

Result: Introduced EAKR and MetaSynDec; successful construction of EAKRs for 58 units with high fidelity and agreement metrics; outperforming direct LLM generation.

Conclusion: EAKR enables validation, traceability, execution, and improved methodological agreement; MetaSynDec demonstrates feasibility of this approach.

Abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [176] [LaCache: Robust Semantic Caching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01718)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaCache通过对查询的首k个预测token也进行缓存命中检查，提供形式化的抗缓存碰撞保证并带来更相关、效率更高的语义缓存检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存通过重用相近请求的回答降低延迟与成本，但受到缓存碰撞攻击（攻击者注入精心设计查询污染缓存，破坏后续合法请求响应）威胁。LaCache旨在通过增加不可完全被攻击者控制的响应侧约束来根本性防御该类攻击。

Method: 对传统基于向量相似度的语义缓存进行改造：在查询级别之外，额外对查询的首k个（推测）解码token进行缓存命中检查与索引匹配；利用这些token约束，推导并证明对抗样本无法同时满足恶意输出与与合法请求发生碰撞的条件。并在实现中对不同k值与模型做效率与准确性评估。

Result: 提出LaCache，一种对抗缓存碰撞攻击的语义缓存方案，通过检查查询的前k个预测解码token的缓存命中来增强检索索引与安全性。证明了无法构造同时引发恶意响应并与合法查询碰撞的对抗输入。实验证明在多种LLM与基准上提高了安全性与效率，并提升了检索相关性。

Conclusion: LaCache在理论上阻断了基于语义缓存的碰撞污染攻击，同时通过扩展索引（首k个token）改善检索相关性。实证结果表明该设计在多模型和基准上兼顾安全性与性能。

Abstract: Semantic caching, which reuses responses to semantically similar requests via their embeddings, has seen growing adoption in LLM serving, offering faster responses and reduced costs. Yet existing schemes are fundamentally vulnerable to cache-collision attacks, wherein an adversary pollutes the cache by injecting crafted queries, corrupting responses to subsequent legitimate requests. We present LaCache, a novel semantic caching scheme that addresses this vulnerability through a conceptually simple yet principled redesign. The key insight is that while the adversary has full control over the adversarial query, it has far less control over its response, which must simultaneously satisfy multiple semantic constraints. Rather than checking only the cache hit of a query, LaCache additionally checks the cache hit of its first k (speculatively) decoded tokens. This design yields two concrete benefits. First, it provides formally guaranteed resilience against cache-collision attacks: we prove that it is impossible to craft adversarial queries that simultaneously elicit malicious responses and collide with benign queries. Second, the enriched index supplies additional semantic context for cache retrieval, improving response relevance. Empirical evaluation across diverse LLMs and benchmarks validates both LaCache's security guarantees and efficiency gains, pointing to a promising direction for robust semantic caching.

</details>


### [177] [DAPD: Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01735)
*Jianyu Wu,Yizhou Wang,Encheng Su,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPSD causes privilege illusion due to information asymmetry; DAPD introduces two-level anchoring (DPA and DSA) to match information between reference and rollout, significantly improving downstream performance and mitigating privilege illusion across model scales.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD uses privileged teacher info in post-training but causes 'privilege illusion' where student behaves as if privileged info remains at inference, degrading performance. The paper aims to identify root cause and resolve it.

Method: They create a self-conditioned bridge (DPA) to align behaviors on matched-information paths and apply anchoring both reference->rollout and rollout->reference (DSA) to reduce reliance on privileged guidance while keeping correct supervision. Extensive experiments validate benefits across scales.

Result: They propose Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD) with Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) and Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA). DAPD aligns reference and rollout behaviors along matched-information paths and applies anchoring in both directions, reducing privilege reliance and improving performance.

Conclusion: DAPD effectively removes information asymmetry, prevents transfer of privilege-dependent behavior, and achieves consistent gains over OPSD across tasks and model sizes.

Abstract: On-policy (self) distillation (OPSD) is increasingly adopted for language-model post-training. It strengthens the teacher with privileged information but can induce a privilege illusion: the student learns privilege-dependent behavior it cannot reproduce from its inference-time context, yet behaves as if the training-time privileged information remained available, ultimately degrading performance. In this paper, we identify information asymmetry between the privileged teacher and the student at inference as the root cause of this failure in OPSD. To resolve this asymmetry, we propose Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD), a unified framework with two levels of anchoring. Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) introduces a self-conditioned bridge and aligns reference and rollout behavior along two matched-information paths, preventing privilege-dependent behavior from being transferred to the inference-time student. Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA) applies these paths in both reference-to-rollout and rollout-to-reference directions, reducing reliance on privileged reference guidance while preserving correctness supervision. Extensive experiments show that DAPD significantly alleviates privilege illusion, outperforming OPSD on Qwen3-4B by +2.00 points on average across tasks. Notably, its gains persist across scales, reaching +2.69 at 4B and +2.78 at 32B.

</details>


### [178] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoEvo-Mem，一种闭环框架联合演化检索策略与记忆库：冻结LLM生成路线化查询重写与路由先验，轻量残差路由器在线修正；检索上下文作为耦合界面，任务结果为路由决策赋信号，轨迹条件反馈更新记忆值与图关系，并通过交替固定一方更新来缓解非平稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常单独优化记忆访问（如迭代查询重写或自适应检索）或记忆演化（如结构化更新），忽视了检索决定哪些记忆被使用并因此接收使用信号，而记忆更新又会反过来改变后续检索——两者存在闭环反馈。

Method: 核心是两部分：1) 检索策略：冻结LLM生成route-specific query rewrites和routing prior，轻量残差路由器在在线阶段修正并选择记忆；2) 记忆演化：基于检索得到的上下文和任务轨迹的反馈更新记忆条目和值以及记忆间的图关系；交替训练（固定记忆更新路由，固定路由演化记忆）以控制耦合带来的非平稳。检索上下文作为耦合接口，任务结果为路由决策赋予信用，轨迹反馈用于记忆更新。

Result: 在七个不同基准上CoEvo-Mem取得SOTA性能，表明联合演化检索与记忆比分离优化更有效。

Conclusion: CoEvo-Mem通过检索-记忆的协同演化显著提升长期LLM代理在多任务/多会话场景的表现，在七个基准上达到了SOTA，证明了闭环设计的重要性。

Abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations. These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed. Across seven diverse benchmarks, \textbf{CoEvo-Mem} achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the importance of retrieval-memory coevolution.

</details>


### [179] [MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01742)
*YuFei Luo,Xiucheng Xu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemSIF mitigates two misalignment patterns in long-horizon LLM memory (TSM and DUM) via Structured Interaction Memory (Topical Segments + Event Trajectories) and Dual-Track Fact Memory (CoreFact + ActiveFact), improving retrieval/QA accuracy across benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing long-term memory systems suffer from Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) — temporal proximity not matching topical/event relatedness — and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM) — write-time salience not predicting future utility. MemSIF aims to mitigate these by structuring interactions and separating stable facts from active, demand-driven facts.

Method: MemSIF builds a two-part memory: Structured Interaction Memory that segments raw interactions into Topical Segments and stitches Event Trajectories; and Dual-Track Fact Memory with CoreFact (schema-guided, consolidated at write time) and ActiveFact (on-demand, promoted by multi-source support and recurring queries). The system routes writes into topical/event structures and extracts/upgrades facts based on recurrence and support.

Result: On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs, MemSIF achieves highest Total ACC in all settings, surpassing the best baseline by 2.29%–8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%–6.15% on LongMemEval-S. Code is released.

Conclusion: Combining structured interaction representations with a dual-track fact memory addresses temporal/topical misalignment and delayed utility, yielding consistent accuracy gains; further analysis of scalability, ablations, and real-world deployment constraints is warranted.

Abstract: Long-term memory is critical for LLM agents operating over long-horizon interactions. However, several persistent limitations of existing memory systems can be traced to two recurring misalignment patterns in long-term interaction settings: Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM). TSM arises when temporal proximity does not reliably align with topical or event-level relatedness, whereas DUM arises when write-time salience does not reliably predict future query utility. To mitigate these misalignment patterns, we propose MemSIF (Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts), a structured interaction-to-fact memory framework. Structured Interaction Memory organizes raw interactions into Topical Segments that preserve local topical coherence and Event Trajectories that maintain cross-time event continuity. Dual-Track Fact Memory uses two complementary tracks: CoreFact memory consolidates stable, schema-guided information at write time, whereas ActiveFact memory forms facts on demand and promotes those supported by multiple historical sources and recurring query demand for reuse. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs show that MemSIF achieves the highest Total ACC in all settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.29%-8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%-6.15% on LongMemEval-S. These results support the effectiveness of combining Structured Interaction Memory with Dual-Track Fact Memory to mitigate TSM and DUM. Code is available at https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF.

</details>


### [180] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RL-Lock：首个基于强化学习的互锁装配生成方法，结合动作分块与基于MCTS的策略-价值学习，在体素表示下将形状分解为互锁部件，有效解决大组合搜索空间问题，并在困难实例上优于传统启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did.

Method: 将生成互锁件问题视为序贯决策任务，采用结构化动作分块（action chunking）减少动作空间，并结合蒙特卡洛树搜索引导的策略-价值学习（MCTS-guided policy-value learning）来训练代理在体素网格上逐像素/逐体素决定分配，实现高效搜索。

Result: RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. Experiments show RL-Lock effectively generates interlocking assemblies, particularly for challenging cases where existing methods are slow or fail to find valid solutions.

Conclusion: RL-Lock enables efficient generation of interlocking assemblies without handcrafted heuristics, improving success rate and speed on difficult instances compared to prior approaches.

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [181] [Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for Verifiable Reasoning in Autonomous Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01755)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Kaixuan Wang,Guli Zhang,Hongyan Xie,Yakun Zhu,Hao Geng,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hide GT trajectories during CoT supervision and recast planning as multiple-choice plus deferred verification to reduce anchoring bias and improve verifiable reasoning in VLA models for driving.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current VLA teacher supervision often conditions on logged ground-truth trajectories, allowing teachers to rationalize outcomes rather than infer decisions from scene evidence, creating a shortcut that harms causal reasoning and increases hallucination in challenging scenes.

Method: Introduce AD-MCQ to transform planning into selection among explicit trajectory candidates; propose DEFT-RLVR to keep future trajectories hidden during CoT generation and use them later for verification/learning, thus separating high-level decision reasoning from low-level trajectory synthesis.

Result: Empirical evidence that removing GT exposure reduces anchoring bias; DEFT-RLVR improves autonomous-driving reasoning and reduces hallucinations, while AD-MCQ enables verifiable, controllable, VLM-only inference and scalable candidate-based evaluation.

Conclusion: Exposing teacher models to ground-truth future trajectories creates trajectory anchoring bias, degrading causal faithfulness and increasing hallucinations in chain-of-thought reasoning for autonomous driving. Casting planning as multiple-choice among candidate trajectories (AD-MCQ) and deferring exposure of GT trajectories to post-decision verification (DEFT-RLVR) mitigates the bias and improves reasoning while preserving visual capabilities.

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD) increasingly utilize chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision to enhance the reasoning capabilities of their Vision-Language Model (VLM) components, yet existing annotation pipelines commonly expose the teacher model to the logged ground-truth (GT) future trajectory. We empirically show that this induces trajectory anchoring bias: teacher models rationalize the revealed outcome rather than infer a decision from scene evidence, producing less causally faithful CoTs and substantially more severe hallucinations, especially in causally challenging scenes. Removing the GT trajectory eliminates this shortcut, but open-ended trajectory generation entangles high-level decision-making with precise geometric synthesis and low-level dynamics. To make trajectory-level driving decisions verifiable without requiring open-ended trajectory synthesis, we introduce Autonomous-Driving Multiple-Choice Question (AD-MCQ), which casts planning as selection among explicit trajectory candidates. Taking this a step further, we propose Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for RLVR (DEFT-RLVR) to transform future trajectories from pre-decision anchors into post-decision verification targets. Experimental results show that DEFT-RLVR improves AD reasoning while preserving or even enhancing general visual capabilities. With VLM-only inference and controllable difficulty through candidate construction, AD-MCQ provides a flexible, scalable, and extensible foundation for future research on verifiable AD reasoning.

</details>


### [182] [Leveraging AI for fine-grained food safety risk forecasting in sparse data conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01767)
*Dongqi Wang,Weiwei Chen,Han Zhou,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用11M+检验记录与统计年鉴指标，使用Wilson区间做部分监督并通过三阶段预训练和半监督精化训练Transformer模型，实现城市级食品安全风险预测与实际监管增效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管资源稀缺且区域样本不平衡，需构建能利用历史数据与置信度信息的模型，以在更细粒度和更早阶段识别食品安全风险，从而提升检查效率与公共卫生响应能力。

Method: 数据整合：11M+检验记录 + 统计年鉴人口经济环境特征；标签与不确定性：用Wilson区间计算置信与排名作为部分监督信号；训练流程：三阶段预训练（详述应包含无监督/有监督/半监督精化阶段）；模型：Transformer架构，输出城市级风险分数；评估：2022年数据离线对比基线并在浙江省监管机构协作下开展实地A/B试验。

Result: 提出了基于Transformer的三阶段预训练框架，结合Wilson区间和半监督标签精化，在城市级细粒度上对食品安全风险进行预测，并在真实监管场景中验证了提升检测率与巡检效率。

Conclusion: 严谨地将大规模公共检测数据、Wilson区间置信建模与先进深度学习相结合，能在样本稀缺情况下实现更早、更细粒度的风险识别，并在实地试验中证明了对监管分配效率的实质性提升。

Abstract: Ensuring food safety represents a critical public health challenge, particularly when inspection resources are limited and regional sampling data are sparse. This study proposes a Transformer-based framework capable of forecasting fine-grained, city-level food safety risks by unifying over 11 million inspection records with supplemental demographic, economic, and environmental indicators extracted from the Statistical Yearbook. A three-stage pretraining design leverages partial supervision from the Wilson interval (capturing both safety and risk rankings), together with semi-supervised label refinement, to effectively utilize historical records even when local sample sizes are insufficient. Experimental evaluations on data from 2022 show that the proposed approach outperforms baselines significantly. A subsequent field experiment in collaboration with the Zhejiang Provincial Administration for Market Regulation further demonstrates improved detection rates and more efficient allocation of inspection resources compared to a manually developed plan. Observations of regulatory decision-making reveal a threshold-based heuristic employed by inspectors, hinting that additional training or decision-support interfaces could further enhance the impact of AI-generated risk scores. Overall, these findings underscore that a rigorous integration of large-scale public inspection data, Wilson interval-based confidence modeling, and advanced deep learning can facilitate earlier and more granular identification of food safety threats. By reducing reliance on reactive measures alone, the proposed framework has the potential to advance proactive, data-driven oversight of the global food supply.

</details>


### [183] [FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01772)
*Xuhui Wang,Ruoqi Shu,Chen Dan,Tianhua Xu,Mengxi Luo,Yanming Mai,Bo Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FRAMES 是一个保留审计性的闭环进化框架，通过一致性变异与帕累托选择在不提升推理成本的前提下改进规则化 LLM 代理的准确性，并提供反回归保证，实验证明其在生产与基准测试上均有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级 LLM 代理在执行规则驱动的工作流时需要一致性、可溯源的数值归属与可审计性，但现实中反馈稀疏、无标签、规则改动会导致回归且不能增加推理成本，故需一种可自我演化且满足工程约束的方案。
