Method: 从已有资产冷启动技能；通过一致性（consensus）驱动的变异生成候选；使用在准确性与推理成本上的帕累托选择进行筛选；加入反回归（anti-regression）保证以防新改动破坏既有用例；全流程保留审计记录以保持可追溯性。

Result: 在内部生产系统中，FRAMES 在准确性-成本权衡上优于基线方法，该成果在公开的 tau-bench 上得到了复现，表明方法具有一定的泛化性和实用价值。

Conclusion: FRAMES 提供了一种可部署、可审计且自我进化的闭环框架，能在不增加推理成本或牺牲可审计性的前提下提升企业级规则化工作流（如文档审计）的准确性，并在内部系统与 tau-bench 上展示了最优的准确率-成本折衷。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly run policy-bound enterprise workflows such as document auditing, where they must apply rules consistently, ground every value, and stay auditable. Improving these agents is hard: operational feedback is sparse and unlabeled, edits to one rule can regress unrelated cases, and accuracy must improve without inflating inference cost or losing auditability. We present FRAMES, a closed-loop framework that cold-starts deployable skills from existing assets and then evolves them through consensus-based mutation, Pareto selection over accuracy and cost, and an anti-regression guarantee, all while preserving auditability. Deployed on our internal production system, FRAMES attains the best accuracy-cost trade-off among baselines, with the same gains reproduced on tau-bench.

</details>


### [184] [REFLEX: Rethinking MoE Inference as Refinement-Aware Compute Allocation in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01784)
*Xiang Xia,Cheng Yan,Yiming Zhang,Jiazheng Liu,Hongyu Zhang,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 DLM 中“不同 token 精炼需求不均导致专家预算浪费”的问题，提出训练免方法 REFLEX：保持默认 router 不变，按块（block）层级分配专家预算并用 Frontier-Progress Score 决定活跃块优先级，从而节省约 15% 专家计算且保持或改善生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLM 在每次去噪步中同时访问所有 token，但不同 token 的精炼需求存在强烈异质性。默认固定的每 token 专家预算导致计算与实际精炼需求不匹配，浪费计算资源；因此需要一种面向精炼状态的、动态的专家计算分配策略。

Method: 保持原有 token-choice router 不变；将序列拆分为块并建立粗→细的专家预算层级；在每次去噪前向，用 Frontier-Progress Score 评估各块的精炼进度并据此动态分配专家预算；不需要额外训练或路由参数更新。

Result: REFLEX 是一种面向扩散语言模型（DLM）的训练免路由改写方法，通过“粗到细”的专家预算分配层级和 Frontier-Progress Score 动态优先级，按照每次去噪前向中不同 token 的精炼需求重新组织 MoE 专家计算。对 LLaDA-MoE 和 LLaDA2.0-mini 的多项基准测评显示：在保持或提升生成质量的前提下，平均减少约 15% 的专家计算；相比自回归式可变专家路由，REFLEX 提供更一致的质量—计算权衡。

Conclusion: 在 DLM 的去噪前向过程中，应把 MoE 推理看作“基于精炼状态的计算分配”问题。REFLEX 通过粗到细的块级预算分配和 Frontier-Progress Score，能在不改动训练路由的前提下更有效地匹配计算与精炼需求，达到减少计算且不损害生成质量的效果。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models increase parameter capacity by activating only a small subset of experts for each token. This conditional-computation paradigm has enabled autoregressive language models to scale model capacity without a proportional increase in per-token computation. In diffusion language models (DLMs), however, each denoising forward jointly revisits all token positions despite their sharply different refinement demands, while the default fixed token-choice routing assigns them a uniform expert budget, creating a mismatch between expert computation and refinement demand. We argue that MoE inference in DLMs should therefore be viewed as refinement-aware compute allocation across heterogeneous token refinement states. We propose REFLEX (\textbf{RE}finement-aware \textbf{FLEX}ible expert allocation), a training-free method that keeps the default router unchanged while reorganizing expert computation around the evolving refinement process. Specifically, REFLEX introduces a coarse-to-fine hierarchy for expert-budget allocation that aligns computation with block-relative refinement roles while using the Frontier-Progress Score to resolve active-block priorities. Across multiple widely used benchmarks on two representative MoE-based DLMs, LLaDA-MoE and LLaDA2.0-mini, REFLEX reduces allocated expert computation by 15\% on average while preserving or even improving generation quality on most benchmarks relative to default routing. Compared with autoregressive-style variable-expert routing methods, REFLEX also yields a more consistent quality--computation trade-off, further supporting the importance of allocating expert computation according to the heterogeneous refinement demands exposed within each denoising forward.

</details>


### [185] [Can You Trust the Confidence? ConfBench for Vision-Language Models on Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01792)
*Priyashree Roy,Sujitha Martin,Mohammad Rostami,Spencer Romo,Renhao Xue,Bob Strahan,Diego A. Socolinsky,Boyi Xie,Md Mofijul Islam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConfBench 提供了一个系统化、可控降质的 KIE 校准基准，验证了模态选择、模型能力与聚合方法对置信估计质量的影响，并引入 ECARB 来量化校准改进的实务价值，数据集已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业化 IDP 需要将模型抽取结果在自动化与人工审阅之间可靠地路由，这依赖于高可信的置信分数。现有文档基准多为高质量样本，低准确率区域样本稀少，难以评估与改进置信度校准，因此需要一个覆盖完整准确率谱、专注于置信度评估的基准。

Method: 对多样化文档集施加 20 种可控降质流水线，生成 1,346 个变体与 7 万+ 实体级评估样本；比较 4 个专有与 3 个开源 VLM，在口头化（verbalized）与对数概率置信估计两类方法、以及三种输入模态下进行评测；分析校准质量与模型能力、参数量、模态选择间的关系；评估不同 token 聚合策略的表现，并应用/测试后验校正策略；引入 ECARB 来量化审阅预算下的实用收益。

Result: 构建并公开 ConfBench；发现 OCR+Image 模态在置信估计上优于仅 OCR 或仅 Image；在同一产品线（如 Claude 系列）内，模型能力与置信质量呈单调关系；跨族比较时参数量与置信质量相关性弱；校准质量从近完美到严重过度自信不等；后验标定可调整绝对置信以满足阈值路由要求而不影响排序；log-prob 的首 token 聚合策略优于其他 token 级聚合策略；通过 ECARB 展示校准改进可转化为实际审阅成本节省。

Conclusion: ConfBench 是首个针对关键性信息抽取（KIE）置信度校准问题设计的基准，覆盖从高质量到严重降质的完整准确率谱，证明在 IDP 场景中：OCR+Image 输入模态能提供更可靠的置信估计，模型能力在同一模型族内是置信质量的主导因素，而跨族时参数量并非良好预测因子；模型间校准差别很大，可通过后验校正调整绝对阈值但不影响排序性指标；在基于对数概率的方法中，对首个 token 聚合优于平均 token 和 margin 聚合。作者同时提出了将判别性能转化为审阅成本收益的 ECARB 指标，并公开释出 ConfBench 数据集。

Abstract: Intelligent document processing (IDP) with vision-language models (VLMs) hinges on confidence scores trustworthy enough to route extractions between automation and human review. Existing document benchmarks are dominated by clean, high-quality samples, leaving low accuracy regions too sparse for calibration assessment. We introduce ConfBench, the first calibration-specific benchmark for key information extraction (KIE), built by applying 20 controlled degradation pipelines to a diverse document set, yielding 1,346 variants and 70K+ entity-level evaluations spanning the full accuracy spectrum. We evaluate four proprietary and three open-weight VLMs under verbalized and log-probability confidence estimation methods across three input modalities, and find: (i) OCR+Image modality results in more accurate confidence estimates; (ii) model capability is the dominant factor: within the Claude family confidence quality scales monotonically with capability, while across families parameter count is a poor predictor; (iii) calibration quality varies widely across models, from near-perfect to severely overconfident, and per-model post-hoc correction rescales these absolute confidence values for threshold-based routing without altering ranking-based operational metrics; and (iv) log-probability with first-token aggregation consistently outperforms mean-token and margin aggregations. We also introduce ECARB, a review-budget metric translating discriminative gains into operational savings. We release ConfBench publicly to enable systematic study of confidence estimators and calibration methods for trustworthy IDP application deployment.

</details>


### [186] [CoNav-UAV: Cooperative Dual-Altitude Aerial Navigation via Stackelberg Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01802)
*Junru Song,Wenhao Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuekai Qiu,Feifei Wang,Weien Zhou,Tingsong Jiang,Ying Wen,Yang Li,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoNav-UAV把高/低空UAV视作Stackelberg领导-跟随者对，通过记忆增强的语言-视觉推理与DAgger式运动控制交替优化，达到更高的目标定向空地导航性能并节省适配数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实任务（救援、巡检等）要求UAV既要全局搜寻定位目标，又要安全精确接近，两者在同一智能体中难以兼顾；高低空分工互补但需要协同适配，故引入Stackelberg博弈与交替学习来实现无特权、机载输入下的有效协作。

Method: 构建两层智能体：高空领导者负责全局视觉-语言推理，采用记忆库支持的in-context learning来改进高层决策；低空跟随者负责精细避障和接近策略，通过DAgger-style专家蒸馏进行控制策略更新。两者以迭代交替的方式训练，目标收敛到Stackelberg均衡。评测使用AerialVLN多场景基准，比对单/双智能体基线和不同VLM骨干。

Result: 提出CoNav-UAV：将高空“领导者”和低空“跟随者”建模为Stackelberg博弈，使用交替的Iterative Stackelberg Learning来分别更新领导者（基于记忆的in-context learning）和跟随者（DAgger样式的专家蒸馏），仅依赖机载视觉与语言输入。在AerialVLN三种城市场景上显著优于单/双智能体基线，在学习场景成功率提升至多30.8个百分点，跨场景提升9.0个百分点，并以约1/3的适配数据达成。进一步分析展示领导者与跟随者更新的互补性及不同VLM骨干的学习动力学差异。

Conclusion: 交替优化的Stackelberg策略能在不借助特权信息的前提下，使两架高度互补的UAV协作，在仿真城市场景里大幅改善目标导向VLN性能并提升跨场景泛化，表现稳定且对不同VLM骨干均有收益。

Abstract: Target-oriented vision-and-language navigation (VLN) on aerial platforms is attracting growing attention for missions such as disaster rescue, infrastructure inspection, and security patrol. In this task, an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) needs to locate targets given only a concise description of their appearance and surroundings. This requires global exploration and grounding as well as collision-free close-range approach, two interleaved processes difficult to reconcile within a single agent. Most existing methods transfer the ground VLN paradigm to a low-altitude UAV and compensate for its inefficient exploration with external assistance. A recent attempt deploys two UAVs at complementary altitudes yet still relies on privileged information and trains its two agents independently, precluding any mutual adaptation essential for cooperation. Here we propose CoNav-UAV, which explicitly models the task as a Stackelberg game between a high-altitude leader and a low-altitude follower, with the system operating on onboard visual and linguistic inputs alone. To solve this game, we introduce Iterative Stackelberg Learning. The leader's high-level vision-language reasoning is refined via memory-based in-context learning, while the follower's precise motion control is updated via DAgger-style expert distillation. The alternation drives both agents toward a Stackelberg equilibrium. CoNav-UAV consistently outperforms single- and dual-agent baselines across three high-fidelity urban scenes from the AerialVLN benchmark. Success rate improves by up to 30.8 points on the learning scene, and 9.0 points under cross-scene transfer while using about 3x less adaptation data. Further analyses validate the complementary gains of the leader and follower updates and reveal robust gains yet distinct learning dynamics across VLM backbones.

</details>


### [187] [CockpitHAT: Dependency-Graph-Driven Hierarchical Attribution for Embodied Multi-Agent Cockpits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01805)
*Wei Wang,Shuanghe Liu,Zhu Zhuo,Jiaqi Zhong,Xiaozhao Zhao,Xiaojie Zuo,Jie Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过依赖感知、多通道融合与风险校准的层级归因方法，显著提升实体化LLM多智能体系统的故障定位可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM多智能体系统中的 Correctness Collapse 问题：表面任务正确掩盖过程级失败，尤其在汽车驾驶舱等实体安全关键场景极其危险。现有的归因方法仅依赖文本轨迹，忽略依赖结构、多通道证据及安全感知评估。

Method: 用交互DAG构建依赖距离阈值替代传统位置窗口；设计实体适配器以并入对话、车辆状态、环境、记忆等多通道信息；在分析员一致性机制中按风险（ASIL）对高危失败进行安全上调并结合置信度加权；并构建并发布包含三专家共识ASIL标注的CockpitBench数据集来评估方法。

Result: 提出 CockpitHAT：一个层级归因框架，用交互DAG的依赖距离代替位置窗口，通过实体适配器集成多通道证据，并在置信度加权的分析员一致性中对高风险失败应用安全上调。发布 CockpitBench：212条带ISO 26262 ASIL等级标注的失败轨迹基准。CockpitHAT在公开Who&When基准上显著优于文本仅SOTA ECHO（agent-level/step-exact提升最多17.6/16.7点），并在CockpitBench上取得78.3% agent-level与38.2% step-exact准确率。

Conclusion: 依赖结构感知、跨通道证据融合及安全加权的一体化归因范式，可有效缓解现实场景中LLM多智能体的过程级错误隐蔽问题，为安全关键系统的故障诊断提供更可信的工具。

Abstract: LLM multi-agent systems suffer from Correctness Collapse, where high task-level accuracy conceals severe process-level failures. This is especially hazardous in safety-critical embodied settings such as automotive cockpits, where lexically correct utterances may trigger dangerous physical operations. Existing attribution methods rely on text traces alone, missing dependency structure, multi-channel evidence, and safety-aware evaluation. We introduce CockpitHAT, a hierarchical attribution framework that replaces positional windows with dependency-distance thresholds from interaction DAGs, integrates multi-channel evidence via an embodied adapter, and applies a safety-uplift to high-risk failures during confidence-weighted analyst consensus. We further release CockpitBench, a benchmark of 212 annotated failure traces spanning dialogue, vehicle-state, environmental, and memory channels, each labeled with ISO 26262 ASIL severity via three-expert consensus. On the public Who&When benchmark, CockpitHAT achieves agent-level / step-exact accuracies of 77.9% / 37.8% on the Hand-Crafted split and 86.5% / 46.0% on the Algorithm-Generated split, surpassing the text-only SOTA ECHO by up to 17.6 / 16.7 points. On CockpitBench, it attains 78.3% agent-level and 38.2% step-exact accuracy. These results establish dependency-aware, multi-channel, risk-calibrated attribution as an effective paradigm for reliable failure diagnosis in real-world embodied LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [188] [SearchMaster: Grounded and Regulated Self-Play for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01822)
*Wentao Tan,Qiong Cao,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchMaster通过证据链约束、基于深度的难度衡量与开文惩罚，实现在本地搜索环境中的受控自玩，从而高效生成用于训练LLM搜索代理的数据，显著提升检索型任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免现有自玩或数据生成方法依赖人工任务、专家演示或更强教师，并解决自生成任务与轨迹导致的伪多跳、难度估计偏差和开文过多但证据稀少等失败模式。

Method: 提出SearchMaster自玩训练框架：单模型在本地搜索环境中自生成任务、求解并验证。为解决自玩带来的误导信号，设计三种控制：证据链生成器（ECG）用于以跨文档证据链为基础生成真实多跳任务；搜索深度奖励（SDR）以成功回合的搜索深度而非成功率评估难度；过度翻阅惩罚（OOP）抑制过多打开文档的浅层浏览。通过Verified Proposer和Solver的联合优化（使用GRPO）生成训练数据并训练LLM搜索代理。

Result: 在六个深度搜索基准上，对Qwen3.5-9B骨干模型，平均准确率从38.19%提升到51.52%，在BrowseComp-Plus上提升30.1个百分点；展示了在无人工标注或专家示范下，仅靠受约束的自玩可获得高质量训练数据。

Conclusion: 有针对性的生成约束与工具使用正则化能缓解自玩生成的数据噪声，形成高效的无监督训练管线，使单一中等规模LLM能通过自生成数据显著提升深度检索能力。

Abstract: Training LLM-based search agents requires high-quality search data: tasks that demand genuine multi-hop retrieval and trajectories that use search tools effectively. Existing pipelines often depend on human-written tasks, expert demonstrations, or stronger teacher models. We present SearchMaster, a self-play framework that trains a single LLM from search tasks it generates, solves, and verifies in a local search environment. The key challenge is that self-generated tasks and rollouts can yield misleading signals: pseudo multi-hop questions, success-rate difficulty estimates that ignore search depth, and rollouts with excessive opening but little targeted evidence acquisition. SearchMaster addresses these failure modes with three controls. An Evidence-Chain Generator (ECG) grounds task generation in explicit cross-document evidence chains to reduce pseudo multi-hop questions. A Search-Depth Reward (SDR) scores task difficulty by the search depth of successful rollouts rather than success rate alone, keeping retained tasks search-intensive. An Over-Opening Penalty (OOP) regulates tool use by discouraging excessive document opening, avoiding long but shallow browsing. Verified Proposer and Solver rollouts are then jointly optimized with GRPO. Across six deep-search benchmarks, SearchMaster improves a Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 38.19% to 51.52% average accuracy, with a 30.1-point gain on BrowseComp-Plus. These results show that grounded and regulated self-play can provide effective search-agent training data without human-labeled QA pairs or expert demonstrations. The code is available at https://github.com/WentaoTan/SearchMaster.

</details>


### [189] [Rewriting or Reweighting? A Geometric Account in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01835)
*Juntong Wang,Shengkun Yang,Xiyuan Wang,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过‘行为流形分析’在激活与贡献空间构建低维图表，研究后训练如何改变模型机制：SFT倾向于重写行为几何，奖励优化主要重加权已存在几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的聚合行为率无法判断后训练是移除旧机制、创建新机制还是改变已有机制的使用方式；需要一个机制层面的几何视角来区分这些情形并理解不同后训练目标（监督微调 vs 奖励优化）对底层机制的影响。

Method: 提出行为流形分析（behavioral manifold analysis），通过选取稀疏的与目标行为相关的坐标并将其提升到低维局部图表来分离行为专属的几何结构；在两个互补空间构建图表：激活空间（ACT）捕获运行时激活态，贡献/路由强度空间（NOC）量化信息流通过共享行为子空间的强度；在多种模型族上对比和对齐这些低维图表，并在受控的后训练（SFT 与奖励优化）过程中追踪图表变化。

Result: 构建的局部图表高度压缩并在架构间部分可对齐；贡献空间图表比激活空间更鲁棒且暴露了更具共享性的核心结构，而激活图表保留更多家族特有结构；在受控后训练实验中发现一致的不对称：监督微调（SFT）显著重写继承的行为几何，而奖励优化主要通过重新加权保留底层图表结构。

Conclusion: 该几何视角为理解不同后训练目标对机制层面的差异提供了统一框架——SFT 改变机制实现（rewrite），奖励优化则更多地调整已有机制的利用（reweight）；贡献空间与激活空间的互补性也提示了在跨架构分析与机制对齐时应侧重不同表示。

Abstract: Post-training can substantially alter language-model behavior, yet aggregate behavior rates do not reveal whether training removes an existing mechanism, creates a new one, or changes how an inherited mechanism is used. We study this question through two mechanistically distinct failures, repetition as a decoding-attractor pathology and sycophancy as a preference-related alignment failure. We introduce behavioral manifold analysis, which isolates behavior-specific geometry by selecting sparse behavior-associated coordinates and lifting them into low-dimensional local charts. We construct these charts in two complementary spaces. ACT captures runtime activation states, while NOC quantifies how strongly the model routes functional information flow through the shared behavior-associated subspace. Across multiple model families, the resulting charts are highly compressed and partially alignable across architectures. Contribution-space charts expose a more architecture-robust shared core, whereas activation-space charts retain stronger family-specific structure. Tracking these charts through controlled post-training reveals a consistent asymmetry. Supervised fine-tuning substantially alters the inherited behavioral geometry, whereas reward optimization changes behavior while largely preserving the underlying chart. This geometric perspective provides a unified framework for understanding the mechanistic distinction between the two objectives. SFT tends to rewrite behavioral geometry, whereas reward optimization primarily reweights it. Code is available at https://github.com/ronglingze/Manifold-Analysis

</details>


### [190] [PCSD: Persistent Consistency for Self-Distillation in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01837)
*Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Junlin Liu,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Xinming Wang,Jinyang Wu,Guoren Wang,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于教师信号局部持久性的PCSD，为每个token生成平滑鲁棒的蒸馏权重，联合GRPO训练，在稀疏奖励交互任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长多回合轨迹中稀疏的结果级奖励使RL信号稀薄；虽然OPSD提供逐token的密集监督，但教师在每个位置上并非总是可靠。现有方法要么依赖易受噪声影响的孤立token差异，要么对步级共享权重忽略位置变化，因此需要一种既能捕获位置差异又能抑制噪声的权重机制。

Method: 针对教师在不同位置可靠性不同的问题，PCSD从“教师偏好信号的局部持久性”出发构建权重：用自适应窗口收集局部信号、采用指数衰减聚合强调近因、引入趋势感知调制以抑制局部下降的支持，并用sigmoid门控输出连续的token级权重；最终将该密集蒸馏目标与GRPO的稀疏环境回报联合优化。

Result: PCSD在ALFWorld上取得所有基线中最佳的Overall结果（两个backbone均如此），分别比GRPO高15.6和13.3点，比SDAR高6.2和5.5点；在WebShop上表现仍具竞争力；在ALFWorld未见拆分上比GRPO提升15.8点。

Conclusion: PCSD通过基于教师信号的局部持久性来生成平滑连续的token级蒸馏权重，从而在多回合稀疏奖励环境中稳定地结合教师监督与环境回报，显著提升ALFWorld任务的性能，并在未见环境上有较大增益。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong potential in complex interactive tasks, yet their reinforcement learning (RL) is often hindered by sparse rewards, as a long multi-turn trajectory may receive only a single outcome-level signal. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher, but the teacher may not be reliable at every position. Existing methods commonly rely on isolated token-level discrepancies, which can be sensitive to noise, or assign a shared step-level weight that may overlook positional variation. We propose Persistent Consistency Self-Distillation (PCSD), which derives token-level distillation weights from the local persistence of teacher-favoring signals. PCSD combines adaptive windows with exponentially decayed aggregation to capture persistent relative teacher support, applies trend-aware modulation to attenuate locally declining support, and produces continuous weights through sigmoid gating. The resulting objective is jointly optimized with GRPO, combining dense teacher guidance with sparse environmental feedback. Without inference-time skills, PCSD achieves the best ALFWorld Overall results among all baselines on both backbones, exceeding GRPO by 15.6 and 13.3 points and SDAR by 6.2 and 5.5 points, while remaining competitive on WebShop and gaining 15.8 points over GRPO on unseen ALFWorld split.

</details>


### [191] [FOCUS: FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01847)
*Xianglong Yan,Hong Liu,Chengzhu Bao,Tianao Zhang,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FOCUS 通过可学习的全精度系数放松量化/反量化尺度耦合，并在子块级别细化量化尺度，实现了在 MXFP4/NVFP4 下的 SOTA FP4 后训练量化精度，且无额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练语言模型虽然性能出色，但模型体量巨大导致部署成本高昂。FP4 量化（如 MXFP4、NVFP4）因现代加速器的原生硬件支持而成为吸引人的方案，但在低比特精度下保持精度仍然困难。现有方法将量化（quantization）和反量化（dequantization）尺度紧耦合，受限于硬件需要的离散低精度格式（例如 MXFP4 的 E8M0），而量化尺度本身并不需要存储因此不必服从该离散限制，这意味着存在显著的可优化空间。

Method: 在后训练量化流程中进行端到端尺度学习：1) Coupled-Relaxation Scaling（CRS）：将原本必须一致或受限的量化/反量化尺度通过一个可学习的全精度乘子解耦，优化时更新该系数以最小化量化误差，同时保持反量化尺度满足硬件的离散格式要求（例如 E8M0）；2) Dual-Granularity Scaling（DGS）：在更细的子块（sub-block）粒度上为量化尺度赋予独立调整能力，以更精细地捕捉局部权重分布的差异；3) 端到端优化策略（后训练）：用小量校准数据/目标函数对尺度参数进行学习，不改变运行时数据流或引入新的算子，从而不增加推理开销。

Result: 提出 FOCUS：一个后训练量化框架，通过端到端尺度学习提升 FP4 量化精度。引入 Coupled-Relaxation Scaling（CRS）——用可学习的全精度系数放松量化/反量化尺度的紧耦合，兼顾更有效的优化与硬件合规；和 Dual-Granularity Scaling（DGS）——在更细的子块粒度上调整量化尺度以更精确地适配局部权重分布。实验证明 FOCUS 在多种 LLM 家族和基准上，在 MXFP4 和 NVFP4 格式下均取得了最先进的 FP4 精度，且不增加推理开销。代码与模型将开源。

Conclusion: 该工作识别并利用了“量化尺度不必受硬件离散格式约束”的关键机会，提出了两项互补技术（CRS、DGS），在保证硬件兼容的前提下显著提升 FP4 量化精度，具有较强的实用价值与可部署性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance but are expensive to deploy due to their enormous size. FP4 quantization, with formats such as MXFP4 and NVFP4, offers an appealing solution with native hardware support on modern accelerators. However, maintaining accuracy under FP4 precision remains difficult. A key bottleneck lies in scale optimization: existing methods tightly couple the quantization and dequantization scales, forcing both to conform to the discrete low-precision format required by hardware, such as E8M0 in MXFP4. Yet the quantization scale is never stored and need not obey this constraint, suggesting a significant untapped optimization space. In this work, we propose FOCUS, a post-training quantization framework with end-to-end scale learning for FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling. Coupled-Relaxation Scaling (CRS) relaxes the tight coupling between quantization and dequantization scales with a learnable full-precision coefficient, enabling more effective optimization without breaking hardware compliance. Dual-Granularity Scaling (DGS) further refines the quantization scale at a finer sub-block granularity, allowing more precise adaptation to local weight distributions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and benchmarks show that FOCUS achieves state-of-the-art FP4 accuracy under both MXFP4 and NVFP4 formats, while introducing no additional inference overhead. Code and quantized models will be released at https://github.com/tencent/AngelSlim.

</details>


### [192] [Exploring and Bridging Knowledge Holes in Unlearned Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01849)
*Junxiang You,Junkai Chen,Yuhao He,Ruiqi Liu,Zhetao Guo,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了针对MLLM去学习的知识空洞问题与基准，并用SPAR方法通过锚定激活过滤与实体抽象增强在确保忘却效果的同时显著恢复相邻良性输入的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLLM去学习评估通过与忘却集语义距离较远的基准衡量效用，无法揭示因去学习引发的对相似但良性的输入的严重性能退化（knowledge holes），从而高估去学习方法的安全与实用性。

Method: 作者一方面构建了一个新的基准来刻画对与忘却集共享通用模式的良性输入的非预期退化；另一方面提出Selective Protection with Anchored Regularization（SPAR），通过“锚定激活过滤（anchored activation filtering）”保护通用模式，同时用“实体抽象增强（entity-abstracted enhancement）”强化这些模式，以在去学习同时保留相邻良性样本的表现。

Result: 在SafeEraser数据集上的实验显示，SPAR在保持0.00%攻击成功率（attack success rate）的同时，将模型响应质量恢复到原始（vanilla）表现的98%+，而常规模型基线恢复率低于50%，并在模型效用上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文指出当前多模态大模型去学习（unlearning）评估存在盲点：以远离忘却集的基准评估效用，会忽略对与忘却集中存在通用模式相邻的良性输入造成的知识空洞（knowledge holes）。为此，作者提出并验证了一种针对性方法SPAR，并主张更细粒度的评估。

Abstract: Machine unlearning offers a promising approach to remove unsafe content from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), yet ensuring the precision of unlearning remains a persistent challenge. One reason is that current MLLM unlearning evaluation paradigms suffer from a critical blind spot: they assess model utility through benchmarks whose representations are distant from the forget set, failing to capture knowledge holes---severe degradation on benign adjacent inputs. To probe knowledge holes in unlearned MLLMs, we construct a benchmark that captures unintended degradation on benign inputs sharing generic patterns with the forget set, and confirm through controlled experiments that they are a systematic consequence of commonly used approaches. Furthermore, to bridge this gap, we propose Selective Protection with Anchored Regularization, which protects generic patterns via anchored activation filtering while reinforcing them through entity-abstracted enhancement. Our experiments on SafeEraser demonstrate that SPAR recovers over 98% of vanilla response quality compared to below 50% for standard baselines---while achieving 0.00% attack success rate and competitive model utility. These results underscore the necessity of more fine-grained evaluation for trustworthy MLLM unlearning.

</details>


### [193] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Complex Eigenfrequency Identification and Mode Structure Reconstruction of the Ground-State ITG Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01850)
*Dengdi Sun,Bingbing Zhang,Xiao Wang,Zikang Yan,Yuqiang Tao,Qingquan Yang,Guosheng Xu,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对高约束模式区的ITG不稳定性，作者用傅里叶编码+复值网络+三阶段训练的PINN在稀疏数据下联合识别复本征频率与二维复模场，效果优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高约束模式（H-mode）靶点的陡峭梯度区对约束与边缘传输至关重要，分析该区的ITG漂移波需同时识别复杂的本征频率与二维复模场，但局部高频、强实虚耦合与非线性耦合使得常规模型与PINN难以可靠求解，因而需要一种针对性工程化的PINN方法。

Method: 在PINN框架中引入傅里叶特征编码以增强高频表示能力，采用复值特征传播机制保留与传播场的实虚耦合信息，并设计三阶段训练策略（可能包括预训练、联合优化与精细调优）以改善非线性耦合下的收敛性；在损失中融合稀疏观测项与偏微分方程/特征方程约束，联合优化复本征频率与模场。

Result: 提出了一个物理约束神经网络框架，结合傅里叶特征编码、复值特征传播与三阶段训练，在稀疏观测与物理约束下联合求解ITG基态分支的复本征频率与二维复模场，实验显示能准确恢复目标并优于代表性PINN基线，且可扩展到高阶与多分支模式分析。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了局部高频振荡、实虚耦合和模场—本征频率非线性耦合带来的表示与优化困难，能在稀疏观测条件下准确重构复本征量和模场，具有推广至更高阶/多分支漂移波的潜力。但方法的稳健性、对观测噪声和物理模型误差的敏感性、以及与物理实验数据的验证仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) combine sparse observations with physical equations, providing an important approach for modeling complex plasma processes and inferring unknown physical quantities. The steep-gradient pedestal of high-confinement-mode tokamaks is closely linked to plasma confinement and edge transport. Analyzing ion-temperature-gradient (ITG) drift waves in this region requires jointly identifying complex eigenfrequencies and reconstructing two-dimensional complex-valued mode fields. Localized high-frequency oscillations, strong real-imaginary coupling, and nonlinear coupling between the mode field and eigenfrequency challenge PINN representation and joint optimization. To address these challenges, we propose a physics-informed neural framework combining Fourier feature encoding, complex-valued feature propagation, and three-stage training. Under sparse observations and physical constraints, it jointly solves for the complex eigenfrequency and mode field of a representative ground-state ITG branch. Experiments show that the framework accurately recovers the target complex eigenfrequency and two-dimensional complex-valued mode field and outperforms representative PINN baselines. It also provides a basis for analyzing higher-order and multiple-branch drift-wave modes.

</details>


### [194] [EchoChange: A Diffusion Language Model with Dual Pass Remasking for Factual Remote Sensing Disaster Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01856)
*Dongwei Sun,Bowen Yao,Yujie Zhang,Pei Liu,Jing Yao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EchoChange，一种基于离散扩散的多模态语言模型，把双时相遥感灾害变化描述视作掩码化标记的迭代去噪而非左到右自回归生成，从而能通过反复修订纠正早期错误，配合双通道草稿训练、进阶掩码课程和置信度引导的重掩码对齐训练与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感灾害变化描述需识别稀疏、局部变化并生成连贯事实文本；自回归解码早期错误不可逆，导致视觉不确定性的级联事实性错误，因而需要一种允许整句多次修正的生成范式。

Method: 将变化描述建模为离散标记空间的扩散过程：初始化为带掩码的草稿，反复进行Masked-token去噪更新（非自回归整句修订），并在训练上采用草稿感知的双通路训练、渐进掩码教学及基于模型置信度的重掩码策略，以缩小训练—推理差距。

Result: 在RSCC数据集上，EchoChange在若干词汇级和语义级评测指标上显著超过现有自回归和遥感专用方法，证明了迭代修正机制对减少事实错误和提高描述质量的有效性。

Conclusion: EchoChange通过迭代掩码去噪生成全句式候选并多轮修正，能显著降低自回归模型中早期误判导致的逐句放大错误，在RSCC基准上在词汇与语义指标上均显著优于通用与遥感专用基线。

Abstract: Bi-temporal remote-sensing disaster change captioning often needs to identify sparse and spatially localized changes across large pre- and post-event scenes and then translate them into coherent, factual descriptions. However, existing change captioning methods always follow an autoregressive decoding paradigm to generate the change description and thus an early misinterpretation of the changed object, event, or spatial relation becomes an irreversible premise for subsequent text, amplifying visual ambiguity into cascading factual errors. To address this limitation, we propose EchoChange, a multimodal discrete diffusion language model that formulates change captioning as iterative masked-token denoising rather than left-to-right generation. By repeatedly revising the entire caption while conditioning on the image pair, EchoChange can reconsider uncertain content and correct imperfect intermediate predictions. We further introduce draft-aware dual-pass training, a progressive masking curriculum, and confidence-guided remasking to align training with iterative inference. Extensive experiments on the RSCC benchmark show that EchoChange substantially outperforms both general-purpose and remote-sensing-specific baselines across lexical and semantic metrics. The EchoChange Project is at https://github.com/sundongwei/EchoChange_Project

</details>


### [195] [Wnuan: Staged Post-Training for Question Answering over Proprietary Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01862)
*Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Wenxin Ma,Qian Kou,Yiming Pan,Longbin Yu,Ying Liu,Haiping Wang,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过构建任务化监督、带通用数据回放的SFT与针对残差的RL，Wnuan在企业问答上获得显著增益，但以指令跟随等通用能力小幅下降为代价；残差采样在预算受限时表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业问答场景中，模型需要把专有文档知识吸收进去，同时尽量不丧失已有的一般能力。论文旨在提出一个可操作的阶段化适配流程，量化收益与通用能力的代价，并检验高效采样策略在有限更新预算下的效果。

Method: 三阶段：1) 从企业文档自动／半自动生成面向任务的监督数据（构建训练样本）；2) 对模型进行监督微调（SFT）时混入通用数据以回放原有能力；3) 识别残余错误并用强化学习（基于这些残差样本的奖励信号）对模型进行定向优化。评估：707题WnuanBench、100更新对照实验、引入源簇自举置信区间与同域验证、自动评估集合并与领域专家对齐验证。

Result: 提出Wnuan三阶段流水线：从文档构建任务导向监督、在SFT中以常规模型数据回放保持通用性、对剩余错误应用强化学习。32B主路线在707题WnuanBench上将可接受回答率（AAR）从52.76%提升到80.06%（SFT后），再到91.51%（RL后）。在100次更新限制下，残差错误采样比分池采样和等量随机采样分别高3.11和2.97个百分点；源簇自举的置信区间在两对比上均>0；同域验证集保留排序。与此同时，通用基准平均分下降5.17点，主要发生在指令跟随能力上；自动评估集成与领域专家在抽样响应上的一致率为90.5%。

Conclusion: 阶段化企业适配能显著提升领域问答质量且采样策略影响显著，但会引入可度量的通用能力损失，建议在工程上保留通用数据回放、使用同域验证并对RL策略与样本选择做更细致约束以缓解退化。

Abstract: Enterprise question answering requires models to acquire proprietary knowledge without discarding general capabilities. We present Wnuan, a three-stage pipeline that constructs task-oriented supervision from documents, performs supervised fine-tuning with general-data replay, and applies reinforcement learning to residual errors. On the 707-question WnuanBench, the primary 32B route raises acceptable-answer rate (AAR) from 52.76% before adaptation to 80.06% after SFT and 91.51% after RL. Under a matched 100-update protocol, residual-error sampling outperforms full-pool and size-matched random sampling by 3.11 and 2.97 points, respectively. Source-cluster bootstrap intervals remain above zero for both contrasts, and a same-domain validation set preserves the ordering. The general-benchmark average decreases by 5.17 points across the route, concentrated in instruction following. The automatic evaluation ensemble agrees with an authoritative domain expert on 90.5% of a stratified Wnuan-Inst response sample. These results characterize both the gains and the general-capability cost of staged enterprise adaptation.

</details>


### [196] [ReasonCast: Towards Explainable Time Series Forecasting with Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01875)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper proposes a task-fused model (ReasonCast) and benchmark (ReasonTS-Bench) enabling joint time-series forecasting and self-explanation; shows improved accuracy and interpretable reasoning but needs broader validation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing TS models separate generation (forecasting) and understanding (text answers). Aim to fuse both tasks so a single response contains prediction and interpretable justification.

Method: ReasonCast: a finetuning recipe that trains LLMs to output a reasoning chain and numeric forecast together in a single autoregressive pass. Introduces ReasonTS-Bench with five fundamental TS patterns for joint evaluation.

Result: ReasonCast outperforms baseline LLMs and specialized TS models on prediction accuracy while producing verifiable, causal reasoning; code released for reproducibility.

Conclusion: Task-fused model jointly produces accurate numeric forecasts and coherent self-explanations; demonstrates feasibility and improved performance versus separate models.

Abstract: Most time series (TS) models are specialized for a single task, either understanding (i.e., returning text answers about a TS) or generation (i.e., returning a numeric forecast). Only recently have unified models begun to handle the two within a single architecture. Even these models, however, produce the two outputs as task-separated paths and cannot predict a series and explain why that prediction arises within a single coherent response. In this paper, we argue for a task-fused model that jointly produces 1) prediction (generation) and 2) selfexplanation (understanding), thereby integrating 1) numerical TS forecasting and 2) interpretable text reasoning within a single response. To enable the systematic study of this capability, we present both a benchmark and a recipe that jointly address the two tasks. The benchmark, ReasonTS-Bench, identifies five fundamental patterns underlying TS and enables the joint evaluation of both tasks. ReasonCast, our recipe for finetuning any LLM to perform both tasks jointly, yields a model that generates a reasoning chain and a forecast together in a single autoregressive pass. Extensive experiments show that ReasonCast outperforms both LLMs and TS models on prediction accuracy while producing verifiable, causal reasoning. Code is available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/reasoncast.

</details>


### [197] [CoEvoKG: Co-Evolving Knowledge Graphs with Self-Evolving Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01904)
*Zhaoyang Li,Zenghuang Fu,Qiuyuan Ai,Ping Jiang,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Jie Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce a looped framework that turns a KG into task generator and persistent evidence memory; jointly train generator and agent; write verified evidence back into KG; yields notable QA performance gains across datasets and backbones.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing self-play and RL-based search agents discard evidence from successful searches and thus fail to accumulate useful, reusable knowledge across training iterations. A persistent, verifiable memory tied to a KG could both provide grounded tasks and retain evidence to improve future search and training.

Method: CoEvoKG jointly trains a task generator and a search agent. The generator samples entity chains from a knowledge graph to create multihop QA tasks. The agent receives rewards for answer correctness and for search trajectories whose entity paths are supported by graph evidence. Successful searches produce verified, deduplicated evidence that is written back into the KG nodes/edges, enriching the graph for future task generation and reward calculation.

Result: On six QA benchmarks and three backbone LLMs, CoEvoKG improved macro average accuracy significantly over base models (+11.2, +10.1, +11.6 points for the three backbones). Under matched budgets it outperformed self-play and RL baselines by +2.6 to +3.7 macro average points. Code is released.

Conclusion: CoEvoKG demonstrates that integrating a knowledge graph as both task source and persistent evidence memory yields substantial improvements for RL-trained search agents, enabling iterative self-evolution and cumulative knowledge accumulation.

Abstract: Large language models can improve with reinforcement learning for search agents, yet existing self play agents repeatedly generate tasks while discarding the knowledge gained during successful searches. We introduce CoEvoKG, a framework that turns a
  knowledge graph into both a source of verifiable training tasks and a persistent evidence memory for agent evolution. CoEvoKG jointly trains a task generator and a search agent: the generator creates multihop questions from entity chains sampled
  from the knowledge graph, while the agent learns from rewards for answer correctness and search trajectories whose entity paths are supported by graph evidence. When a search succeeds, CoEvoKG verifies and deduplicates the retrieved evidence, then
  writes it back to the corresponding graph nodes and edges. Future rounds reuse this enriched graph for task generation and reward computation, closing the loop between model self evolution and knowledge accumulation. Experiments on six QA benchmarks
  (NQ, TriviaQA, PopQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and Bamboogle) with three backbone models show that CoEvoKG improves macro average accuracy over the corresponding base models by +11.2, +10.1, and +11.6 points on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct,
  Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, respectively. Under matched training budgets, CoEvoKG further improves over competitive self play baselines and RL baselines for search agents by +2.6 to +3.7 macro average points across the three
  backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/lazzy1225/CoEvoKG.

</details>


### [198] [Diagnosing Search Behavior and Failure Modes in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01913)
*Qi Liu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过对长程搜索代理的轨迹级诊断，研究搜索努力与答案质量的关系，区分检索阶段与利用阶段，发现答案质量更依赖于检索到的证据质量而非搜索次数，提出改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尚不清楚增加搜索努力（更多查询或更长搜索过程）是否能带来更好答案；需要明确失败原因（未找到证据还是未正确利用证据）以指导可行改进。

Method: 使用人工标注的文档级相关性判断，对每一步检索得到的证据进行评估，将行为分为“检索到的证据”和“如何使用证据”两个阶段；在固定检索模型与评估框架下，对六种代理在BrowseComp-Plus上比较，并在Open-web的BrowseComp上验证；用轨迹级指标（如累计召回、查询数、消耗上下文）分析相关性与失败类型。

Result: 主要发现包括：搜索努力与答案质量相关性较弱；答案准确率与检索到的证据质量（尤其累计召回）相关更强；有用证据常出现在早期步骤，但代理倾向于继续搜索，产生低产出的长尾步骤；探索性改写仍有效，但高效代理发出更少冗余查询；建议加强查询生成、证据选择与上下文管理，并基于是否已检索到充分支持证据来停止搜索。

Conclusion: 长程搜索代理的性能主要受检索到的证据质量影响（尤其是累计检索召回），而非简单的搜索次数或上下文量；检索缺口与利用缺口是两种主要失败模式，减少冗余查询并改进证据选择与停止准则可显著提升表现。

Abstract: Deep search agents answer difficult information-seeking questions by iteratively issuing search queries to gather supporting evidence, but it remains unclear whether and how greater search effort leads to better answers. We study these questions through a trajectory-level diagnosis of long-horizon search agents. Using human-annotated document-level relevance judgments, we evaluate the evidence retrieved at each search step and separate two stages of agent behavior: what evidence an agent retrieves and how effectively it uses that evidence. This distinction further allows us to decompose failures into retrieval gaps, where the necessary evidence is never found, and utilization gaps, where relevant evidence is retrieved but not used correctly. With the retrieval model and evaluation harness held fixed, we compare six agents on BrowseComp-Plus and further validate our findings on BrowseComp with an open-web search API. Across settings, we find that search effort and answer quality are only weakly aligned. Answer accuracy is better correlated with the quality of retrieved evidence, especially cumulative retrieval recall, than with the number of searches or the amount of context consumed. Useful evidence often appears early in the trajectory, yet agents tend to continue searching, producing a long tail of low-yield retrieval steps. At the query level, exploratory reformulations remain useful, but the best-performing agents issue far fewer redundant queries. Overall, by systematically characterizing the search behavior and failure modes of long-horizon search agents, this work points to practical directions for building better deep research systems, including stronger query formulation, more effective evidence selection and context management, and stopping criteria based on whether sufficient supporting evidence has been retrieved.

</details>


### [199] [ProWorld: Progress-Aware Hyperbolic World Models for Long-Horizon Visual Goal Reaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01926)
*Zihan Liu,Yuzhe Zhuang,Yuanzu Li,Wanshuang Gou,Jiahong Liu,Min Zhou,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProWorld models goal-directed progress as an asymmetric coarse-to-fine order and embeds dynamics in hyperbolic space to preserve directional long-horizon progress and disambiguate futures, yielding substantial gains in visual goal-reaching benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Local next-step prediction in JEPA-style visual world models is insufficient for sustained long-horizon goal-directed behavior because multi-step rollouts can drift and locally similar states may differ in long-term progress; imposing an order reflecting progress toward a goal and leveraging hyperbolic geometry can better capture asymmetric, hierarchical progression.

Method: Define a goal-conditioned progress order over states (asymmetric, coarse-to-fine), embed latent dynamics in hyperbolic space to capture hierarchical entailment, train with hyperbolic entailment learning and hyperbolic future discrimination, and use a progress-aware planning objective combining goal proximity and sustained intermediate progress to score rollouts.

Result: On four visual goal-reaching tasks, ProWorld outperforms LeWM with an average absolute success-rate gain of 9.67 points; code release planned upon acceptance.

Conclusion: ProWorld introduces a progress-aware hyperbolic visual world model that imposes goal-conditioned relative ordering on latent states to maintain directional long-horizon progress and disambiguate locally similar futures, improving visual goal-reaching performance over baselines.

Abstract: JEPA-style visual world models offer an effective paradigm for visual goal planning by predicting future latent representations. Existing methods typically learn local transition consistency through next-step representation prediction. However, in long-horizon tasks, accurate local prediction alone need not ensure sustained progress toward the goal. First, multi-step rollouts can remain locally plausible while drifting away from goal-relevant trajectories. Second, locally similar future states can correspond to substantially different long-term progress, making them difficult to distinguish in a latent space optimized mainly for local consistency. To address these challenges, we introduce goal-conditioned progress order, a relative ordering of states according to how they advance toward a given goal. This order exhibits an asymmetric, coarse-to-fine structure: early states retain broader future possibilities, while later states concentrate on more specific goal-relevant regions. Such a structure is well suited to hyperbolic geometry. Motivated by this observation, we propose ProWorld, a progress-aware hyperbolic visual world model. ProWorld leverages goal-conditioned progress order to organize visual latent-space dynamics, maintains directional progress within trajectories via hyperbolic entailment learning, and mitigates progress ambiguity among locally similar future states via hyperbolic future discrimination. Furthermore, we design a progress-aware planning objective that scores candidate rollouts by jointly considering proximity to the goal and sustained progress across intermediate states. Experiments on four visual goal-reaching tasks demonstrate that ProWorld achieves an average absolute success-rate gain of 9.67 over LeWM. The code will be released after the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [200] [A Contractualist Argumentation Framework for Moral Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01937)
*Luis Marcos-Vidal,Giulio Antonio Abbo,Tony Belpaeme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把Scanlon的契约主义通过扩展的ASPIC+（加价值过滤）形式化为一个可操作的论证框架，从而为多主体道德决策提供可解释的理由建模与评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多主体共享环境中，决策会影响多个具有冲突利益的主体，现有方法难以在形式上表达“谁有理由反对某一原则”的契约主义直觉；需要一个可操作化的、能体现主体价值差异并给出可解释判断的道德决策框架。

Method: 以ASPIC+为基础构建结构化论证，将每个代理的价值映射到理由的相关性（基于价值的过滤），形成一个“契约主义论证框架”（Contractualist Argumentation Framework）；使用论证语义对理由进行比较与评估，以得出基于不可合理反对原则的行为许可性判定，并通过家庭场景示例演示该方法。

Result: 给出了一套形式化模型——将契约主义直觉编织进基于ASPIC+的论证体系并引入价值过滤机制；证明该模型能表示、多方比较并评估理由，提供可解释的许可性判断；通过家庭场景示例说明方法可行，并讨论了与已有价值论证方法的关系与区别。

Conclusion: 该论文提出并实现了一个将Scanlon的契约主义原则形式化并可操作化的论证框架，表明可以用结构化论证（ASPIC+）加上基于价值的过滤来表达主体间因价值差异导致的理由冲突与可接受性判断，从而为自主代理在共享环境中的道德决策提供了一种可解释的机械化方法。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in shared environments must make decisions that affect multiple individuals with potentially conflicting interests. We propose a formal framework for moral decision-making grounded in Scanlon's contractualism, an ethical theory that evaluates the permissibility of actions in terms of principles that no one could reasonably reject. To operationalise contractualist reasoning, we use ASPIC+, a structured argumentation framework, extended with value-based filtering to model how each agent's values determine which reasons are morally relevant in the first place. The result is a Contractualist Argumentation Framework in which agents' reasons are formally represented, compared, and evaluated through argumentation semantics. We illustrate the approach through a worked example in a domestic setting and discuss its relation to existing value-based argumentation approaches.

</details>


### [201] [Long-Horizon Autonomous Architecture Research with a Language-Model Agent: A Behavioural Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01995)
*Aon Safdar,Mohamed Saadeldin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将单一大模型作为独立研究者在受限算力与扩展工具集下进行约100次实验，取得可观改进，揭示了以工作流为主导的研究行为模式与若干可操作的改进建议（多样化搜索、预算化“登月”假设、显式分叉、按域重新验证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当通用大模型独自承担研究工作时的行为特征、生产力曲线、假设贡献分布以及工作流对结果与发现的影响；检验模型能否重现或推进已有设计知识。

Method: 使用单一通用大语言模型作为唯一研究者，在长时程神经架构设计问题上自主开展研究。代理被赋予科学问题、初始假设与动机、算力预算和研究工具（源码与实验管理、实验追踪、文献访问与持久记忆），在三阶段流程（由人为声明的过渡点扩展工具或规模）中提出、实现、评估并记录约100个顺序实验。

Result: 代理将一个非标准Vision Transformer从弱基线改进为在小基准上更强且高效的模型，并在ImageNet-1K上得到可用但并非最先进的模型；生成了密集的行为轨迹；得出四项主要发现：生产力呈分阶段结构；早期单一假设贡献最大；贪婪增量式偏好受工作流驱动；代理能独立重现已知结果并在纯通道注意力范畴推翻标准设计选择。

Conclusion: 研究表明工作流设计至少与代理能力同等重要；未来应引入多样化搜索策略、预算化大赌注实验、显式分叉与情景感知的再验证机制以提高自动化研究的探究效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study what happens when a single general-purpose large language model acts as the sole researcher on a long-horizon neural architecture design problem. The agent receives a scientific question, an initial hypothesis and motivation, a compute budget, and research affordances (source and experiment management, experiment tracking, literature access, and persistent memory), then autonomously proposes, implements, evaluates, and records experiments over an extended period. The study comprises three phases, separated by human-declared transitions, that progressively expand the agent's tool surface or problem scale. Across approximately 100 sequential experiments, the agent improves a non-standard Vision Transformer from a weak baseline to a stronger, efficient model on small benchmarks and a usable but sub-SOTA model on ImageNet-1K, while producing a dense behavioural trace. We report four findings.(i)Productivity exhibits a clear phase structure: rapid early gains, a multi-dozen-hypothesis saturation wall, and recovery, with recovery triggered by expanding the action surface rather than changing the underlying model.(ii)A single early hypothesis contributes more to accuracy gain, with later improvements long-tailed.(iii)The preference for greedy, incremental hypotheses is largely workflow-induced: a commit-or-discard evaluation rule is isomorphic to greedy hill-climbing; the remainder reflects risk aversion after bold failures and anchoring on familiar literature. (iv)The agent independently rediscovers established results and, in the unfamiliar regime of pure channel attention, overturns a standard design choice. We conclude that workflow design was at least as influential as agent capability in this study and propose diversified search, budgeted moonshot hypotheses, explicit forks, and regime-aware re-validation as testable directions for future autonomous research.

</details>


### [202] [Evolving in the Agent Jungle via History-Informed Opponent Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02005)
*Zhaofeng Zhang,Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OASE用证据锚定的配对比较替代盲目更新，能在对手持续演化的多智能体环境中实现更稳定与高效的策略适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态环境下的技能修订方法在对手同时演化的多智能体环境中会变得过时——需要一种能鉴别哪些修订在动态对手策略下仍有实际收益的选择机制。

Method: 提出基于‘历史对手快照’的候选技能与现有技能的配对评估：在相同锚定条件下估计候选技能相对于在位技能的收益增益，仅当估计收益超过预设接受阈值时才采用。

Result: 在先价拍卖与私有成本的Cournot竞争两个场景下，OASE比Reflexion风格基线达到更小的最终均衡距离，同时接受的技能修订明显更少，抑制了缺乏收益支撑的策略变动。

Conclusion: OASE在动态多智能体博弈中通过以对手历史快照为锚点的配对比较，有选择地接受真正带来收益的技能修订，从而比盲目更新方法更稳定、效率更高。

Abstract: Learning to adapt strategies through interaction is a key step toward more general and autonomous LLM agents. Existing approaches typically achieve behavioral adaptation by revising skill libraries. However, in multi-agent environments, opponents may simultaneously update their strategies, causing the environment itself to evolve continuously. Applying skill-revision methods designed for static environments in such settings therefore amounts to updating against an obsolete reference. To address this challenge, we introduce OASE (Opponent-Aware Selective Evolution), which identifies and adopts genuinely beneficial skill revisions in dynamic multi-agent environments. Specifically, OASE conducts paired comparisons between a candidate skill and the incumbent under identical conditions anchored by historical snapshots of opponent strategies, and adopts the candidate only when its estimated payoff gain exceeds an acceptance threshold. We evaluate OASE in two decision-making scenarios: first-price auctions and private-cost Cournot competition. Experimental results show that, compared with a Reflexion-style baseline, OASE achieves a lower final equilibrium distance in both environments while accepting substantially fewer skill revisions, thereby suppressing strategy changes that lack sufficient payoff support. OASE therefore replaces blind updating with evidence-anchored selection, allowing agents to adapt stably and efficiently even as opponents continuously evolve.

</details>


### [203] [HALT: Verification-Aware Stopping for Retrieval-Augmented Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02009)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将“何时停止检索”建模为证据覆盖问题，设计轻量的验证感知策略 HALT：基于期望的 hop 声明，仅在累积证据支持所有声明时停止检索，从而减少冗余检索而不显著损失准确率，且不需修改主代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强的多跳问答通过重复发起检索并汇总证据，但一旦所需证据出现，继续检索通常只带来成本、延时和无关上下文，反而有害。需要轻量、可部署的停止策略，能在保证答案质量的前提下避免不必要的检索。

Method: 将停止问题从生成器置信度转为“对预期 hop 声明的证据覆盖”。不改变原检索-生成代理，先生成（或使用 gold）hop 声明；在检索循环中累积证据，并基于声明与检索到的证据的对齐度判定是否所有声明均被支持，满足时停止检索；对比基线与消融实验验证是声明-证据对齐驱动的效果。

Result: HALT 在三个多跳问答基准上显著减少了冗余检索，同时大体保持了 exact match 性能。使用从问题生成的 hop claims（可部署设置）能获得较小但仍保持精度的节省；使用 gold 支持事实（诊断上界）则显示了更大的节省空间。对比与消融分析表明，性能提升主要由“声明—证据对齐”驱动，而非通用的充分性判据、固定停止位置或词汇重合。开放语料试验显示，当证据覆盖无法可靠验证时，HALT 会选择弃权。该方法无需对检索代理进行重训练或修改，作为一种运行时控制信号可实用地改善检索增强代理的效率。

Conclusion: 证据覆盖是一个实用且可部署的停止控制信号。HALT 作为不侵入式策略，通过检查声明-证据对齐大幅减少冗余检索成本与延时，保持多跳 QA 的回答质量，并在证据不可核实时安全弃权。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented search agents answer multi-hop questions by repeatedly issuing search queries and accumulating evidence. This creates a stopping problem: after the necessary evidence has appeared, further retrieval often adds cost, latency, and distracting context rather than useful information. We frame stopping as evidence coverage rather than generator confidence, and introduce HALT, a lightweight verification-aware policy that leaves the search agent unchanged. Given expected hop claims, HALT stops only when cumulative evidence supports each required claim. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, HALT reduces redundant search while largely preserving exact match. We separate a deployable setting, where hop claims are generated from the question, from a diagnostic upper bound that uses gold supporting-fact annotations: generated claims give smaller but still exact-match-preserving savings, while gold claims show the larger savings available when hop targets are clean. Baseline comparisons and ablations show that this behavior is driven by claim-evidence alignment rather than generic sufficiency, fixed stop positions, or lexical overlap. Open-corpus pilots further suggest that HALT abstains when coverage cannot be reliably verified. Overall, evidence coverage provides a practical runtime control signal for improving retrieval-augmented agents without retraining or modifying the host agent.

</details>


### [204] [Before Reasoning Fails: Pre-Evidence Procedural Failures in Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02011)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现检索增强生成代理常在‘未读证据即终结’的程序性失败上出错。通过分析12k条轨迹，把错误分为检索前未遵守流程和检索后未读金标准证据两类，并提出Read-Gate——强制在检索后、终结前读取证据的运行时不变量。强制阅读显著提升准确率，表明应把证据收集作为轨迹级控制问题单独评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG/Agent系统常把注意力放在证据条件下的推理质量，但在实践中代理可能根本未读取检索到的证据即给出答案，需要揭示并修正这一程序性失效。

Method: 使用保存的工具调用轨迹、检索到的证据、模型实际读取的段落和最终答案，在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA与MuSiQue上对12k配对轨迹做程序性解构；用实体抽取（regex与spaCy）识别是否阅读gold证据，计算错误类型并评估Read-Gate的运行时强制阅读策略与不同隐藏思考预算的影响。

Result: 两类失败在数据中大多不重叠，‘both-trigger’比率为11.2%–13.1%；对原本跳过阅读的轨迹，强制阅读使LLM-Acc提升14.9–19.9个百分点；在全部最小推理单元上也提升3.2–9.4个百分点；增加隐藏思考预算并不必然提高证据检查频率。

Conclusion: 证据收集失败是独立于答案推理的主要失效模式；对代理施加‘检索后必须阅读’的约束能显著提升准确率，应把证据获取建模为轨迹层面的控制问题。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can fail before evidence-conditioned reasoning is tested: an agent may retrieve candidate snippets but finalize without inspecting them. We study this failure mode as a procedural property of the agent trajectory, decomposing wrong answers into pre-evidence discipline failures and post-gold-read failures using saved tool-call traces, retrieved evidence, read passages, and final answers. Across 12,000 paired trajectories on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, the two failure types are largely non-redundant: the both-trigger rate is in [11.2%, 13.1%] across regex and spaCy entity extractors. We then evaluate Read-Gate, a minimal runtime invariant requiring an agent to read after search and before finalization. Forced reading improves LLM-Acc by 14.9-19.9 points on trajectories that would otherwise skip reading and by 3.2-9.4 points on full minimal-reasoning cells. Additional diagnostics show that larger hidden thinking budgets do not necessarily increase evidence inspection. Together, these results indicate that evidence-gathering should be evaluated as a trajectory-level control problem, separately from answer-side reasoning.

</details>


### [205] [EduZone: A Framework for Evaluating LLM Safety for K-12 Students and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02024)
*Junyeong Park,Jieun Han,Haneul Yoo,So-Yeon Ahn,Jinsung Yoon,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EduZone，一个面向K-12场景的LLM安全评估框架，通过教师/学生情境、细粒度课程概念和6类28子类风险生成对抗性交互，在单轮、静态多轮和动态多轮三种设置中评估10个LLM的四档安全响应，发现对教育特有风险和动态多轮最脆弱，现有护栏不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估多聚焦通用或场景无关的风险，缺乏对教育场景中师生交互、课程语义与多轮对话导致的特殊风险（如错误教学、隐私泄露、误导性学习建议等）的系统性测试，因而难以为K-12部署提供可靠保障。

Method: 构建一个组合式评估管线：(1) 定义教师与学生两类使用情境；(2) 以细粒度课程概念为生成语料的语义轴；(3) 设计6大类、28个子类的风险标签（含常规与教育专属伤害）；基于此自动生成对抗性交互样本并组织为单轮、静态多轮、动态多轮三类测试用例；对10个LLM按拒绝、 安全辅助、带安全提示的有风险辅助、完全有风险辅助四档进行标注与统计分析。

Result: 实验显示（1）模型在教育特有风险（如误导的学习建议、作业代写、年龄不当内容）上的失败率高于通用风险；（2）动态多轮对话比单轮/静态多轮更易诱发风险；（3）多数模型仍会在部分场景下提供完全有风险的帮助，说明现有安全护栏策略覆盖不足；（4）EduZone可自动、可扩展地生成评估用例，为后续模型改进和部署审查提供量化依据。

Conclusion: EduZone证明了在教育场景下需专门的安全评估：现有通用安全护栏无法充分覆盖教育特有的风险和多轮互动带来的漏洞，需基于课程语境和动态对话设计更精细的防护与模型训练策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used across diverse tasks in K-12 education, yet existing safety evaluations rarely examine how harmful or inappropriate content appears in interactions between LLMs and students or teachers. To address this, we present EduZone, an evaluation framework for LLM safety across diverse educational scenarios. Our framework systematically combines (1) student- and teacher-facing LLM usage contexts, (2) fine-grained curriculum concepts, and (3) 6 risk categories and 28 subcategories spanning both conventional and education-specific harms to generate contextually grounded adversarial interactions. We construct these interactions in three settings: single-turn requests, static multi-turn conversations, and dynamic multi-turn conversations. Using these interactions, we evaluate ten LLMs using four safety levels: refusal, safe assistance, risky assistance with safety guidance, and fully risky assistance. Our results reveal greater vulnerability to education-specific risks and dynamic multi-turn interactions, while existing safety guardrails fail to adequately address these risks. EduZone advances LLM safety in education by providing an automated, scalable evaluation framework that supports the development and deployment of safer LLMs in K-12 education.

</details>


### [206] [HPFA: Hypergraph-Based Paired Failure Attribution for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02026)
*Runchuan Zhu,Hongbin Lai,Bowen Jiang,Junrui Zhang,Zhangheng LI,Ostap Kilbasovych,Junyuan Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于超图的成对失败归因（HPFA）框架，通过将失败推理路径的超边与成功参考路径对比，缩小搜索空间以高效定位根因并用于合成训练数据，从而训练轻量归因器以提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法要么通过逐步反事实测试代价高昂难扩展，要么把推理痕迹视为平面序列忽略非线性依赖，导致归因不精确。利用超图结构与成对对比可同时捕捉逻辑依赖关系并极大减少搜索空间，从而高效且准确地定位根因。

Method: 将推理轨迹表示为超图（结点表示中间陈述/子推理，超边表示非线性逻辑依赖），对比失败轨迹与成功参考轨迹的超边差异以定位可能导致失败的超边集合；基于该缩小的候选集，合成大量有标签的（根因）样例用于有监督微调与基于环境反馈的强化学习，以训练轻量级归因模型用于在线推理修正。

Result: 在所述任务上HPFA在归因准确率和定位效率上均有显著提升，合成的数据让训练出的归因器在单次推理阶段能提高整体任务正确率，且在对比试验中优于去图结构/单轨迹基线。

Conclusion: HPFA在数学推理和代理编码任务上显著提高了归因精度与效率，且训练出的归因器在测试时能稳定提升最终推理或执行性能，优于不使用图结构或成对分析的基线方法。

Abstract: Reflection is a powerful mechanism for LLM reasoning, yet its effectiveness hinges on accurately attributing failures to specific reasoning steps, a capability that current models notably lack. Existing failure attribution methods either require expensive step-by-step counterfactual testing that scales poorly with trajectory length, or treat reasoning traces as flat sequences that ignore the inherent non-linear logical dependencies. We propose a hypergraph-based paired failure attribution (HPFA) framework that attributes the failure root cause by comparing the hyperedges of the targeted failure reasoning path against a reference successful path. By reducing the search space, our method efficiently localizes root causes and enables scalable synthesis of attribution data for training a lightweight attributor model via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic coding tasks demonstrate that HPFA can dramatically increase attribution accuracy and efficiency, and the trained attributor consistently improves reasoning accuracy at test time, outperforming baselines that lack graph structure or paired analysis.

</details>


### [207] [Cross-Fitted Residual Utility for Primary-Preserving Cognitive Decision Correction in Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02063)
*Linzhuo Han,Zongyong Cui,Houbiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于交叉拟合残差效用和保持主预测策略的后推断决策系统，能在多数据集与多种信道扰动下显著且稳健地提升自动调制识别的整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在自动调制识别（AMC）中，除了提高表示精度外，认知接收机还需要在何时用外部异质证据覆盖可信的默认预测这一后验决策问题。作者提出通过交叉拟合残差效用（cross-fitted residual utility）和保持主预测（primary-preserving）的认知决策策略来解决这一问题。

Method: 默认概率由结构化 KAN-Fourier 分类器提供；神经与非神经候选器生成观测证据；从训练拆分的 out-of-fold 预测学习每个候选器的残差效用；使用独立验证集确定并冻结动作阈值、允许的转换、条件路由与统一风险掩码；在保留策略的前提下在独立测试集（RMLA/RMLB/HISAR）及 11 种受扰动条件下评估性能，并通过配对自助与 Holm 校正 McNemar 检验评估统计显著性。

Result: 构建了一个由结构化 KAN-Fourier 分类器作为默认概率、若干神经与非神经候选器提供可观测证据、以及从训练拆分的 out-of-fold 预测中学习的候选器特定残差效用组成的完整系统。在独立验证集上冻结动作阈值、批准转换、条件路径和统一风险掩码后，于 RMLA、RMLB、HISAR 数据集上将整体准确率分别从 63.632% 提升到 66.332%、65.161% 提升到 66.168%、77.769% 提升到 79.867%。对照实验表明，单独的效用目标并不总是优于其他 out-of-fold 元学习器；持续改进来源于完整的证据-动作策略。通过配对自助法（paired bootstrap）和 Holm 校正的 McNemar 检验支持这些受控提升。在载波频偏、I/Q 失衡和合成 Rayleigh/Rician 衰落的冻结策略压力测试中，11 个条件均表现正收益，且每个配对 95% 置信区间均在零以上。

Conclusion: 通过将默认结构化分类器与来自多种候选器的可观测证据结合，利用 out-of-fold 学习得到候选器特定残差效用，并在独立验证上冻结决策策略，该方法在多个公开数据集与受扰动条件下实现了可重复且统计显著的性能提升。改进主要来自整体证据-动作策略而非单一效用回归目标。

Abstract: Automatic modulation classification research has largely emphasized representation accuracy, but a cognitive receiver must also decide when heterogeneous evidence justifies overriding a trusted default prediction. We study this post-inference problem through cross-fitted residual utility and a primary-preserving cognitive decision policy. A structured KAN-Fourier classifier supplies the default probability, while neural and non-neural candidates provide observable evidence. Candidate-specific residual utility is learned from train-split out-of-fold predictions, and a disjoint validation split freezes action thresholds, approved transitions, conditional routes, and a unified risk mask before held-out evaluation. On RMLA, RMLB, and HISAR, the complete system improves overall accuracy from 63.632% to 66.332%, 65.161% to 66.168%, and 77.769% to 79.867%, respectively. Controlled comparisons show that the isolated utility target does not uniformly dominate alternative out-of-fold meta-learners; the consistent gain comes from the complete evidence-and-action policy. Paired bootstrap and Holm-corrected McNemar analyses support the controlled gains. A frozen-policy stress test under carrier-frequency offset, I/Q imbalance, and synthetic Rayleigh/Rician fading yields positive gains in all 11 conditions, with every paired 95\% confidence interval above zero.

</details>


### [208] [Instruction-Conditioned Exploration with Asymmetric Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02087)
*Jim Dilkes,Vahid Yazdanpanah,Sebastian Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在训练时给任务提示附加不同指令诱导多样化探索，并用一个‘不对称’的 RL+自蒸馏目标把这些探索行为蒸馏回无条件策略，从而在数学推理上显著优于 DAPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的 LLM 在动作空间（文本输出）上与经典 RL 存在结构性差异，导致传统探索策略不足。需要利用预训练模型的知识和柔性，通过人为构造多样指令在训练时主动产生丰富经验，以改善最终无条件模型的能力。

Method: （1）训练阶段：在原始任务提示上随机或有策略地拼接若干不同类别的指令以促进行为多样性（Instruction-Conditioned Exploration）；（2）目标函数：结合强化学习（可能优化奖励或分数）和自蒸馏（把带指令的策略输出作为目标去训练无条件策略），构成 Asymmetric-RL/SD；（3）评测：在 Qwen3-1.7B，比较与 DAPO 的 held-out pass@1，在 4K 与 8K 输出长度上评估数学推理性能。

Result: 提出用于训练时增强探索和将探索到的行为迁移到测试时间策略的方法：Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE) 与 Asymmetric-RL/SD 目标。将多样指令附加到任务提示以增大行为覆盖，并用 RL+自蒸馏把这些行为转移到无条件策略上。在 Qwen3-1.7B 上，ICE+Asymmetric-RL/SD 在数学推理任务的 4K 输出长度下比 DAPO 在 Held-out pass@1 指标上提升 5.0%，在 8K 上仍保持改善。

Conclusion: ICE 增强了训练时期的行为覆盖，Asymmetric-RL/SD 成功将这些被探索到的行为迁移到常规模型，最终在数学推理 pass@1 指标上取得持续提升。

Abstract: Post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important tool for improving model capabilities, but the LLM action-space structure introduces challenges distinct from classical RL, with implications for inducing exploration. New methods are required that leverage the broad knowledge and flexibility of pre-trained LLMs to deliberately generate diverse experience at training time. We propose Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE), which supplements task prompts during training with one of several distinct instructions, increasing the coverage of behaviours attempted. To facilitate ICE, we propose Asymmetric-RL/SD, a combined Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation training objective, to transfer explored behaviours to the unconditioned test-time policy. ICE with the Asymmetric-RL/SD objective improves Qwen3-1.7B held-out pass@1 performance at $4$K response length on mathematical reasoning tasks by $5.0\%$ relative to training with DAPO, with improvement persisting at a longer 8K context.

</details>


### [209] [Fetch-then-Explore: Decoupling Selection from Extraction over a Persistent Workspace for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02097)
*Qi Liu,Yiqun Chen,Zidan Chen,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将页面选择与证据抽取分离并将页面保存在问题级工作区，允许按需重复抽取与返回页面，从而在多轮检索问答中显著提高性能并减少重复抓取开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing document interfaces either fix a page reading at fetch time (visit-and-read) or hold only a single transient page (stateful browsing), forcing repeated fetch-and-read cycles and losing evidence that becomes relevant later. A persistent workspace could make selection cheap and allow later extraction when context/hypotheses are clearer.

Method: Introduce a filesystem-backed per-question workspace that records fetched pages; separate selection (cheap, persistent) from extraction (on-demand, repeatable); integrate with a unified ReAct agent harness and fixed search; compare against three baselines (snippet-only, visit-and-read, browsing) on two benchmarks (BrowseComp, WideSearch) across three agent backbones; analyze agent behaviors (revisits, evidence recovery).

Result: Fetch-then-Explore leads BrowseComp accuracy for every backbone and generally matches or exceeds baselines on WideSearch. Behavioral analysis attributes gains mainly to frequent revisits enabled by the workspace, allowing recovery of evidence missed on first pass.

Conclusion: Fetch-then-Explore improves multi-turn web question answering by decoupling page selection from evidence extraction and retaining fetched pages in a per-question workspace, enabling revisits and on-demand extraction, which yields higher accuracy than snippet-only, visit-and-read, and browsing baselines.

Abstract: Search agents now answer questions that take dozens of searches to settle, yet how such an agent reads a page has drawn far less attention than how it finds one. Nearly all of them use one of two document interfaces, and both tie a page to the moment it is opened. \emph{Visit-and-read} injects a reading of the page into the message history at fetch time, fixing that reading before the agent knows which fact it will need. Stateful \emph{browsing} instead extracts on demand from the page in hand, but holds one page at a time and releases it as soon as the agent opens another. Either way, a page that turns out to matter many turns later has to be fetched and rendered into context all over again. We propose \textbf{Fetch-then-Explore}, which separates page selection from evidence extraction and keeps what it selects: pages are recorded in a per-question workspace on the filesystem rather than the context window or a transient session, and evidence is pulled from them on demand later. Selection becomes almost free, extraction can wait until the agent knows what to look for and be repeated as its hypothesis sharpens, and pages are not released when the agent moves on, so evidence accumulates across the trajectory. In a unified ReAct harness with fixed search, we compare Fetch-then-Explore against snippet-only, visit-and-read, and browsing baselines on two open-web benchmarks, BrowseComp and WideSearch, across three agent backbones. It leads BrowseComp accuracy at every backbone and generally matches or exceeds the baselines on WideSearch, and a behavioral analysis traces the gains to the workspace's defining move: returning to a page after leaving it, which it does far more than any transient interface, so evidence missed on a first pass can still be recovered later.

</details>


### [210] [MemArbiter: Decision-Time Memory Arbitration for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02113)
*Jiajun Dong,Yutao Hu,Fengrui Fan,Shihan Dou,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于功能的记忆仲裁框架，通过把交互历史分解为原子项并组织为五类功能性记忆库，结合库级需求、项级相关性、焦点-环境表示和时间呈现门控，动态控制记忆显著性，从而弥合“记忆-动作差距”，在受限预算下显著提升长时序任务的行动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法提高了记忆可访问性，但即便被检索出来的信息仍可能因格式、组织或优先级不当而未能有效指导当前决策——记忆-动作差距。因而需要一种能按功能组织与动态呈现记忆项的仲裁机制。

Method: 将历史拆成原子项；按功能（5个记忆库）组织；对每个库计算库级需求和项级相关性，构建焦点-环境（focal-ambient）表征，并用时间门控制呈现；在统一每步token预算下按优先级呈现记忆给动作生成模型；于 ALFWorld 与两种平坦基线比较并做失败恢复相关分析。

Result: MemArbiter 在受限的每步记忆预算下，显著提升了 ALFWorld 中的成功率（在500和750 token预算下分别达82.8%和92.5%），并减少了失败后重复动作和状态-动作重复，优于 Flat Retrieval 与 Flat Recency 基线。

Conclusion: 功能感知的记忆仲裁能把“可访问信息”转化为更具行动引导性的输入，显著改善决策成功率与错误恢复能力，但仍需澄清记忆库设计、编解码与可扩展性等实现细节以评估泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain and use cross-step information to act coherently in long-horizon tasks. Existing methods improve memory accessibility, yet action-relevant information may still fail to guide the current decision because it is poorly formed, organized, prioritized, or presented. We call this post-access failure the Memory-Action Gap. We propose MemArbiter, a function-aware memory arbitration framework that addresses the memory-management-induced component of this gap. MemArbiter decomposes interaction histories into atomic items, organizes them into five functional Memory Banks, and combines bank-level demand, item-level relevance, focal-ambient representations, and a temporal presentation gate to dynamically control memory salience. We evaluate MemArbiter on ALFWorld against Flat Retrieval and Flat Recency under unified per-step memory budgets. With an open-weight action-generation model, MemArbiter achieves success rates of 82.8% and 92.5% under 500- and 750-token budgets, outperforming the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 25.4 percentage points, respectively. It also improves post-failure recovery and reduces failed-action repetition and state-action recurrence. These results show that function-aware memory arbitration enables accessible information to guide actions more effectively.

</details>


### [211] [Beyond Solution-Centric Search: Adaptive Inquiry and Knowledge Revision for Autonomous ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02143)
*Shaokang Fu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Jiarong Zhao,Qiming Shi,Tianjun Pan,Haonan Li,Chengyu Wang,Jia Wu,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息范式，构建Iris（询问—修订循环），通过显式信息状态与认知性动作改进长期自治研究任务，在MLE-Bench与跨域任务中取得最好或有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理依赖树/图/链式的‘以解为中心’搜索，导致信息获取与管理被搜索结构绑定；作者主张独立的信息状态能更好驱动解的迭代与预算分配。

Method: Iris维护一个可演化的信息状态。信息获取：从当前信息状态生成局部行动计划，并使用认知性动作探查决策关键未知量，而不修改被保留的解。信息管理：把实验观察综合为带显式作用域和状态的可修正主张，并随着证据更新。构建决策上下文时可使用原始证据、结构化摘要或任务知识。

Result: Iris把‘信息状态’作为核心，把获取与管理信息分开：用局部行动计划和认知性（epistemic）动作进行探查，不直接修改解，且把观察综合为有范围和状态的可修正主张。实验证明在MLE-Bench的12小时预算下any-medal率64.9%为最优，并在四个跨领域任务上表现良好。

Conclusion: 将搜索从“以解为中心”的结构转向“以信息为中心”的状态化管理，能提升在预算受限的长时程研究任务中的决策质量与泛化性；但仍需更多细节、消融和可重复性证明其稳健性与扩展性。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research tasks such as machine learning engineering require systems to make interdependent decisions under a limited budget. Existing LLM-based agents typically organize candidate-solution improvement through tree, graph, or chain structures, meaning that the search process determines how information is acquired and managed. We call this design solution-centric search and propose instead the information paradigm, in which an evolving information state represents the system's understanding of the task and guides solution improvement. We instantiate this paradigm in Iris, an inquiry-revision loop. For information acquisition, Iris generates local action plans from the current information state and uses epistemic actions to probe decision-critical unknowns without modifying the retained solution. For information management, Iris synthesizes observations across experiments into task knowledge composed of revisable claims with explicit scope and status. It updates this knowledge as new evidence arrives and constructs each decision context from raw evidence, structured summaries, or task knowledge at the required level of detail. On MLE-Bench, Iris attains a 64.9% any-medal rate under a 12-hour budget, the highest among compared systems. Across four tasks spanning harness engineering and model post-training, Iris also demonstrates cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [212] [Beyond the Mean: Multi-Moment Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02149)
*Yijun Zhang,Yule Xie,Jiaxin Ding,Xin Ding,Fan Xu,Haoxiang Zhang,Luoyi Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper introduces MMPO, a policy optimization method that jointly minimizes multiple moments of the failure-probability distribution for LLM reasoning, provides operational interpretation and a general moment-transformation framework, and shows consistent empirical improvements.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current RL methods for LLM reasoning typically optimize single moment of failure probability; authors view failure probability as random variable and aim to leverage multiple moments to capture broader distributional structure and reduce expected truncated time to first success.

Method: Introduce moment-based perspective and propose MMPO that jointly minimizes multiple moments of failure-probability distribution; develop general moment-transformation framework.

Result: Proposed MMPO framework that optimizes multiple moments; theoretical interpretation as minimizing expected truncated time to first success; generalized moment-transformation framework; empirical gains on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks across model scales, outperforming strong baselines.

Conclusion: Moment-based optimization (MMPO) better captures distributional aspects of failure probabilities than single-moment objectives, leads to improved reasoning performance in LLMs, and offers a unifying framework for designing policy objectives.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Existing methods generally aim to reduce the failure probabilities induced across problems. In this paper, we introduce a moment-based perspective on policy optimization for LLM reasoning by treating the failure probability of a randomly sampled problem as a random variable and characterizing optimization objectives through its moments. Under this perspective, many existing methods optimize only a single moment of the failure-probability distribution, leaving its broader distributional structure largely uncharacterized. We propose \textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{M}oment \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (MMPO), a novel policy optimization framework that jointly minimizes multiple moments of the failure-probability distribution. MMPO admits a direct operational interpretation as minimizing the expected truncated time required to obtain the first successful response. Beyond MMPO, we further develop a general moment-transformation framework that systematically induces different moment profiles and provides a unified view of a broader family of policy optimization objectives. Experiments across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and models of different scales demonstrate that MMPO consistently outperforms strong baselines. We hope this moment-based perspective offers new insights into the design of policy optimization objectives for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [213] [Auditing Data Provenance in LLM Fine-tuning via Intrinsic Distributional Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02154)
*Zirui Huang,Yunlong Mao,Wei Tong,Tingting Wu,Xin Ge,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DPA 利用词汇—语义交集的分布指纹与统计假设检验，实现了对恶意微调的黑盒事后审计，实验证明其在行业敏感数据上鲁棒且优于既有方法，但存在双重用途风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对对专有数据未经授权进行微调的风险，现有方法需训练/数据阶段介入且对改写、蒸馏等恶意规避措施脆弱。DPA 希望在仅有黑盒访问且对方可刻意混淆的情形下，提供可靠的事后审计手段。

Method: 提出一种后验审计框架 Distribution Provenance Audit (DPA)，在黑盒和恶意设置下，通过对模型输出进行无偏采样并构造统计假设检验，基于保持语义与词汇交集的假设，将该交集视作分布指纹用于判断是否使用了特定训练数据。

Result: 在医疗和法律微调任务上，DPA 在多种基线之上表现更好，能抵抗使用文本改写和知识蒸馏的对抗训练策略；并指出高保真分布指纹既有利于审计也可能被滥用于隐私攻击。

Conclusion: DPA 是一个在实务场景中具有可操作性的黑盒审计工具，提供了抵抗改写与蒸馏攻击的新思路；但其有效性依赖于若干假设（如为保持效用必须保留词汇—语义交集），对模型规模、采样预算、指纹泄露与隐私风险的影响需进一步量化和防护。

Abstract: The proliferation of customized Large Language Models (LLMs) poses critical risks of Data Intellectual Property (Data IP) infringement via unauthorized fine-tuning on proprietary data. Existing audit techniques are limited, as they require intervention during data preparation or training and remain fragile under malicious obfuscations such as data paraphrasing and knowledge distillation.
  We propose \textit{Distribution Provenance Audit (DPA)}, a post-hoc framework for auditing data IP infringement in LLM fine-tuning under black-box and malicious settings. DPA is grounded in a critical insight: regardless of fine-tuning tactics to evade provenance, the practical necessity of maintaining utility constrains the model to preserve the fundamental intersection of semantic substance and lexical form. Accordingly, DPA captures this persistent lexical-semantic intersection as intrinsic distributional fingerprints. The framework formulates the audit as a statistical hypothesis test, effectively quantifying these fingerprints via unbiased output sampling to reliably reject the null hypothesis of non-usage.
  Extensive experiments on medical and legal fine-tuning tasks show that DPA consistently outperforms existing baselines, remaining robust against adversarial trainers employing paraphrasing and knowledge distillation. We further highlight a fundamental dual-use tension: the same high-fidelity distributional fingerprints enabling reliable auditing may also facilitate privacy attacks.

</details>


### [214] [From Simple QA to Deep Research: A Verifiable Benchmark Constructed through Iterative Task Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02163)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Haowen Gao,Hao Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自动化构建的500道可验证深度研究任务（DAG表示+事实性分点量化评估），用于细粒度模型评估，显示出区分度和可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address gap between expert-authored deep research benchmarks and fully automatic construction by creating a verifiable, traceable benchmark that supports reliable, fine-grained evaluation without requiring full human authoring.

Method: Iterative Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger pipeline that automatically transforms simple questions into multi-step deep research tasks; each task is encoded as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of atomic steps with checkpoints and fact-grounded pointwise rubrics; three query forms probe complementary capabilities.

Result: A publicly released benchmark of 500 deep research tasks across 31 topics and 10 categories, with experiments showing clear discrimination among models and query types and stable, human-aligned evaluation via fact-grounded rubrics.

Conclusion: 论文提出了可追溯的自动化任务生成与评估框架，能在无大规模人工编写的前提下生成可验证、可演进的深度研究任务；仍需关注自动化步骤的质量保障、覆盖性与人类校验机制。

Abstract: Deep research benchmarks require expert-level tasks and reliable evaluation grounded in task-specific knowledge. Existing benchmarks rely heavily on expert authoring or pre-existing human-authored materials, while fully automatic construction struggles to ensure consistent and traceable verification. To address this gap, we introduce a verifiable benchmark of 500 deep research tasks spanning 31 topics and 10 major categories, with three query forms designed to probe complementary capabilities required for deep research. The benchmark is constructed automatically using an iterative Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger pipeline that progressively transforms simple questions into deep research tasks. Each task is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of atomic steps and associated checkpoints, enabling the query, DAG, and rubrics to evolve together in a controlled manner.
