Experiments demonstrate that the benchmark clearly discriminates among models and query types, while its fact-grounded pointwise rubrics enable fine-grained, human-aligned, and stable evaluation. Our data, implementation, and results are publicly available.

</details>


### [215] [From Profiling to Synthesis: Benchmarking Implicit Behavioral Alignment in Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02171)
*Jiajia Song,Bobo Li,Haiwen Yi,Zibo Ji,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Min Zhang,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Presents IBA-Bench for implicit behavioral alignment from longitudinal noisy interactions and IBA-Agent for trajectory-level personalized task execution; shows gap in current agents and improvements from the proposed method.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Personalization for autonomous LLM agents is essential but existing benchmarks use static preferences or fixed logs, failing to capture evolving, implicit, noisy user behavior and neglecting preference-conditioned task execution (knowledge-to-action gap).

Method: Construct longitudinal interaction histories with noise and temporal inconsistencies; define evaluation that requires inferring implicit constraints and executing tasks under those constraints; design IBA-Agent framework employing broad retrieval of past interactions and alignment at the trajectory level to reconcile conflicts; evaluate across nine application domains comparing to SOTA agents.

Result: Introduces IBA-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark with noisy implicit cues testing agents' ability to infer constraints and execute tasks accordingly; proposes IBA-Agent, which uses broad retrieval and trajectory-level alignment to reconcile conflicting priorities. Experiments show SOTA agents struggle, and IBA-Agent improves behavioral alignment across nine domains.

Conclusion: Effective personalization remains a significant open challenge; IBA-Bench provides a more realistic evaluation and IBA-Agent demonstrates promising gains via retrieval and trajectory alignment, but further work is needed.

Abstract: Large Language Models have enabled increasingly capable autonomous agents, yet personalization remains critical for making such agents practically useful. Recent benchmarks have begun evaluating personalization in agents, but they largely rely on static preference snapshots, fixed interaction logs, or question answering over predefined user profiles. Such designs fail to capture the complexity of evolving user preferences and neglect preference-conditioned task execution-a discrepancy we term as the knowledge-to-action gap. To address this challenge, we introduce IBA-Bench, a benchmark for implicit behavioral alignment constructed from longitudinal interaction histories that contain noise, implicit cues, and temporal inconsistencies. Unlike prior work, IBA-Bench evaluates whether an agent can execute tasks while satisfying implicit user constraints inferred from historical interactions. We further propose IBA-Agent, an agent framework that reconciles conflicting priorities through broad retrieval and trajectory-level alignment. Experiment results on IBA-Bench show that effective personalization remains a significant challenge for state-of-the-art LLM agents, and the proposed IBA-Agent substantially improves behavioral alignment in complex scenarios across nine application domains.

</details>


### [216] [PAC Approximation and DIRECT Optimization for Parametric Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02184)
*Zhiming Chi,Ying Liu,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,David N. Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过场景方法得到PAC多项式近似并结合DIRECT进行无导数全局优化，为pMDP参数合成与优化提供理论保证并在大规模基准上验证性能/折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决参数马尔可夫决策过程（pMDPs）的参数合成与优化问题。精确计算将参数赋值映射到PRCTL性质满足度的有理函数代价高昂，且最优策略可能随参数变化，导致问题更复杂。

Method: 对参数空间进行采样，基于样本构建并求解线性规划以拟合多项式近似（场景方法）；将该PAC近似与统计模型检测结合以支持黑盒模型；采用DIRECT算法进行导数自由的全局优化；在Lipschitz与PAC-good-set假设下推导误差界，并通过2997个基准进行实证评估。

Result: 采用场景方法通过对参数采样并求解线性规划，构造出多项式的probably approximately correct（PAC）近似函数Approx_f，保证在给定置信度下，对除δ比例外的参数域满足误差界ε。该框架可与统计模型检测（SMC）结合处理黑盒pMDP；在此基础上，引入DIRECT无导数全局优化算法并在显式Lipschitz与PAC-good-set假设下证明条件性最优差界。实验（2997个基准）表明：DIRECT变体在求解实例数上少于场景优化器，但在共同成功的实例上常给出略优且通常更快的解，且结果通常在PAC误差范围内。

Conclusion: 该工作在理论与实践上展示了用场景方法+SMC获得可证近似并用DIRECT执行有效全局优化的可行性：提供误差与最优差界的条件性保证，但DIRECT的可解率低于场景优化器，呈现效率—鲁棒性的权衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the parameter synthesis and optimization problem for parametric Markov decision processes (pMDPs), the extension of classical MDPs where exact probability values are replaced by parametric expressions. Computing the rational function $f_{\lsf}$ that maps parameter valuations to the satisfaction value of a PRCTL property $\lsf$ is a computationally expensive task, particularly for pMDPs where the optimal policy may vary across the parameter space. We adopt the \emph{scenario approach} to efficiently synthesize a probably approximately correct (PAC) approximation $\ApproxFunOfProperty{f}$ of $f_{\lsf}$: by sampling parameter configurations and solving a linear program, we obtain a polynomial approximation whose error margin $\margin$ is guaranteed, with prescribed confidence, for all but an $\errorRate$-fraction of the parameter domain under the sampling distribution. We further show how this PAC framework can be combined with statistical model checking (SMC), enabling the analysis of black-box parametric models. Building on the PAC approximation, we integrate the DIRECT (DIviding RECTangles) algorithm for derivative-free global optimization over the parameter space. We establish conditional optimality-gap guarantees: under explicit Lipschitz and PAC-good-set assumptions, the difference between the true optimum $f_{\lsf}(\parameters^{*})$ and the value found by DIRECT is bounded by a partition-diameter term and, in the PAC case, an additional approximation-error term. An empirical evaluation on 2997 benchmarks focuses on the new DIRECT-based optimization component. The results show that DIRECT variants solve fewer instances than the scenario optimizer, but on their common successful instances they often return slightly better objective values and usually run faster, while remaining close to the scenario values within the PAC margin.

</details>


### [217] [MEGRAG: Multi-Granular Evidence Graphs for Answer-Aware Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02195)
*Weidong Bao,Yingying Sun,Jun Yang,Yilin Wang,Zili Wei,Yubin Bao,Fangling Leng,Minghe Yu,Tiancheng Zhang,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MEGRAG：一种将多跳推理建模为路径结构多粒度证据图的答案感知RAG框架，离线构建跨粒度索引，在线从三元组开始按需扩展证据并基于中间答案决定是否继续检索，实验显示优于多种RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多跳问答中证据分散带来的挑战，尤其是现有迭代式RAG方法存在（1）单一粒度证据难以在信息密度和上下文噪声间权衡，（2）仅在所有中间证据聚合后才给出最终答案，易积累冗余或检索错误从而降低最终答案质量。MEGRAG旨在通过多粒度且答案感知的检索-推理机制来缓解这些问题。

Method: 构建了一个答案感知（answer-aware）的多粒度证据图（path-structured multi-granular evidence graph）。离线阶段通过跨粒度索引将文档、句子与抽取的三元组关联；在线阶段首先检索与当前查询的段落并对齐证据，从紧凑的三元组开始，按需扩展到句子或段落上下文；使用生成的中间答案和已有推理判断初始问题是否已被解答，若未解答则识别缺失信息并构造聚焦下一步查询，直至停止检索并返回答案。

Result: 在若干RAG基线上进行的大量实验显示，MEGRAG在多跳问答任务上取得了稳定的性能提升，表明多粒度证据与基于中间答案的早期停止/聚焦检索策略能够提高准确性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入跨粒度索引与答案感知的检索停止/扩展策略，MEGRAG在保持信息密度同时抑制上下文噪声和中间错误累积方面提供了有效方案，实验验证其在多跳检索增强生成中的改进效果。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering is a fundamental challenge in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), because deriving an answer requires integrating dispersed evidence. Iterative RAG (iRAG) is widely used for this challenge, but existing methods have two limitations. First, most methods still support each reasoning step with single-granularity evidence, making it difficult to balance information density and contextual noise. Second, existing methods often answer the original question only after aggregating evidence retrieved across intermediate steps, so redundant evidence and intermediate retrieval errors may accumulate and degrade the final answer. To address these limitations, we propose MEGRAG, an answer-aware framework that represents multi-hop reasoning as a path-structured multi-granular evidence graph. Offline, MEGRAG links passages to their sentences and extracted triples through a cross-granularity index. Online, it retrieves passages for the current query and selects aligned evidence, starting with compact triples and adding sentence or passage context as needed. MEGRAG uses the resulting intermediate answer and prior reasoning to decide whether the Initial Query has been resolved. If not, it identifies the missing information and formulates a focused next query; otherwise, it stops retrieval and returns the answer. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains over a diverse set of RAG baselines.

</details>


### [218] [PosterMELD: Multi-Agent Paper-to-Poster Generation for Controllable Design Diversity with Editable Print-Ready Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02218)
*Haojie Hu,Chenhao Dang,Yaojia Liu,Hengrui Kang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PosterMELD 通过模板化多代理＋VLM 复核和有界修复，显著提高可编辑、可打印学术海报的通过率与质量，同时保持低成本与导出原生可编辑文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将冗长的多模态论文压缩为可读、可编辑且可打印的学术海报；弥补直接图像生成不可编辑与基于代码的代理流水线成本高昂的不足。

Method: 模板条件化 → 容量感知槽位分配（写作前规划）→ 多代理生成（文本、图表、布局）→ 确定性门控判定初始输出合格与否→ 冻结VLM做视觉＋事实复核→ 不合格路由到有限次数的修复流程→ 接受后导出 PPTX/PNG 并可生成同论文设计变体。

Result: PosterMELD 是一种 template-conditioned 的多代理流水线，使用 capacity-aware slots 在渲染前引导写作，并通过确定性门控与冻结视觉语言模型（VLM）复核，将失败路由到有限的修复流程。输出可导出为可编辑的 PPTX 与 PNG，支持同论文的设计变体。基于 621 篇论文评估，Print-Ready Rate（PRR）为 81.3%，远超 P2P（≈23.8%）和 PosterGen（≈15.6%），并在多个可打印输出的生成方法中取得最高的条件 Craftsmanship–Harmony–Expressiveness（CHE）分数。均摊成本约 USD 0.38/请求，代码与资源公开。

Conclusion: PosterMELD 在可编辑性、打印就绪率和可控设计变体三方面取得实用性提升，但依赖冻结VLM评分、评估度量与修复细节仍需更完整的公开与用户研究以验证泛化与主观质量。

Abstract: Scientific poster construction compresses a long multimodal paper into a readable, editable canvas. Existing systems hide request-level failures by scoring only completed outputs; direct image generation is not element-editable, while coding-agent workflows are costly. PosterMELD is a template-conditioned multi-agent pipeline: capacity-aware slots guide writing before rendering, and deterministic gates plus vision-language model (VLM) review route failures to bounded repair. Each accepted request exports editable PowerPoint (PPTX) and Portable Network Graphics (PNG) artifacts; explicit design controls yield same-paper variants. Across 621 papers, Print-Ready Rate (PRR) counts requests passing geometric, readability, asset-integrity, and obvious-factual-error checks, with native editability reported separately. A frozen VLM assigns conditional Craftsmanship-Harmony-Expressiveness (CHE) scores to print-ready outputs. PosterMELD attains 81.3% PRR, 3.4 times P2P's rate and 5.2 times PosterGen's, and the highest conditional CHE among generated methods with multiple print-ready outputs. Native editability and explicit design controls are retained at a mean cost of USD 0.38 per request, 3.5% of Codex+Skill's. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/Shannon4Science/PosterMELD.

</details>


### [219] [Trustworthy AI in Digital Health: A Comprehensive Review of Robustness and Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02238)
*Abdullah Mamun,Shovito Barua Soumma,Hassan Ghasemzadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇综述聚焦于数字健康中促进信任的技术：从问题与伦理挑战出发，结合各应用场景的信任需求，系统评述了提升模型鲁棒性和可解释性的技术与评价方法，并提出面向实务的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强调在数字健康等高风险领域安全且伦理地整合机器学习，特别聚焦于可信赖AI的关键维度（鲁棒性、可解释性等）在生命周期各阶段的实现；弥补现有综述在鲁棒性与可解释性、且面向医疗场景的系统性整合方面的空白。

Method: 基于文献综述的方法：梳理并分类近期关于鲁棒性与可解释性的技术与应用报告，按主题（理论方法、应用域、评估指标）组织内容，穿插实践案例讨论，并涵盖LLM相关发展与评估维度。

Result: 构建了面向数字健康的可访问性框架，系统综述了相关方法、挑战与解决方案；按三部分组织：可信AI支柱与挑战、不同医疗子领域的信任考量、提升鲁棒性（数据稀缺与分布转移）与可解释性（特征归因、梯度方法、反事实解释等）的最新技术，并讨论了LLM时代的可信构建与评估指标。

Conclusion: 论文为研究者与实践者提供了关于数字健康领域可信AI（尤指鲁棒性与可解释性）的结构化概览，强调需要标准化的评估、跨学科的人机交互研究、在真实临床路径中验证方法，并呼吁更强的可复现性与持续监测机制。

Abstract: Ensuring trust in AI systems is essential for the safe and ethical integration of machine learning systems into high-stakes domains such as digital health. Key dimensions, including robustness, explainability, fairness, accountability, and privacy, need to be addressed throughout the AI lifecycle, from problem formulation and data collection to model deployment and human interaction. While various contributions address different aspects of trustworthy AI, a focused synthesis on robustness and explainability, especially tailored to the healthcare context, remains limited. This review addresses that need by organizing recent advancements into an accessible framework, highlighting both technical and practical considerations. We present a structured overview of methods, challenges, and solutions, aiming to support researchers and practitioners in developing reliable and explainable AI solutions for digital health. This review article is organized into three main parts. First, we introduce the pillars of trustworthy AI and discuss the technical and ethical challenges, particularly in the context of digital health. Second, we explore application-specific trust considerations across domains such as intensive care, neonatal health, and metabolic health, highlighting how robustness and explainability support trust. Lastly, we present recent advancements in techniques aimed at improving robustness under data scarcity and distributional shifts, as well as explainable AI methods ranging from feature attribution to gradient-based interpretations and counterfactual explanations. This paper is further enriched with detailed discussions of the contributions toward robustness and explainability in digital health, the development of trustworthy AI systems in the era of LLMs, and various evaluation metrics for measuring trust and related parameters such as validity, fidelity, and diversity.

</details>


### [220] [Homebot: A Personal AI Agent for Conversational Home Assistance and Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02254)
*Shengyuan Ye,Yixin Zhang,Han Liang,Liekang Zeng,Jiangsu Du,Mu Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Homebot 是一套面向家庭的本地可部署对话代理，结合本地唤醒词、流式 ASR/TTS、对话状态协议与工具/技能注册，侧重隐私、免提使用与可定制性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为家庭环境提供一个本地可部署的、支持语音与即时消息的对话式助理，兼顾隐私、可定制性与免提操作能力，通过将语言模型响应与注册的工具、任务特定技能结合，简化日常家庭自动化和查询操作。

Method: 架构上使用共享运行时负责调度：自然语言请求经 LLM 处理并可调用注册工具/技能；会话层按通道/聊天隔离消息历史，语音交互通过本地唤醒词触发并启用流式 ASR + TTS；引入显式对话状态协议（结束/跟进/继续）以支持免提场景；为工具与技能定义清晰的契约以便安全、安全沙箱和易定制扩展。

Result: 提出并实现了 Homebot 的设计要点：共享运行时将 LLM 响应与工具/技能注册整合；会话所有权与请求处理分离（消息按频道/聊天隔离，语音受唤醒词约束）；支持本地唤醒词检测、流式语音识别与合成；明确定义对话状态协议以结束/跟进/继续会话；并提供通道、工具与技能契约以便定制。

Conclusion: 该设计在隐私保护、免提交互与模块化定制方面具有优势，但要落地仍需关注权限与安全、上下文管理与多用户歧义、低延迟部署与鲁棒性评估；后续工作应补充评测、用户研究与工程细节。

Abstract: \texttt{Homebot} is a locally deployable AI agent for conversational household assistance and automation. It accepts voice and instant-messaging requests through a shared runtime that combines language-model responses with registered tools and task-specific skills. The design separates common request processing from session ownership: messaging history remains scoped to a channel and chat, whereas voice interaction is bounded by wake-word activation. For hands-free use, \texttt{Homebot} combines local wake-word detection, streaming speech recognition and synthesis, and an explicit dialogue-state protocol for ending, following up, or continuing a conversation. Clear channel, tool, and skill contracts support practical customization for household use.

</details>


### [221] [Self-Certification of Representation Adequacy: Sequential Certification at Minimum Task Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02267)
*Zijie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一个层次化的自证表示充分性理论：静态判定+一次性检验，序贯认证的下界与渐近最优策略，以及表示/策略切换的边界示例与未决结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是：当智能体在压缩历史表示上决策时，表示可能把不同最优动作的历史弄混（别名化），导致不可避免的单位时间损失且智能体自身可能无法从自身轨迹中察觉这种不足，因而需要理论化“自证表示充分性”的检测与量化。

Method: 方法包括：静态层通过Bayes风险分组恒等式和基于全变差距离的一次性外部检验来定义表示充分性；序贯层把认证建成以任务损失为货币的最优停时问题，构造一个覆盖型线性规划来给出环境依赖的认证复杂度常数，证明信息-任务损失的下界，并设计一个Certification Track-and-Stop策略以渐近匹配下界；边界层通过显式核切换例子展示边界并指出扩展到策略/表示切换所需的未决定理。

Result: 主要结果为：静态的决策理论充分性定义与基于总变差的外部检验门槛；序贯层的认证复杂度常数定义（覆盖线性规划）、对任意δ-正确策略的信息-任务损失下界证明，以及构造的Track-and-Stop策略在代价上渐近达到下界；并给出核切换示例说明固定核外的挑战与未决问题。

Conclusion: 论文结论：提出并形式化了一个四层“自证表示充分性”的理论框架，给出静态判定、序贯认证的问题形式化与上下界，并构造了渐近最优的序贯认证策略；同时指出在表示切换或修复情形下仍需额外理论结果。

Abstract: Agents that act on a compressed representation of their history face a structural risk: if the representation aliases histories with different optimal actions, no rule measurable with respect to the representation can avoid an irreducible per-round loss, and the agent may be unable to detect this from its own transcript. This paper develops a four-layer theory of self-certification of representation adequacy. The static layer defines decision-theoretic adequacy through a Bayes-risk grouping identity and prices a one-shot external verification by an exact total-variation threshold. The sequential layer poses certification as an optimal-stopping problem in the currency of task loss: we define an environment-wise certification complexity constant through a covering linear program, prove an information-task-loss lower bound for every delta-correct strategy, and give a Certification Track-and-Stop policy whose cost matches the bound asymptotically. A final boundary layer gives an explicit kernel-switching example and identifies the open theorem needed to cover policy switching or representation repair; it does not claim that the fixed-kernel guarantees extend to representation revision. The proofs of the two main theorems are given in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [222] [Harness-R1: Learning to Edit Executable Runtime Harnesses from Agent Failure Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02276)
*Shuai Shao,Kangning Zhang,Qingyao Li,Shijian Wang,Hao Wang,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Yi Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Harness-R1——一个以RL训练的“harness engineer”，在线编辑并修复已部署代理的执行运行时（harness），通过对失败样本批次产生可执行补丁并在冷启动的固定目标上复跑以获取真实成效奖励，实现生命周期级、按失败触发的可学习编辑。实验在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench上对Qwen3.5-9B带来显著成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署期间不断积累的交互轨迹包含修正可执行harness的信号：与直接改权重相比，编辑harness可以更安全、快速地修复执行流程、工具调用与恢复逻辑，从而提升任务成功率。

Method: 训练一个专门的harness engineer：先用监督学习冷启动编辑策略（将失败轨迹映射为补丁），然后用在线强化学习（group-relative policy optimization）基于同批次在冻结目标上复跑后的任务成功作为奖励继续训练。生成的补丁在同批次内被验证为可执行，训练仅更新编辑者，不改变目标模型。

Result: 在原始Qwen3.5-9B上，Harness-R1将平均成功率从44.3%提升到53.6%（+9.3个百分点）。在对目标模型做直接微调后，使用目标专属的engineer进一步将平均成功率从59.2%提升到64.2%（+5.0个百分点）。

Conclusion: Harness-R1 展示了通过学习编辑代理的harness来提升已部署大型语言模型代理成功率的可行路径；该方法与直接微调目标互补，可实现协同进化。

Abstract: Agents built around large language models continually accumulate interaction trajectories during deployment, yet their behavior typically remains fixed. Beyond updating model weights, these trajectories can improve the agent harness that constructs context, mediates tools, validates actions, and recovers execution. We introduce Harness-R1, the first method, to our knowledge, that makes failure-conditioned, lifecycle-wide editing of an existing executable runtime a learned capability. It post-trains a dedicated harness engineer with online reinforcement learning so that its edits are optimized for the realized task success they produce, rather than proposed by a fixed editor. A separate 9B engineer converts batches of target-agent failures into validated executable patches; fresh same-batch reruns of the frozen target provide outcome rewards, so training updates only the engineer. Cold-start supervised fine-tuning initializes this editing policy, which is then trained online with group-relative policy optimization. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and DBBench, Harness-R1 raises vanilla Qwen3.5-9B success from 44.3% to 53.6% (+9.3 percentage points). After direct target-agent fine-tuning, a target-specific engineer raises the average further from 59.2% to 64.2% (+5.0 points); because these gains hold both before and after fine-tuning the target, Harness-R1 points toward co-evolving the harness engineer and the target agent.

</details>


### [223] [SKT: Skill-Use Training at Scale via Verified Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02287)
*Zelin Tan,Yiqun Zhang,Hao Li,Zhiyao Cui,Hejia Geng,Shao Zhang,Hangfan Zhang,Yang Chen,Xiaosong Wang,Lilong Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Shuyue Hu,Chen Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKT generates verified, skill-grounded tasks and trajectories from many skills; fine-tuning on this data improves agents' ability to use skills, validated by a new SkillEval benchmark.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent skills are reusable procedural modules for language-model agents, but models struggle to identify, apply, and coordinate them. The paper aims to improve skill-use capabilities by generating verified training data that grounds skills in tasks and executable trajectories.

Method: SKT selects single- and multi-skill configurations, synthesizes tasks, uses rule-based and agent-based verification with feedback-guided repair, and retains only trajectories that substantially use required skills; it outputs task packages and verified trajectories and is used to fine-tune models and build SkillEval.

Result: They introduce SKT, a verified data synthesis pipeline producing 4,000 task packages and 27,164 verified trajectories from 2,000 public skills, and create SkillEval, a held-out executable benchmark. Fine-tuning on SKT data improves model skill-use across models and harnesses; gains rely on verification and scale with skill coverage.

Conclusion: Verified data synthesis via SKT is an effective scalable method for training language-model agents to identify, apply, and coordinate skills; high-quality verification and broad skill coverage are key.

Abstract: Agent skills have become an important mechanism for equipping language-model agents with reusable procedural knowledge. However, providing skills alone does not guarantee that current models can effectively identify, apply, and coordinate them. To improve skill-use capabilities, we introduce SKT, a verified data synthesis pipeline that constructs skill-grounded tasks and executable trajectories from large collections of agent skills. SKT selects suitable single-skill and multi-skill configurations, synthesizes tasks through rule-based and agent-based verification with feedback-guided repair, and retains only successful trajectories that substantially use every required skill. Using 2,000 public skills, SKT produces 4,000 task packages and 27,164 verified trajectories. Based on the same pipeline and a disjoint test pool, we further construct SkillEval, a held-out executable benchmark for evaluating skill use. Experiments across diverse models, benchmarks, and agent harnesses show that supervised fine-tuning on SKT-generated trajectories consistently improves skill-use performance. Verification ablations, cross-harness evaluation, and scaling experiments further demonstrate that these gains depend on high-quality supervision, extend beyond a single agent interface, and increase with broader skill coverage. Together, these results establish verified data synthesis as an effective and scalable approach for skill-use training.

</details>


### [224] [Shared Prefixes, Better Credit: Adaptive Routing for Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02291)
*Yiqing Liu,Zihao Wang,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TreeCredit assigns credits by comparing state-matched continuations in a shared-prefix tree, trains a lightweight router with pairwise state-local preferences, and improves MAR efficiency and accuracy–cost balance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing adaptive MAR approaches rely on coarse supervision (query-level labels or trajectory-level returns) that cannot accurately estimate the state-conditioned utility of individual operators in multi-step multi-agent collaborations; a finer-grained, state-matched credit assignment is needed to guide routing decisions.

Method: TreeCredit framework: builds shared-prefix collaboration trees by expanding candidate operators from the same intermediate state; assigns each state-operator pair a correctness-prioritized suffix credit based on terminal correctness and cumulative additional cost of the complete continuation; converts structured suffix credits into state-local pairwise operator preferences to train a lightweight pairwise state router that dynamically selects the next admissible operator at inference.

Result: Across six reasoning benchmarks, TreeCredit yields modest accuracy improvements while substantially reducing inference cost, achieving a superior accuracy–cost trade-off compared to representative adaptive MAR baselines.

Conclusion: State-conditioned, shared-prefix credit assignment provides more precise operator utility estimates, enabling dynamic, cost-effective routing in MAR. The method is promising for broader MAR settings and invites further study on credit formulations, scalability, and end-to-end integration.

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning (MAR) improves reasoning reliability through iterative solution exchange and refinement. Existing adaptive MAR methods typically learn routing decisions from query-level labels or trajectory-level returns, but such coarse supervision cannot accurately estimate the state-conditioned utility of individual operators in multi-step collaboration. We propose TreeCredit, a shared-prefix credit assignment framework for efficient adaptive MAR. Its core insight is to estimate operator utility through state-matched downstream comparisons, rather than directly attributing trajectory-level outcomes to preceding decisions. TreeCredit constructs shared-prefix collaboration trees by expanding candidate operators from the same intermediate state and assigns each state--operator pair a correctness-prioritized suffix credit based on the terminal correctness and cumulative additional cost of its complete continuation. These structured credits are converted into state-local operator preferences to train a lightweight pairwise state router, which dynamically selects the next admissible operator during inference. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that TreeCredit modestly improves accuracy while substantially reducing inference cost, achieving a better accuracy--cost trade-off than representative MAR methods.

</details>


### [225] [MechGeo: Autoformalizing and Proving Euclidean Geometry in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02295)
*Hao Shen,Junyu Guo,Tian Cui,Yuxuan Xiao,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MechGeo 是一个结合自动形式化、反例驱动修复和经核验证代数化证明的 Lean 4 原生框架，在多种 LLM 和 IMO 题集上显著提高了几何题的自动证明率并能生成 Lean 内核检查的证明与反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服人工作量大且易错的手工形式化流程，建立一个对复杂几何命题能给出可信（由交互式证明内核检验）自动形式化与证明的实用体系，使得高难度竞赛题目（如 IMO 题）能被自动形式化并自动或半自动证明，从而推动可信可复现的数学自动化研究。

Method: 构建两阶段 agent：GeoFormalizer（GeoIR 表示 + 确定性从 GeoIR 到 Lean4 的翻译 + 结构性诊断与语义评估驱动的迭代修复），GeoProver（基于几何证明规划产生中间引理，选择性把适合子目标代数化，使用经 Lean 验证的库将代数化子目标交给 Singular/SymPy 生成代数证书；最终所有证明与反例由 Lean 内核检验）。结合多种 LLM backbones 做翻译与修复策略，并用反例作为反馈闭环提高形式化质量。

Result: MechGeo 提出一个面向欧氏几何的端到端 Mathlib 原生 agent 框架，联合解决可信的自动形式化和经核验证的证明构造。系统包含 GeoFormalizer（把非正式问题表示为 GeoIR、确定性翻译为 Lean 4、以及基于结构诊断和语义评估的迭代修复）和 GeoProver（构建几何证明计划、生成中间引理，并把合适子目标代数化，通过 Lean 验证的库调用 Singular/SymPy 生成代数证书，所有证明与反例最终经 Lean 内核检验）。在七个 LLM backbone 上的实验显示显著提升：43 道历史 IMO 几何题中，GeoFormalizer 生成的形式化命题有 29 道被 GeoProver 证明，剩余 14 道构造出 Lean 验证的反例，专家修正后全部通过；在 LEAP 的 Lean-IMO-Bench 的 14 道几何题中，MechGeo 首次证明 12 道，形式反驳 2 道并修正后证明成功。总的来说，方法把反例引导诊断、几何推理与经核验证的符号计算结合，成为可信形式化几何的实用基础。

Conclusion: 论文在欧氏几何自动形式化与经核验证证明方面做出实质性进步：系统化地把翻译、诊断修复、证明计划和受核验证的代数证书串联，实验覆盖高难度竞赛题并取得领先结果。缺点在于领域局限（欧氏几何）、对后端代数工具与 LLM 的依赖、以及需要更多消融/可重复性细节来评估可扩展性与稳健性。总体为值得重视且可用于后续扩展的工作。

Abstract: We present MechGeo, a Mathlib native agentic framework that jointly addresses faithful autoformalization and certified proof construction for Euclidean geometry. In this framework, GeoFormalizer represents informal problems in GeoIR, deterministically translates them into Lean 4, and iteratively repairs candidate statements using structural diagnostics and semantic evaluation. GeoProver constructs geometric proof plans, derives intermediate lemmas, and selectively algebraizes suitable subgoals through a library verified in Lean. Singular or SymPy may generate algebraic certificates, but all resulting proofs and counterexamples are checked by Lean's kernel. Experiments across seven LLM backbones show substantial improvements in autoformalization, particularly for models with weaker direct translation performance. On 43 historical IMO geometry problems, GeoFormalizer generates formal statements that GeoProver proves in 29 cases; for the remaining 14, it constructs counterexamples verified in Lean and proves all repaired statements after expert correction. Together with IMO 2026 Problem 2, this yields, to the best of our knowledge, the largest reported collection of automated, kernel-checked Lean proofs for IMO geometry problems. On the 14 geometry statements in LEAP's Lean-IMO-Bench, MechGeo proves 12 for the first time, formally refutes the remaining two, and proves both repaired statements. These results establish counterexample guided diagnosis, geometric reasoning, and certified symbolic computation as a practical foundation for trustworthy formal geometry.

</details>


### [226] [Hard Constraints, Smooth Gradients: Learning Feasible Inventory Policies via Differentiable Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02343)
*Patrick Helm,Jan-Niklas Doerr,Joren Gijsbrechts,Stefan Minner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将可微凸投影与对偶引导整数映射集成到DRL策略中，以严格处理联互约束的组合动作空间，实证显示在产库存规划上优于既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹问题中常见的联互硬约束和巨大的离散动作空间使得MILP在随机环境下难以扩展，传统DRL方法要么放松约束要么依赖脆弱的可行性机制；因此需要兼顾可行性保障与可训练性的混合方法。

Method: 神经网络产生连续动作目标；通过凸二次规划（QP）将目标投影到约束的线性放松可行域；使用利用QP对偶信息的整数映射将解映回整数空间，确保可行性；在可微模拟器下用路径梯度端到端训练。

Result: 本文提出将可微分凸优化模块嵌入策略网络：网络输出连续动作目标，二次规划（QP）将其投影到放松可行集合，随后基于对偶信息的整数映射恢复整数并保持可行性。策略在可微模拟器下通过路径梯度端到端训练，并就可行性给出有界误差保证与可达性证明。应用于多阶段产库存规划，取得小实例<1%最优差距，并在较大网络和工业规模案例中优于基准策略（最高节约3.22%）。

Conclusion: 方法在保持硬约束可行性的同时实现可训练、可扩展的决策规则，并在产库存规划上提供显著成本改进；但对可微模拟器、计算开销及整数映射细节存在依赖与潜在局限。

Abstract: Many operational problems are constrained sequential decision processes with large, combinatorial action spaces and interdependent feasibility constraints. Mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) handle such constraints flexibly but scale poorly in stochastic environments. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) promises scalable decision rules, but existing methods either penalize constraints rather than enforce them, or rely on feasibility mechanisms that break down once constraints interact. We bridge this gap by embedding a differentiable convex optimization module inside the policy: a neural network proposes continuous action targets, a quadratic program projects them onto the relaxed feasible set, and a dual-informed integer mapping restores integrality while preserving feasibility. Given a differentiable simulator, the policy trains end to end from sampled trajectories using pathwise gradients, while handling hard constraints with similar flexibility to MILPs. We show that our feasibility enforcement has bounded error relative to an exact integer projection and ensures the entire feasible action space is reachable. We apply the method to multi-echelon production-inventory planning under shared resource and material constraints. Our policy attains an average optimality gap below 1% on small instances. It further outperforms state-of-the-art echelon base-stock policies by up to 9.75% and a rolling-horizon multi-stage stochastic program by at least 7.7% in larger networks. On an industry-scale case study from ASML, it reduces average cost by up to 3.22% relative to the best-known benchmark policy. The savings are largest where planning is hardest: in tightly capacitated systems with high demand variability. More broadly, our work shows that DRL can deliver economically significant savings in sequential decision problems with interdependent hard constraints, which are widespread in practice.

</details>


### [227] [Mamba with Hierarchical Memory: Solving Representation Bottleneck in Long Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02347)
*Qinwen Wang,Jieping Luo,Aoxiang Qin,Ruoyu Zhao,Jianxiong Tang,Wei Zhang,Zhichao Lu,Luziwei Leng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HMM在Mamba上增设工作记忆与长期记忆层次，低成本显著提升长序列检索与推理性能并实现跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recurrent linear attention模型（如Mamba）在时间线性复杂度上高效，但其固定容量的递归状态限制了长序列建模能力；受人类分层记忆系统启发，期望通过层次化语义存储与检索扩展长期上下文能力。

Method: 基于预训练的Mamba（感官记忆）隐藏态，增加一个轻量级工作记忆模块来抽取慢变化的段落级语义（PLS），将PLS压缩入持久的长期记忆以供任务相关检索；通过参数化的记忆学得和层次化处理实现跨任务泛化。

Result: 在Passkey Retrieval与LongBench-E数据集上，相较于强基线的Mamba变体，检索成功率提升34.3–37.1%，推理准确率提升1.6–14.2%，仅增加约2%的参数且训练开销极小；且声称实现了其他长上下文Mamba变体未具备的跨任务参数化泛化能力。

Conclusion: HMM通过在预训练Mamba骨干上引入分层记忆（工作记忆抽取段落级语义并压缩为长期记忆），有效突破了RLA固定容量的表征瓶颈，在检索与长上下文推理任务上带来显著提升，同时仅增加极少参数与训练开销。

Abstract: Recurrent linear attention models (RLAs) such as Mamba offer efficient linear-time sequence modeling as an alternative to Transformers, yet their fixed-capacity recurrent states limit long-sequence modeling. Drawing inspiration from hierarchical human memory, we propose Hierarchical Memory Mamba (HMM) to address this limitation. Building upon a pre-trained Mamba backbone, HMM integrates a lightweight working memory that extracts slow paragraph-level semantics (PLS) from the fast sensory memory embedded in the backbone's hidden states. The PLS is subsequently compressed into persistent long-term memory for task-relevant retrieval. The hierarchical processing of semantic information overcomes the representation bottleneck of RLAs and endows HMM cross-task generalization through parametric learning, which is not observed in other long-context enhanced Mamba variants. Evaluations on Passkey Retrieval and LongBench-E tasks demonstrate that HMM improves retrieval success by 34.3--37.1% and reasoning accuracy by 1.6--14.2% over strong Mamba-based models, while adding only 2% extra parameters and with minimal training overhead.

</details>


### [228] [KC-Agent: A Dual-Process Cognitive Architecture for Efficient ML Model Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02351)
*Gusseppe Bravo-Rocca,Jordi Guitart,Ajay Dholakia,David Ellison,Puneet Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KC-Agent 用双流程+结构化记忆解决数据漂移问题，在准确率与效率间取得平衡，并提供原子更新与回滚保证，实验显示显著优于若干现有认知型代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境下数据漂移频发，要求模型既能快速响应（低延迟、低成本）又能进行可靠、可验证的更新；现有自动化改进方法要么昂贵重算要么缺乏稳健回滚与记忆重用能力。

Method: 提出由 System 1（基于记忆的快速模式识别）与 System 2（增量、可验证的策略搜索/模型更新）组成的协同框架；System 2 发现并验证解决方案后将成功策略写入结构化记忆，System 1 在相似情形下直接复用以快速响应。引入原子变更与回滚保障更新可靠性，并实现知识巩固以加速后续推理。

Result: 在五个数据集（含真实 NASA turbofan 时序退化）上的实证表明：KC-Agent 达到 76.8% 准确率，13.2s 执行时间；相较于 CodeAct/Tree of Thoughts/ReAct/Reflexion 分别提高约 2.4%、3.6%、8.0%、8.9%；由 LLM 评审得到 Smartness 8.33/10；知识巩固比慢变体加速 91% 且保持更高准确率。

Conclusion: KC-Agent 通过双过程认知架构（快速的 System 1 与审慎的 System 2），结合结构化记忆与知识巩固，实现对生产环境中数据漂移的高效且可靠模型改进，既能保持较高精度，又能显著降低重算开销，并提供原子性变更与回滚机制以利生产部署。

Abstract: Data drift poses significant challenges for machine learning systems in production, requiring continuous model updates to maintain performance. We present KC-Agent, a dual-process cognitive architecture for automated ML model improvement that combines fast pattern recognition (System 1) with deliberate incremental updates (System 2). Our approach implements structured memory systems enabling System 1 to leverage successful solutions previously discovered by System 2, achieving efficient pattern-based responses without costly re-computation. KC-Agent incorporates atomic change principles and rollback capabilities to ensure reliable, verifiable updates in production environments. We evaluate our method on five datasets including real-world NASA turbofan data with authentic temporal degradation and synthetic datasets with controlled drift scenarios. KC-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance (76.8% accuracy) while maintaining optimal efficiency (13.2s execution time), outperforming established cognitive architectures: CodeAct (+2.4%), Tree of Thoughts (+3.6%), ReAct (+8.0%), and Reflexion (+8.9%). Consensus evaluation by a panel of state-of-the-art LLMs confirms superior strategic efficacy (8.33/10 Smartness score), significantly outperforming baseline agents. The knowledge consolidation mechanism delivers 91% speedup over the slow variant while maintaining higher accuracy. Our approach demonstrates both theoretical foundations and practical viability for cognitive-inspired automated ML improvement systems capable of handling complex real-world data drift scenarios.

</details>


### [229] [SkillTrace: Traversing a Query-Skill Graph for Composable LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02356)
*Yue Yao,Shengyuan Wang,Xin Chen,Minke Zhang,Jia He,Bingjun Luo,Tom Gedeon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillTrace，一种基于三层图（查询的组合关系、查询与候选技能的相似度、候选技能间的依赖关系）的技能检索与组合方法：将用户查询构建成语义层次、匹配技能候选并在依赖图上传播，从而检索出完整可执行的技能组合。在SkillsBench与ALFWorld上分别取得53.17%和91.43%的成功率，并在不同主干大模型上均有稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单独检索与排序相关技能无法保证得到完整且可执行的技能序列；需要同时考虑查询的组合结构与技能间的依赖关系，才能构造可执行的技能组合，从而更好地解决复杂任务。

Method: 构建一个三层图：1) 将用户查询解析为语义层次以刻画组合关系；2) 计算查询子项与技能库候选的相似度以得到候选匹配；3) 建立候选技能间的依赖边并在此图上进行传播与筛选以保证可执行性。整体流程为：语义解析→相似性匹配→依赖传播与组合选择。使用不同主干大模型作为编码/匹配器，在SkillsBench与ALFWorld上做对比实验与消融验证。

Result: 在两个基准上均达到或超过现有最优：SkillsBench成功率53.17%、ALFWorld成功率91.43%；在不同主干大模型上均有一致性提升，验证了方法的通用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在技能库检索与组合场景下，通过将问题建模为多层图并联合考虑组合结构、相似性匹配与依赖传播，可以显著提升检索到完整且可执行技能序列的能力，具有较好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve complex tasks by composing reusable skills from a library. To address this, the key challenge is not merely to retrieve individually relevant skills, but to identify a complete and executable skill composition. In this paper, we argue that this problem can be solved in a graph with three levels: compositional relations among skill queries, similarity between queries and candidates in the skill library, and the dependencies among the selected candidates. We introduce SkillTrace, which organizes the user query into a semantic hierarchy, matches skill queries and candidates, and propagates over the skill dependencies. Experiments on SkillsBench and ALFWorld demonstrate that SkillTrace achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching a success rate of 53.17% on SkillsBench and 91.43% on ALFWorld. SkillTrace also delivers consistent improvements across different backbone language models, demonstrating the generality and robustness of graph-based skill retrieval.

</details>


### [230] [Faster-WAM: Do World Action Models Need Deep Action Modules?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02365)
*Liheng Ma,Rui Heng Yang,Zhanguang Zhang,Mateo Clemente,Ziwen Hu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过把预训练视频Transformer作为表示枢纽并“停靠”轻量任务头，DoT/Faster-WAM在保持动作预测性能和泛化能力的同时，大幅降低推理延迟，适合低延迟、灵活头设计的机器人感知预测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models通常将动作模块深度与视频骨干绑定（共享骨干或MoT设计），导致动作模块深且计算开销大、推理延迟高。需要一种能灵活设计任务专用头、并降低推理成本的体系结构。

Method: 提出Dock of Transformer (DoT)设计原则：把预训练视频Transformer作为表示中心，通过轻量输出头通过停靠接口连接，允许输出头直接访问骨干网络所有层的键/值并进行RoPE（旋转位置编码）对齐。基于此，设计Faster-WAM——在30层视频骨干上停靠单层动作头，融合所有视频层的k/v并应用RoPE重对齐，实现极短的端到端推理延迟。

Result: Faster-WAM在未做额外机器人领域预训练的情况下，在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上达到有竞争力的性能，在LIBERO-Plus上表现出较强的分布外泛化，并在受控比较中以66.5 ms/次的端到端延迟达到最低——比Fast-WAM快约3.2倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的视频中心化DoT架构与其在WAM上的实例Faster-WAM，证明了将预训练视频Transformer作为表示枢纽并通过轻量“停靠”头访问所有层表示，可以在显著降低推理延迟的同时保持或接近现有方法的动作预测性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple robot action prediction with video world models. Existing WAMs with shared-backbone and Mixture-of-Transformers designs generally tie the depth of the action module to that of the video backbone, resulting in substantial computational overhead and high inference latency. To address this limitation, we introduce Dock of Transformer (DoT), a video-centric design principle that treats a pretrained video Transformer as a representation hub and connects lightweight output-heads through docking interfaces. This enables flexible output-head design while providing direct access to representations from all layers of the backbone. We then introduce \textbf{Faster-WAM}, an instantiation of DoT for WAMs, which docks a single-layer action head onto a 30-layer video backbone. The docking interface fuses keys and values from all video layers and applies RoPE realignment. Without additional embodied pretraining, Faster-WAM achieves competitive performance on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 while demonstrating strong out-of-distribution generalization on LIBERO-Plus. Faster-WAM also achieves the lowest end-to-end latency in our controlled comparison, requiring only 66.5 ms per inference --- a \(3.2\times\) speedup over Fast-WAM. Overall, these results demonstrate that the video-centric DoT architecture supports flexible task-specific head design while delivering low inference latency, strong action-prediction performance, and robust generalization.

</details>


### [231] [Chess on Ice: Curling Tactical Decision-Making via Backward Induction and Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02379)
*Patrick Oberlin,Matteo Cederle,Aren Karapetyan,Saverio Bolognani,Gian Antonio Susto,Florian Dörfler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于强化学习的框架（DDPG 变体）来在连续、随机且对微小动作敏感的冰壶博弈中学习战术策略，在四石简化变体上自监督训练，策略表现可与手工专家启发式相匹配；学到的价值网络能对连续动作空间提供密集估值，便于战术比较与赛后分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰壶的战术决策复杂但在机器学习领域研究稀少；希望提供一个能在连续且随机的物理博弈中量化并比较战术选择的工具，弥补仅有统计方法的不足，为赛后分析与训练决策支持提供量化依据。

Method: 将 Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient（DDPG）演员-评论家算法适配到有限回合结构上以处理连续状态/动作、带噪声的动作结果和对执行扰动敏感的状态转移；通过完全自监督的模拟对弈在简化四石规则上训练策略与价值网络，并与手工启发式进行定量对比。

Result: 在四石变体下，训练出的策略在启发式近似最优的情形与该启发式表现齐平；价值网络在连续动作空间中输出密集估值，可用于评估不同战术选择的相对优劣，并通过与变体的锤子优势比较来量化策略质量。

Conclusion: 在受限的四石变体中，使用经适配的 DDPG 可在无人工标注下学习出与专家启发式相当的策略；同时学到的评论家（价值函数）能用于连续动作的定量比较，具备实际分析与备赛辅助的潜力。

Abstract: Curling is often referred to as "Chess on Ice", owing to the tactical complexity of its decision-making process. Yet unlike chess, curling remains largely underexplored from a machine learning perspective, with prior work confined mainly to statistical approaches. We propose a reinforcement learning framework capable of quantitatively evaluating and comparing tactical options in curling. The game poses several modeling challenges: continuous state and action spaces, stochastic action outcomes reflecting player skill variability, and state transitions that are highly sensitive to small perturbations in the executed action. To address them, we employ the Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient actor-critic algorithm, adapted to exploit the finite-horizon structure of the game. Our experiments show that effective curling strategies can be acquired in a fully self-supervised manner, without any human-annotated data: on a reduced four-rock variant, the learned agent matches a hand-crafted expert heuristic in a regime where that heuristic is close to optimal, a parity we quantify against the intrinsic hammer advantage of the variant. Beyond the resulting policy, the learned critic provides a dense value estimate over the entire continuous action space, enabling the quantitative comparison of tactical alternatives for applications such as post-game performance analysis and decision support during athlete preparation.

</details>


### [232] [Cooperative Coevolution for Resource-Constrained Agentic LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02391)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Shengcai Liu,Jiahao Wu,Ning Lu,Hui Ouyang,Shaofeng Zhang,Haoze Lv,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoPES uses cooperative coevolutionary ES over parameter subspaces to enable memory-light, competitive post-training of tool-using LLM agents, achieving near-GRPO performance with much lower GPU memory and better efficiency than standard ES.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents produce long trajectories that make gradient-based post-training memory-intensive. Evolution strategies avoid backpropagation and reduce memory but require many GPU-hours. Need methods that reduce training time and memory footprint for resource-limited settings.

Method: Introduce Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES): a cooperative coevolutionary ES that splits the full model parameter space into lower-dimensional subspaces and optimizes them cooperatively without backpropagation; applied to post-train a Qwen3.5-4B agent using ES-style search under constrained GPU-hours.

Result: On math and QA benchmarks: under the GPU-hour budget of full-parameter GRPO's best checkpoint, CoPES recovers 92% of GRPO's validation-accuracy gain vs 67% for standard ES; theoretical GPU memory <1/8 of full-parameter GRPO; consistently outperforms standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO on pass@k metrics across five benchmarks; demonstrated advantage on question-answering tasks.

Conclusion: CoPES provides a memory-efficient and time-effective way to post-train tool-using LLM agents by decomposing full parameter space into cooperating low-dimensional subspaces, recovering most of gradient-based gains while requiring far less GPU memory and outperforming standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO across evaluated benchmarks.

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents produce long, multi-turn trajectories, making gradient-based post-training memory-intensive. Evolution strategies (ES) enable memory-efficient full-parameter post-training without backpropagation and can eventually match the performance of gradient-based reinforcement learning (RL). However, resource-constrained settings typically offer only a few GPUs, so the high GPU-hour requirements of ES translate into prohibitively long training times. To address this, we introduce Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES), a cooperative coevolutionary method that decomposes the full parameter space into lower-dimensional subspaces and searches over them cooperatively to improve optimization efficiency. We post-train a Qwen3.5-4B tool-using agent for the math task and evaluate it on five benchmarks of varying difficulty. Under the GPU-hour budget of full-parameter GRPO's best validation checkpoint, CoPES recovers 92% of GRPO's validation-accuracy gain, versus 67% for standard ES, while its theoretical GPU memory requirement is less than one-eighth that of full-parameter GRPO. It consistently outperforms standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO on all evaluated pass@k metrics across the five benchmarks. Additional experiments further show the advantage of CoPES on the question-answering task. These results demonstrate an improved trade-off between memory requirements and training time for agentic LLM post-training under resource constraints. The code is open-sourced in https://github.com/MetaronWang/CoPES

</details>


### [233] [MonitrLLM: A Community-Centered Evaluation Infrastructure for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02409)
*Victor Ojewale,Ro Encarnación,Suresh Venkatasubramanian,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MonitrLLM——开源基础设施，将完整对话记录与用户上报的任务意图与结果评价一并作为主要评估信号，弥合现有基准、对话语料与界面反馈三者之间的缺口。进行了为期两周的可行性试点：26名大学生、206份评估报告，展示出对话轨迹与用户报告结果相连的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估手段各有侧重：基准套件控制任务、对话语料反映自然使用、界面反馈记录满意度，但没有常规化基础设施把交互轨迹与用户定义的任务结果直接关联，从而无法评估实际任务完成情况与对话过程的关系。

Method: 设计并发布 MonitrLLM 开源系统以收集三类主信号（完整对话转录、用户任务意图、用户结果评价）；通过两周可行性试点，26名参与者在使用 ChatGPT 时提交206份包含上述三类信息的报告进行分析。

Result: 参与者平均满意度为4.19/5，但任务失败率为23.1%。多轮对话的失败率是单轮的2.5倍，表明多轮常与困难相关。试点证明了该方法的可行性与揭示性，支持将用户结果报告纳入常规评估。

Conclusion: 将用户自报的任务意图与结果作为与对话记录同等重要的评估信号，能揭示传统满意度指标无法反映的问题（例如高满意度下的显著任务失败率），并提示多轮对话常常是困难的信号而非参与度的证据。

Abstract: Benchmark suites assess model capability on controlled tasks; large-scale conversation corpora capture naturalistic use without user feedback; and in-interface feedback mechanisms record satisfaction without task purpose. Together, they leave a critical gap in LLM evaluation: no existing infrastructure routinely links interaction trajectories to user-defined outcomes. We introduce MonitrLLM, open-source infrastructure for community-centered LLM evaluations that links full conversation transcripts to user-reported task intent and outcome assessments, treating all three as primary evaluative signals rather than optional metadata.
  To demonstrate the value of this approach, we conducted a two-week feasibility pilot with 26 college students using ChatGPT, collecting 206 evaluation reports with full conversation transcripts. The findings from our pilot demonstrate the value of connecting conversation trajectories with user-reported outcomes. For instance, despite reporting high average satisfaction (4.19/5) with their LLM interactions, participants also experience a substantial 23.1% failure rate on their goal tasks. We also find that multi-turn conversations are reported as failing at 2.5 times the rate of single-turn exchanges, a pattern that reframes extended interaction as a signal of difficulty rather than engagement. We conclude by discussing the value of incorporating direct user feedback with observational data for robust LLM evaluations, and the possibilities for infrastructure that enables this goal.

</details>


### [234] [xPress: Parallel Refinement for Diffusion Drafters in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02438)
*Zheng Wang,Davis Wertheimer,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Mudhakar Srivatsa,Raghu K. Ganti,Minjia Zhang,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: xPress restores causality to block-diffusion drafts via a cheap parallel refiner, substantially improving acceptance length and throughput for speculative decoding without reverting to token-by-token autoregression.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Block-diffusion drafters produce per-position marginal samplings that lack causal dependencies, causing drafts to be joint-improbable and frequently rejected during speculative decoding.

Method: Apply a lightweight causal refiner that operates on the whole diffused draft block in parallel to reconcile per-position marginals into a causally coherent sequence, propagating dependencies across positions without sequential token-by-token loops.

Result: xPress, a lightweight causal refiner that reconciles an entire diffusion-generated block via parallel refinement, increases acceptance length by ≈30% on average (up to +56%) and end-to-end decoding throughput by ≈1.3× on average (up to 1.7×) on Qwen3-8B across seven benchmarks vs. original dFlash.

Conclusion: Parallel causal refinement can recover missing inter-token dependencies in diffusion drafters, yielding longer accepted drafts and faster end-to-end decoding; xPress demonstrates practical gains on math, code, and chat tasks with modest overhead.

Abstract: Block-diffusion drafters like dFlash generate an entire block of draft tokens in a single forward pass, drastically reducing the overhead of multiple-token drafting in speculative decoding. The crucial final step of the single-pass discrete denoising process involves using the logit distribution at each position to sample conditionally independent tokens. The resulting draft is thus a set of per-position marginals, rather than a joint distribution: no draft token is guaranteed to depend on its predecessors. Such independently sampled marginals tend to produce sequences with tokens that are individually likely, but jointly improbable under the target model's distribution, which verifies each token conditionally. This can cause early rejection and limits acceptance length. To address this, we propose xPress as a means to restore the missing causality in diffusion drafters. xPress is a lightweight causal refiner that reconciles the whole diffusion block at once through parallel refinement, restoring and propagating causal dependencies across the draft without a token-by-token loop. On Qwen3-8B, across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, xPress raises acceptance length by about 30% on average (up to +56%) and its end-to-end decoding throughput by about 1.3 on average (up to 1.7) compared to the original dFlash diffusion drafter.

</details>


### [235] [Agentic Commerce World: An Auditable and Verifiable Environment for Vibe Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02441)
*Shicheng Fan,Mingdai Yang,Duohao Wang,Canyu Chen,Yongfeng Zhang,Hua Wei,Manling Li,Julian McAuley,Kun Zhang,Philip S. Yu,Kejing Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld) 与 Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP)，用于评估独立控制的 Buyer 与 Merchant 代理在保留私有目标与权限下的交互。基准包括 200 任务能力覆盖与 60 任务大目录两条赛道，展示了不同模型在过程级别证据上的差异与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将“vibe coding”思路扩展到商务场景：让用户用自然语言描述买卖目标并由独立 Buyer/ Merchant 代理执行，同时必须保证代理各自私有目标、权限隔离与市场交互的透明可审计性。

Method: 设计 ACWorld 环境与 VCP 协议，VCP 在更新共享交易状态前验证代理动作并记录交互，保证行为可审计与评估可重现。构建两条基准赛道（能力覆盖与大目录搜索），并用十个模型进行评测，记录跨阶段表现与得分。

Result: 在两条赛道上，十个模型的平均分分别为 65.9%–85.6%（能力覆盖）和 56.1%–91.4%（大目录）。分析表明单一最终状态不足以评估行为，轨迹信息与过程日志对检测错误与识别瓶颈至关重要。

Conclusion: 过程级证据不可或缺：仅看最终状态会漏评错误，未完成的轨迹仍含有有用信号，而大目录任务能暴露阶段性瓶颈。ACWorld 与 VCP 为可审计、可重现的代理间商务评估提供了实用平台。

Abstract: In vibe coding, people describe software in natural language and delegate implementation to AI agents. By analogy, vibe commerce allows people to express buying or selling goals in natural language and delegate the corresponding tasks to agents. Commerce, however, requires independently controlled Buyer and Merchant agents to interact in a shared market while preserving their private objectives and distinct authority. We introduce Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld), an environment for evaluating such agents across ongoing transactions. Through its Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP), ACWorld validates agent actions before updating shared transaction state and records the resulting interactions, making agent behavior auditable and evaluation reproducible. The ACWorld Benchmark contains a 200-task capability-coverage track and a 60-task large-catalog track that searches 785,022 transactable listings. Across ten models, mean scores range from 65.9% to 85.6% and from 56.1% to 91.4%, respectively. Our analysis shows that process-level evidence is necessary: final state alone can miss evaluated errors, incomplete trajectories still retain useful process signals, and large-catalog tasks expose bottlenecks across stages.

</details>


### [236] [Right Answer, Wrong Method: Shortcut Hacking Misleads the Evaluation of LLM Reasoning on Frontier Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02442)
*Xuan Ren,Weiqi Zhai,Tianle Pu,Yihua Zhu,Yihua Zhu,Hu Wei,Bing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 仅看最终答案会高估 LLM 的科学推理能力：许多“正确”答案是通过无效捷径得到的。引入自动判别与测试时指令能抑制这些捷径，暴露出更真实的推理水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 担心最终答案准确率并不能说明模型是否具备目标化科学推理能力，需要识别并抑制通过非推理捷径得出正确结论的行为，以建立更可信的能力评价。

Method: 系统性横向分析：在多难度（常见、奥赛级、HLE）与多学科数据集上测量‘被劫解’答案比例；构造检测器（专家启发的自动判官）与测试时指令以抑制捷径；比较抑制前后报告准确率与非劫解正确率的变化，量化捷径对评估的影响。

Result: Paper identifies and quantifies “Solution Hacking”, where LLMs reach correct final answers via invalid shortcuts (numerical search, enumeration, guessing, answer-first verification) without valid task-targeted derivations; reports hacking rates rising with difficulty (2.2% common → 28.3% Olympiad → 37.4% HLE) and that 8.2%–44.1% of credited correct answers across frontier models are hacked; develops anti-hacking strategies (automatic judge and test-time instruction) that reduce reported accuracy substantially while having smaller impact on non-hacked accuracy, concluding that answer-only evaluation overestimates scientific reasoning.

Conclusion: 基于最终答案的评估会系统性高估前沿 LLM 的科学推理能力。应将评估重心转向对过程（stepwise derivations）和推理有效性的验证；自动评判与测试时约束能抑制简单捷径并更准确反映可靠能力，但仍需人类标注、跨域验证与更鲁棒的判别器。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate large language models (LLMs) using final-answer accuracy. However, a correct answer does not necessarily demonstrate the reasoning capability targeted by the problem. We identify Solution Hacking, a failure mode in which an LLM reaches the correct answer through invalid shortcuts, such as numerical search, enumeration, guessing, or answer-first verification, without providing a valid task-targeted derivation. We systematically analyze this phenomenon across difficulty levels, scientific domains, and frontier models. Solution hacking increases sharply with benchmark difficulty, from 2.2\% on common problems to 28.3\% on Olympiad-level problems and 37.4\% on HLE. Moreover, 8.2\%-44.1\% of answers credited as correct across frontier models are identified as hacked solutions. We further develop expert-inspired anti-hacking strategies, including an automatic judge and a test-time instruction. The results show that suppressing shortcut behavior substantially reduces reported accuracy while having a smaller effect on correct and non-hacked accuracy. These findings reveal that answer-only evaluation can overestimate the scientific reasoning capabilities of frontier LLMs.

</details>


### [237] [ParEvalLayer: When Partial LLM-Agent Evaluations Support a Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02444)
*Wei-Jung Huang,Bonan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ParEvalLayer通过对成对任务结果应用预先指定的比较策略，在部分完成的基准运行中给出四类判断（显著更好/不更好/需更多证据/弃权），并能在许多情况下于观察到少量任务后就重现完成评估结论，提示应在报告中包含决策规则与未决比较数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当在LLM agent评估中提前产生部分结果时，直接报告部分分数可能会误导结论，因为早期任务可能遗漏基准关键部分或偏向简单任务。需要一种方法判断部分结果是否已经能代表完成评价的结论。

Method: 定义一个决策层，输入为两个系统成对任务结果与预先选择的比较策略；在每个部分运行点，判断是否已达成显著更好、显著不更好、需更多证据或弃权。通过回放已完成的基准数据，在每个中间点仅用已观察结果应用策略并与完整评估结果比对，统计达到一致结论所需的观察比例。

Result: ParEvalLayer是一种用于在LLM agent评估中对部分完成的基准测试运行做出可靠比较判断的决策层。该方法在每次部分运行时读取两个代理系统的成对任务结果，并基于事先指定的比较策略输出四种可能结论之一：被测系统显著更好、未显著更好、需要更多证据或应当弃权。论文通过回放已完成的公共基准数据进行评估：对每个中间点仅使用已观察到的结果应用比较策略，并将早期判断与完成后的实际判断比对。主要比较规则下，3个公共基准在仅观察15%至25%任务结果时就能达到与完成评价一致的结论；其他基准需要更多任务结果。该工作强调仅报告部分分数可能误导，建议报告同时包含所使用的比较规则及尚未决策的比较对数量。

Conclusion: ParEvalLayer能在部分基准运行时为成对系统比较提供可靠的早期决策，并在多数但非全部基准上显著减少所需任务数；因此，应在报告部分分数时同时公布比较规则与未决项数量以避免误导。

Abstract: LLM-agent evaluations often produce task outcomes long before the full benchmark run is complete. A partial score is tempting to report, but it does not show whether the observed tasks support the same conclusion as the completed evaluation. Early tasks can omit important parts of a benchmark, running cheaper tasks first can distort the observed sample, and a rule that decides only easy pairs can appear accurate while leaving many comparisons unresolved. We introduce ParEvalLayer, a decision layer that reads paired outcomes for two agent systems and a comparison policy chosen in advance. For each partial run, it records whether the tested agent system is better by the required amount, is not better by that amount, needs more evidence, or should abstain. We evaluate ParEvalLayer by replaying completed public benchmark data as if each evaluation had stopped earlier. At each point, ParEvalLayer applies the policy using only the outcomes observed so far; if it reaches one of the two comparison judgments, we check whether that judgment matches the completed data for the same system pair. With the main comparison rule, three of the public benchmarks reach the same decision as the completed evaluation after observing only 15% to 25% of task outcomes. Other benchmarks require more task outcomes. This variation shows why a partial score alone is not enough: reports should also state the decision rule and how many comparisons remain without a decision.

</details>


### [238] [Infinite Trace Objectives with Finite Trace Techniques: Translating LTL to LTLf+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02454)
*Christoph Weinhuber,Maximilian Prokop,Giuseppe De Giacomo,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了把 LTL 翻译为 LTLf+ 的首个方法：先将 LTL 规范化为 Manna–Pnueli 的反应性片段，再对各片段成分做线性翻译，从而能把 LTL 问题交由基于有限字自动机的 LTLf+ 工具链处理，且复杂度仍为双重指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL 广泛用于表述 AI 中的时序目标，但将 LTL 公式翻译为在无限字上的确定性自动机是理论与实践上的难点。LTLf+ 将有限轨迹逻辑 LTLf 提升到无限轨迹，保持与 LTL 等 expressive 能力的同时，能够基于有限字自动机利用其确定化和最小化优势。因此希望将 LTL 的问题桥接到 LTLf+，以利用更高效的工具链。

Method: 首先对任意 LTL 公式做语法上规范化，使其属于 Manna–Pnueli 层次中的反应性片段（即把公式分解为若干标准成分）；然后对该片段的每个成分给出线性大小的翻译规则，将其逐个映射为等价的 LTLf+ 子公式；最后将得到的 LTLf+ 公式翻译为基于有限字的自动机，证明整个管道的复杂度界为双重指数。

Result: 给出首个从 LTL 到 LTLf+ 的翻译。通过将 LTL 公式规范化为 Manna–Pnueli 层次结构中的语法反应性片段（syntactic reactivity fragment），构造 LTLf+ 的片段化形状，并为该片段的每个成分给出线性（线性大小）翻译。证明经由 LTLf+ 到自动机的整体流水线在渐近复杂度上仍为双重指数，因此没有额外的渐近代价。

Conclusion: 该翻译把 LTL 问题带入了以有限字自动机为中心的 LTLf+ 生态，允许利用已有的确定化与最小化技术来处理原先以无限字自动机为主的问题；尽管在最坏情况下复杂度未下降（仍为双重指数），但在实践中可借助 LTLf+ 的工具链带来工程与性能上的优势。

Abstract: Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) is one of the most widely adopted languages for specifying temporal extended objectives in AI, with applications ranging from reactive synthesis to stochastic planning in Markov decision processes and reinforcement learning. Traditionally, solving any of these problems requires translating the LTL specification to a nondeterministic automata on infinite words and then determinizing it, a step that is notoriously difficult in theory and in practice. Recent work has introduced LTLf+, which lifts the finite-trace logic LTLf to infinite traces. LTLf+ has the same expressive power as LTL, yet it retains most of the crucial advantages of its base logic LTLf. Most reasoning in LTLf+ rests on finite automata on finite words, for which we have not only a canonical minimal representation but also an efficient determinization procedure. In this work we present the first translation from LTL to LTLf+. We first normalize an LTL formula into the syntactic reactivity fragment of the Manna-Pnueli hierarchy, to create the general fragment-based shape of LTLf+. We then present linear translations for each individual component of that fragment. As a consequence of this translation, the expanding body of techniques developed for LTLf+ now becomes available to many AI problems currently formulated in LTL. We further show that this comes at no asymptotic cost, as the pipeline from LTL to automaton via LTLf+ remains doubly exponential.

</details>


### [239] [Long-term Measurements: Towards a Longitudinal Understanding of Human-AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02491)
*Nicole Mitchell,Dhruv Agarwal,Maty Bohacek,Remi Denton,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Proposes pivoting NLP evaluation toward long-term, diachronic measurement of human behavioral changes from model interactions, leveraging social science longitudinal methods to detect and mitigate risks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper claims that language models' human-like behavior and integration can cause longitudinal cognitive, developmental, socio-affective risks, motivating a shift to diachronic evaluation using social science measurements.

Method: Survey and conceptual framing: drawing from social science longitudinal measurement methods and proposing integration with NLP; advocating modeling behavioral shifts and online detection within alignment frameworks. No new empirical studies reported.

Result: Argues for integrating longitudinal measurements and proposes approaches but lacks empirical validation; highlights potential for online detection and steering models to positive outcomes.

Conclusion: Calls for combining computational NLP methods with social-science longitudinal measures to monitor and steer long-term human-model interaction outcomes; emphasizes online detection and alignment to reduce long-term risks.

Abstract: Language models have taken on the role of a very new type of technology, by virtue of their "human-ness" and rapid integration into users' daily lives. This combination of features can introduce longitudinal risks---cognitive, developmental and socio-affective changes in humans---that might not surface in short-term interactions, but can have lasting long-term effects on users. This forms the basis of a critical new mission for NLP: to pivot from static, short-term evaluations of text generations to long-term measurements of behavioral changes, towards a diachronic understanding of human-model interactions. In this work, we draw from measurements used in social science fields that are crucial to understand emergent phenomena in longitudinal data. We discuss how computational methods in the field of NLP need to be combined with such measurements, not only to understand long-term safety risks of human-model interactions, but to help steer model development towards positive rather than negative outcomes for users. This ability to model human behavioral shifts as a function of model interactions can facilitate online rather than post-hoc detection of problematic behaviors, and should be leveraged in alignment frameworks to mitigate long-term risks in users.

</details>


### [240] [CMuon: Accelerating and Stabilizing Diffusion Transformer Training via Chunked Momentum Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02502)
*Chuyan Chen,Peng Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对 DiT 中被融合的功能性权重进行分块正交化，能消除 Muon 导致的子空间耦合，显著加速训练并恢复后期收敛，实验证明在 ImageNet 256 上达到了 200 epoch 即 FID 1.18 的高效结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 作为 AdamW 的替代优化器在许多场景表现良好，但直接用于 DiT 时出现后期收敛停滞。作者追踪到这是架构性权重融合与正交化相互作用导致的隐式耦合问题，因此需要在优化器层面做结构感知的调整。

Method: 识别问题源自于 DiT 为计算效率将功能不同的权重（如 AdaLN、QKV）拼接成统一张量；在正交化时对整张量施加约束会引入跨功能子空间耦合。提出简单的分块策略（Chunked Muon），在对张量正交化前将其分割为独立子组件并分别正交化。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256 上，使用 CMuon 训练的 675M 参数 DiT 在 200 个 epoch 即达到 FID 1.18，较 AdamW 达成相同质量所需训练量减少超过 2 倍，并且比原始 Muon 消除了后期收敛平台期。

Conclusion: Chunked Muon (CMuon) 有效解决了将 Momentum Orthogonalization (Muon) 直接应用于 Diffusion Transformers（DiTs）时的后期收敛瓶颈，通过在正交化前把被融合的功能性权重分块独立处理，避免了隐式子空间耦合，从而恢复并提升全局优化性能。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in visual generative modeling, yet their training remains computationally prohibitive. While the recently proposed Momentum Orthogonalization (Muon) optimizer offers a promising alternative to AdamW, its direct application to DiTs yields suboptimal late-stage convergence. In this paper, we identify the root cause of this bottleneck: standard DiT architectures fuse functionally distinct weights (e.g., within AdaLN and QKV layers) into unified tensors for computational efficiency. Applying Muon to these fused tensors inadvertently induces implicit subspace coupling, which distorts update directions and degrades global optimization. To address this, we introduce Chunked Muon (CMuon), a simple yet highly effective strategy that partitions these matrices into independent sub-components prior to orthogonalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a 675M-parameter DiT trained with CMuon achieves a FID of 1.18 on ImageNet 256 in just 200 epochs. This represents more than a 2x training speedup over AdamW, while effectively overcoming the late-stage convergence plateaus of vanilla Muon.

</details>


### [241] [Abduction Without a Body? Representational Grounding and the Abduction Loop for Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02505)
*Michael Farmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Identity-focused scientific abduction can be achieved via representational grounding and convention-space canonicalization rather than ongoing sensorimotor embodiment; the paper presents an Abduction Loop architecture and a benchmark (DAB-30) to operationalize and test this claim.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Challenge claims that hypothesis generation requires continuous embodiment; show that representation transformations can expose latent invariants enabling identity inference across independently evolved structures and disciplines.

Method: Proposes the Abduction Loop architecture: (1) generate representations (e.g., diagrams), (2) extract motifs, (3) canonicalize into a convention space that normalizes symmetry/topology/operators, (4) retrieve cross-domain analogs, (5) propose identity hypotheses, (6) perform adversarial verification with abstention as default. Uses a documented episode (multimodal model mapping differential complex to Kaiser–Squires complex) as a motivating case and proposes the DAB-30 benchmark for evaluation.

Result: A mechanistic proposal (architecture), a formalization of 'convention space' to solve cross-domain retrieval, a motivating example, and a falsifiable evaluation program (DAB-30). No broad empirical claim—architecture abstracted from a single episode.

Conclusion: Online sensorimotor embodiment is not necessary for all forms of scientific abduction; a narrower form—identity abduction—can occur through representational grounding and cross-domain canonicalization without continuous bodily coupling.

Abstract: Can scientific abduction occur without continuous sensorimotor embodiment? Recent arguments in AI and philosophy of science hold that genuine hypothesis generation requires an agent continuously coupled to the physical world. We defend a narrower claim: online embodiment is not necessary for every abductive scientific act. Our focus is identity abduction: the inference that two independently developed structures are one object under an explicit correspondence, reached through representational grounding rather than bodily interaction. An agent may acquire new inferential affordances not through physical interaction but through transformations into representations that expose latent invariants. Scientific diagrams are a practical substrate because they embody independently evolved conventions that partially canonicalize symmetry, topology, and operator structure across disciplines - a property we develop as convention space, which answers a hard retrieval problem: finding mathematically related work when two fields share no discriminating vocabulary. We operationalize the mechanism as an architecture, the Abduction Loop: representation generation, motif extraction, convention-space canonicalization, cross-domain retrieval, identity-hypothesis generation, and adversarial verification, with abstention as the designed default. A documented episode, in which a multimodal model given a figure of a gravitational-memory transport model generated and then verified the hypothesis that its central differential complex is equivalent to the spherical Kaiser-Squires mass-mapping complex of weak-lensing cosmology, serves as a motivating possibility witness from which the architecture is abstracted, not as evidence of general capability. We close with a falsifiable evaluation program, the DAB-30 benchmark. The contribution is a mechanistic proposal, an architecture, and a test program.

</details>


### [242] [Optimizing Minimax Regret in Uncertain MDPs with Small Sets of Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02509)
*Sterre Lutz,Daniël Vos,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Anna Lukina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出k-adaptable策略合成，在模型不确定性很快消除且只能预备有限策略集的场景下，基于最小化最大后悔进行优化；证明问题为NP-hard，给出精确的嵌套分支定界算法KAPS（含特定界和启发式），实验表明从1到2个策略的收益最大，KAPS在单策略情形下与现有方法竞争并更常证明最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实顺序决策中常遇到环境模型不确定，但模型会在执行前很快被明确，同时受制于部署/操作/可解释性等约束只能准备少量策略；因此需要在有限策略集中预先合成能应对不同可能环境的策略集合，最小化在最坏情况下的后悔。

Method: 形式化问题为在给定的UMDP（有有限候选MDP）中，预先合成k个策略并在执行前根据解析到的真实MDP从中选择一个，目标是最小化最大后悔。证明NP-hard性；提出KAPS，一种嵌套的分支与定界算法，利用问题结构设计上界与下界以及启发式分支规则，联合优化MDP的分配（哪些MDP共享同一策略）和对应策略参数；在多种基准上与现有方法比较性能与证明最优性的能力。

Result: 对论文摘要的综合分析

Conclusion: k-adaptable策略合成在受限策略数的适应性场景中是一个合理且有力的形式化方法——在理论上为NP-hard，但提供了精确可行的解法（KAPS）并在多数基准上表现良好；在工程上通常两套策略就能显著降低后悔，后续工作应关注可扩展性、近似算法与更一般的不确定性模型。

Abstract: Sequential decision-making in real-world applications often involves uncertainty about the environment's model. Uncertain Markov decision processes (UMDPs) represent the possible environments as a set of MDPs with shared states and actions but potentially different transition probabilities and rewards. Optimizing a single policy across all possible MDPs may sacrifice performance, while preparing an individually optimized policy for every MDP may violate operational, regulatory, or interpretability constraints on the number of policies that can be prepared and deployed. We consider settings in which model uncertainty is resolved shortly before execution, allowing the most suitable policy to be selected from a limited set prepared in advance. We introduce $k$-adaptable policy synthesis, which optimizes such a set of $k$ policies under a minimax-regret objective. We prove that the problem is NP-hard and develop KAPS, an exact nested branch-and-bound algorithm with problem-specific bounds and heuristics. KAPS jointly optimizes which MDPs share a policy and the policies themselves. Experiments across various UMDP benchmarks show that the largest reduction in regret consistently occurs when increasing from one to two policies. In the single-policy setting, KAPS is competitive with existing methods in solution quality and proves optimality substantially more often.

</details>


### [243] [Magnet: Detecting Cross-Session AI Misuse Through Capability Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02518)
*Natalie Isak,Matthew Dressman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 攻击者可把有害目标拆成看似无害的多次 agent 会话以规避检测；Magnet 通过在更高层级（如用户ID）汇聚跨会话产出的能力证据，能检测到这种分散式规避行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的滥用检测方法主要面向单次会话或单模型场景，无法应对由多 agent 协同与跨会话目标分解带来的规避攻击风险，需要新的监测框架来识别分散在多次会话中的“能力碎片”。

Method: 定义“能力”作为可组合的产出单元；通过对比实验或示例展示跨会话分解攻击如何逐步获得比单会话更强的能力；设计 Magnet 框架：对会话产出进行特征化、跨会话索引/吸引（按用户ID等关联器）、构建紧凑证据包并交由检测器判断。

Result: 一方面证明了跨会话目标分解能够比等价的单会话/多轮攻击获得更强的有害能力（通过分布式积累可组合的‘能力’证据）；另一方面提出了 Magnet——一种在用户级别聚合跨会话能力证据的高效检测方案，能把零散的有害线索吸引成紧凑的证据包供检测器使用。

Conclusion: 跨会话的能力积累是现实且危险的规避路径，必须把检测粒度从会话级上移到用户/相关体级；Magnet 展示了可行的证据聚合思路，不过需在隐私、可扩展性和误报控制上做进一步评估与工程化。

Abstract: The most capable AI deployments are not single models but ensembles of specialized agents that delegate and act in coordination. This architecture unlocks powerful new capabilities, and it also introduces risks that existing frameworks for monitoring, detection, and mitigation were not designed to address. Most state-of-the-art AI abuse detection literature focuses on single-turn or multi-turn (single-session) threat models. This leaves a critical gap: an attacker can decompose a harmful goal into innocuous-looking units and execute each in isolated agentic sessions. The agent is stateless between conversations, but the attacker is not. This asymmetry allows for cross-session trajectories that are effective at evading detection. Our contributions are twofold. First, we demonstrate cross-session goal decomposition as an evasion technique, showing it may elicit more harmful capability than equivalent single-session or multi-turn attacks. By capability we mean an artifact produced at one step of an objective, evidenced by what an interaction produced (model responses and tool-call results), and composable with capabilities accrued elsewhere into a harmful whole. Second, we propose Magnet: an efficient and robust detection approach that models relevant capabilities accrued over time and across agentic conversations, aggregated at a higher-level correlator (in this case, a user ID) rather than per-conversation state. The main challenge is assembling the evidence bundle Magnet reasons over. The incriminating artifacts may be needles scattered through a haystack of benign sessions that are individually harmless, dangerous only once collected. Rather than searching the haystack straw-by-straw (i.e. per-session inspection), Magnet does what its name implies: it attracts the relevant needles out of the hay, across sessions and across time, into a compact evidence bundle a detector can act on.

</details>


### [244] [A Taxonomy of Cognitive Capability Gaps in Generative and Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02553)
*Taye Akinrele,Sindhuja Penchala,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个以分类学为主导的综述，把影响‘认知型AI’发展的关键能力缺口组织为五个维度，回顾每一维度的进展与局限，提出概念性架构ACIA并讨论评估方向，旨在为长期可靠的推理、自适应决策与持续学习提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clarify and organize the fragmented landscape of cognitive capabilities in AI, identify capability gaps that limit sustained, reliable operation over long time horizons, and provide a roadmap toward more cognitively capable systems.

Method: 对相关文献进行分类学驱动的系统综述：围绕五大维度梳理研究进展、识别重复性限制、总结开放挑战；在此基础上提出概念架构并讨论评估指标与方向。

Result: A taxonomy of five cognitive dimensions (persistent state modeling, goal-directed autonomy, self-monitoring and control, environment interaction, learning and adaptation), a survey of recent advances and limitations, proposal of an Adaptive Cognitive Intelligence Architecture (ACIA), and directions for cognition-centric evaluation and future research toward Cognitive AI/AGI.

Conclusion: 该工作整合现有研究，强调需在持久状态建模、目标导向自治、自我监控、环境交互与持续学习五方面取得突破，并以ACIA与评估框架引导未来系统设计，推动从生成/代理能力向更完整的认知能力演进。

Abstract: Cognitive AI seeks to move beyond language generation and autonomous task execution toward systems capable of sustained reasoning, adaptive behavior, persistent memory, and self-regulation. While generative and agentic AI have demonstrated impressive capabilities across a wide range of tasks, many fundamental cognitive functions remain fragmented or weakly developed, limiting reliable operation over extended time horizons. This paper presents a taxonomy-driven survey of the major cognitive capability gaps that continue to constrain the development of Cognitive AI. The literature is organized around five dimensions: persistent state modeling, goal-directed autonomy, self-monitoring and control, environment interaction, and learning and adaptation. For each dimension, we review recent advances, identify recurring limitations, and discuss open research challenges. Building on these insights, we outline a conceptual Adaptive Cognitive Intelligence Architecture (ACIA) and examine emerging directions in cognition-centric evaluation. The proposed taxonomy provides a unified framework for organizing existing research, identifying unresolved challenges, and guiding the design of future cognitively capable systems. Together, the taxonomy, architectural perspective, and evaluation framework offer a roadmap for advancing AI systems that exhibit more reliable long-term reasoning, adaptive decision-making, and continual learning. The survey highlights key research opportunities toward more adaptive, reliable, and cognitively capable AI systems, providing a foundation for future progress toward Cognitive AI and, ultimately, Artificial General Intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [245] [AtumAI: A Principled Framework for Agentic Generation of Datacenter Control-Plane Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02569)
*Qiushi Lin,Chaojie Zhang,Íñigo Goiri,Aditya Akella,Ricardo Bianchini,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtumAI formalizes datacenter policy synthesis and combines generative models, evolutionary search, and surrogate modeling to autonomously produce high-performing control-plane policies from natural-language requests, validated on three tasks with better-than-expert results.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Datacenter control-plane policy design is increasingly complex, slow to prototype, and interdependent across layers; off-the-shelf agentic LLM approaches lack formal task representations, problem-to-problem transfer, and systematic, broad exploration of the design space. AtumAI aims to make policy synthesis formal, transferable, and systematic.

Method: AtumAI combines a Datacenter Task Compiler that translates natural-language goals into a formal, machine-checkable task specification (objectives, constraints, decision variables, evaluation protocol) with an Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop that searches the specification space using a diffusion model to generate candidates, an evolutionary algorithm to explore and recombine designs, and a surrogate model to predict performance and guide search. The loop autonomously proposes, tests, and refines policies until constraints and goals are met.

Result: AtumAI reduces task onboarding from months to writing a natural-language description and, when evaluated on three distinct control-plane tasks (workload placement, resource scaling, power management), generated policies that consistently outperform expert-engineered baselines.

Conclusion: The framework shows promise to accelerate datacenter policy design and broaden search beyond LLM-only candidate generation; however, key details (specification language, surrogate fidelity, evaluation cost, and generalization limits) remain to be fully disclosed and tested for real-world deployment.

Abstract: The efficiency of a datacenter rests on its control plane policies. Designing these policies is increasingly hard: the hardware-software stack grows fast, the design space is vast and interdependent, and prototyping a single policy takes months. Agentic AI promises to automate this search. Off the shelf, however, it falls short on three fronts. It is not formal: with no structured, searchable statement of the problem, the search has little structure to exploit and hard constraints are not guaranteed. It is not transferable: each task is solved from scratch, so nothing learned on one task carries to the next. Finally, it is not systematic: relying on the LLM as the sole source of candidates, it explores a narrow slice of the design space and settles into local optima. We introduce AtumAI, a framework that generates datacenter control-plane policies with agentic AI, making the process formal, transferable, and systematic. From a goal stated in plain language, AtumAI autonomously proposes, tests, and refines candidate policies until one satisfies the request. It does so through two components. The Datacenter Task Compiler automates problem formulation: it compiles the request into a formal, machine-checkable, and searchable specification of the task's objectives, constraints, decision variables, and evaluation methodology. The Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop then searches this specification, expanding the search beyond the LLM itself via a diffusion model, an evolutionary algorithm, and a surrogate model. Together, they reduce onboarding a new task from months of engineering to writing its description. We evaluate AtumAI on three control-plane tasks with distinct problem scopes, design spaces, and trade-offs: workload placement, resource scaling, and power management. Across all tasks, the policies generated by AtumAI consistently outperform expert-engineered baselines.

</details>
